Abstract: Breast ultrasound imaging plays an important role in the early detection and diagnosis of breast cancer, particularly for patients with dense breast tissue. However, developing reliable deep learning models for ultrasound analysis is challenging due to limited annotated medical data and the need for interpretable predictions. To address these challenges, this paper proposes ProtoCAM, an explainable few-shot learning framework for breast lesion classification that integrates mask-guided feature encoding, prototypical metric learning, and gradient-based visual explanations. The proposed approach leverages lesion masks to guide feature extraction and constructs class prototypes within an embedding space to enable robust classification under limited training samples. The framework was evaluated on the BUSI dataset using a stratified group k-fold cross-validation protocol to prevent patient-level data leakage. Experimental results demonstrate ProtoCAM's high performance in low-data scenarios. In a 3-way 5-shot setting, the proposed method achieves a macro F1-score of 0.910, representing a substantial improvement over standard supervised CNN models. Among the evaluated backbone networks, ResNet18 achieved the best performance, reaching a macro F1-score of 91.65% under a 15-shot configuration, providing interpretable insights into the classification decisions. These results highlight the potential of explainable few-shot learning frameworks for reliable computer-aided breast cancer diagnosis in data-scarce medical imaging environments.

</details>


### [25] [ConeGaussian: Anti-Aliased Gaussian Ray-Tracing for Generic Central Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13397)
*Deheng Zhang,Letian Shi,Runyi Yang,Zhendong Li,Lei Sun,Kanzhi Wu,Ajad Chhatkuli,Danda Pani Paudel,Luc Van Gool*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ConeGaussian：一种与相机模型无关的高斯光线渲染抗锯齿框架，直接从相机原生逆映射生成的相邻光线构建各向异性像素足迹，实现跨标定中心相机模型与多种高斯光线渲染骨干的统一滤波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯光线追踪虽通过逆射线映射支持带光心的通用相机，但通常将每个像素简化为单条中心光线，忽略了相机相关的像素足迹。这导致缩小(minification)时出现锯齿，而放大(magnification)时无约束高斯又暴露不支持的频率，缺乏统一且忠实的渲染公式。

Method: ConeGaussian不在相机特定图像平面上定义像素滤波器，而是直接从相机原生逆映射产生的相邻光线构建各向异性足迹。在有限像素足迹的局部线性、深度局部、矩匹配近似下推导闭式响应；同一几何同时定义每个高斯的训练频率下限。

Result: 在针孔与强畸变鱼眼拍摄上，ConeGaussian一致地改进了两种不同的光线渲染骨干，1/8分辨率下最高提升4.3 dB；在透视屏幕平面足迹公式无法直接适用的鱼眼场景中，将LPIPS降低30%。

Conclusion: 该滤波原理按构造可无修改地用于各种标定中心相机模型和多种高斯光线渲染骨干；与mip-splatting不同，其场景空间频率下限与滤波支持渲染时轻松组合，可去除多余模糊。

Abstract: In rendering, a camera is a sampling operator that maps each finite pixel to a bundle of rays. Different camera models change the geometry of this bundle, thus making a unified and faithful rendering formulation challenging. Consequently, Gaussian ray tracing supports generic cameras (with optical center) through their inverse ray mappings, yet typically reduces every pixel to a single center ray. This ignores the camera-dependent pixel footprint, causing aliasing under minification, while unconstrained Gaussians expose unsupported frequencies under magnification. We present ConeGaussian, a camera-model-agnostic anti-aliasing framework for Gaussian ray-based rendering. Instead of defining the pixel filter on a camera-specific image plane, ConeGaussian constructs an anisotropic footprint directly from neighboring rays produced by the camera's native inverse mapping. We derive a closed-form response under a locally linear, depth-local, moment-matched approximation of the finite pixel footprint, while the same geometry defines a per-Gaussian training-frequency floor. Notably, by construction, our filtering principle can be used unmodified across calibrated central camera models and multiple Gaussian ray-rendering backbones. Additionally, unlike in mip-splatting, our scene-space frequency floor and filtering enable trivial composition at render time, allowing us to remove excess blurring. On pinhole and strongly distorted fisheye captures, ConeGaussian consistently improves two distinct ray-based backbones, by up to 4.3 dB at 1/8 resolution, and reduces fisheye LPIPS by 30% where perspective screen-plane footprint formulations are not directly applicable.

</details>


### [26] [RIGOR: Rig-Informed Geometry for Omnidirectional Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13504)
*Tingjun Huang,Dmitry Rudshin,Mathieu Meyer,Pietro Bonazzi,Marc Pollefeys,Emilia Szymańska*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RIGOR 是一个针对重力对齐全景视频的大规模重建流水线，复用冻结的前馈透视骨干网络，将每张全景图视为四视角虚拟相机架，以检测并修复局部不一致、检索回环并几何验证重访，最终用 Sim(3) 位姿图优化修正漂移，在困难施工场地序列上提升轨迹与几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式三维重建虽能仅凭图像流恢复稠密场景与相机运动，但在长轨迹及重复结构、弱纹理、动态物体等困难环境下易出现不一致。全景相机可提供更宽覆盖与更丰富视觉信息，但多数模型不支持 360 度影像或需额外微调，因此需要弥合这两方面。

Method: 提出 RIGOR：面向重力对齐全景视频的大规模重建流水线。保留冻结的前馈透视骨干网络，将每张全景图作为四视角虚拟相机架使用；利用该结构检测并修复局部不一致预测，通过循环四视角一致性检索回环，并在全局优化前对候选重访进行几何验证；验证后的约束驱动 Sim(3) 位姿图，修正累积的旋转、平移与尺度漂移。

Result: 在具有挑战性的施工场地序列上，所提一致性机制相较前馈基线同时提升了轨迹精度与重建几何质量。

Conclusion: RIGOR 通过四视角虚拟相机架的一致性机制与 Sim(3) 位姿图优化，使冻结的前馈透视模型无需微调即可用于全景视频重建，并改善长序列漂移问题；代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent developments in feed-forward 3D reconstruction resulted in models which can recover dense scene representations and camera motion solely from an image stream. However, such predictions are prone to becoming inconsistent over long trajectories, specifically in demanding environments with repetitive structures, weak textures and dynamic objects or people. One way to mitigate those challenges is to use an omnidirectional camera, which provides wide spatial coverage and captures richer visual information. Yet, the majority of models do not offer support for 360-degree imagery or require additional fine-tuning. To bridge these two aspects, we present RIGOR: a large-scale reconstruction pipeline for gravity-aligned omnidirectional videos that retains a frozen feed-forward perspective backbone and exploits each panorama as a four-view virtual rig. The rig structure is used to detect and repair locally inconsistent predictions, to retrieve loop closures through cyclic four-view consensus, and to geometrically verify candidate revisits before global optimization. Verified constraints drive a Sim(3) pose graph that corrects accumulated rotation, translation, and scale drift along the sequence. We demonstrate that the proposed consistency mechanisms improve both trajectory accuracy and reconstructed geometry over a feed-forward baseline on challenging construction-site sequences. The code is made available under this link: https://github.com/TangentH/RIGOR.

</details>


### [27] [Towards Practical Precision Agriculture: Real-Time Fruit Detection and Video Analytics on Embedded Edge Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13551)
*Ivica Dimitrovski,Vlatko Spasev,Ivan Kitanovski,Petre Lameski,Dane Boshev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究构建了面向果园视频分析的端到端实时框架，在Jetson Orin Nano Super上实现水果检测、跟踪与计数，并系统评估了精度、速度、能耗及采集几何对跟踪计数的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态图像基准无法反映实际果园视频分析中的计算与时间需求，需要面向嵌入式边缘平台的实时检测-跟踪-计数一体化方案，并明确不同采集几何对时序关联性能的影响。

Method: 在苹果、芒果、蓝莓、草莓四个公开数据集上以统一协议独立训练轻量YOLO26s检测器；用PyTorch和TensorRT在FP32、FP16、INT8精度下部署于Jetson Orin Nano Super；选用APPLE MOTS进行时序视频分析；将FP16 TensorRT检测器集成到NVIDIA DeepStream管线，结合硬件加速解码、ByteTrack跟踪和运动感知越线计数。

Result: 四个检测任务测试mAP@50:95为0.4957-0.8656；TensorRT FP16在Jetson上达66.76-74.56 images/s、延迟13.41-14.98 ms，相比PyTorch FP32仅降低mAP@50:95约0.0020-0.0054，总能耗降低约64-66%；完整管线达44.96-54.11 FPS，可稳定维持30 FPS输入且无丢帧。留出果园视频上HOTA为0.345-0.538，事件级计数F1为0.611-0.803，相对计数误差6.2%-51.6%；近侧向行视角跟踪计数最稳定，前向行进受关联与召回限制。

Conclusion: 实用的边缘端水果监测需要高效检测器与有利于可靠时序关联的采集几何，仅靠空间自适应计数几何不足以弥补前向行进的关联与召回短板。

Abstract: Static-image benchmarks do not capture the computational and temporal requirements of practical orchard video analytics. This study presents an end-to-end framework for real-time fruit detection, tracking, and counting on the NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano Super. A lightweight YOLO26s detector is trained independently on four public datasets representing apples, mangoes, blueberries, and strawberries under a common protocol. The models are deployed on embedded platform using PyTorch and TensorRT at FP32, FP16, and INT8 precision. APPLE MOTS is then used for temporal video analytics because it provides orchard sequences with persistent fruit identities, enabling evaluation of multi-object tracking and unique-fruit counting. The selected FP16 TensorRT detector is integrated into an NVIDIA DeepStream pipeline combining hardware-accelerated decoding, ByteTrack tracking, and motion-aware line-crossing analytics. Across the four detection tasks, mean test mAP@50:95 ranges from 0.4957 to 0.8656. On the Jetson, TensorRT FP16 achieves 66.76-74.56 images/s at 13.41-14.98 ms prediction latency, while reducing mAP@50:95 by only 0.0020-0.0054 and gross energy consumption by approximately 64-66% relative to PyTorch FP32. The complete detector-tracker-analytics pipeline reaches 44.96-54.11 FPS and sustains the configured 30-FPS input rate without output-frame loss. On held-out orchard video sequences, HOTA ranges from 0.345 to 0.538, event-level counting F1 from 0.611 to 0.803, and relative count error from 6.2% to 51.6%. Performance varies across acquisition geometries: near-lateral row viewing yields the most stable tracking and counting, whereas forward traversal remains association- and recall-limited despite spatially adaptive counting geometry. These results show that practical edge-based fruit monitoring requires efficient detection and acquisition geometries that support reliable temporal association.

</details>


### [28] [From Advertised Improvements to Measured Capabilities: Evaluating ChatGPT Images 2.5 on Forgery Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13617)
*Ankit Raj,Yuxin Zhang,Kidus Zewde,Tommy Duong,Jiaqi Gan,Xingyu Shen,Yuchen Zhou,Huaiyu Guo,Siyu Zhang,Simiao Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估 ChatGPT Images 2.5 在既定答案的伪造任务上是否优于 GPT-Image-2，发现其在减少周边文本改动和提升产品编码可读性方面有所改善，但目标字段正确性未提升，细字渲染仍难判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对厂商宣称的图像生成能力提升，作者希望检验这些改进在具有确定答案的伪造类任务中是否真实转化为性能提升，并探讨自动检测手段能得出何种结论及其局限。

Method: 在同一周内对比 Flare 与 Sunburst API 模型和 GPT-Image-2，任务包括收据字段编辑、重复编辑、产品植入与细字渲染；经图像配准后，用 OCR 检测周边收据文本变化，并考察目标字段正确性、早期编辑保留、产品编码可读性与尺寸、拒答与定位表现，以及固定阈值下 Community Forensics 的检出率。

Result: Flare 与 Sunburst 对周边收据文本的 OCR 检出改动更少（31.7% 和 31.2%，对比两个 GPT-Image-2 基线的 44.2%），主要体现在 CORD 收据上，但目标字段正确性无可检测提升；Flare 在 CORD 收据上保留更少早期编辑，照片编辑序列区分度低；产品编码更常可读且产品植入更大，但无法确立独立于尺寸的保真度提升；细字改进受 OCR 可靠性限制未能判定；两代模型拒答均罕见、定位能力弱。固定阈值下，Community Forensics 对受控 Images 2.5 图像平均检出 68.6%，而网络自报图像为 35.9%。

Conclusion: 广告宣称的能力提升并非普遍成立，需针对具体任务进行评估，并对自动检查所能确立的结论设定明确边界。

Abstract: We evaluate whether the improvements advertised for ChatGPT Images 2.5 translate into better performance on forgery tasks with predetermined answers. We compare its Flare and Sunburst API models with GPT-Image-2 re-run in the same week, using receipt-field edits, repeated editing, product placement and fine-print rendering. After image registration, Flare and Sunburst show fewer OCR-detected changes to surrounding receipt text (31.7% and 31.2% versus 44.2% for both GPT-Image-2 baselines), mainly on CORD receipts, without a detectable improvement in target-field correctness. Flare retains fewer earlier edits on CORD receipts, while photo-edit sequences provide little separation between models. Product codes are more often legible with Images 2.5, alongside larger product placement; the analyses do not establish a fidelity gain independent of size. Fine-print improvements remain unresolved below the OCR reliability limit. Refusals are rare and localisation is weak in both generations. At a fixed detection threshold, Community Forensics flags 68.6% of controlled Images 2.5 images averaged across cells, versus 35.9% of self-reported images posted online. These results motivate task-specific evaluation of advertised capabilities and defences, with explicit limits on what automatic checks can establish.

</details>


### [29] [YOLO12-MambaScan: An Efficient Object Detector with High-Frequency Enhancement and State-Space Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13647)
*Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于YOLO12的航拍图像检测器，融合高频增强卷积、感受野坐标注意力卷积和Mamba全局上下文模块，在VisDrone上实现60.0% mAP@50和38.6% mAP@50:95。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机航拍图像中的小目标检测困难，原因在于目标像素极少、高频线索易丢失、杂乱场景中全局上下文难以建模，且现有检测器保留的边缘、角点和纹理信息不足。

Method: 在YOLO12架构基础上，提出三路径高频增强卷积模块（TriPathHFConv）、感受野坐标注意力卷积（RFCAConv）以及基于Mamba的全局上下文模块。

Result: 在VisDrone数据集、960×960输入分辨率下，达到60.0% mAP@50和38.6% mAP@50:95，在精度与效率之间取得良好平衡。

Conclusion: 该方法面向航拍小目标检测，展现了较优的精度—效率权衡，实验遵循VisDrone挑战赛的基准与数据集协议。

Abstract: The rapid development of unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) technology has made aerial-image object detection increasingly important for natural-resource monitoring, traffic management, and disaster response. Detecting small objects in aerial images remains difficult because objects occupy very few pixels, high-frequency cues are easily lost, and global context is hard to model in cluttered scenes. Existing detectors often retain insufficient edge, corner, and texture information. We propose \ours, an aerial-image detector built on the YOLO12 architecture. The model combines a triple-path high-frequency enhancement convolution module (TriPathHFConv), receptive-field coordinate-attention convolution (RFCAConv), and a Mamba-based global-context module. On VisDrone, at an input resolution of 960*960, ours achieves 60.0% mAP@50 and 38.6%mAP@50:95, demonstrating a favorable accuracy--efficiency trade-off for small-object detection. The benchmark and dataset protocol follow the VisDrone challenge setup.

</details>


### [30] [Multimodal Foundation Models Adaptation based on Domain-Aware Relaxed Orthogonal Subspace for Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13654)
*Han Luo,Ruoyu Yang,Yinhe Liu,Yanfei Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DROS框架，将低秩适配重构为数据驱动的子空间学习与灵活子空间适配，解决遥感任务中的子空间不匹配问题，并扩展至多模态设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练基础模型微调成本高，LoRA等PEFT方法虽高效，但其固定低秩子空间未观测下游激活分布，在域偏移显著的遥感任务中提供劣质坐标系，即"子空间不匹配"现象。

Method: DROS将权重分解条件化于下游训练分布估计的二阶激活统计量，使初始化反映遥感数据诱导的特征几何，并通过松弛正交参数化实现灵活几何变换；进一步提出MM-DROS，在多模态特定子空间间共享变换结构以促进跨模态交互。

Result: 在多个遥感基准上，DROS达到最优性能，甚至超越全量微调，且不增加额外推理开销。

Conclusion: 数据条件化的子空间学习与灵活子空间适配能有效缓解子空间不匹配，为遥感及多模态PEFT提供统一且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Pretrained foundation models (FMs) have achieved remarkable success in computer vision, yet their high fine-tuning cost limits practical deployment. Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) improve efficiency by constraining updates to a predefined low-rank subspace. However, when applied to remote sensing tasks with substantial domain shifts, the fixed subspace is constructed without observing the downstream activation distribution and can therefore provide a poor coordinate system for adaptation, a phenomenon herein termed subspace mismatch. To address this issue, a unified framework is introduced, termed Domain-aware Relaxed Orthogonal Subspace adaptation (DROS), which reformulates low-rank adaptation as data-conditioned subspace learning and flexible subspace adaptation. Specifically, the weight decomposition is conditioned on second-order activation statistics estimated from the downstream training distribution, so that the initialization reflects the feature geometry actually induced by the remote-sensing data, followed by flexible geometric transformations enabled by a relaxed orthogonal parameterization. Furthermore, the framework is extended to multimodal settings (MM-DROS) by sharing transformation structures across modality-specific subspaces, facilitating efficient cross-modal interaction. Extensive experiments on multiple remote sensing benchmarks demonstrate that DROS achieves state-of-the-art performance, even surpassing full fine-tuning, without additional inference overhead.

</details>


### [31] [MARC: Morphology-Aware Regression of Consensus for Cell Segmentation in Subcellular Spatial Transcriptomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13665)
*Xinyu Shu,Andrew Zhang,Jean Yang,Jinman Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MARC框架通过形态感知回归预测多方法共识支持图，无需多方法推理即可逼近显式跨方法共识，提升SST细胞分割评估的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SST中细胞分割是构建细胞级表示的基础，但边界错误会导致转录本错误分配。由于形态和转录信号不完整，可靠的ground-truth边界无法获得，人工标注大量细胞耗时；而显式构建多方法共识需执行多个计算昂贵的流程。因此需要一种能预测共识支持图的方法。

Method: 提出MARC（形态感知共识回归）框架，预测SST分割的多方法共识支持图。训练采用留一方法共识伪标签（leave-one-method-out consensus pseudo-targets）和前景并集共识损失（Foreground-Union Consensus Loss），将监督集中于候选与共识前景区域。

Result: 在Xenium肾脏组织的4,642个留出切片块上评估，平均Dice为0.90，平均IoU为0.82，细胞级Spearman相关为0.79（对比显式计算的跨方法共识图）。预测的共识图能定位弱支持区域，保持基于共识的排序，并识别低共识细胞以供人工复核。

Conclusion: MARC在不进行多方法推理的情况下紧密逼近显式跨方法共识，有望促进大规模SST研究中细胞分割的稳健、共识感知评估。

Abstract: Accurate cell segmentation remains a major bottleneck in subcellular spatial transcriptomics (SST), in which morphological images and spatially resolved RNA transcripts are used to partition tissues into individual cellular instances. As segmentation serves as the foundation for constructing cell-level representations, boundary errors can lead to incorrect transcript assignments and compromise downstream analyses. However, reliable ground-truth boundaries are unavailable because they must be inferred from incomplete morphological and transcript signals. Furthermore, manual annotation of a large number of cells is time-consuming. Agreement among complementary segmentation methods provides a practical surrogate for identifying well-supported and ambiguous regions, but explicit consensus construction requires executing multiple computationally intensive pipelines. In this study, we propose MARC (Morphology-Aware Regression of Consensus), a framework that predicts a multi-method consensus-support map for SST segmentation. MARC is trained with leave-one-method-out consensus pseudo-targets and a Foreground-Union Consensus Loss that focuses supervision on candidate and consensus foreground. We evaluated MARC on 4,642 held-out tiles from Xenium kidney tissue, achieving a mean Dice score of 0.90, a mean intersection-over-union of 0.82, and a mean cell-level Spearman correlation of 0.79 against explicitly computed cross-method consensus maps. We demonstrate that the predicted consensus maps localise weakly supported regions while preserving consensus-based rankings and identifying low-consensus cells for manual review. These results show that MARC closely approximates explicit cross-method consensus without multi-method inference and therefore has the potential to facilitate robust, consensus-aware evaluation of cell segmentation in large-scale SST studies.

</details>


### [32] [Rank-Consistent Set Reasoning for Co-Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13706)
*Yuan Xiang,Matteo Rossi,Yingzhou Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Rank-Consistent Set Reasoning（RCSR）监督式密集预测框架，将图像组建模为无序集合，通过多尺度组槽排序与稳健修剪统计实现共显著目标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共显著目标检测需要同时识别单图显著且被整组图像支持的前景区域。现有方法常将图像组建模为序列或依赖语义标签，易受偶然成对匹配和个别异常成员影响。

Method: RCSR 在每个尺度上对空间区域与少量学习到的组槽之间的一致性进行排序，并用稳健修剪统计聚合排名；集合编码器从多尺度视觉特征构建组槽，秩一致性门控衡量候选区域排序在组内成员间的稳定性，门控槽与单图特征联合解码生成共显著图。模型不含自然语言分支、开放词汇检测器或外部分割模型。还引入组置换目标和难干扰物增强。

Result: 在 CoCA、CoSal2015、CoSOD3k 上制定了评估协议，并进行了组大小鲁棒性、干扰物抑制、顺序不变性和跨数据集迁移测试。

Conclusion: RCSR 以无序集合推理和秩一致性门控为核心，减少偶然匹配与异常成员主导问题，不依赖语言或外部模型，并提供较全面的共显著检测评测维度。

Abstract: Co-salient object detection (Co-SOD) requires a model to find foreground regions that are salient in individual images and supported by the image group. We present \emph{Rank-Consistent Set Reasoning} (RCSR), a supervised dense-prediction framework that models a group as an unordered set rather than as a sequence of images or a semantic label. The core idea is to rank how strongly each spatial region agrees with a small collection of learned group slots at every image scale, and to aggregate these ranks with a robust trimmed statistic. This suppresses accidental pairwise matches and prevents one atypical group member from dominating the shared representation. A set encoder builds group slots directly from multi-scale visual features, while a rank-consistency gate measures whether the ordering of candidate regions is stable across group members. The gated slots are decoded jointly with per-image features to produce co-saliency maps. The model contains no natural-language branch, no open-vocabulary detector, and no external segmentation model. We further introduce a group permutation objective and hard-distractor augmentation so that the model learns the properties of a set-level target rather than memorizing image order or isolated visual saliency. We formulate an evaluation protocol for CoCA, CoSal2015, and CoSOD3k, together with tests of group-size robustness, distractor rejection, order invariance, and cross-dataset transfer.

</details>


### [33] [Hyper-LLaVA: Hyperbolic Uncertainty-aware Modality-Balanced Routing for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13742)
*Kunlun Xu,Yanqin Zhang,Wenwen Qiang,Jiahuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Hyper-LLaVA，通过双曲空间分布相似度与模态不确定性建模改进多模态持续指令微调中的参数路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态持续指令微调方法依赖"样本到任务中心相似度"及等权重的跨模态融合进行参数路由，存在两大缺陷：(1) 单模态内仅用样本到任务中心的距离会忽略任务内丰富的多样性信息，路由次优；(2) 不同模态在各任务上可靠性不同，存在任务间歧义的模态容易误导路由结果。

Method: 提出Hyperbolic Uncertainty-aware Modality-Balanced Routing（Hyper-LLaVA），基于跨模态任务特征不确定性建模提升路由能力：一是在双曲空间中度量样本到任务的分布相似度，改善模态内任务匹配；二是量化各模态内的任务匹配歧义，实现模态间任务匹配的自适应平衡。

Result: 结合模态内与模态间任务匹配的互补增强，Hyper-LLaVA大幅超越现有最先进方法。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 从分布相似度与不确定性平衡两个角度重构参数路由，可有效提升多模态持续指令微调的性能。

Abstract: Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT) aims to exploit the incrementally accumulated knowledge to process multimodal inputs of diverse tasks, where parameter routing plays an important role. State-of-the-art methods rely on sample-to-task center similarity and cross-modal fusion with equal weight during routing. However, such solutions face two fundamental flaws: (1) Within each modality, the sample-to-task center distance is sub-optimal for routing since the abundant intra-task diversity information is underleveraged. (2) Different modalities exhibit varying reliability across tasks, where the modality with inter-task ambiguity can easily misguide the routing result. To address these problems, we propose Hyperbolic Uncertainty-aware Modality-Balanced Routing (Hyper-LLaVA) to improve parameter routing capacity based on cross-modality task feature uncertainty modeling. Specifically, to improve intra-modality task matching, Hyper-LLaVA accesses the sample-to-task distribution similarity in the Hyperbolic space. Besides, to alleviate the degradation brought by unreliable modalities, Hyper-LLaVA quantifies the task matching ambiguity within each modality to achieve adaptive balancing between task matching across modalities. Based on the complementary intra- and inter-modality task matching enhancement, our Hyper-LLaVA outperforms state-of-the-art approaches by large margins. Our source code is available at https://github.com/zhoujiahuan1991/ICML2026-Hyper-LLaVA

</details>


### [34] [GEAR: From Dynamic Encoding to Dynamic Activation in Social Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13778)
*Jiaheng Chen,Jiaxing Li,Leixia Wang,Jianan Ju,Tinghe Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出GEAR模型，通过生成感知的偏置激活机制，动态调控个体运动与社会交互信息在未来轨迹生成中的作用强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行人轨迹预测方法多关注社会信息如何被编码，而忽视编码后的社会上下文在轨迹生成阶段应如何生效。作者指出，动态社会编码不等于动态社会激活：相同交互上下文在不同未来时段与场景密度下需要不同的激活强度，交互证据强时应增强社会线索，弱或嘈杂时应抑制。

Method: 提出GEAR（生成感知偏置激活模型）。基于偏置分解的轨迹生成公式，在每个未来时间步对个体运动偏置项与社会共振偏置项进行动态激活，再进行最终轨迹合成，从而显式控制个体与社会偏置分量何时参与生成以及以多强强度参与。

Result: 在ETH-UCY、SDD和NBA数据集上，GEAR持续优于基于共振的基线，并达到有竞争力的先进水平；激活模式分析与按密度分组误差分析验证了在轨迹生成中校准编码社会上下文的重要性。

Conclusion: 仅编码社会上下文不足以保证其有效利用，需在生成阶段对其进行自适应激活与校准；GEAR通过生成感知的偏置激活实现该目标，并取得良好性能与可解释性验证。

Abstract: Human trajectory prediction requires modeling both individual motion patterns and social interactions among agents. Existing methods have made substantial progress by using attention mechanisms, graph structures, and temporal encoders to capture dynamic social context. However, most of them primarily focus on how social information is encoded, while paying less explicit attention to how the encoded social context should take effect during future trajectory generation. In this paper, we argue that dynamic social encoding does not necessarily imply dynamic social activation. The same interaction context may require different activation strengths across future horizons and scene densities: social cues should be strengthened when interaction evidence is strong, but suppressed when they are weak or noisy. To address this issue, we propose GEAR, a generation-aware bias activation model for human trajectory prediction. Built upon a bias-decomposed trajectory generation formulation, GEAR dynamically activates the individual-motion and social-resonance bias terms at each future step before final trajectory composition. This allows the model to explicitly control when and how strongly individual and social bias components participate in generation. Experiments on ETH-UCY, SDD, and NBA show that GEAR consistently improves the resonance-based baseline and achieves competitive state-of-the-art performance. Further analyses of activation patterns and density-grouped errors validate the importance of calibrating encoded social context during trajectory generation. Our code is available at https://github.com/11isnotavailable/GEAR.git.

</details>


### [35] [StepPrune: Adaptive Sequential Visual Token Selection across Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13804)
*Hansen Zhang,Landi He,Mingde Yao,Lijian Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 StepPrune，将视觉 token 剪枝建模为自适应序列决策过程，通过 STOP 动作自动确定保留数量，在多个 MLLM 上实现高效加速且性能损失小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型中视觉前缀占每层计算的主要部分，剪枝视觉 token 可直接加速推理。但现有 top-K 方法独立评估 token 并统一设定预算，忽略了选择依赖交互和样本间视觉复杂度差异。

Method: StepPrune 将视觉 token 剪枝形式化为自适应序列决策，基于已选 token 与文本上下文逐步构建保留子集，并通过学习的 STOP 动作自动决定子集大小；训练时使用保方差噪声门作为离散选择的代理，推理时在语言模型 prefill 前物理移除未选 token；分组选择机制将其扩展到高分辨率输入。

Result: 在 LLaVA-1.5、LLaVA-NeXT、Qwen2.5-VL 和 InternVL3 上，StepPrune 在多数模型上取得最佳平均归一化性能保留，在 LLaVA-NeXT 的长 AnyRes 前缀上具竞争力。LLaVA-1.5 上剪去 88.9% 视觉 token 仍保留 94.6% 性能；平均保留 64 个 token 时，prefill 延迟从 59.95 ms 降至 40.05 ms，加速 1.50 倍。

Conclusion: StepPrune 通过自适应序列决策与自动停止机制，优于固定预算的 top-K 剪枝，在显著削减视觉 token 的同时保持性能并有效降低 prefill 延迟。

Abstract: Visual prefixes account for a major portion of the per-layer computation in multimodal large language models (MLLMs), making visual-token pruning a direct approach to accelerating inference. Existing top-K methods typically evaluate tokens independently and apply a uniform budget to all inputs, overlooking both selection-dependent interactions and variations in visual complexity across samples. In contrast, we propose StepPrune, which formulates visual-token pruning as an adaptive sequential decision process. Conditioned on previously selected tokens and textual context, StepPrune progressively constructs the retained subset and automatically determines its size through a learned STOP action. During training, a variance-preserving noise gate provides a differentiable surrogate for the discrete selection process, whereas during inference, unselected tokens are physically removed before language-model prefill. A grouped selection mechanism further extends StepPrune to high-resolution inputs. Experiments across LLaVA-1.5, LLaVA-NeXT, Qwen2.5-VL, and InternVL3 show that StepPrune achieves the best average normalized performance retention across all evaluated pruning rates on LLaVA-1.5, Qwen2.5-VL, and InternVL3, while remaining competitive on the substantially longer AnyRes prefixes of LLaVA-NeXT. On LLaVA-1.5, StepPrune retains 94.6% of the full-prefix normalized performance while pruning 88.9% of the visual tokens. At a mean retained count of 64, StepPrune reduces prefill latency from 59.95 ms to 40.05 ms, corresponding to a 1.50x prefill speed-up.

</details>


### [36] [Realtime-Venus: A full-duplex interaction system with asynchronous delegation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13814)
*Ruixiang Zhao,Hualei Wang,Renhe Sun,Enzhi Zhou,Jincenzi Wu,Xujie Song,Kexin Shi,Zihang Liu,Pengcheng Zhu,Jiayi Zhou,Baoyue Zhang,Changhao Zhang,Zitong Wang,Jinhong Wang,Tong Niu,Jingjing Liu,Junan Lin,Haolin He,Hengshuo Chu,Yuhui Chen,Jian Liu,Yuge Huang,Junliang Xing,Yuntao Wang,Weiqiang Wang,Chun Yu,Yuanchun Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Realtime-Venus 是一套主动式全双工交互系统，包含 9B 的音视频模型与语音模型，通过共享因果时间线和双循环运行时实现连续感知、对话控制与原生语音生成，并在多项视频、音频及全双工基准上取得领先成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字与物理环境中的自然交互需要连续感知与及时响应。语音对话依赖声学与语言线索，视频交互还需将对话锚定在不断变化的视觉上下文中，现有系统难以同时满足这些需求。

Method: 提出 Realtime-Venus，包含两个独立训练的 9B 模型：Realtime-Venus-Omni（音视频交互）与 Realtime-Venus-Audio（语音交互）。每个模型通过共享因果时间线整合连续感知、对话控制与原生语音生成。采用双循环运行时协调实时交互与后台推理及工具执行，前台交互持续进行，同时 Realtime-Venus-Harness 异步执行任务并回传结果。两模型共享同一后训练配方，结合离线理解、主动全双工轨迹与委派工作流。

Result: 在线模型中，Realtime-Venus-Omni 在八个视频基准中的六个取得最高分，包括 StreamingBench（70.2%）、OVO-Bench（64.7%）、Daily-Omni（81.3%）。Realtime-Venus-Audio 在八项音频理解与口语问答基准中领先，如 MMAU（78.0%）、MMAU-Pro（63.2%）、Llama Questions（83.8%）、Speech CMMLU（67.8%），并在 VoiceBench AlpacaEval 上取得最佳 4.81 分。在 Full-Duplex-Bench v1.5 上，对用户打断的响应率为 75%，在 backchannels、other-directed speech 和 background speech 下的延续率分别为 97%、88%、86%，三项延续指标均超过 Gemini 3.1 Live 与 GPT-4o。

Conclusion: Realtime-Venus 通过双模型架构、共享因果时间线与双循环运行时，实现了主动式全双工音视频与语音交互，在视频理解、音频理解及全双工对话基准上表现优异，验证了其作为完整对话前端的有效性。

Abstract: Natural interaction in digital and physical environments requires continuous perception and timely responses. Spoken dialogue relies on acoustic and linguistic cues, while video interaction also requires grounding the conversation in evolving visual context. We present Realtime-Venus, a proactive full-duplex interaction system with two separately trained 9B models: Realtime-Venus-Omni for audio-visual interaction and Realtime-Venus-Audio for spoken interaction. Each model serves as a complete conversational frontend, integrating continuous perception, conversational control, and native speech generation through a shared causal timeline for user inputs, model outputs, and delegation events.
  A dual-loop runtime coordinates live interaction with background reasoning and tool execution. Foreground interaction continues while Realtime-Venus-Harness executes tasks asynchronously and returns results for integration into the ongoing dialogue.
  Both models follow a common post-training recipe combining offline understanding, proactive full-duplex trajectories, and delegation workflows.
  Among the evaluated online models, Realtime-Venus-Omni achieves the highest scores on six of eight video benchmarks, including StreamingBench (70.2%), OVO-Bench (64.7%), and Daily-Omni (81.3%). Across eight audio understanding and spoken question answering benchmarks, Realtime-Venus-Audio leads the compared models on MMAU (78.0%), MMAU-Pro (63.2%), Llama Questions (83.8%), and Speech CMMLU (67.8%), while matching the best VoiceBench AlpacaEval score of 4.81. On Full-Duplex-Bench v1.5, Realtime-Venus-Audio responds to 75% of user interruptions and achieves continuation rates of 97%, 88%, and 86% under backchannels, other-directed speech, and background speech, respectively, exceeding Gemini 3.1 Live and GPT-4o on all three continuation metrics.

</details>


### [37] [DiVA: Enabling Interactive Digital Life Simulation via Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13830)
*Cheng Chen,Hao Ouyang,Qiuyu Wang,Ka Leong Cheng,Wen Wang,Yihao Meng,Hanlin Wang,Yixuan Li,Jiacheng Wei,Zhenshan Tan,Yanhong Zeng,Yujun Shen,Guosheng Lin,Fayao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiVA 是一个深度交互式数字生命模拟器，通过 MLLM 路由与堆叠视频流水线，实现长期开放式的多轮交互，并以锚定视频延续模块保证连贯性与高保真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字角色交互难以支持长期、开放式的多轮体验，且视频生成过程中容易出现画质退化、镜头抖动与不一致等问题，因此需要一种能维持长时间视觉质量与真实感的系统。

Method: 将多模态大语言模型作为路由器，搭配精心设计的堆叠视频流水线；把生成建模为等待视频、动作视频及其过渡三部分耦合系统；由锚定视频延续（AVC）模块编码前段动作视频信息，将角色回归稳定状态以实现平滑过渡，并支持坐立等复杂姿态变化。

Result: 通过替换核心生成模块与主流长视频、续写及插值方法对比，并分析三阶段设计必要性、锚定状态选择、过渡自然度、空间接地和时延权衡，同时扩展对比其他长时音频驱动虚拟人模型，DiVA 在长期视觉质量与真实感上明显更优。

Conclusion: DiVA 验证了其作为可持续、交互式数字生命模拟系统的有效性，在长期开放式交互中能保持高保真身份、连贯性与动态表现。

Abstract: We present DiVA, a deeply interactive digital life simulator pioneering a new paradigm for long-term, open-ended interactive experiences within digital character worlds. DiVA's architecture pairs a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) as a router with a meticulously designed stacked video pipeline for seamless, multi-turn interactions with action and audio response. To maintain continuity and avoid degradation, we model generation as a three-part coupled system: waiting video, action video, and the transitions between them. These transitions are critically handled by our Anchored Video Continuation (AVC) module, which returns the character to stable states to prevent degradation. By encoding information from the preceding action video segment, AVC ensures smooth transitions, significantly reducing camera jitter and inconsistencies common in current video transition methods. This design also enables complex pose changes (e.g., sitting to standing) typically difficult for audio-driven models. These system designs together ensure high-fidelity identity, coherence, and dynamics for extended experiences. To validate our pipeline design, we comprehensively compare our system against alternatives by replacing our core generation module with mainstream long-video, continuation, and interpolation methods. We further analyze the necessity of the three-stage design, anchor-state selection, transition naturalness, spatial grounding, and the quality-latency trade-off, and we expand the comparison to additional long-form audio-driven avatar models. Results confirm DiVA is markedly superior in maintaining long-term visual quality and realism, validating its effectiveness as a sustainable, interactive simulation.

</details>


### [38] [TotalSynth: Robust Whole-Body Synthetic CT from MRI and CBCT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13838)
*Valentin Boussot,Cedric Hemon,Anais Barateau,Caroline Lafond,Jean-Claude Nunes,Jean-Louis Dillenseger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TotalSynth是一个可复用的预训练全身合成CT生成框架，可从MRI和CBCT生成CT；在公开数据上表现良好，但外部数据存在明显域偏移，需局部验证与微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成CT（sCT）生成通常缺乏跨解剖部位、跨模态（MRI与CBCT）以及可复用的通用框架，且现有模型在跨中心/域数据上的泛化能力不明确，因此需要开发并评估一个统一的预训练框架。

Method: 回顾性技术研究，数据集汇集2020—2026年SynthRAD挑战数据、四个前列腺队列及BIC-MAC。经配准质控后保留1800对公开挑战数据中的1450对，排除350对；另纳入84例前列腺MRI/CT和60例外部队列BIC-MAC MRI/CT。评估三个5折模型族，使用图像域、解剖感知、基于配准及不确定性指标进行评价。

Result: MRI-to-CT模型总体MAE 67.49 HU、SSIM 0.920、PSNR 29.28 dB；CBCT-to-CT模型MAE 53.55 HU、SSIM 0.939、PSNR 32.09 dB。统一模型在MRI输入（MAE 67.68 HU）与CBCT输入（MAE 54.22 HU）上性能相近。在外部BIC-MAC数据上，MRI-to-CT未微调MAE为100.91 HU，微调后降至62.21 HU。

Conclusion: TotalSynth提供了覆盖广泛解剖部位的MRI与CBCT合成CT可复用模型，但外部评估表明在域偏移下仍需局部验证并可选择微调。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop and evaluate TotalSynth, a reusable pretrained framework for whole-body synthetic CT (sCT) generation from MRI and cone-beam CT (CBCT) images. Materials and Methods: In this retrospective technical study, the dataset was assembled between 2020 and 2026 from SynthRAD challenge data, four prostate cohorts, and BIC-MAC. After registration quality control, 1450 of 1800 public challenge pairs were retained; 350 were excluded for insufficient registration quality or major source/CT mismatch. The corpus also included 84 additional prostate MRI/CT and 60 external BIC-MAC MRI/CT cases. Three 5-fold model families were evaluated with image-domain, anatomy-aware, registration-based, and uncertainty metrics. Age and sex were not consistently available across public datasets. Results: The released MRI-to-CT model achieved an overall MAE of 67.49 HU, SSIM of 0.920, and PSNR of 29.28 dB. The CBCT-to-CT model achieved an overall MAE of 53.55 HU, SSIM of 0.939, and PSNR of 32.09 dB. The unified model maintained similar performance on MRI inputs (MAE, 67.68 HU) and CBCT inputs (MAE, 54.22 HU). On external BIC-MAC data, MRI-to-CT MAE was 100.91 HU without fine-tuning and 62.21 HU after fine-tuning. Conclusion: TotalSynth provides reusable MRI- and CBCT-based CT synthesis models with broad anatomical coverage, while external evaluation highlights the need for local validation and optional fine-tuning under domain shift.

</details>

*Aleksandr Semerikov,Pakizar Shamoi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从照片中进行事件级情感识别的端到端流程：先检测人脸、用深度CNN分类表情，再将人脸级情感概率聚合为公众事件的整体情感分布，并在真实事件数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境下的面部表情识别受多人脸、遮挡、姿态变化和复杂光照等因素影响，仍具挑战性。现有研究多聚焦于个体面部情感分类，缺乏对事件层面集体情感状态的分析。

Conclusion: 事件级聚合方法在自然场景情感分析中具有有效性，为事件层面情感识别提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Facial emotion recognition (FER) in real-world environments remains challenging due to unconstrained imaging conditions, including multiple faces, occlusions, pose variations, and complex lighting. Most existing studies focus on individual facial emotion classification and do not address the analysis of collective emotional states at the event level. This paper proposes an end-to-end pipeline for event-level emotion recognition from photographs. The approach detects faces in each image, classifies facial expressions using a deep convolutional neural network, and aggregates face-level emotion probabilities to estimate the overall emotional distribution of a public event. A comparative evaluation of several CNN architectures on the FER- 2013 and RAF-DB datasets demonstrates that transfer learning with EfficientNet-B2 trained on RAF-DB is more suitable for real-world RGB data. The proposed method is evaluated on a real-world event dataset containing 1658 images. Experimental results show stable emotion distributions across event subsets, confirming the effectiveness of event-level aggregation for emotion analysis in the wild.

</details>

*Rajiv Ranjan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该提案提出一个"分阶段证伪"研究计划，检验地球基础模型是否应显式保留物理场的几何类型（标量、极向量、轴向量、余向量、高阶张量），而非仅用常规嵌入加数据增强。先在ERA5上做低计算量对照实验，只有在显式几何分型带来可复现增益时，才推进到多模态地球基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测基础模型擅长学习高维语义地理空间嵌入，天气气候模型则证明球面几何、球面算子、网格与混合物理求解器能显著提升预测。但二者并不等价：常规潜在嵌入没有内禀物理变换律，而标量场、切向极向量场、轴/伪向量、余向量和高阶张量在旋转、反射及局部坐标变换下行为各异。核心问题是：通用地球基础模型应显式保留这些区别，还是标准嵌入加增强就已学到关键信息。

Method: 方法上不作更复杂架构的先验假设，而是采用分阶段证伪方案。第一步是计算量可控的ERA5"干跑"实验，在空间、时间、朝向及低数据量等分布偏移下，对比四类变体：常规嵌入、增强匹配基线、类型化等变模型、以及Hodge/Helmholtz分解变体。仅当显式几何分型产生可复现改进时，才推进到语义嵌入与物理类型化场共存的阶段，构建多模态地球基础模型。

Result: 摘要未给出实证结果，属于研究提案。作者指出所设定的差距比"现有模型完全忽略几何"更窄、更可辩护：已有若干系统尊重球面域几何，也有新兴工作显式在球面上学习标量/向量场。尚未解决的关键问题是：基础模型规模、多模态、宇称感知的场分型是否能超越这些已有方法带来实际增益。

Conclusion: 结论是研究应首先回答"显式几何类型是否优于标准嵌入加增强"这一可证伪问题，而非默认采用更复杂架构。只有通过ERA5对照实验获得可复现增益证据后，才值得投入多模态、宇称感知的地球基础模型建设。

Abstract: Earth-observation (EO) foundation models have become exceptionally effective at learning se mantic, high-dimensional geospatial embeddings, while modern weather and climate models have demonstrated that Earth-specific geometry, spherical operators, meshes, and hybrid physical solvers can materially improve prediction. Yet these two advances are not equivalent. A conventional latent embedding has no inherent physical transformation law, whereas scalar fields, tangent polar-vector fields, axial/pseudovector quantities, covectors, and higher-order tensors transform differently under rotations, reflections, and changes of local coordinate frame. This proposal asks whether a general purpose Earth foundation model should preserve those distinctions explicitly, or whether standard embeddings plus augmentation already learn everything that matters. The central contribution is therefore not a more complicated architecture by assumption, but a staged falsification program. A compute-conscious ERA5 dry run first compares conventional, augmentation-matched, typed equivariant, and Hodge/Helmholtz variants under spatial, temporal, orientation, and low-data shifts. Only if explicit geometric typing yields reproducible improvements does the program advance toward a multimodal Earth foundation model in which semantic embeddings coexist with physically typed fields. The proposed gap is narrower and more defensible than claiming that current models ignore geometry entirely: several systems already respect spherical domain geometry, and emerging work explicitly learns scalar/vector fields on spheres. The unresolved question is whether foundation-scale, multimodal, parity-aware field typing produces practical gains beyond those existing approaches.

</details>


### [41] [SkyAnchor: Updating Metric-scale Aerial 3D Gaussian Scenes from Unposed Ground-View Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13903)
*Zhuoxiao Li,Xinyi Liu,Taoyu Wu,Yinrui Ren,Tongyan Hua,Ou Jing,Shuai Zhang,Dongli Wu,Rongjun Qin,Ge Lin Kan,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SkyAnchor，将已建好的航拍 SfM/3DGS 场景作为固定支架，用未标定地面序列进行配准与场景更新，实现地面轨迹恢复与航拍保真渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有航拍场景含可靠 SfM 与 3DGS 模型，后续采集的地面序列无位姿、无全局尺度，需将其配准到航拍重建以补充街景外观。单帧跨视角定位脆弱，长轨迹易漂移，联合从零重建航拍与地面代价高且不稳定。

Method: 以航拍场景为固定支架：先用几何验证的航拍支撑对连续短帧分组定位，得到稀疏锚点位姿；再用锚点约束子图恢复完整地面轨迹，在增量配准与光束法平差中固定前后锚点位姿；最后在保持航拍视图的前提下插入筛选后的地面高斯，并进行轻量联合优化。

Result: 在七个真实航拍—地面场景上，方法获得准确的米制地面轨迹，并更新出航拍与地面视角渲染质量均优的 3D 高斯场景。

Conclusion: SkyAnchor 证明将已有航拍重建作为固定锚定支架，可有效解决无位姿地面序列的配准漂移问题，实现高保真街景增强与几何一致的场景更新。

Abstract: We study how to update a pre-built aerial scene with a newly captured, unposed ground-view sequence. The aerial scene already contains a reliable metric Structure-from-Motion (SfM) reconstruction and a pre-trained 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) model, whereas the ground-view sequence is collected later to add street-level appearance but has unknown camera poses and global scale. Registering this sequence to the aerial SfM reconstruction is challenging because single-image cross-view localization is brittle and long trajectories are prone to drift. To address these challenges, we present SkyAnchor, which treats the existing aerial scene as a fixed scaffold for ground-view registration and scene update instead of jointly reconstructing aerial and ground imagery from scratch. It first localizes short groups of consecutive ground frames against geometrically verified aerial support, producing sparse anchor poses. It then recovers the full ground trajectory with anchor-constrained submaps, fixing the front and rear anchor poses during incremental registration and bundle adjustment. Finally, it inserts filtered ground Gaussians while preserving the aerial view, followed by lightweight joint refinement. Experiments on seven real aerial--ground scenes show accurate metric ground trajectories and updated 3D Gaussian scenes with strong aerial- and ground-view rendering quality.

</details>


### [42] [BLInD: Learning Driver Intent as a Distribution over Future Ego Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13941)
*Flavian Pegado,Ronit Hire,Shreyas Rajesh,Soham Phade*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BLInD是一个仅依赖车辆状态历史（速度、曲率、转向灯、车型）的紧凑网络，无需相机、LiDAR、地图或目标跟踪输入，即可预测未来自车轨迹的多模态分布，并在多数据集上实现跨域泛化，运行于车载ECU上，可支持AEB等下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型分布模型依赖场景上下文，而仅用车辆状态的方法通常只输出单一轨迹。作者希望验证：仅凭车辆状态历史是否足以学习有用的多模态近期自车轨迹分布，并实现低延迟、跨域、可部署的意图采样接口。

Method: 提出BLInD（Blind Learned Intent Distribution），将近期车辆状态历史（速度、曲率、转向灯、车型）映射为未来自车轨迹的top-k分布。研究两种分布架构：自回归（AR）与流匹配（flow-matching），并在混合平台开源数据集和Wayve数据集上训练，无需数据集特定适配。将分布集成到AEB触发任务中进行评估。

Result: 流匹配模型取得最佳top-k ADE/FDE：Wayve 0.15/0.37 m，Waymo 0.15/0.36 m，nuScenes 0.28/0.59 m，AR模型覆盖度相当。在AEB触发任务中，严格全候选策略将误报从1.51%降至AR的0.11%（13.7倍降低，94.9% TP）和流匹配的0.06%（25.1倍降低，98.7% TP）。运行时间为AR头0.87 ms、流匹配头2.9 ms（NVIDIA DRIVE Orin ECU）。

Conclusion: BLInD同时具备学习型、盲式（无感知输入）和跨域三个特性，这一组合此前未见报道。结果表明该分布可为下游系统提供可控且合理的意图采样接口，AEB是其中一个实例，且其延迟满足汽车ECU实时部署要求。

Abstract: We present BLInD (Blind Learned Intent Distribution), a compact network that maps recent vehicle-state history (e.g. speed, curvature, indicator, and vehicle type) to a top-k distribution of future ego trajectories, with no camera, LiDAR, map, or object-track inputs. We find that vehiclestate history alone is sufficient to learn a useful multimodal distribution over near-term ego trajectories, and its low-latency nature makes it well-suited for safety-critical deployment. We investigate two distribution architectures, autoregressive (AR) and flow-matching, and train on both mixed-platform opensource and Wayve datasets. Both generalize without datasetspecific adaptation; the flow-matching model achieves best topk ADE/FDE of 0.15/0.37 m on Wayve, 0.15/0.36 m on Waymo, and 0.28/0.59 m on nuScenes, with the AR model reaching comparable coverage. Integrating the distributions into an AEB trigger task, a strict all-candidates policy reduces false positives from 1.51% to 0.11% with AR (13.7x reduction, 94.9% TP) and to 0.06% with flow-matching (25.1x reduction, 98.7% TP) compared to a 1-CTRV policy with 100% true positive score. BLInD runs in 0.87 ms with the AR head and 2.9 ms with the flow-matching head on an NVIDIA DRIVE Orin ECU making it compatible with real-time deployment on automotive ECUs. While existing learned distribution models rely on scene context and blind vehicle-state models typically collapse to a single path, BLInD is learned, blind, and cross-domain simultaneously, a combination not demonstrated by prior work. These results show that such a distribution provides a controllable and plausible intent sampling interface for downstream systems, with AEB as one instantiation.

</details>


### [43] [SGWIB:Sliced Gromov-Wasserstein Information Bottleneck for Video Highlight Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13966)
*Hanjuan Huang,Yung-Chieh Yeh,Hsing-Kuo Pao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SGMG结构感知正则项与SGWIB信息瓶颈框架，用于视频高光检测，在保持片段间时序结构的同时学习紧凑表示，并结合上下文解耦降低体育场景偏差，在MrHiSum和MoSu上取得最优单模态结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频高光检测需要兼具判别性的片段表示与片段间时序关系的保持。信息瓶颈原理虽能学习紧凑且任务相关的表示，但尚未用于高光检测，且直接套用传统形式会忽略片段间关系结构，并在压缩过程中破坏与高光相关的时序组织。

Method: 提出Sliced Gromov-Monge Gap（SGMG）结构感知正则项，衡量给定源到瓶颈映射相较于最优切片结构对应所引入的额外关系失真；基于SGMG构建SGWIB信息瓶颈框架，学习紧凑瓶颈表示并保持片段间时序结构；引入Home-Away-Related Contextual Pseudo-Labels与上下文解耦模块，将高光导向信息与上下文模式分离，以减少体育场景特有的上下文偏差。

Result: 在MrHiSum和MoSu数据集上，SGWIB在Kendall's tau、Spearman's rho、mAP@50、mAP@30四项指标上均优于所比较的单模态方法。在MrHiSum上，视觉模型较此前最优结果分别提升0.031、0.031、0.87、0.75。

Conclusion: 结构感知的信息瓶颈正则化结合上下文解耦能够有效提升片段级高光预测性能。

Abstract: Video highlight detection aims to identify temporally important segments that capture the most informative or engaging events in a video. Reliable prediction therefore requires not only discriminative segment representations but also preservation of the temporal relationships among neighboring and distant segments. The information bottleneck principle has proven effective for learning compact and task-relevant representations, yet it has not been explored for video highlight detection, and applying conventional formulations directly would overlook inter-segment relational structure and distort highlight relevant temporal organization during compression. We therefore introduce the Sliced Gromov-Monge Gap (SGMG), a structure aware regularizer that measures the excess relational distortion induced by a prescribed source-to-bottleneck mapping relative to an optimal sliced structural correspondence. Building on SGMG, we develop SGWIB, an information-bottleneck framework for single-modal video highlight detection that learns compact bottleneck representations while preserving inter-segment temporal structure. We further introduce Home-Away-Related Contextual Pseudo-Labels and a contextual disentanglement module that reduce sports-specific contextual bias by separating highlight oriented information from contextual patterns. Experiments on MrHiSum and MoSu show that SGWIB attains the best Kendall's tau, Spearman's rho, mAP@50, and mAP@30 among the compared single-modal methods on both datasets. On MrHiSum, the visual model improves the strongest previous results by 0.031, 0.031, 0.87, and 0.75 on these four metrics, respectively. These results show that structure-aware information-bottleneck regularization combined with contextual disentanglement improves segment-level highlight prediction.

</details>


### [44] [Zero-Shot Cross-Material Ptychographic Phase Reconstruction Using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13969)
*Wen-Chun Lin,Yu-Chee Tseng,Jen-Jee Chen,Nan-You Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种直接“局部到全局”学习框架，无需推理期迭代即可从衍射数据重建全场相位，跨材料零样本迁移表现最优，端到端重建速度较ePIE提升约10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统叠层成像相位重建通常被建模为迭代逆问题，需要反复更新物体与探针，在大规模4D-STEM数据下计算成本高昂。

Method: 网络从单幅衍射图预测局部包裹相位块，采用正弦-余弦（sine-cosine）表示；再结合标定扫描位置与高斯加权拼接将局部预测组装为全场重建。训练于一种材料，并零样本直接迁移至另一种材料，无需目标域微调。

Result: 在AuPd与MoS₂上实现双向一致的跨材料迁移，全场MSE、PSNR、MS-SSIM均在所评估的学习类方法中最佳；相较迭代ePIE，端到端重建时间约减少10倍。

Conclusion: 该直接局部到全局流程高效且具迁移性，展示了在叠层成像重建中免迭代、可跨材料应用的潜力。

Abstract: Ptychographic phase reconstruction is commonly formulated as an iterative inverse problem, requiring repeated object-probe updates and resulting in substantial computational cost for large-scale 4D-STEM data. We present a direct local-to-global learning framework that reconstructs full-field phase maps from diffraction measurements without iterative refinement during inference. The proposed network predicts local wrapped-phase patches from individual diffraction patterns using a sine-cosine representation, and the predictions are assembled into a full-field reconstruction using calibrated scan positions and Gaussian-weighted stitching. To evaluate generalization beyond the training domain, the model is trained on one material and directly applied to another in a zero-shot setting without target-domain fine-tuning. Experiments on AuPd and MoS$_2$ demonstrate consistent cross-material transfer in both directions, with the proposed method achieving the best full-field MSE, PSNR, and MS-SSIM among the evaluated learning-based methods. Compared with the iterative ePIE approach, the proposed direct local-to-global pipeline reduces end-to-end reconstruction time by approximately 10x, demonstrating its potential for efficient and transferable ptychographic reconstruction.

</details>


### [45] [Mind2Cloud: EEG-to-Point Cloud Generation with Two-Granularity Diffusion Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13991)
*Yongyi Lu,Xiongfeng Huang,Zhijing Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Mind2Cloud，一个基于双粒度扩散解码的脑电（EEG）到三维点云生成框架，通过时间感知解码器在不同扩散时间步自适应切换全局与局部建模粒度，在EEG-3D数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从脑信号重建三维物体有助于理解人类视觉认知。已有EEG三维重建方法多采用统一的扩散解码器，忽略了EEG表征与扩散去噪过程中语义粒度的动态演化特征，导致重建效果受限。

Method: 提出Mind2Cloud框架，核心为时间感知解码器：在扩散不同时间步通过可学习的融合掩码整合全局Transformer分支与局部Point-Voxel CNN（PVCNN）分支。早期上采样阶段使用Transformer层捕捉高不确定性下的全局物体结构，后期阶段使用PVCNN模块细化局部几何细节，从而依据扩散由粗到细的去噪轨迹动态调整空间粒度，契合EEG视觉表征的层次性。此外引入对抗式细化模块以增强几何真实感与语义一致性。

Result: 在EEG-3D数据集全部12名被试上的大量实验表明，Mind2Cloud在几何精度与语义对齐两方面均优于已有工作，为EEG到点云生成树立了新基准。

Conclusion: 双粒度、时间感知的扩散解码设计能够有效利用EEG表征的层次性与扩散去噪的粗到细特性，配合对抗式细化，显著提升EEG到三维点云的重建质量，代码已开源。

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D objects from brain signals offers a promising avenue for understanding human visual cognition. While prior work has shown initial success using EEG signals for 3D reconstruction, existing methods typically employ a uniform diffusion decoder, overlooking the evolving semantic granularity of both EEG representations and the diffusion denoising process. In this paper, we propose Mind2Cloud, a novel EEG-to-point-cloud generation framework based on two-granularity diffusion decoding. The core of Mind2Cloud is a time-aware decoder that integrates a global Transformer branch and a local Point-Voxel CNN (PVCNN) branch across diffusion timesteps through a learnable fusion mask. Specifically, Transformer layers are incorporated into the early upsampling stages to capture global object structure under high uncertainty, while PVCNN modules are used in later stages to refine local geometric details. Inspired by the hierarchical nature of EEG-based visual representations, this design dynamically adapts its spatial granularity in accordance with the coarse-to-fine trajectory of diffusion denoising. We further introduce an adversarial refinement module to enhance geometric realism and semantic consistency. Extensive experiments on the EEG-3D dataset across all 12 subjects demonstrate that Mind2Cloud outperforms prior work in both geometric accuracy and semantic alignment, setting a new benchmark for EEG-to-point-cloud generation. Our source code is available at https://github.com/duasoi/Mind2Cloud.

</details>


### [46] [CirrGuide: A Deep Cascaded Framework for Liver Cirrhosis Segmentation and Severity Classification from T2-Weighted MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14010)
*Muntaqim Ahmed Raju,Ruizhe Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CirrGuide是一个深度级联框架，先分割肝硬化肝脏，再以分割掩膜作为解剖先验进行轻度/中度/重度分级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肝硬化引起肝脏进行性结构改变并可导致严重并发症，因此严重程度评估对疾病监测和治疗规划很重要。但分级困难，因为影像模式往往细微、空间多变，且相邻阶段之间相似。作者希望显式地将定位与分类联系起来以应对该挑战。

Method: 采用级联框架：首先用ResNet50编码器加Attention U-Net解码器预测软性肝硬化肝脏掩膜；随后将该掩膜作为解剖先验输入基于ResNet50的分类分支，该分支结合全局多尺度特征与掩膜引导的注意力池化区域特征，将肝硬化分为轻度、中度、重度。

Result: 在官方CirrMRI600+ T2加权（T2W）2D划分上，分割达到89.83% Dice和84.14% mIoU；严重程度分类达到69.58%准确率和61.55%宏F1分数。与仅分割、仅分类及多任务基线相比，CirrGuide在定位和分级上均有提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将预测的肝硬化肝脏掩膜作为解剖先验用于肝硬化分析具有明显收益，可同时改善定位与严重程度分级。

Abstract: We present CirrGuide, a deep cascaded framework for cirrhotic liver segmentation and severity classification. Cirrhosis causes progressive structural changes in the liver and can lead to serious clinical complications, making severity assessment important for disease monitoring and treatment planning. However, severity classification is challenging because imaging patterns are often subtle, spatially variable, and similar across adjacent stages. CirrGuide addresses this by explicitly linking localization with classification. A ResNet50 encoder with an Attention U-Net decoder first predicts a soft cirrhotic liver mask, which is then used as an anatomical prior in a ResNet50-based classification branch. This branch combines global multi-scale features with mask-guided attention-pooled regional features to classify Mild, Moderate, and Severe cirrhosis. On the official CirrMRI600+ T2-weighted (T2W) 2D split, CirrGuide achieves 89.83% Dice and 84.14% mIoU for segmentation, 69.58% accuracy and 61.55% macro F1-score for severity classification. Compared with segmentation-only, classification-only, and multi-task baselines, CirrGuide improves both localization and severity classification, demonstrating the benefit of using predicted cirrhotic liver masks as anatomical priors for cirrhosis analysis.

</details>


### [47] [Quantum-Gated LiteSSD: A Parameter-Efficient Lightweight Hybrid Quantum-Classical Framework for Forward-Looking Sonar Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14025)
*Niloy Kumar Mondal,Poulomi Sarker Puja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Quantum-Gated LiteSSD，一种轻量级混合量子-经典前视声纳目标检测器，通过将量子处理重构为通道门控机制来大幅压缩参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前视声纳目标检测对水下感知至关重要，但在嵌入式平台上部署要求模型高度紧凑，现有检测器参数过多难以满足需求。

Method: 结合量子计算，将QuCNet风格的多电路量子处理重构为以恒等为中心的通道门控机制，用于空间特征调制，构建参数高效的混合量子-经典检测器。

Result: 在Marine Debris Watertank数据集上达到90.84% mAP50，参数比YOLO26s少约62倍、比SSD-VGG16少约164.3倍；在UATD上以仅0.150M参数达到70.37% mAP50，比SSGA-YOLO小约4.1倍。

Conclusion: 该方法在参数与精度之间实现了有效的权衡，适合资源受限平台的声纳目标检测部署。

Abstract: Forward-looking sonar object detection is essential for underwater perception, yet deployment on embedded platforms requires highly compact models. To address this challenge, we explore quantum computing and introduce Quantum-Gated LiteSSD, a parameter-efficient hybrid quantum--classical detector that reformulates QuCNet-style multi-circuit quantum processing as an identity-centered channel-gating mechanism for spatial feature modulation. Experiments on the Marine Debris Watertank dataset and UATD forward-looking sonar benchmarks demonstrate an effective parameter--accuracy trade-off. The proposed detector achieves 90.84% $\mathrm{mAP}_{50}$ on Watertank with approximately $62\times$ fewer parameters than YOLO26s and $164.3\times$ fewer parameters than SSD-VGG16. On UATD, the model achieves 70.37% $\mathrm{mAP}_{50}$ with only 0.150M parameters, making it approximately $4.1\times$ smaller than SSGA-YOLO while retaining meaningful multi-class detection capability.

</details>


### [48] [LPA-CWM: A Learned Physical Adjudicator for Motion Reasoning with Counterfactual World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14073)
*Kunwei Wu,Xiang Liu,Guocai Yao,Junming Chen,Zhikang Chen,Min Zhang,Pengwei Wang,Sen Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对反事实世界模型(CWM)中不同目标帧掩码下生成的响应可靠性不一、均匀聚合权重相同的问题，提出LPA-CWM，用一个轻量级学习物理裁决器(LPA)学习候选响应可靠性并进行加权聚合，同时提出完整性感知运动对应(CMC)评测协议。在DAVIS和Kinetics子集上，LPA-CWM相比Uniform CWM将DCA_avg分别提升60.0%和29.0%，并提升TAP-Vid First下的跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实世界模型(CWM)通过比较事实预测与干预预测，从预训练视频预测器中提取运动信息。然而，在不同目标帧掩码下生成的响应可靠性存在差异，而现有方法采用均匀聚合，将所有响应同等对待，忽略了候选间的可靠性差别，导致运动提取质量受限。此外，现有评测缺少对轨迹完整性、可见性等维度的综合衡量。

Method: 1) 将响应聚合形式化为候选可靠性学习问题，提出LPA-CWM，引入轻量级学习物理裁决器(LPA)。2) LPA仅3.0M参数，在密集MOVi-F轨迹上训练，对无序候选集合比较视觉上下文与响应结构，预测相对权重；CWM预测器与干预生成器保持冻结。3) 加权后的响应经窗口化定位和一次配对重评估以恢复运动。4) 提出完整性感知运动对应(CMC)评测协议，以真值为锚，联合衡量定位、轨迹完整性、可见性与连续性，并将可见动态点上的缺失预测计为失败。

Result: 在评测的DAVIS与Kinetics子集上，LPA-CWM相对Uniform CWM将DCA_avg分别提升60.0%与29.0%，同时在TAP-Vid First指标下也提升了跟踪精度。

Conclusion: 通过学习候选响应可靠性并对反事实世界模型的响应进行加权聚合，可显著提升运动提取与跟踪性能；所提出的CMC评测协议提供了更全面的真值锚定评估方式。整体方法轻量、模块化，且不修改底层冻结的CWM预测器与干预生成器。

Abstract: Counterfactual world models (CWM) extract motion from pretrained video predictors by comparing factual and intervened predictions. However, responses generated under different target-frame masks vary in reliability, while uniform aggregation weights them equally. We formulate response aggregation as candidate reliability learning and propose LPA-CWM with a lightweight Learned Physical Adjudicator (LPA). Trained on dense MOVi-F trajectories, the 3.0M-parameter LPA compares visual context and response structure across an unordered candidate set to predict relative weights, while the CWM predictor and intervention generator remain frozen. The weighted responses undergo windowed localization and one paired re-evaluation to recover motion. We also introduce Completeness-aware Motion Correspondence (CMC), a ground-truth-anchored evaluation protocol that jointly measures localization, trajectory completeness, visibility, and continuity, counting missing predictions as failures on visible dynamic points. On the evaluated DAVIS and Kinetics subsets, LPA-CWM improves $\mathrm{DCA}_{\mathrm{avg}}$ over Uniform CWM by 60.0\% and 29.0\%, respectively, and also improves tracking accuracy under TAP-Vid First. A quick overview is available at https://LPA-CWM.github.io.

</details>


### [49] [Adapting Open-Weight MLLMs to Generate Point Prompts for Electron Microscopy Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14080)
*Samia Mohinta,Albert Cardona*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首次验证开放权重多模态大模型（MLLM）可作为电镜点提示生成器，通过自然语言指令输出坐标驱动冻结的分割器，效果接近自动提示生成基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: microSAM 等可提示模型需人工点提示才能分割电镜图像，自动化关键在于无需用户输入即可生成提示。作者探索开放权重 MLLM 能否依据自然语言请求生成坐标提示，建立可检查、语言驱动的定位与掩码解码链路。

Method: 将三个线粒体数据集的掩码转为训练样本，把图像与指令同质心坐标配对；冻结 MLLM 主干与 microSAM，仅训练 LoRA 适配器；采用监督微调加奖励优化；与监督式质心热图检测器对比。

Result: Qwen3-VL 经微调与奖励优化后 AP50 达 0.736，未适配时为 0.247，自动提示生成（APG）为 0.773；另两个 MLLM 也提升并达到或超过 APG；监督质心热图检测器 AP50 为 0.904，但 Qwen3-VL 与标注点集和实例数更吻合；两数据集训练可迁移到未见第三数据集，三数据集训练可迁移到独立电镜体数据；对未见自然语言表述稳健，坐标可复用至第二个分割器。

Conclusion: 这是开放权重 MLLM 作为电镜点生成器的首个可行性研究，证明其能提供可解释、语言引导的定位-分割接口，具备跨数据集与跨分割器迁移能力。

Abstract: Promptable models such as microSAM segment electron microscopy (EM) images from point prompts, but automation requires generating prompts without user input. We ask whether open-weight multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can generate them from natural-language requests by returning coordinates to a frozen segmenter. To that end, we convert masks from three mitochondria datasets into training examples, pairing images and instructions with centroid coordinates, then train LoRA adapters while freezing the MLLM backbone and microSAM. We find that Qwen3-VL reaches segmentation AP$_{50}$ $0.736$ after supervised fine-tuning and reward optimization, up from $0.247$ without adaptation, while automatic prompt generation (APG) achieves $0.773$. In addition, two other MLLMs improve, reaching or exceeding APG. When compared with a supervised centroid-heatmap detector that reaches AP$_{50}$ $0.904$ for this mitochondria task, Qwen3-VL more closely matches the annotated point set and instance counts. Moreover, training on two public datasets transfers to an unseen third, while training on all three transfers to an independent EM volume. Robustness tests show stable performance under unseen formulations of the natural-language request, while the coordinates can be reused by a second segmenter. To our knowledge, this is the first feasibility study of open-weight MLLMs as EM point generators, providing an inspectable, language-directed link between localization and mask decoding.

</details>


### [50] [Bridging the Synthetic-to-Real Gap for Few-Shot Cryo-ET Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14097)
*Siddhant Bharadwaj,Ashish Vashist,Rashi Singh,Pranav Vinodh,Nishanth Artham,Runmin Jiang,Xingjian Li,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种可学习变换模块驱动的合成到真实域适应框架，在输入与特征两个层面缩小合成与真实子断层图的域差距，在少样本场景下优于现有迁移学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷冻电子断层扫描中的子断层图分类因标注样本稀缺而困难；虽然模拟器可生成无限合成数据，但合成与真实数据之间存在显著域差距，限制其实际应用。

Method: 设计了一个新颖的合成到真实域适应框架，其中包含可学习的变换模块，在输入层面和特征层面同时桥接域差距。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在少样本设置下持续优于现有的迁移学习基线。

Conclusion: 通过输入与特征双层面的合成到真实适应，可有效缓解标注稀缺问题，为cryo-ET子断层图分类提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Subtomogram classification in cryo-electron tomography (cryo-ET) is a challenging problem due to the scarcity of labeled examples. While cryo-ET simulators can be adopted to generate unlimited synthetic data, the substantial domain gap between synthetic and real subtomograms hinders its practical utilization. In this work, we propose a novel synthetic-to-real adaptation framework with a learnable transformation module, bridging this gap at both the input and feature levels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms existing transfer learning baselines in few-shot settings.

</details>


### [51] [RA-CoA: Training-free Fashion Image Captioning via Retrieval-Augmented Chain-of-Attributes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14100)
*Abhirama Subramanyam Penamakuri,Shreya Shukla,Anand Mishra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的RA-CoA框架，通过检索增强的属性链技术提升时尚图像描述中细粒度属性的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时尚图像描述需细粒度视觉推理与领域术语知识，且时尚库存快速演变要求免训练方案以保证可扩展性与适应性。现有通用视觉语言模型虽具零样本能力，但属性覆盖与精度不足，易幻觉或误识别细节，难以用于产品编目等高质量场景。

Method: RA-CoA将任务解耦为两个可解释阶段：(i)从产品知识库检索相关属性集合；(ii)属性级推理生成最终描述。方法为模型无关，可搭配冻结的视觉语言模型，无需微调。

Result: 在多种VLM模型系列与不同提示范式下评测，RA-CoA显著提升描述质量，METEOR较零样本描述平均提升26.3%。

Conclusion: RA-CoA是一种免训练、模型无关的框架，无需微调即可提升时尚产品描述的细粒度属性精度，代码已公开。

Abstract: Fashion Image Captioning (FIC) plays a vital role in enhancing user experience and product search in e-commerce platforms. Unlike natural scene image captioning, FIC requires fine-grained visual reasoning and knowledge of domain-specific terminology to capture subtle attributes such as neckline and closure types, graphic patterns, and dress silhouettes. Moreover, as fashion inventories evolve rapidly with new trends, styles, and frequently emerging vocabulary, developing training-free captioning solution becomes essential for scalability and real-world adaptability. Instruction-tuned vision-language models (VLMs) offer a promising solution to fashion image captioning dueto their strong zero-shot capabilities and natural language fluency. However, these general-purpose models often lack attribute-level coverage and precision, and tend to hallucinate or misidentify fine-grained fashion details, making them less suitable for high-fidelity applications like product cataloging or personalized recommendations. To address this, we propose RA-CoA (Retrieval-Augmented Chain-of-Attributes), a novel, training-free framework that disentangles fashion image captioning into two interpretable stages: (i) retrieval of relevant attribute sets from a product knowledge base, and (ii) attribute-level reasoning to generate the final caption. RA-CoA is a model-agnostic approach that works with frozen VLMs to improve fine-grained attribute precision in product captions without the need for fine-tuning. Extensive evaluations across diverse VLM model families under different prompting paradigms demonstrate that RA-CoA significantly improves caption quality, achieving an average gain of 26.3% METEOR score over zero-shot captioning. We make our code publicly available.

</details>


### [52] [A Voxel-Spacing-Aware Extension of PyRadiomics for Anisotropic Texture Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14103)
*David Corral Fontecha,Juan Miranda Bautista,Pablo Menendez Fernández-Miranda,Andrea Trapote Fernandez,Lara Lloret Iglesias,Jose A. Vega*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文实现并验证了PyRadiomics的体素间距感知扩展，在不生成插值灰度级的前提下将间距信息融入纹理特征计算，兼容原有标准实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射组学纹理特征常从各向异性CT和MRI采集数据中提取，相同体素偏移可能对应不同物理距离，传统方法未考虑这一间距差异，可能影响特征准确性。

Method: 在PyRadiomics的Python前端、C封装与计算后端实现间距感知扩展：GLCM采用各向异性相对的特征级角度聚合，NGTDM采用各向异性相对加权邻域平均，GLRLM、GLDM和GLSZM基于各向异性原生网格的有限体积零阶保持表示计算。

Result: 合成3D体模验证显示：关闭间距感知模式时与标准PyRadiomics完全一致；各向同性间距下75个纹理特征等价；各向异性间距下选择性改变纹理特征族，且与最近邻、线性和B样条重采样数值不同；计算性能有适度运行时和内存增加；间距感知差异在不同binWidth设置下持续存在。

Conclusion: 该框架为未来在异构医学影像数据集中评估间距感知放射组学提供了向后兼容的技术基础。

Abstract: Radiomic texture features are commonly extracted from anisotropic CT and MRI acquisitions, where identical voxel offsets may represent different physical distances. We implemented and validated a voxel-spacing-aware extension of PyRadiomics that incorporates spacing information without generating interpolated gray levels. The framework operates across the Python frontend, C wrapper, and computational backend. GLCM uses anisotropy-relative feature-level angular aggregation, NGTDM uses anisotropy-relative weighted neighborhood averaging, and GLRLM, GLDM, and GLSZM are computed on a finite-volume zero-order-hold representation derived from the native anisotropic grid. Synthetic 3D phantoms were used for software validation. The modified implementation reproduced standard PyRadiomics exactly when spacing-aware mode was disabled and remained equivalent under isotropic spacing across 75 texture features. Under anisotropic spacing, the method selectively modified texture families and was numerically distinct from nearest-neighbor, linear, and B-spline resampling. Computational profiling showed moderate runtime and memory increases, while sensitivity analyses quantified finite-volume rounding effects and confirmed that spacing-aware differences persisted across binWidth settings. The framework provides a backward-compatible technical basis for future evaluation of spacing-aware radiomics in heterogeneous medical imaging datasets.

</details>


### [53] [Accelerating HKTex without Mesh Eigensystems: Local Unfolding and Randomized Thermal Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14105)
*Zhewen He,Junyi Hu,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出两种方法（LocalHK 与 ThermalRF）来消除 Heat Kernel Textures（HKTex）中 50 次全局 Laplace-Beltrami 特征分解和常驻基矩阵带来的计算瓶颈，同时保持训练器、密度控制和合成流程不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HKTex 用内在各向异性核实表示表面外观，但依赖 50 次全局 Laplace-Beltrami 特征分解和形状为 [50,V,256] 的常驻基，成为效率与可扩展性的瓶颈。

Method: LocalHK 利用已训练核的局部性，用半径受限的 hinge unfolding 和解析 log-map 核替代谱评估；ThermalRF 保留离散各向异性热半群，通过 GPU 稀疏 Chebyshev 动作和随机化 range finding 构造全局低秩热因子，避免网格规模特征向量，并用编译求值器混合四个邻近热响应。

Result: LocalHK 在 10 个 Objaverse 网格和 8 网格低多边形留出集上，将平均视角 PSNR 从 31.35 提升至 32.00 dB、从 29.82 提升至 30.76 dB，初始化减少 40.5 倍，并支持 749,570 顶点代理运行（谱基线失败）。ThermalRF 在 spot 与 thin-stem 挑战上，端到端预处理、初始化与 5000 步优化减少 29.3% 和 24.4%，各项 PSNR 变化在 0.12 dB 内，训练分配减少约 90%。

Conclusion: 两条路线揭示了在最大局部性与尺度 versus 忠实于热 PDE 之间的设计取舍；更广泛的热特征评估与真实大场景仍为未来工作。

Abstract: Heat Kernel Textures (HKTex) represent surface appearance with intrinsic anisotropic
  kernels, but evaluate them using 50 global Laplace-Beltrami eigendecompositions and a
  resident basis of shape [50,V,256]. We study two complementary ways to remove this
  bottleneck while leaving the trainer, GeodesicOpt, density control, and compositing
  unchanged. LocalHK exploits the measured locality of trained kernels and replaces
  spectral evaluation by radius-bounded hinge unfolding and an analytic log-map kernel.
  On 10 Objaverse meshes and an 8-mesh low-poly holdout, it changes mean view PSNR from
  31.35 to 32.00 and from 29.82 to 30.76 dB, respectively, while reducing
  initialization by 40.5 times and enabling a 749,570-vertex proxy-backed run where the
  spectral baseline fails. ThermalRF instead preserves the discrete anisotropic heat
  semigroup: GPU sparse Chebyshev actions and randomized range finding construct global
  low-rank heat factors without mesh-sized eigenvectors, and a compiled evaluator mixes
  four neighboring thermal responses. On spot and a thin-stem challenge, ThermalRF
  reduces end-to-end preprocessing, initialization, and 5,000-step optimization by
  29.3% and 24.4%, with every surface, atlas, or view PSNR change within 0.12 dB and
  training allocation reduced by about 90%. The two routes expose a useful design
  choice: maximal locality and scale versus fidelity to the thermal PDE. Broader
  thermal-feature evaluation and real large scenes remain future work.

</details>


### [54] [Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Foundation Models for Liver Tumor Segmentation in CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14106)
*Ramtin Mojtahedi,Mohammad Hamghalam,Jacob J. Peoples,Richard K. G. Do,Amber L. Simpson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了在结直肠癌肝转移腹部CT的肝脏肿瘤分割中，对SAM进行参数高效微调（PEFT），比较了LoRA、QLoRA、Conv-Adapter及提出的DiSCo方法，验证了在计算和数据受限下PEFT的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肝脏肿瘤分割在结直肠癌肝转移腹部CT中具有重要意义，而全量微调SAM成本高昂；在计算资源与标注数据有限时，需要低成本的适配方案。

Method: 仅训练适配器并冻结SAM主干，比较LoRA、4-bit QLoRA、卷积适配器Conv-Adapter与提出的DiSCo（从行归一化权重的SVD导出谱基，学习秩门控谱系数、逐输出幅值偏移和谱增益，可选推理时Top-K秩选择，仅0.14M可训练参数）；在无提示、单点、多点、IoU 0.50与0.75边界框五种提示模式下进行基准测试。

Result: Conv-Adapter与LoRA精度最高（总体Dice 0.793和0.792；单点Dice 0.795和0.792；HD95 32 mm）。QLoRA接近（总体Dice 0.766；单点Dice 0.768；HD95 36.41 mm），0.91M可训练参数、120 ms延迟、4.9 GB峰值内存。DiSCo单位百万参数Dice最高（4.66），总体Dice 0.653、单点Dice 0.698、HD95 49.53 mm。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了精度-效率的权衡，支持在计算与标注数据有限时采用PEFT进行肝脏肿瘤分割以降低适配成本。

Abstract: We evaluated parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) of the Segment Anything Model (SAM) for liver tumor segmentation in abdominal CT of colorectal liver metastases. We compared Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), 4-bit Quantized LoRA (QLoRA), a convolutional adapter (Conv-Adapter), and our Directional Spectral Top-K adapter (DiSCo), training only adapters while freezing the SAM backbone. DiSCo derives spectral bases from singular value decomposition of row-normalized weights and learns rank-gated spectral coefficients, per-output magnitude offsets, and a spectral gain, with optional Top-K rank selection at inference and 0.14 M trainable parameters. We benchmarked five prompting regimes: no prompt, single-point, multi-point, and bounding boxes at intersection over union 0.50 and 0.75. Conv-Adapter and LoRA achieved the highest accuracy (overall Dice 0.793 and 0.792; single-point Dice 0.795 and 0.792; 95th-percentile Hausdorff distance (HD95) 32 mm). QLoRA was close (overall Dice 0.766; single-point Dice 0.768; HD95 36.41 mm), with 0.91 M trainable parameters, 120 ms latency, and 4.9 GB peak memory. DiSCo achieved the highest Dice per million trainable parameters (4.66), with overall Dice 0.653, single-point Dice 0.698, and HD95 49.53 mm. These results show an accuracy-efficiency trade-off and support PEFT for liver tumor segmentation with reduced adaptation costs when compute and labeled data are limited. Code: https://github.com/Ramtin-Mojtahedi/PEFT-SAM-Liver-CT

</details>


### [55] [Talking to Me or Someone Else? Rethinking Talk-to-Me Detection in Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14118)
*Feiyu Du,Xi He,Jia Li,Yapeng Tian,Weili Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将"对我说话"(TTM)识别从离线片段级二分类重构为在线帧级多状态预测任务，构建了约90万帧标注的Online TTM数据集，并提出融合音、视、语音语义线索的多模态模型，在TTM上达到75.5%的帧级F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTM研究多采用离线片段级识别，与在线交互场景不匹配，且忽略了对他人说话、自言自语、背景等多样非TTM说话状态，导致模型与真实第一人称交互需求脱节。

Method: 将TTM重新定义为在线帧级预测任务，建模多种非TTM状态而非单一负类；基于Ego4D社交交互基准构建Online TTM Dataset（406个第一人称视频片段、约90万帧，逐帧标注背景、TTM、对他人说话、自言自语等类别）；评估五种适配基线，并提出跨模态整合社交线索的新模型，联合利用音频、视觉与语音语义线索。

Result: 多模态模型在TTM帧级F1上达到75.5%，优于强基线，并支持系统分析不同说话状态对TTM识别的影响。

Conclusion: 将TTM转向在线帧级、多说话状态建模更贴合真实第一人称社交交互，多模态社交线索融合有效提升识别性能，并为后续研究提供了新的数据集与基准。

Abstract: Online understanding of who is talking to the camera wearer is a key capability for egocentric social interaction. However, existing talk-to-me (TTM) studies are commonly formulated as offline clip-level recognition, which is poorly aligned with online interaction and overlooks the diverse non-TTM speaking states that naturally arise in egocentric videos. In this paper, we revisit this problem by reformulating it as an online, frame-level prediction task. Instead of treating TTM as a binary problem against a single negative class, we model it in the presence of diverse and previously underexplored non-TTM states, such as talking-to-others, self-talking, and background conditions. To support this new formulation, we construct an Online TTM Dataset consisting of 406 egocentric video clips with approximately 900K annotated frames, each labeled with frame-level social interaction categories (e.g., background, TTM, talking-to-others, self-talking), by extending the Ego4D social interaction benchmark. In this benchmark, we evaluate five adapted baselines and develop a new model that integrates social cues across modalities. Experimental results show that our multimodal model, which jointly leverages audio, visual, and speech-semantic cues, achieves 75.5% frame-level F1 on TTM, outperforming strong baselines and enabling a systematic analysis of how different speaking states affect TTM recognition.

</details>


### [56] [SignMimic: Robust High-Quality Sign Language Motion Generation via Human-Shape-Oblivious Pose Transfer Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14122)
*Zhewen He,Junyi Yu,Haomian Huang,Zhenhua Li,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SignMimic，将手语视频模仿解耦为刚性规范化、非刚性适配与姿态补全，再接入条件扩散生成，提升形状与时空一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语视频模仿需在保留身份与语言形式的同时复现源动作，但已有单体式生成器将刚性运动、非刚性形变与视角相关补全纠缠在一起，导致手形漂移和时空不稳定。

Method: SignMimic 采用三阶段分解：(i) 基于 TNet 的 SE(3) 刚性规范化稳定全局姿态；(ii) 在规范空间用 NIF2D 进行非刚性适配，保留手部/面部细粒度关节器与协同发音；(iii) 条件视频扩散前使用 Pose-MAE 风格补全，从而注入几何与语言学先验。

Result: 在 ASL 50K、How2Sign、CSL News 等大规模数据集上，视频质量、身份相似度与帧连续性达到 SOTA 级水平，生成视频回译（SLT）损失最小；消融实验验证了三个模块的作用。

Conclusion: 将刚性规范化、非刚性适配与补全显式解耦并注入几何和语言先验，可有效提升手语视频模仿的形状与时空一致性。代码、模型权重与视频示例将公开。

Abstract: We study the challenge of sign language video mimicking: given a driving video and a single reference frame, synthesize a video where the target signer reproduces the source
  motion while preserving identity and linguistic form. Prior pipelines entangle rigid motion, non-rigid deformation, and view-dependent completion in a monolithic generator,
  causing handshape drift and spatio-temporal instability. We present SignMimic, which (i) applies a TNet-based model to study SE(3) rigid canonicalization to stabilize global
  pose, (ii) performs non-rigid adaptation in a canonical space to preserve fine-grained articulators (hands/face) and coarticulation via NIF2D, and (iii) uses Pose-MAE-style
  completion before conditional video diffusion. This factorization injects geometric and linguistic priors, yielding shape and spatio-temporal consistency. On several large-scale
  datasets (ASL 50K, How2Sign, CSL News), SignMimic achieves state-of-the-art-level performance on video quality, identity similarity, and frame continuity while also achieving
  minimal loss when performing back translation (SLT) on generated videos. Ablations confirm the role of rigid canonicalization, non-rigid adaptation, and completion. Code, model
  checkpoints, and video examples will be released.

</details>


### [57] [3D Gait-Based Autism Classification Using Attention-Enhanced Deep Learning with Cross-Fold Statistical Stability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14159)
*Md Nadim Mahamood,Md Arif Shahriar,Md Parvej Sikder,Md Rasul Islam,Md Shafi Ud Doula,Md Ashraful Alam,Kamrul Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种注意力增强的Transformer框架用于自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）步态分类，在两个结构不同的3D步态特征集上验证，公共数据集达99.00%准确率，私有数据集达95.00%准确率，并通过五折交叉验证评估了稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASD早期诊断依赖的临床评估往往主观、耗时且需专家参与。步态作为非侵入性行为生物标志物具有自动化筛查潜力，但现有研究多局限于单数据集评估、卷积架构，且仅对交叉验证性能作描述性总结，缺乏对折间稳定性的正式评估。

Method: 提出注意力增强的Transformer框架，在两个结构不同的3D步态特征表示上进行ASD分类评估：一是预计算的统计步态描述符，二是原始生物力学地面反作用力测量。采用五折交叉验证。

Result: 在公开的基于Kinect的基准数据集上，准确率99.00%、精确率99.02%、召回率99.00%、F1值99.00%、特异性99.00%，优于所比较的现有方法。在独立的私有测力板数据集上，平均准确率95.00%、精确率93.81%、召回率96.67%、F1值95.13%、特异性93.33%。

Conclusion: 注意力增强Transformer框架在两类步态特征表示上均取得较高的ASD分类性能，并在公开数据集上超越现有方法，显示出作为自动化ASD筛查工具的潜力。

Abstract: Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) is a neurodevelopmental condition whose early diagnosis remains challenging because conventional clinical assessments are often subjective, time-consuming, and require expert evaluation. Gait provides a promising non-invasive behavioral biomarker for auto- mated ASD screening; however, existing studies have primarily relied on single-dataset evaluations, convolutional architectures, and descriptive summaries of cross-validation performance without formally assessing fold-to-fold stability. This study addresses these gaps with an attention-enhanced Transformer framework for ASD classification, evaluated on two structurally different 3D gait feature representations: precomputed statistical gait descriptors and raw biomechanical ground-reaction- force measurements. Under five-fold cross-validation, the proposed framework achieved 99.00% accuracy, 99.02% precision, 99.00% recall, 99.00% F1-score, and 99.00% specificity on the public Kinect-based benchmark, exceeding the performance of the compared state-of-the-art methods. On the independent private force-plate dataset, it achieved mean values of 95.00% accuracy, 93.81% precision, 96.67% recall, 95.13% F1-score, and 93.33% specificity.

</details>


### [58] [Bi-Level Routing and Sparse Spatial Attention based Multi-View BEV 3D Object Detection for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14185)
*Jing Zhang,Jiaqi Liu,Zibo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出改进的BEV三维目标检测算法Sparse-BEVNet，通过BRA、CGA和稀疏空间交叉注意力机制解决计算复杂度、多尺度特征提取和视图变换效率问题，在nuScenes上mAP达45.2%、NDS达54.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于鸟瞰图（BEV）的多视图三维目标检测面临计算复杂度高、多尺度特征提取困难以及密集2D到BEV视图变换效率低等挑战，本文旨在解决这些问题。

Method: 提出Sparse-BEVNet：1）在图像特征提取网络中引入双层路由注意力（BRA）机制以降低主干计算负担；2）在特征融合模块采用级联组注意力（CGA），在不增加额外计算开销的情况下增强不同层级特征的深度交互；3）采用稀疏空间交叉注意力机制替代传统密集视图投影流程。

Result: 在公开nuScenes数据集上，所提方法取得45.2%的mAP和54.5%的NDS，相较基线模型分别提升3.6%和2.8%。

Conclusion: Sparse-BEVNet通过注意力机制与稀疏视图变换的有效结合，在提升检测精度的同时缓解了计算与效率问题，验证了其在BEV三维目标检测中的有效性。

Abstract: Bird's Eye View (BEV)-based multi-view 3D object detection suffers from challenges of computational complexity, multi-scale feature extraction, and efficiency of dense 2D-to-BEV view transformation. To address these problems, this paper proposes an improved BEV 3D object detection algorithm Sparse-BEVNet. Firstly, a Bi-Level Routing Attention (BRA) mechanism is introduced into the image feature extraction network to reduce the computational burden of the backbone. Second, Cascaded Group Attention (CGA) is employed in the feature fusion module, which enhances deep interaction across features of different hierarchical levels without introducing additional computational overhead. Furthermore, a Sparse Spatial Cross-Attention mechanism is adopted to replace the conventional dense view projection pipeline.
