Experimental results on the public nuScenes dataset demonstrate that the proposed method achieves a mean Average Precision (mAP) of 45.2% and a nuScenes Detection Score (NDS) of 54.5%, corresponding to 3.6% and 2.8% improvements relative to the baseline model, respectively.

</details>


### [59] [What Input Resolution Is Required for Bird Species Identification, and What Is Its Latency Cost on an Edge Device? A Study of 14 Input Resolutions and Six Architectures with On-Device Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14247)
*Takeshi Nishikawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模因子实验（14种输入分辨率×6种架构×2种训练评估机制×30个随机种子，共2520个检查点和5040次评估）系统研究鸟类撞击缓解分类器中输入分辨率N的设计问题，并在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano上实测延迟，得出四条关键结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风电场鸟类撞击缓解需要识别仅占几十像素的远距离鸟类，因此分类器的输入分辨率N是一个设计变量而非固定规格，需要系统研究其最优选择及相关权衡。

Method: 采用因子实验设计，覆盖14种边长N（16至224）、6种架构、2种训练与评估机制、30个随机种子，共2520个检查点和5040次评估，并在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano上测量延迟。

Result: (1) 最优N取决于目标：ResNet50在N=112达到0.90（估计1.85 ms，测试0.8980），DINOv2-L在N=144达到0.95（12.70 ms）；换模型比提高N收益更大（+5.93 vs +2.33）。(2) 降低N的收益取决于预处理路径：N=224→80在逐文件解码时节省13.5%，检测器一次解码4K帧时节省46.2%；Pareto集从20增至23。(3) 精度需在部署引擎上测量：半精度使ViT-S/16在N≥96时损失4-7个点，CNN在0.1点内；176个目标中26个选择不同；损坏的FP16引擎可能更快且无法从延迟察觉，纳入14个ViT-S/16 FP32配置可将推荐移至0.931-0.938区间并低于10 ms预算。(4) ViT-L规模模型可适配该设备但激活超出FP16范围；在transformer块边界切分图可将FP32限制在受影响段，使DINOv2-L链比单引擎构建快1.85倍。此外量化了机制差异符号随种子数的稳定性，去除部分组共享的敏感性划分保留了选择边界处全部14个非平凡符号。

Conclusion: 输入分辨率应作为设计变量依目标选择；降低分辨率的收益依赖预处理路径；精度必须在部署引擎上验证（尤其半精度对ViT影响大）；大模型可通过图切分在受限设备上高效部署，同时机制差异符号对种子数和敏感性划分具有稳定性。

Abstract: Bird-strike mitigation at wind farms requires identifying distant birds that span only tens of pixels, so the classifier's input resolution N is a design variable, not a fixed specification. We study it with a factorial design over 14 side lengths N (16 to 224), six architectures, two training and evaluation regimes and 30 random seeds -- 2,520 checkpoints and 5,040 evaluations -- plus latency measured on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano. Four results. (1) The selected N depends on the target: 0.90 is met on validation by ResNet50 at N=112 in an estimated 1.85 ms (0.8980 on test) and 0.95 by DINOv2-L at N=144 in 12.70 ms; changing the model buys more accuracy than raising N (+5.93 versus +2.33 points at N=112). (2) The benefit of lowering N depends on the assumed preprocessing path: N=224 -> 80 saves 13.5% when each individual is decoded from its own file but 46.2% when the detector decodes the 4K frame once; the Pareto set grows from 20 to 23 configurations. (3) Accuracy must be measured on the deployed engine: half precision costs ViT-S/16 alone 4 to 7 points at N>=96 while the CNNs stay within 0.1 points, and with selection held at validation the choice differs at 26 of 176 targets. A broken FP16 engine can run faster than a correct one, undetectable from latency; admitting 14 ViT-S/16 FP32 configurations moves the recommendation over the 0.931-0.938 band and under the 10 ms budget. (4) ViT-L-scale models fit this device, but activations exceed the FP16 range; splitting the graph at transformer-block boundaries confines FP32 to the affected segments, making the deployed DINOv2-L chain 1.85x faster than the single-engine build. We also quantify how the regime-difference sign stabilises with seed count; a sensitivity split removing some forms of group sharing preserves all 14 non-trivial signs at the selection boundary.

</details>


### [60] [Sparsity-Adaptive Sharpness-Aware Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14274)
*Shiryu Ueno,Yoshikazu Hayashi,Kunihito Kato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出稀疏自适应锐度感知最小化（SA-SAM）与幅度加权Hessian（MWH）方法，以在高稀疏度下保持深度神经网络对常见损坏的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实部署场景中，模型需要既紧凑又对常见损坏鲁棒。但在部署相关的高稀疏度下，标准剪枝流程往往会降低损坏鲁棒性，而现有的锐度感知训练/剪枝方法带来的鲁棒性提升有限。因此需要一种在提高稀疏度的同时不损害鲁棒性的方案。

Method: 提出稀疏自适应锐度感知最小化（SA-SAM），通过保持平均绝对扰动（基于ℓ1的代理指标）在稀疏度增加时近似不变，从而推导出依赖于稀疏度的SAM/ASAM扰动半径。此外，作为简单补充选项，评估了源自二阶移除路径分析的幅度加权Hessian（MWH），其重要性正比于Diag(F)_i·|w_i|，其中Diag(F)为实现中用作曲率代理的对角经验Fisher。

Result: 在CIFAR-10-C、CIFAR-100-C和ImageNet-100-C上，该方法在80–90%稀疏度下取得了比所比较的剪枝基线更强的损坏鲁棒性，同时保持了干净精度。此外，通过报告部署相关稀疏度水平下稀疏执行的实测推理吞吐量，量化了鲁棒性—吞吐量权衡。

Conclusion: SA-SAM与MWH能够在部署相关的高稀疏度下有效提升损坏鲁棒性并保持干净精度，为紧凑且鲁棒的深度模型部署提供了可行方案，同时给出了稀疏执行的鲁棒性—吞吐量权衡分析。

Abstract: Deploying deep neural networks in real-world settings requires models that are both compact and robust to common corruptions. However, at deployment-relevant high sparsity, standard pruning pipelines often degrade corruption robustness, and existing sharpness-aware training/pruning approaches provide limited robustness gains. We address this issue by introducing Sparsity-Adaptive Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SA-SAM), which derives a sparsity-dependent SAM/ASAM perturbation radius by keeping the mean absolute perturbation (an $\ell_1$-based proxy) approximately invariant as sparsity increases. As a simple complementary option, we evaluate Magnitude-Weighted Hessian (MWH), derived from a second-order removal-path analysis, yielding an importance proportional to $\mathrm{Diag}(F)_i\,|w_i|$, where $\mathrm{Diag}(F)$ is the diagonal empirical Fisher used as a curvature proxy in our implementation. Across CIFAR-10-C, CIFAR-100-C, and ImageNet-100-C, our approach achieved stronger corruption robustness than the considered pruning baselines at 80--90\% sparsity, while preserving clean accuracy. We additionally quantify the robustness--throughput trade-off by reporting measured inference throughput under sparse execution at deployment-relevant sparsity levels.

</details>


### [61] [SpermYOLO: A Coordinated YOLO-Based Detector for Accurate and Efficient Sperm and Impurity Detection in Microscopic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14278)
*Shengqi Chen,Zilin Wang,Xingyu Pan,Wenting Yu,Pengchao Deng,Guohua Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SpermYOLO框架，用于显微图像中精子与杂质联合检测，在SVIA基准达97.2%精子AP与75.4%杂质AP，并在跨场景SDTB上保持有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显微图像中精子检测受密集分布、视觉相似的伪影及类精子杂质干扰，准确检测对计算机辅助精液分析至关重要，但现有方法面临挑战。

Method: 基于YOLOv11构建协调紧凑框架SpermYOLO，引入四项改进：C3k2-IDB用于通道判别特征提取，D2SEM用于空间-光谱语义增强，MFM用于自适应多尺度特征融合，DESD Head用于细节增强共享预测。

Result: SVIA基准上精子AP 97.2%、杂质AP 75.4%，优于通用检测器、专用精子检测模型及改进YOLO变体；相比基线，精子AP、杂质AP、mAP50、mAP50:95分别提升1.6、10.0、5.8、2.7个百分点且保持轻量。跨场景SDTB上mAP50与mAP50:95最高达74.8%和31.2%。

Conclusion: 消融实验与定性分析验证了各模块贡献及更聚焦的特征响应，表明SpermYOLO是应对挑战性显微成像场景下精子检测的有效且高效方案。

Abstract: Accurate sperm detection is essential for computer-assisted semen analysis, yet it remains challenging in microscopic images due to dense distributions, visually similar artifacts, and sperm-like impurities. In this paper, we propose SpermYOLO, a coordinated and compact YOLOv11-derived framework for joint sperm and impurity detection in microscopic images. SpermYOLO introduces four architectural improvements: C3k2-IDB for channel-wise discriminative feature extraction, D2SEM for spatial--spectral semantic enhancement, MFM for adaptive multi-scale feature fusion, and the DESD Head for detail-enhanced shared prediction. Experiments on the SVIA semen microscopic imaging benchmark show that SpermYOLO achieves 97.2\% sperm AP and 75.4\% impurity AP, outperforming generic detectors, dedicated sperm detection models, and improved YOLO variants. Compared with the baseline model, SpermYOLO improves sperm AP, impurity AP, $\mathrm{mAP}_{50}$, and $\mathrm{mAP}_{50:95}$ by 1.6, 10.0, 5.8, and 2.7 percentage points, respectively, while preserving a lightweight model scale. Cross-scene evaluation on the SDTB testicular-biopsy microscopy benchmark shows that SpermYOLO remains effective with extremely small sperm targets and complex tissue backgrounds, achieving the highest $\mathrm{mAP}_{50}$ and $\mathrm{mAP}_{50:95}$ of 74.8\% and 31.2\%, respectively. Ablation studies and qualitative analyses further support these improvements by demonstrating the contributions of the proposed modules and showing more focused feature response patterns than the baseline model. These findings suggest that SpermYOLO is an effective and efficient approach for sperm detection in challenging microscopic imaging scenarios.

</details>


### [62] [Vision-Language Models for Criterion-Level Grading of Handwritten Examinations in Outcome-Based Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14284)
*Md Khalid Syfullah,Asif Hasan Tonmoy,Saad Ahmed,S. M. Jahangir Alam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估视觉语言模型在基于成果的手写评分中的表现，覆盖准确率、人机一致性、重复运行可靠性、错误集中度和解释质量五个维度，并发布了评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准级评分能连接考试表现与学习成果，但人工评分带来工作量大和评分者间差异问题。研究旨在检验视觉语言模型能否用于手写、基于成果的评分场景。

Method: 使用485份本科生考试答案中的1,982条准则级记录，比较20种配置（Qwen2.5-VL、InternVL3、Pixtral、Donut基线及级联集成），评估设置包括零样本、少样本提示、部分微调和LoRA。两名独立教师重评全部291条测试准则作为人类一致性基线。

Result: Qwen2.5-VL+LoRA达到QWK 0.727、平均绝对误差0.435分，优于人类配对平均QWK 0.551。LoRA优于部分微调，少样本提示在所有配置中降低QWK。组内相关为0.790-0.874，但50.2-63.6%的准则在五次运行中改变分数。注意力引导删除相较随机掩码无显著优势，四位教师对解释有用性未达成共识。

Conclusion: 研究强调需要针对评分细则的校准、可重复评分、结果性错误审查和解释的独立验证。发布的评估协议支持准则级评估研究及有人工监督的评分工具。

Abstract: Criterion-level grading connects examination performance to learning outcomes, but manual marking introduces workload and variation between markers. This study evaluates vision-language models (VLMs) for handwritten outcome-based assessment across five dimensions: accuracy, human agreement, repeated-run reliability, error concentration, and explanation quality. Using 1,982 criterion-level records from 485 undergraduate examination answers, we compare 20 configurations spanning Qwen2.5-VL, InternVL3, Pixtral, a Donut baseline, and a cascade ensemble. Evaluation setups include zero-shot prompting, few-shot prompting, partial fine-tuning, and Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). Two independent faculty markers regraded all 291 test criteria, providing a human agreement baseline on the same assessment materials. Qwen2.5-VL with LoRA achieved Quadratic Weighted Kappa (QWK) of 0.727 and mean absolute error of 0.435 marks against the examiner, compared with mean human-pair QWK of 0.551. This comparison reflects calibration to the examiner's training marks. LoRA outperformed partial fine-tuning for all three instruction-tuned VLMs, while few-shot prompting reduced QWK in every configuration with valid prompted scores. Aggregate reliability and exact repeatability diverged: intraclass correlations ranged from 0.790 to 0.874, yet 50.2-63.6% of criteria changed marks across five sampled runs. Attention-guided deletion showed no statistically significant advantage over random masking, and four faculty reviewers reached no consensus on explanation usefulness. These findings highlight the need for rubric-specific calibration, repeatable scoring, review of consequential errors, and separate validation of explanations. The released evaluation protocol supports criterion-level assessment research and grading tools with teacher oversight.

</details>


### [63] [Robust low-rank tensor completion via factorized weighted tensor schatten-p norm minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14307)
*Binghao Wang,Feng Zhang,Wendong Wang,Jianjun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种加权Schatten-p张量分解模型WSpTFI与WSpTFII，在t-product框架下实现免SVD、组件特定加权的张量补全，并给出收敛性理论保证与多任务实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩张量分解的谱正则化器对所有奇异分量施加统一收缩，易过度衰减主导低秩分量；而分解式变体要么缺少组件特定加权，要么依赖昂贵的SVD，因此需要新的加权方案。

Method: 在t-product框架下提出WSpTFI与WSpTFII。WSpTFI基于分解式加权张量Schatten-p范数恒等式，允许灵活（可非对称）的因子指数；WSpTFII利用变换域列对能量构造正则项，实现免SVD主因子更新与列剪枝机制。分别设计IRW-ADMM与IRLS-BSUM算法。

Result: 理论上建立了加权分解关系，并给出WSpTFI的条件极限KKT刻画；对WSpTFII证明了固定δ平滑目标下的充分下降机制，进而保证渐近正则性及尾部累积点的平稳性。实验在合成张量补全、彩色图像恢复、高光谱修复和印刷电路板缺陷检测上均展现出有竞争力的重建质量与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 两种模型在多种退化条件下兼具重建质量与鲁棒性，为免SVD、组件加权的低秩张量补全提供了有效且具理论支撑的方案。

Abstract: Low-rank tensor factorization provides a flexible framework for completing multidimensional data from incomplete and corrupted observations. However, unweighted spectral regularizers impose a common shrinkage profile across singular components, which may excessively attenuate dominant low-rank components, and factorized variants either lack component-specific weighting or require costly singular value decompositions (SVDs). This paper proposes two weighted Schatten-$p$ tensor factorization models, termed \WSpTFI{} and \WSpTFII{}, under the tensor-tensor product (t-product) framework to address these limitations. \WSpTFI{} is motivated by a factorized weighted tensor Schatten-$p$ norm identity and permits flexible, possibly asymmetric factor exponents. \WSpTFII{} constructs a regularizer from transform-domain column-pair energies, yielding SVD-free main factor updates and a column-pruning mechanism for reducing redundant rank components. This paper further develops an iteratively reweighted alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM)-type scheme for \WSpTFI{} and an iteratively reweighted least squares (IRLS)--block successive upper-bound minimization (BSUM) scheme for \WSpTFII{}. Theoretical analysis establishes the weighted factorization relation and provides a conditional limiting Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) characterization for \WSpTFI{}. For \WSpTFII{}, the actual damped quadratic block updates yield a quantitative sufficient-decrease mechanism for the fixed-$δ$ smoothed factor objective. This implies asymptotic regularity, and every accumulation point of the fixed-dimensional tail is stationary. Experiments on synthetic tensor completion, color-image restoration, hyperspectral inpainting, and printed-circuit-board defect detection demonstrate competitive reconstruction quality and robustness under various degradation conditions.

</details>


### [64] [S3-Tracker: Self-Supervised Surgical Tissue Tracking With Contrastive Random Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14313)
*Jiaming Zhang,Zijian Wu,Mehran Armand,Septimiu Salcudean*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自监督的"任意点跟踪"方法，利用无标注手术视频，通过全局像素对应和对比随机游走推断点轨迹，在内窥镜视频中达到与半监督方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内窥镜视频中的鲁棒点跟踪对计算机辅助干预和自主机器人手术至关重要，可在软组织形变下实现术中视频与术前影像的持续配准。但监督跟踪方法依赖大规模标注数据，而手术场景使可靠轨迹标注十分困难，因此需要减少对标注数据的依赖。

Method: 提出自监督的 Track-Any-Point 方法：从无标注手术视频中学习，建立全局像素对应关系，并通过对比随机游走（contrastive random walks）推断点轨迹，无需人工标注即可训练。

Result: 在无标注训练条件下，该方法性能与现有半监督方法相当，并能隐式处理软组织形变。

Conclusion: 研究证明了在手术环境中进行自监督点跟踪的可行性，并显示出减少对标注数据依赖的潜力。

Abstract: Robust point tracking in endoscopic videos is essential for computer-assisted intervention and autonomous robotic surgery, enabling continuous registration between intraoperative video and preoperative imaging despite soft tissue deformation. However, supervised tracking methods depend on large annotated datasets, while surgical conditions make reliable trajectory annotation challenging. We propose a self-supervised Track-Any-Point approach that learns from unlabeled surgical videos by establishing global pixel correspondences and inferring point trajectories through contrastive random walks. Trained without annotations, our method achieves performance comparable to existing semi-supervised approaches while implicitly handling tissue deformation. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of self-supervised point tracking in surgical environments and its potential to reduce reliance on annotated data.

</details>


### [65] [Learning Continuous Source Responses For Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14316)
*Manni Cui,Ruiqi Liu,Zijian Yu,Hao Tan,Zibo Wei,Zian Wang,Ziheng Qin,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Jun Lan,Shu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CuRe 框架，将 AI 生成图像检测从二分类任务重构为对真伪混合比例的连续回归，并选取紧凑的源响应子空间以抑制捷径线索，在十个基准上平均平衡准确率达 89.7%，超出第二名 5.2 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像生成技术进步使合成图像难以与真实照片区分，威胁视觉媒体可信度。现有检测器在域内表现良好，但鲁棒性和跨生成器泛化能力有限，通常归因于对捷径线索的过拟合。尽管许多方法试图抑制捷径学习，但大多仍保留二分类作为训练任务，未重新思考任务本身如何塑造所学表示。

Method: 提出 CuRe 框架，学习连续源响应（Continuous Source Responses）。将主干网络适配重构为对真实—生成混合比例的回归，提供更细粒度监督，促使模型捕捉超越二分类端点分离的真伪相关变化；并选取紧凑的源响应子空间以抑制无关变化，限制最终分类器接触潜在捷径线索。

Result: 在十个公开基准上平均平衡准确率为 89.7%，比第二名方法高出 5.2 个百分点。进一步实验表明在不同视觉主干网络上具有一致的泛化增益，并对常见图像退化具备强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 从训练任务视角重构真伪检测任务可有效提升 AI 生成图像检测的泛化性与鲁棒性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Advances in image generation have made synthetic images increasingly difficult to distinguish from real photographs, raising concerns about the trustworthiness of visual media. Existing AI-generated image detectors often perform well on in-domain data, but their robustness and cross-generator generalization remain limited. These limitations are commonly attributed to overfitting to shortcut cues. Although many methods seek to suppress shortcut learning, most retain binary classification as the training task without reconsidering how the task itself shapes the learned representations. We introduce CuRe, a framework for learning Continuous Source Responses that revisits authenticity detection from the perspective of the training task. CuRe reformulates backbone adaptation as regression of real-generated mixing ratios, providing finer supervision that encourages the model to capture authenticity-related variation beyond binary endpoint separation. We further select a compact source-response subspace to suppress nuisance variation and limit the final classifier's access to potential shortcut cues. Across ten public benchmarks, CuRe achieves an average balanced accuracy of 89.7%, exceeding the second-best method by 5.2 percentage points. Further experiments demonstrate consistent generalization gains across visual backbones and strong robustness to common image degradations. Code is available at https://github.com/manic-cui/CuRe

</details>


### [66] [Mobile CT Services for Rural, Regional, and Remote Areas: Current Practice and Future Integration with Telehealth and Regulatory-Authorised AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14347)
*Zhicheng Lu,Md Zahid Islam,M Mamun Huda,Kristie Sweeney,Shayne Chau,Oliver Mulcock,Corey Hemopo,Catherine Keniry,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述移动CT、远程医疗与AI在CT中的应用，评估其临床功能、技术成熟度与部署障碍，指出现有技术个体成熟但整合应用仍有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农村、偏远及资源匮乏地区CT成像与专业解读的可及性仍然受限，需要借助移动CT、远程医疗和AI扩展先进影像服务。

Method: 综述性研究，考察部署于卡车、拖车、救护车等平台的移动CT系统、支持CT医疗的远程医疗技术，以及已获监管授权或已临床部署的CT AI；按筛查诊断、患者监测、风险预测、干预/治疗决策支持四类临床功能评估，涵盖神经、胸部、心血管、腹部、肿瘤、肌骨及介入影像，并比较监管状态、部署状态与估计技术成熟度（TRL）。

Result: 当前证据显示，移动CT、远程医疗和常规CT的AI各自相对成熟，但三者的完整整合在临床环境中部署和验证仍不广泛。

Conclusion: 关键障碍包括监管差异、域偏移、连接需求、成本、工作流整合、网络安全及患者层面获益证据有限；未来研究应优先在真实世界和欠服务临床环境中开展前瞻性、多中心整合CT系统评估。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT) plays an essential role in clinical workflow to improve patient outcomes. However, access to CT imaging and specialist interpretation remains limited, particularly in rural, regional, remote (RRR), and other resource-limited settings. Recent advances in mobile CT, telehealth, and artificial intelligence (AI) provide opportunities to extend advanced imaging services to populations in RRR settings. This review examines: 1) mobile CT systems deployed in trucks, trailers, ambulances, and other mobile platforms; 2) telehealth technologies supporting CT-based healthcare; and 3) AI for CT that has received regulatory authorisation or is currently deployed in clinical practice. Applications are evaluated across four clinical functions: screening and diagnosis, patient monitoring, risk prediction, and intervention or therapeutic decision support. The review covers neurological, thoracic, cardiovascular, abdominal, oncological, musculoskeletal, and interventional imaging, with particular attention to stroke, cancer, and other image-guided treatment. Other factors such as regulatory status, deployment status, and estimated technology readiness (TRL) level are compared. Current evidence indicates that mobile CT, telehealth, and AI for conventional CT are individually relatively mature, but fully integration of these technologies remains less widely deployed and validated in the clinical settings. Key barriers include regulatory variation, domain shift, connectivity requirements, cost, workflow integration, cybersecurity, and limited evidence of patient-level benefit. Future research should prioritise prospective, multicentre evaluation of integrated CT systems in real-world and underserved clinical settings.

</details>


### [67] [Two-Stage Mixture-of-LoRA for Multi-Task Medical Vision-Language Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14350)
*Zhanghao Chen,Yuanyuan Li,Zhenyu Lu,Shuo Gao,Guangquan Zhou,Yikun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段Mixture-of-LoRA框架（基于MedGemma-1.5-4B），通过共享LoRA与六个任务专家LoRA结合两阶段训练，应对医学视觉语言模型联合适配中的异构输出、梯度冲突与数据不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型需在单一模型中完成从诊断分类到报告生成等多种临床任务，但联合适配面临三大挑战：输出格式异构、任务梯度冲突以及训练数据不平衡。

Method: 构建共享-专用Mixture-of-LoRA架构，包含一个共享LoRA和六个任务专用专家LoRA，并采用两阶段训练：第一阶段在所有任务上联合训练共享LoRA与全部专家LoRA；第二阶段冻结骨干、共享LoRA及非目标任务专家，逐一对单个任务专家进行微调。分类与回归任务额外进行模态平衡的延续训练，将较小的模态组重复采样至与最大组规模一致。

Result: 在FLARE 2026 Task 3测试集上，分类平衡准确率达0.85，多标签分类micro-F1为0.48，检测F1为0.79，回归MAE为17.39。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 两阶段Mixture-of-LoRA能够有效缓解多任务联合适配的格式异构、梯度冲突与数据不平衡问题，在FLARE 2026 Task 3多项任务上取得较强性能。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) allow a single model to perform clinical image analysis tasks ranging from diagnosis classification to report generation. However, joint adaptation is challenged by heterogeneous output formats, conflicting task gradients, and imbalanced training data. Hence, we present \textbf{Two-Stage Mixture-of-LoRA}, a framework built on MedGemma-1.5-4B. The framework uses a shared-specific Mixture-of-LoRA architecture comprising one shared LoRA and six task-specific expert LoRAs, together with a two-stage training procedure. In Stage 1, we jointly train the shared LoRA and all task-specific expert LoRAs on all tasks. In Stage 2, we first freeze the backbone, the shared LoRA, and all non-target experts, and refine one task expert at a time. Classification and regression then receive an additional modality-balanced continuation, in which smaller modality groups are repeated to match the largest group. In the FLARE 2026 Task 3 test sets, the proposed method achieves 0.85 balanced accuracy for classification, 0.48 micro-F1 for multi-label classification, 0.79 detection F1, and 17.39 regression MAE. Code is available at https://github.com/YuanYL03/MICCAI-FLARE-2026-Challenge-Task3-2D.

</details>


### [68] [Beyond Natural Images: Rethinking AI-Generated Image Detection in Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14352)
*Zhangjie Fu,Jiazhen Yan,Yuanwen Chen,Xinquan Yu,Yanzhe Li,Hui Jiang,Lei Gao,Chenfu Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了面向AI生成文档图像的诊断基准AIGDoc-Pilot与大规模数据集AIGDoc，揭示现有检测器在文档图像上性能显著下降（平均AUC降低超7%），并指出生成伪影的局部空间不一致性与文本密度对真伪可分性的影响，为文档场景检测研究提供数据与洞见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测研究主要面向自然图像，而发票、报销单、证书、医疗记录等文档图像常出现在敏感现实场景中，却严重缺乏评估与数据支撑。作者希望填补这一空白，探究现有检测器在AI生成文档图像上的实际表现与失效原因。

Method: 1）构建受控诊断基准AIGDoc-Pilot，用以量化现有检测器在AI生成文档图像上的性能退化；2）基于诊断结果总结两条文档特有性质：生成伪影在局部区域存在强空间不一致性，文本密度显著影响真伪可分性（文本密集区域判别证据更强）；3）据此构建更大规模、以文档为中心的数据集AIGDoc，涵盖多样真实文档及由多种先进生成与编辑模型产出的AI生成版本。

Result: 在AIGDoc-Pilot上，现有检测器在AI生成文档图像上性能大幅下降，平均AUC下降超过7%。在AIGDoc上的大量实验表明，现有检测器仍难以可靠识别AI生成文档，而基于文档数据的训练能够部分缩小这一差距。

Conclusion: 现有检测器在文档场景下泛化能力不足，文档特有性质（空间不一致伪影、文本密度影响）是关键线索，文档数据训练有助于提升表现。该工作为开发可靠、可泛化的文档场景AI生成图像检测器提供了数据集与实证洞见，代码与数据将在论文接收后公开。

Abstract: AI-generated image detection has attracted increasing attention, but existing evaluations mainly focus on natural images, leaving AI-generated document images largely underexplored. This omission is concerning because documents often appear in sensitive real-world scenarios, such as invoices, expense reports, certificates, and medical records. In this paper, we first construct a controlled diagnostic benchmark, AIGDoc-Pilot, and reveal that existing detectors suffer substantial performance degradation on AI-generated document images, with the mean AUC dropping by more than 7%. Based on this, we further reveal two document-specific properties behind this gap: generation artifacts exhibit strong spatial inconsistency across local regions, and text density significantly affects real-synthetic separability, where text-dense regions offer stronger discriminative evidence. Motivated by these findings, we construct AIGDoc, a larger document-centric dataset containing diverse real-world documents and AI-generated counterparts produced by multiple advanced generation and editing models. Extensive experiments on AIGDoc demonstrate that existing detectors still struggle to reliably identify AI-generated documents, while document-based training partially narrows the gap. Together, these results offer valuable insights for developing dependable and generalizable detectors in document-centric scenarios. The code and datasets will be made publicly available upon acceptance of the paper.

</details>


### [69] [Rethinking Camouflage Image Generation towards a Training-Free Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14377)
*Haodong Yang,Zhongling Huang,Gong Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出免训练的伪装图像生成范式，并实例化为 FreeCam，基于冻结的修复扩散框架，通过上下文推理模块与内在外观模块联合实现语义兼容与外观同化，取得最优生成质量与伪装效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有伪装图像生成方法依赖在伪装数据集上的任务特定训练，计算成本高，且泛化能力受限于训练域。因此需要一个无需参数更新、可泛化的新范式。

Method: 将免训练 CIG 形式化为"面向隐藏"的范式：保留目标同时降低其与合成环境的感知可分性，而非维持视觉显著性。基于冻结的修复扩散框架构建 FreeCam，其中上下文推理模块利用冻结的多模态先验推断有利于隐藏的环境以促进语义兼容，内在外观模块从前景提取低级颜色与纹理线索引导背景合成以实现外观同化。

Result: 大量实验表明，FreeCam 在无任务特定训练的情况下实现了最先进的生成质量与伪装效果；其生成图像可为伪装目标检测提供合成监督，并降低通用目标检测器下的目标可检测性。

Conclusion: 免训练 CIG 范式是可行且有效的，FreeCam 兼顾前景保留、语义兼容与外观同化，在生成质量、伪装效果及下游检测任务上均具优势。

Abstract: Camouflage image generation (CIG) aims to synthesize realistic camouflaged images by blending foreground objects into concealment-compatible background contexts. Achieving this objective requires jointly satisfying three coupled requirements: foreground preservation to retain target integrity, semantic compatibility to select plausible concealment contexts, and appearance assimilation to reduce visual discrepancies. Recent approaches predominantly rely on task-specific training on camouflage datasets to address these requirements, incurring substantial computational cost and limiting generalization beyond the training domain. To address these limitations, we formulate training-free CIG as a concealment-oriented paradigm that preserves the target while reducing its perceptual separability from the synthesized surroundings, rather than maintaining its visual prominence, without parameter updates. We instantiate this paradigm with FreeCam based on a frozen inpainting diffusion framework to preserve the foreground. Within this framework, a Contextual Reasoning Module exploits frozen multimodal priors to infer an environment favorable to concealment, thereby promoting semantic compatibility, while an Intrinsic Appearance Module extracts low-level color and texture cues from the foreground to guide background synthesis toward appearance assimilation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FreeCam achieves state-of-the-art generation quality and camouflage effectiveness without task-specific training, while its generated images provide synthetic supervision for camouflaged object detection and reduce target detectability under general object detectors.

</details>


### [70] [Contour-Guided Spectral Routing for Robust Real-Time Pedestrian Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14383)
*Sam Williams,Yuan Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出一种"轮廓引导频谱路由"的统一视角，将频域处理、注意力和边界推理整合为有序的信息路由流程，用于驾驶场景实时行人检测，并在CityPersons上取得优于RT-DETR的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶场景实时行人检测受三类耦合失效模式制约：微小目标缺乏判别性证据、遮挡削弱几何支撑、天气与光照变化扭曲外观统计。现有方法把频率处理、注意力与边界推理当作独立附加模块，缺乏统一建模。

Method: 提出轮廓引导频谱路由框架，按固定顺序路由信息：先用全局频谱上下文增强空间证据，再让深层表示交换空间与频谱线索，最后以边界—语义分歧为条件进行跨尺度融合；同时保留小波子带训练变换，独立扰动低/高频系数以应对雾、雨、雪和低光照；每阶段仅暴露一个路由变量，区分可复用信号变换与任务特定注入策略。

Result: 在CityPersons上，所提检测器达到70.4 AP50与44.2 AP50:95，而RT-DETR为68.1与42.2；加入完整小波增强配置后进一步提升至71.1与44.6。

Conclusion: 将频率处理、注意力与边界推理统一为有序频谱路由，可构建紧凑表示流水线：低频上下文稳定全局结构，高频证据保护小目标轮廓，从而同时改善小目标、遮挡与恶劣天气下的检测性能。

Abstract: Real-time pedestrian detection in driving scenes is constrained by three coupled failure modes: tiny targets lose discriminative evidence, occlusion weakens geometric support, and weather or illumination changes distort appearance statistics. We formulate the detector through a unified \emph{contour-guided spectral routing} view rather than treating frequency processing, attention, and boundary reasoning as independent add-ons. The detector routes information in a prescribed order: spatial evidence is first augmented with global spectral context, deep representations then exchange spatial and spectral cues, and cross-scale fusion is finally conditioned on boundary--semantic disagreement. This ordering yields a compact representation pipeline in which low-frequency context stabilizes global structure while high-frequency evidence protects small-object contours. We further retain a wavelet-subband training transformation that perturbs low- and high-frequency coefficients independently, targeting appearance shifts caused by fog, rain, snow, and low illumination. The formulation exposes a single routing variable at each stage and distinguishes reusable signal transforms from the task-specific policy that decides where each signal is injected. On CityPersons, the proposed detector obtains 70.4 AP$_{50}$ and 44.2 AP$_{50:95}$, compared with 68.1 and 42.2 for RT-DETR, while the full wavelet-augmented configuration reaches 71.1 and 44.6.

</details>


### [71] [Newton Deep Unfolding for Compressed Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14391)
*Changhua He,Xianchao Xiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Newton深度展开网络NDU-Net，首次将二阶优化用于压缩感知重建，通过牛顿更新模块与牛顿引导多尺度先验模块提升性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度展开方法多基于一阶优化，且未能充分利用重建过程中产生的优化状态信息，限制了压缩感知的重建性能。

Method: NDU-Net引入牛顿更新（NU）模块估计牛顿型更新方向并生成刻画当前重建过程的优化状态；设计牛顿引导的多尺度先验（MP）模块，将这些优化状态融入多尺度特征恢复，使学习到的先验自适应于当前重建阶段。

Result: 在不同压缩感知采样率下的实验表明，NDU-Net取得了良好的重建性能，并展现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: NDU-Net是首个利用二阶优化的压缩感知深度展开框架，验证了牛顿型优化状态结合多尺度先验的有效性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Compressed sensing (CS) reconstructs images from highly limited measurements, but existing deep unfolding methods are typically driven by first-order optimization and weakly exploit the optimization states generated during reconstruction. To address these limitations, we propose a Newton deep unfolding network (NDU-Net), which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first deep unfolding framework that leverages second-order optimization for CS reconstruction. Specifically, NDU-Net introduces a Newton update (NU) module to estimate Newton-type update directions and generate optimization states that characterize the current reconstruction process. Furthermore, a Newton-guided multi-scale prior (MP) module is designed to incorporate these optimization states into multi-scale feature restoration, thereby enabling the learned prior to adapt to the current reconstruction stage. Experimental results under different CS ratios confirm that our proposed NDU-Net achieves promising reconstruction performance and exhibits enhanced robustness. Our code is available at https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/DNU-Net.

</details>


### [72] [A Generative AI Integrated Multimodal Framework for Low-Latency Multi-Camera Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14419)
*Leon Fernando,C Dombawala,P. Hettigoda,Vanodhya G. Warnasooriya,Ishara Neranjana,Rashmika Nawaratne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向行人重识别的生成式AI多模态框架，采用成本感知的提前退出级联策略，在保持检索性能的同时显著降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多摄像头监控与跟踪中的行人重识别面临视角与光照变化、遮挡、背景杂乱、低分辨率等挑战，且现有方法在多模态线索缺失与低延迟部署方面不够鲁棒高效。

Method: 融合三类模态：(i) 分割行人区域的全局视觉嵌入；(ii) 视觉语言模型自动生成的细粒度语义属性描述；(iii) 人脸观测可靠时的可选人脸嵌入。采用成本感知的提前退出级联替代全模态融合：先检查top-k检索结果判断查询是否明确，若最佳匹配与其余候选差异显著则提前终止以减少延迟；歧义情况下保留多个假设并以自适应可靠性加权调用额外模态进行精化。

Result: 在Market-1501和DukeMTMC-reID基准上以mAP和Rank-1评估。自适应提前退出级联分别在不调用语义推理的情况下处理了DukeMTMC-reID 60.7%和Market-1501 68.4%的查询，降低了计算开销并保持有竞争力的检索性能。

Conclusion: 成本感知的提前退出级联多模态框架可在多模态线索缺失与低延迟约束下有效实现行人重识别，兼顾准确率与效率。

Abstract: Person re-identification (ReID) is essential for multi-camera surveillance and tracking, yet remains difficult due to viewpoint and illumination changes, occlusion, background clutter, and low resolution imagery. We propose a generative AI integrated multimodal ReID framework designed explicitly for robustness under missing cues and low latency deployment. The key idea is a cost aware early-exit cascade that prioritizes inexpensive, high confidence evidence and only triggers expensive modalities for ambiguous cases.
  Our system integrates (i) global visual embeddings from segmented person regions, (ii) automatically generated fine grained semantic attribute descriptions generated by vision-language models (VLMs), and (iii) optional facial embeddings when face observations are reliable. To optimize the balance between accuracy and latency, we use a cost aware early-exit cascade instead of fusing all modalities. Specifically, we first inspect the top-k retrieval results to determine whether the query is unambiguous. If the best match is clearly separated from the remaining candidates, we stop early and return the result to minimize latency; in ambiguous cases, we keep multiple hypotheses and invoke additional modalities (face/semantic) with adaptive reliability weighting to refine the decision.
  We report person re-identification performance using mAP and Rank-1 accuracy on the Market-1501 and DukeMTMC-reID benchmarks. The proposed adaptive early-exit cascade resolves 60.7% of DukeMTMC-reID queries and 68.4% of Market-1501 queries without invoking semantic reasoning, reducing computational overhead while maintaining competitive retrieval performance.

</details>


### [73] [Lightweight Generalized DeepFake Face Detection with WAVIE: Wavelet Augmented Vision Intermediate Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14437)
*Arya Pulkit,Aditya Ruhela,Akarshan Kapoor,Arnav Bhavsar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WAVIE，在冻结CLIP中间特征上融合小波频域与空间信息，提升跨数据集深度伪造检测泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器在训练所用伪造方法上表现良好，但部署到未见过的篡改方法时准确率急剧下降，泛化能力不足，制约了其在虚假信息治理、数字取证与以人为中心的AI系统中的可靠性。因此需要弥合跨方法泛化差距。

Method: WAVIE是一种端到端架构，基于冻结的CLIP骨干网络，结合空间与频率线索。方法将中间Transformer嵌入经轻量可学习模块投影，施加三级Daubechies-6离散小波变换，对低频分支进行细化并保留高频分支，再通过逆小波变换重建特征并进行分类。

Result: 仅在FaceForensics++上训练，帧级别AUROC在Celeb-DF-v1为0.852、Celeb-DF-v2为0.852、WildDeepFake为0.831，优于多个先进泛化基线；消融实验证实小波模块与中间特征聚合对跨数据集性能均关键，说明联合利用空间与频率域的必要性。

Conclusion: WAVIE可作为真实场景深度伪造检测的有力基线，通过空间-频率联合建模有效提升跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Deepfake detection systems often exhibit significant performance degradation when deployed on unseen manipulation methods, limiting their reliability in real-world multimedia environments. This lack of generalization poses critical challenges for misinformation mitigation, digital forensics, and human-centric AI systems. Existing detectors perform well on the forgery methods they are trained on, but their accuracy drops sharply on unseen pipelines. To bridge this generalization gap, we propose WAVIE (Wavelet Augmented Vision Intermediate Embeddings), an end-to-end architecture that combines complementary spatial and frequency cues on top of a frozen CLIP backbone. WAVIE projects intermediate transformer embeddings through a lightweight learnable module, applies a three-level Daubechies-6 (db6) discrete wavelet transform (DWT), refines the low-frequency branch while preserving the high-frequency branch, reconstructs the feature via inverse DWT, and performs classification.
  Trained only on FaceForensics++, WAVIE achieves AUROC = 0.852 on Celeb-DF-v1, 0.852 on Celeb-DF-v2 and 0.831 on WildDeepFake (WDF) at the frame level, outperforming several state-of-the-art generalization baselines. Extensive ablation studies confirm the importance of both the wavelet module and the intermediate-feature aggregation for cross-dataset performance, highlighting the necessity of jointly leveraging spatial and frequency domains. These results position WAVIE as a strong baseline for deepfake detection in the wild.

</details>


### [74] [Context-Aware Mutual Learning for Blind Image Inpainting and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14439)
*Haoru Zhao,Yufeng Wang,Zhaorui Gu,Bing Zheng,Haiyong Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文感知互学习（CAML）框架，将盲图像修复中的掩码估计与图像修复两个阶段联合起来，互相利用上下文信息，在盲图像修复及去雪、去影、去水印任务上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲图像修复在未知掩码下恢复受污染图像十分困难。受人类视觉与知识启发，该任务可分解为掩码估计与基于估计掩码的图像修复两阶段，两阶段方案相比单阶段能提升修复质量并增强对未知真实污染的泛化能力。然而现有两阶段方法过度强调掩码估计对图像修复的单向关系，忽视了二者之间的相互关系：掩码估计可为图像修复提供更多上下文语义，图像修复可为掩码估计提供更多上下文细节（如纹理与边缘）。

Method: 提出上下文感知互学习（CAML）框架，联合掩码估计与图像修复以互相利用上下文信息。框架中包含两个学习器：图像修复引导的上下文互学习器（IGCM），从图像修复中获取互补的上下文细节以辅助掩码估计；掩码估计引导的上下文互学习器（EGCM），从掩码估计中强化对上下文语义的理解以辅助图像修复。

Result: 消融实验验证了CAML各模块的有效性。大量实验表明，CAML在盲图像修复以及去雪、去影、去水印等额外视觉任务上均达到当前最优性能，体现了其优越性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模掩码估计与图像修复之间的双向互惠关系，CAML有效融合语义与细节上下文信息，提升盲图像修复质量与泛化能力，并可扩展至多种图像恢复任务。

Abstract: Blind image inpainting, aiming to recover contaminated images in the case of unknown masks, is a challenging task. Motivated by the perspective of human vision and knowledge, blind image inpainting can be decomposed into two stages: mask estimation and image inpainting based on the estimated mask. The two-stage idea exhibits evident advantages in enhancing inpainting quality and augmenting the generalization capability of unknown real-world contamination by explicitly employing the estimated mask for image inpainting compared to one-stage scheme. This two-stage idea has also been intuitively implemented. However, existing two-stage methods excessively emphasize the unilateral relationship of mask estimation to image inpainting, and may overlook the mutual relations between them. Specifically, mask estimation can provide more contextual semantics for image inpainting to strengthen the understanding of semantics, and image inpainting can offer more contextual details (e.g., textures and edges) for mask estimation to improve the learning of details. In this work, we propose a novel Context-Aware Mutual Learning (CAML) framework for blind image inpainting that joints mask estimation and image inpainting to mutually exploit contextual information. In the CAML framework, we design the Inpainting-Guided Context-Mutual (IGCM) learner to acquire the complementary contextual details from image inpainting for assisting mask estimation, and the Estimation-Guided Context-Mutual (EGCM) learner to strengthen the understanding of contextual semantics from mask estimation for assisting image inpainting. Ablation studies validate the efficacy of our CAML. Extensive experiments show that our CAML achieves state-of-the-art performance on both blind image inpainting and additional vision tasks, i.e., snow removal, shadow removal, and watermark removal, indicating its superiority.

</details>


### [75] [From Visual Attribution to Clinical Reasoning: Explainable Parkinson's Disease Screening from Hand-Drawn Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14441)
*Aritra Dey,Utsav Kumar Nareti,Chandranath Adak,Soumi Chattopadhyay,Krishna Gopal Sasmal,Saeed Anwar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可解释的帕金森病（PD）筛查框架，基于离线手绘图形（螺旋、回旋线），将判别式视觉建模与临床可解释推理相结合，实现竞争性的预测性能与临床一致的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PD早期神经运动损伤可通过手绘图形中的震颤振荡、笔迹不规则和曲率不稳定体现，但现有方法多为黑箱分类，缺乏与临床PD症状学的可解释关联。

Method: 预测模型捕捉分布式结构畸变与细粒度纹理变化，并在受试者不相交（subject-disjoint）协议下评估以保证泛化；构建多阶段可解释性管线：先用注意力与梯度定位显著区域；再提取临床相关运动描述符（轮廓粗糙度、曲率不规则性、笔迹变异性、震颤频率能量）；最后通过基于语言的推理模块将其转化为连贯的临床理由。

Result: 在公开PD手写数据集上取得竞争性的预测性能，并生成与临床一致的解释。

Conclusion: 将视觉归因与临床解读相桥接，推动手绘图形神经筛查中可解释文档智能的发展。

Abstract: Parkinson's disease (PD) manifests early neuromotor impairments that become observable in controlled hand-drawn patterns such as spirals and meanders, where tremor-induced oscillations, stroke irregularity, and curvature instability reflect underlying motor degradation. In this work, we present an explainable framework for PD screening from offline hand-drawn patterns that integrates discriminative visual modeling with clinically grounded reasoning. The predictive model captures distributed structural distortions and fine-grained texture variations. It is evaluated under subject-disjoint protocols to ensure reliable generalization. To move beyond black-box classification, we introduce a multi-stage explainability pipeline that combines visual attribution with structured symptom abstraction. Salient regions are identified using attention- and gradient-based localization, followed by extraction of clinically meaningful motor descriptors quantifying contour roughness, curvature irregularity, stroke variability, and tremor-frequency energy. These descriptors are subsequently translated into coherent clinical rationales through a language-based reasoning module, linking model evidence to established PD symptomatology. By bridging visual attribution and clinical interpretation, the proposed framework advances interpretable document intelligence for neurological screening using hand-drawn patterns. Experimental results on publicly available Parkinson's disease handwriting datasets demonstrate competitive predictive performance and clinically consistent explanations.

</details>


### [76] [AlayaVista: Streaming World Modeling from Panoramic States to Perspective Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14462)
*Jiaming Tan,Mingliang Zhai,Zhen Li,Yuwei Wu,Chuanhao Li,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AlayaVista 是一种相机可控的流式视频世界模型，通过解耦全景世界演化与视角观测合成，在单张透视图像条件下实现全景场景先验构建、相机条件化的全景隐状态演化，以及低延迟高保真视角视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式视频世界模型需要在大幅相机运动下保持广泛场景上下文，同时以低延迟生成高保真观测。现有方法存在表示权衡：透视模型基于局部视角，需在长时间 rollout 中保留屏幕外内容；而更广空间覆盖通常依赖合成全球面视频或构建显式 3D 表示。受视觉感知中全局上下文与选择性局部敏锐度互补作用的启发，作者提出解耦全景演化与透视观测合成。

Method: 1. 给定单张透视图像，使用预训练全景扩展模型构建 360 度场景先验；
2. 将场景演化为相机条件化的全景隐状态；
3. 通过隐视角渲染器（latent viewport renderer）将全景隐状态映射为目标透视视频隐表示；
4. 使用透视精修器（perspective refiner）恢复细节、抑制伪影并进行超分辨率；
5. 为支持高效流式生成，将全景生成器适配为 chunk-autoregressive 生成，并将全景生成与透视精修蒸馏为少步过程；
6. 构建 MUGEN 大规模真实世界全景视频数据集，包含 1,318 小时、至少 4K 分辨率视频及丰富语义与几何标注，提供监督信号。

Result: 摘要中未给出定量实验结果。论文贡献包括：提出解耦全景与透视的相机可控流式世界模型 AlayaVista；实现全景隐状态演化、隐视角渲染与透视精修流程；通过 chunk-autoregressive 与少步蒸馏支持高效流式生成；构建 1,318 小时、至少 4K 分辨率的 MUGEN 全景视频数据集及语义、几何标注。

Conclusion: AlayaVista 通过解耦全景世界演化与透视观测合成，兼顾全局场景上下文与局部高保真细节，并借助 chunk-autoregressive 与少步蒸馏实现低延迟流式视频生成；配套的 MUGEN 数据集为该设计提供大规模真实世界全景视频监督，展示了构建交互式视频世界模型的新思路。

Abstract: Interactive video world models must maintain broad scene context under camera motion while producing high-fidelity observations with low latency. Existing approaches face a representation trade-off: perspective models operate on local views and must preserve off-screen content over long rollouts, whereas broader spatial coverage is typically obtained by synthesizing full-sphere videos or constructing explicit 3D representations. Motivated by the complementary roles of global context and selective local acuity in visual perception, we present AlayaVista, a camera-controllable streaming video world model that decouples panoramic world evolution from perspective observation synthesis. Given a single perspective image, AlayaVista constructs a 360-degree scene prior using a pretrained panorama expansion model and then evolves the scene as a camera-conditioned panoramic latent state. A latent viewport renderer maps this state to the requested perspective video latents, while a perspective refiner restores details, suppresses artifacts, and performs super-resolution. To support efficient streaming, we adapt the panoramic generator to chunk-autoregressive generation and distill both panoramic generation and perspective refinement into few-step processes. To provide the supervision required by this design, we construct MUGEN, a large-scale real-world panoramic video dataset containing 1,318 hours of videos at resolutions of at least 4K, together with rich semantic and geometric annotations.

</details>


### [77] [DynEoMT: Learning Object Dynamicity from Online Segmentation Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14466)
*Calvin Galagain,Martyna Poreba,François Goulette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DynEoMT，在视频分割中为每个区域在线预测动态/静态属性，仅用当前帧与传播查询，无需光流、深度、位姿等，并保留分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频分割模型只识别和跟踪目标，不判断每个分割区域是否相对相机独立运动；该动态性属性无法仅从语义推断，且受相机自运动干扰。

Method: 基于查询的视频分割框架，联合输出原始分割结果与区域级动态/静态状态；推理时仅用当前帧和传播查询。同时提出类别无关的离线监督流程，使用相机补偿光流和置信度感知的时间滤波生成监督标签。

Result: 在 VIPSeg、OVIS、YouTube-VIS 2022、VSPW 上平衡准确率分别为 84.3、68.0、68.6、87.6，同时基本保持分割性能。

Conclusion: 分割区域的动态性可从传播查询中学习，实现无需专用运动处理流程的在线预测；代码将开源以支持复现。

Abstract: Video segmentation models recognize and track objects over time, but they do not indicate whether each segmented region moves independently of the observing camera. This dynamicity attribute cannot be inferred from semantics alone and is confounded by camera ego-motion. We introduce \method, an online framework that augments query-based video segmentation with region-level dynamicity prediction. It jointly produces the original segmentation outputs and a dynamic or static state for each predicted region. At inference, DynEoMT uses only the current frame and propagated queries, without optical flow, depth, camera pose, previous RGB frames, or feature maps. Because established video segmentation benchmarks do not annotate this attribute, we also introduce a class-agnostic offline supervision pipeline using camera-compensated optical flow and confidence-aware temporal filtering. Across VIPSeg, OVIS, YouTube-VIS 2022, and VSPW, DynEoMT achieves balanced accuracies of 84.3, 68.0, 68.6, and 87.6, respectively, while largely preserving segmentation performance. These results show that segmentation-region dynamicity can be learned from propagated queries, enabling its online prediction without a dedicated motion-processing pipeline at inference. The complete code will be released as open source to enable full reproduction of the method and experiments.

</details>


### [78] [PRI-Net: A Lightweight Multimodal Framework for 3D UAV Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14469)
*Zhixuan Chen,Jialiang Lu,Zhong Ye,Yinghui He,Guanding Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PRI-Net轻量多模态融合框架用于无人机3D定位，结合点云泼溅、残差注意力融合与信息瓶颈，兼顾精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机多模态3D定位方法面临三大问题：LiDAR几何稀疏、模态融合不平衡、边缘到服务器的特征传输冗余，导致定位精度与效率受限。

Method: 提出PRI-Net框架，包含三个模块：3D点云泼溅（3DPCS）将稀疏LiDAR观测转为几何一致的稠密深度图；残差注意力融合（RAF）用图像分支做粗估计、门控融合分支做精化以缓解模态偏置；多模态信息瓶颈（MIB）过滤任务无关冗余以压缩特征。

Result: 在轻量架构下实现较高定位精度，同时降低特征维度，提升边缘到服务器无人机感知的效率与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PRI-Net通过稀疏点云稠密化、平衡融合与信息瓶颈压缩，有效解决无人机多模态定位的精度与传输效率矛盾，适用于边缘计算场景。

Abstract: Accurate 3D localization of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) remains challenging for existing multimodal approaches due to sparse LiDAR geometry, modality-imbalanced fusion, and redundant feature transmission over constrained edge-to-server links. To address these limitations, we propose PRI-Net, an efficient and lightweight multimodal fusion framework for UAV localization that integrates point cloud splatting, residual attention fusion, and an information bottleneck. Specifically, a 3D point cloud splatting (3DPCS) strategy is introduced to transform sparse LiDAR observations into geometrically consistent dense depth maps. A residual attention fusion (RAF) module is then designed to alleviate modal bias by using an image branch for coarse estimation and a gated fusion branch for refinement. In addition, a multimodal information bottleneck (MIB) module compacts features by filtering task-irrelevant redundancy. Experiments show that PRI-Net achieves high localization accuracy with lightweight architectures, while reducing feature dimensionality and improving edge-to-server UAV sensing efficiency and robustness.

</details>


### [79] [PuzzleMate: Benchmarking MLLMs for Egocentric Puzzle Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14473)
*Avijit Dasgupta,Shayon Dasgupta,Zakaria Laskar,C. V. Jawahar,Karteek Alahari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PuzzleMate 框架与基准，用于评估多模态大模型在拼图任务中的逐步指导能力，发现GPT-5.2、Gemini-2.5-Pro等顶尖模型存在显著性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人AI助手需从数字界面进化为具身伙伴，不仅识别物体，还要提供契合用户实时进度的精确分步指导；而MLLM在细粒度操作任务的顺序指导能力尚未被验证。

Method: 以拼图为战略性测试平台（需高精度空间推理、细微几何差异辨别与严格顺序逻辑），提出PuzzleMate框架，通过第一人称视角采集拼图过程，采用用户参与式研究评估MLLM对当前拼图状态的感知与下一步指令生成，并据此构建系统性评测基准。

Result: 分析揭示了限制模型有效性的七个关键瓶颈；基准评测显示GPT-5.2与Gemini-2.5-Pro等当前模型存在显著性能差距。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM虽能力强大，但在拼图辅助所需的复杂推理与顺序逻辑方面仍有明显不足，距成为可靠的具身指导助手尚有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Personal AI assistants hold the potential to evolve from digital interfaces into embodied companions capable of guiding users through complex physical activities. For these assistants to become integral to daily life, they must do more than identify objects; they must provide precise, step-by-step instructions that align with a user's real-time progress. While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show promise in general visual understanding, their ability to deliver grounded, sequential guidance for fine-grained manipulation tasks remains largely unverified.
  In this paper, we choose the jigsaw puzzle as a strategic testbed for this capability. Unlike general object recognition, puzzle solving demands high-precision spatial reasoning, the ability to distinguish between minute geometric variations, and a rigorous adherence to sequential logic.
  We investigate this capability through PuzzleMate, a novel framework focused on jigsaw puzzle solving captured through an egocentric viewpoint. We deploy PuzzleMate in a user-in-the-loop study to evaluate how well state-of-the-art MLLMs perceive the current puzzle state and generate actionable next-step instructions. Our analysis reveals seven key bottlenecks that limit their effectiveness. Building on these insights, we propose a benchmark that enables systematic evaluation of MLLMs' reasoning capabilities for puzzle solving. Our findings reveal a substantial performance gap in current models like GPT-5.2 and Gemini-2.5-Pro; while these MLLMs are highly capable, they struggle to navigate the intricate reasoning and sequential logic essential for jigsaw puzzle assistance.

</details>


### [80] [MCIQA-2K: A Multi-Dimensional Dataset and No-Reference Quality Assessment Benchmark for Colorized Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14495)
*Yunkai Zhuang,Qihang Yan,Zicheng Zhang,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向着色图像的无参考质量评估基准MCIQA-2K及多分支NR-IQA框架MCIQA，在自建基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像着色本质上是病态问题，单张灰度图可对应多种合理着色结果，因此传统全参考IQA指标无法准确反映人类对彩色化图像的感知偏好，缺乏专门的评估基准与指标。

Method: 构建MCIQA-2K大规模多维基准数据集：包含由五种代表性着色模型生成的2,000张彩色化图像，并配有人工标注，覆盖色彩拖影、语义色彩错位、全局自然度三个感知维度；在此基础上提出MCIQA，一个专为彩色化图像设计的多分支无参考IQA框架。

Result: 大量实验表明，MCIQA在所提基准上显著优于现有全参考与无参考IQA方法，同时在多个常用IQA数据集上展现出有竞争力的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为彩色化图像的无参考质量评估提供了基准数据与专用方法，数据集与代码已公开。

Abstract: Image colorization is an inherently ill-posed task, since a single grayscale image may correspond to multiple plausible colorized results. Consequently, conventional full-reference image quality assessment (IQA) metrics fail to accurately reflect human perceptual preferences for colorized images. In this paper, we present MCIQA-2K, a large-scale multi-dimensional benchmark specifically designed for no-reference quality assessment of colorized images. We construct a dataset containing 2,000 colorized images generated by five representative colorization models, together with human annotations across three perceptual dimensions: color smearing, semantic color misalignment, and global naturalness. Building upon the proposed benchmark, we further introduce MCIQA, a dedicated multi-branch NR-IQA framework for colorized images. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MCIQA significantly outperforms existing full-reference and no-reference IQA methods on the proposed benchmark, while also exhibiting competitive generalization capability on several widely-used IQA datasets. The dataset and code are publicly available at https://github.com/ARBEZ-ZEBRA/MCIQA.

</details>


### [81] [CGGT: Curve-Grounded Geometry Transformer for 3D Parametric Curve Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14521)
*Zhirui Gao,Renjiao Yi,Yunfan Ye,Ruizhen Hu,Chenyang Zhu,Wei Chen,Kai Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CGGT，从稀疏无位姿多视图图像中恢复可编辑3D参数曲线，并构建Wireframe-100K数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NeRF和3DGS方法依赖稠密标定视图、预计算2D边缘图和昂贵的逐场景优化，难以应用于随意拍摄的真实输入，因此需要一种能从稀疏无位姿图像直接重建3D参数曲线的方法。

Method: CGGT结合几何感知Transformer编码器进行多视图特征学习，以及曲线感知掩码注意力解码器进行跨视图实例关联；单次前向传播预测相机参数、稠密深度图和实例级2D曲线掩码，再提升到3D并经快速参数优化恢复紧凑可编辑的3D曲线基元。同时构建Wireframe-100K数据集（10万个CAD模型，含多样拓扑、逼真多视图渲染和精确参数曲线标注）。

Result: 大量实验表明，该框架在重建精度和效率上均有显著提升，尤其在挑战性稀疏视图设置下，以及在区分持久3D结构边缘与由轮廓、纹理和外观变化引起的视角相关图像边缘方面表现突出。尽管仅在合成数据上训练，CGGT对真实图像也有良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: CGGT为从无约束视觉输入进行CAD风格线框重建提供了潜力，弥合了基于像素的感知与基于矢量的CAD建模。

Abstract: Recovering editable 3D parametric curves from 2D images is a fundamental challenge in computer graphics, bridging pixel-based perception and vector-based CAD modeling. Existing NeRF- and 3DGS-based methods often rely on dense calibrated views, precomputed 2D edge maps, and costly per-scene optimization, limiting their applicability to casually captured real-world inputs. We propose CGGT, a Curve-Grounded Geometry Transformer that directly grounds 3D-consistent 2D curve instances in the image space from sparse, unposed multi-view images. CGGT combines a geometry-aware transformer encoder for multi-view feature learning with a curve-aware masked-attention decoder for cross-view instance association. In a single forward pass, it predicts camera parameters, dense depth maps, and instance-level 2D curve masks, which are then lifted into 3D and refined through a fast parametric optimization stage to recover compact, editable 3D curve primitives. To support structured curve learning, we introduce Wireframe-100K, a large-scale dataset comprising 100,000 CAD models with diverse topologies, realistic multi-view renderings, and accurate parametric curve annotations. Extensive experiments show that our framework achieves substantial improvements in both reconstruction accuracy and efficiency, particularly under challenging sparse-view settings and in separating persistent 3D structural edges from view-dependent image edges caused by silhouettes, textures, and appearance variations. Despite being trained solely on synthetic data, CGGT generalizes well to real-world images, demonstrating its potential for practical CAD-style wireframe reconstruction from unconstrained visual inputs.

</details>


### [82] [Selective Tool Use for Agentic Change Visual Question Answering in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14523)
*Yakoub Bazi,Mohamad M. Al Rahhal,Mohamed A. Mekhtiche,Mansour Zuair*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对变化视觉问答（Change VQA）中VLM在转换统计、面积测量和空间信息类问题上不可靠的问题，提出选择性工具调用框架：单个VLM可直接作答或调用确定性变化分析工具获取问题特定证据，并基于双时相语义图返回结构化观测。作者构建了覆盖八类问题、三类工具的CDVQA扩展数据集，用LoRA微调Qwen3.5-4B联合学习直接作答、工具调用与证据条件作答。实验表明，使用参考语义图时总体准确率由73.77%提升至88.79%，平均类别准确率由69.11%提升至89.65%；使用自动预测语义图时为77.47%和75.06%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变化视觉问答需要理解双时相遥感影像中的语义变化。尽管VLM在该任务上表现可观，但在涉及显式转换统计、面积测量或空间信息的问答上仍不可靠，缺乏精确、可验证的定量与空间证据。

Method: 提出选择性工具使用框架：单一VLM可选择直接作答，或调用确定性的变化分析工具（基于双时相语义图），获得结构化观测后再由同一VLM生成最终答案。构建CDVQA的工具增强扩展，涵盖八类问题族与过渡、空间、时间三类分析工具，工具使用监督与观测均由原始语义标注自动导出，无需额外人工标注。采用LoRA适配Qwen3.5-4B，联合学习直接作答、工具调用与证据条件作答。

Result: 在7,164个测试问题上，使用参考语义图的选择性工具使用将总体准确率从73.77%提升至88.79%，平均类别准确率从69.11%提升至89.65%；当语义图由模型自动预测时，框架达到77.47%总体准确率与75.06%平均类别准确率。

Conclusion: 结果证明问题特定的语义证据对Change VQA具有明显收益，同时表明语义预测质量会显著影响最终性能。代码与工具增强标注将公开。

Abstract: Change visual question answering (Change VQA) requires understanding semantic changes across bi-temporal remote sensing images. Although vision language models (VLMs) have shown promising performance on this task, they remain unreliable when answering questions that require explicit transition statistics, area measurements, or spatial information. To address this limitation, we propose a selective tool use framework in which a single VLM either answers directly or invokes a deterministic change analysis tool to obtain question specific evidence. Specifically, the selected tool operates on bi-temporal semantic maps and returns a structured observation, which the same VLM uses to generate its final answer. To support this framework, we construct a tool augmented extension of CDVQA covering eight question families and three tools for transition, spatial, and temporal analysis. Tool use supervision and observations are derived automatically from the original semantic annotations, without additional manual labeling. We then adapt Qwen3.5-4B using Low Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to jointly learn direct answering, tool invocation, and evidence conditioned answering. Experiments on 7,164 test questions show that selective tool use with reference semantic maps improves overall accuracy from 73.77% to 88.79% and average family accuracy from 69.11% to 89.65%. When the semantic maps are predicted automatically, the framework achieves 77.47% overall accuracy and 75.06% average family accuracy. These results demonstrate the benefit of question-specific semantic evidence for Change VQA, while highlighting the influence of semantic prediction quality on the resulting performance. Code and tool-augmented annotations will be made publicly available at https://github.com/yakoubbazi/ToolChangeVQA.

</details>


### [83] [Sharing standardized image-derived data in computational pathology using DICOM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14530)
*Daniela P. Schacherer,Christopher P. Bridge,David Clunie,Igor Octaviano,André Homeyer,Markus D. Herrmann,Olivier Gevaert,Tabita Ghete,Markus Metzler,Henning Hoefener,Tahsin Kurc,Curtis Lisle,Kenneth Philbrick,Joel Saltz,Yuanning Zheng,Andrey Fedorov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文描述了在NCI影像数据共享平台IDC中，采用DICOM标准对病理图像衍生数据（如ROI标注、分割掩膜）进行标准化编码与共享的方法，并展示了五个代表性数据集的转换与公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算病理学方法的开发与评估需要大规模、多样化的数据集。过去十年间，病理影像数据的收集与共享已有大量投入，但图像衍生数据（如感兴趣区勾画、分割掩膜）的共享发展相对滞后。同时，IDC依赖DICOM标准进行数据协调，而DICOM在病理图像衍生内容中的应用此前基本未被探索。

Method: 以NCI影像数据共享平台IDC为依托，采用DICOM标准对病理图像衍生数据进行标准化编码。将五个代表性数据集从其原始表示形式转换协调为DICOM格式，并公开共享于IDC中。

Result: 成功完成了五个代表性数据集的DICOM协调转换与公开共享，展示了该标准化方法的优势，并对关键开源工具做出了贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了采用DICOM标准编码和共享病理图像衍生数据的可行性与价值，并讨论了更广泛推广DICOM应用于病理图像衍生数据所需考虑的技术问题。

Abstract: Development and evaluation of computational pathology methods require access to large and diverse datasets. Over the past decade, various initiatives invested significantly into collecting, centralizing, and sharing pathology imaging data. In contrast, sharing of image-derived data such as region-of-interest delineations or segmentation masks is less well developed. In this work, we describe our approach to encoding and sharing image-derived pathology data in a standardized manner within the National Cancer Institute (NCI) Imaging Data Commons (IDC), a platform that hosts and provides public access to de-identified radiology and pathology data. The IDC relies on the Digital Imaging and Communications in Medicine (DICOM) standard for data harmonization, yet the adoption of DICOM for pathology image-derived content has remained largely unexplored until now. Here, we present five representative datasets harmonized by conversion from their original representations into DICOM and shared publicly in the IDC. We demonstrate the benefits of this harmonization, describe contributions to critical open-source tooling, and discuss technical considerations relevant to broader adoption of DICOM for pathology image-derived data.

</details>


### [84] [Small Object Detection in Drone Aerial Imagery with LAF-YOLOv10](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14560)
*Quratulain Nayeem,Fahmina Taranum,Mohammed Mudassir Uddin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文直接检验"独立验证过的架构技巧组合后收益可叠加"这一假设，将四种技术集成到YOLOv10n形成LAF-YOLOv10，在VisDrone-DET2019上却比原版低7.8个mAP点，证明可组合性假设不成立，且缺陷可追溯到P2/P5头部替换与PC-C2f主干的具体交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机图像中的目标往往只有几个像素，且机载算力有限，通用目标检测器在此场景精度明显下降。既有工作习惯把各自独立验证有效的架构技巧拼装成单一检测器，并默认这些单独报告的性能增益在组合后仍能保留，但这一假设从未被直接检验。本文正是要对这一假设进行压力测试。

Method: 作者构建LAF-YOLOv10，在YOLOv10n上集成四项技术：PC-C2f部分卷积主干块、AG-FPN注意力引导特征金字塔、以P2检测头替代大目标P5头、以及Wise-IoU v3回归损失。在VisDrone-DET2019上以三个随机种子（42、123、256）各训练三次，与未修改的YOLOv10n对比，并使用TIDE误差分解、逐类别分析、逐组件消融、注意力与损失对比、向UAVDT的零样本迁移以及留出集/测试开发集评估来定位组合成功或失败的位置。

Result: 可组合性在此并不成立。LAF-YOLOv10在2.14M参数下仅达24.0±0.4% mAP@0.5，比YOLOv10n（31.8%）低7.8个点，该差距迁移到UAVDT仍为-10.0点，并经留出集与测试开发集（23.5%、22.5%）确认。背景误报、定位误差和重复检测的变化方向恰恰与AG-FPN和Wise-IoU的设计初衷相反。消融将缺陷追溯到具体来源：P2/P5头部替换单独即损失2.5点，叠加在被PC-C2f削弱的主干上又产生2.5点交互惩罚，而PC-C2f自身的2.0点损失与部分预训练权重移植（150个主干张量中仅73个成功迁移）相符。

Conclusion: 失败应归因于特定组件之间的交互作用，而非四个组件各自本身。研究结论是：可组合性必须被直接验证，而不能被默认假定。

Abstract: General-purpose object detectors lose accuracy on UAV footage, where targets span only a handful of pixels and onboard compute is limited. Prior work composes independently-validated architectural techniques into one detector, assuming gains reported in isolation transfer once combined. We stress-test that assumption directly. LAF-YOLOv10 integrates four techniques into YOLOv10n: a Partial Convolution C2f (PC-C2f) backbone block, an Attention-Guided Feature Pyramid Network (AG-FPN), a P2 detection head replacing the large-object P5 head, and Wise-IoU v3 regression, asking whether their combined effect matches what each contributes alone. We train LAF-YOLOv10 three times (seeds 42, 123, 256) on VisDrone-DET2019, benchmark against unmodified YOLOv10n, and use TIDE error decomposition, per-category breakdown, per-component ablation, attention/loss comparisons, zero-shot transfer to UAVDT, and held-out/test-dev evaluation to localize where the combination succeeds or fails. Composability does not hold here. LAF-YOLOv10 reaches 24.0+/-0.4% mAP@0.5 at 2.14M parameters, 7.8 points below YOLOv10n (31.8%), a deficit that transfers to UAVDT (-10.0 points) and is confirmed by held-out and test-dev evaluation (23.5%, 22.5%). Background false positives, localization error, and duplicate detections move in the direction AG-FPN and Wise-IoU were designed to push. Ablation traces the deficit to a specific source: the P2/-P5 head swap costs 2.5 points independently plus a 2.5-point interaction penalty when layered onto a backbone weakened by PC-C2f, whose own 2.0-point loss is consistent with a partial pretrained-weight transplant (73/150 backbone tensors transfer). The failure is attributable to a specific interaction, not the four components individually. Composability must be verified directly, not assumed. Code/checkpoints: https://github.com/Mudassiruddin7/Small-Object-Detection-in-UAV-Imagery.

</details>


### [85] [GroundBench: A Factorized, Counterfactual Benchmark for Locating VLM Affordance Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13308)
*Sarthak Sattigeri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GroundBench 诊断基准，通过分支合并条件分离视觉定位、机械推理与类别—动作关联，发现模型表现多依赖类别到动作的文本捷径而非真正的视觉 grounding。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究发现，在操作提示中命名目标部件可显著提升动作准确率（0.32-0.63），但命名本身提供了真实系统本应自行推断的信息，从而混淆了视觉定位、机械推理与类别到动作的关联三种解释。论文希望区分这些因素，检验看似 grounded 的表现是否可由文本捷径复现。

Method: 设计 GroundBench 诊断基准，包含六个分支合并条件，每个条件逐步加入受控信息包，并设置针对同一图像中真实可见的替代部件进行反事实重问。在三个 OpenAI 模型、1,068 条预测上测试；并加入无视觉对照，以及联合运动类型与运动轴的额外信息条件，在 32 个物体、74 对反事实样本上评估合规率与捷径率。

Result: 仅提供目标区域而不给身份时，动作准确率处于或低于 0.53 多数基线（0.26、0.26、0.53）；仅给身份而不给位置则为 0.74、0.68、0.68。所有高于基线的增益都出现在所给类别本身决定动作的情形。无视觉对照下 GPT-5 分数不变或提升，提示存在显著的类别—动作关联；但 GPT-4o mini 在一个条件下降，说明该解释并非普遍。加入联合类型与运动轴在六个模型-层比较中均未提升准确率。反事实测试中 GPT-5 达到 0.86 加权合规率、0.07 捷径率，但在所有 push-to-lift-vertical 案例上失败。

Conclusion: GroundBench 能识别哪些供给信息真正改变 affordance 行为，并检验看似 grounded 的性能是否可通过文本捷径复现。结果表明许多模型的提升主要来自类别到动作的关联而非视觉 grounding，但这一结论并非对所有模型普遍成立。

Abstract: A companion evaluation found that naming the target part in a manipulation prompt increased action accuracy by 0.32-0.63 across eight vision-language models, with no model outperforming a constant baseline until the part was named. However, naming the part supplies information that a real system must infer, confounding visual grounding, mechanical reasoning, and category-to-action association. We introduce GroundBench, a diagnostic benchmark that separates these explanations through six branch-and-merge conditions, each adding a controlled information bundle, and a counterfactual re-ask targeting a real alternate part visible in the same image. Across three OpenAI models and 1,068 predictions, supplying the target region without its identity leaves action accuracy at or below the 0.53 majority baseline (0.26, 0.26, and 0.53), although the models largely reproduce the supplied region. Supplying identity without location instead yields 0.74, 0.68, and 0.68. Every above-baseline gain in this curated set occurs where the supplied part category itself determines the action. A no-vision control leaves GPT-5's scores unchanged or improved, providing evidence consistent with substantial category-to-action association. GPT-4o mini declines on one condition, so this interpretation is not universal. Adding joint type and motion axis does not improve accuracy across six model-stratum comparisons. On 74 counterfactual pairs from 32 objects, GPT-5 achieves 0.86 pair-weighted compliance with a 0.07 shortcut rate but fails all observed push-to-lift-vertical cases. GroundBench identifies which supplied information changes affordance behavior and tests whether apparently grounded performance can be reproduced through textual shortcuts.

</details>


### [86] [A Building as a Repository: KIR, a Typed Intermediate Representation for Agent-Authored Building Information Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14578)
*Dmitry Kuklev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KIR 是一种类型化中间表示，把建筑模型当作可版本化仓库中的程序来编写，并降级为宿主应用（如 Revit）的构建目标。它围绕"生成器写入有状态、部分可观测宿主时七类静默失败"设计数据结构表达，并在固定快照上用 83 个操作契约做离线实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体在编写建筑信息模型时，不仅需要宿主 API，还需要表达其意图、编译器代为决策、被拒绝的内容、执行后观测结果以及未知部分。作者希望通过 KIR 为这些"沉默失败"提供显式的数据表示，避免生成器在状态化、部分可观测宿主中悄悄出错。

Method: KIR 将建筑作为程序保存在版本化仓库，并降级为宿主应用的构建目标。其围绕七类静默失败设计表示：模糊选择器变为带候选的类型化拒绝；默认值保留来源；未被检查的义务在执名前命名；空洞见证被静态拒绝；丢失的事务响应变为 UNCONFIRMED 状态并采用"先验证再重试"规则；反向路径遵守普查不变量；决策通过摘要绑定到所依据的状态。实验在固定快照上使用 83 个操作契约，仅以 Revit 为后端。

Result: 在 42 个压力测试程序中，29 个被拒绝并给出诊断代码，无未捕获异常；377 个见证义务中有 38 个被标记为不可见证；100 种执行、见证与验收状态组合中，在七项声明不变量下接纳 31 种；219 次证书运行未发现空洞见证；一栋 60 层塔楼以 KIR 表示为 11,263 字符，而生成 C# 为 3,709,235 字符。原生 Revit 运行结果来自项目记录，与可复现结果分开报告。与直接编写宿主代码的智能体的受控对比已设计但尚未执行，是主要开放问题。

Conclusion: KIR 通过类型化中间表示显式建模生成器在部分可观测宿主中的七类静默失败，离线实验表明其能以诊断方式拒绝多数压力测试程序、识别不可见证义务、在声明不变量下筛选合法状态组合、避免空洞见证，并大幅压缩表示体积。与直接写宿主代码方案的受控对比仍是主要待解问题。

Abstract: Autonomous agents that author building information models need more than access to a host API. They need a representation of what they intended, what a compiler decided on their behalf, what was refused, what was observed after execution and what remains unknown. We present KIR, a typed intermediate representation in which a building is authored as a program held in a versioned repository and lowered to host applications as build targets. KIR is organised around seven ways in which a generator writing into a stateful, partially observable host goes silently wrong, and gives each a representation in data: ambiguous selectors become typed refusals with candidates; defaults keep their provenance; obligations that will not be checked are named before execution; vacuous witnesses are rejected statically; a lost transaction response becomes the state UNCONFIRMED with a verify-before-retry rule; the reverse path obeys a census invariant; and decisions are bound by digest to the state they were made against. On a pinned snapshot with 83 operation contracts and Revit as the only backend, offline experiments refuse 29 of 42 stress-test programs with diagnostic codes and no uncaught exception, name 38 of 377 witness obligations as unwitnessable, admit 31 of 100 combinations of execution, witness and acceptance states under seven stated invariants, and find no vacuous witness in 219 certificate runs; a 60-storey tower is 11,263 characters as KIR against 3,709,235 characters of emitted C#. Native Revit runs are reported from project records and kept separate from reproduced results. A controlled comparison with agents that write host code directly is specified but not yet executed; it is the principal open question.

</details>


### [87] [Reverse Spatio-Temporal Disease Progression Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14590)
*Ulugbek Shernazarov,Moucheng Xu,Inomjon Ramatov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"逆向疾病进展预测"任务：给定后期病变解剖图像，重建其之前未观测到的更健康状态。方法结合冻结的3D VQ自编码器与神经常微分方程（ODE），在潜空间中反向积分轨迹，并在Morpho-MNIST和ADNI数据上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的时空疾病进展模型多为"前向"预测（从观测基线预测未来衰退），忽略了进行性疾病的潜伏期，导致难以用于早期干预。而临床中影像往往在病理已疑似或可见时才采集，患者更早期的健康参照数据从未获得，因此需要从后期病变状态反推此前更健康状态。

Method: 提出两阶段模型：冻结的3D向量量化自编码器（VQ-VAE）定义紧凑的离散潜空间；神经常微分方程（ODE）在该潜空间中学习连续时间动态。循环编码器以逆时间顺序读取后期观测，初始化潜状态，并将ODE沿轨迹反向积分以重建更早的健康状态。

Result: 在带正弦扰动的受控Morpho-MNIST基准上，模型成功从非单调轨迹的后期观测中恢复出未见的先前状态。在ADNI纵向脑MRI上，从观测到的后期病变状态恢复此前未见的通向健康状态的轨迹任务中，模型优于copy-nearest和mean-observed基线，且在每个诊断分层中均取得正的疾病逆转分数。

Conclusion: 本文为从单次扫描中发现疾病潜伏期、并基于影像开发疾病早期干预提供了新思路与工具。

Abstract: Deep learning-based spatio-temporal disease progression models commonly overlook the incubation period of progressive diseases, limiting the use of those models in early interventions, which are vital for not easily reversible diseases such as Alzheimer's. This is because, the existing deep learning based longitudinal disease-progression models are almost always run forward: from an observed baseline they predict future decline. In many clinical settings, however, imaging begins only after pathology is suspected or already visible, the earlier, healthier patient-specific reference was never acquired. To address this, we propose to study reverse disease progression prediction: given later diseased anatomy, reconstruct the unobserved healthier anatomy that preceded it. We use a two-stage model in which a frozen 3D vector-quantised autoencoder defines a compact discrete latent space, while a Neural Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) learns continuous-time dynamics in that space. A recurrent encoder reads late observations in reverse temporal order, initialises the latent state, and the ODE is integrated backwards across the trajectory. On a controlled Morpho-MNIST benchmark with a sinusoidal perturbation, our model successfully recovered the unseen previous states from later observations of the non-monotonic trajectory. On longitudinal brain MRIs from Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative, at the task to recover the previous unseen trajectory towards healthy states of the patients from observed later diseased states, our model outperforms the baselines that uses copy-nearest and mean-observed, with positive disease-reversal scores in every diagnostic stratum. We hope that our work can provide insights and tools towards discovering the incubation periods from single-shot scans, and developing early interventions of diseases based on imaging.

</details>


### [88] [Direct Conditional Transition Sampling for Diffusion Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14596)
*Qi Yu,Hanlin Wu,Xiaohui Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Direct Conditional Transition Sampling (DCTS)，一种无需训练的扩散逆问题求解器，通过直接随机流近似理想的后验刷新目标，避免显式的干净样本采样和昂贵的 ODE 积分/MCMC，实现最高 16.8 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练扩散逆求解器面临两难：局部测量引导效果有限，而干净空间后验更新虽能提升全局校正但计算代价高昂（需概率流 ODE 积分与干净空间 MCMC）。急需一种能保留全局校正优势、又低成本的实用方案。

Method: DCTS 不显式抽取干净样本，而是沿一条短内部路径估计测量条件下的干净均值，并将高斯源噪声直接输运到下一噪声状态。为此利用去噪器兼容的充分统计量以及协方差缩放的算子更新来完成条件均值估计。

Result: 在四个逆问题上的实验表明，DCTS 取得有竞争力的重建质量，同时相比竞争方法实现最高 16.8 倍的加速。

Conclusion: DCTS 通过直接随机流近似后验刷新目标，在保持重建质量的同时显著提升效率，为免训练扩散逆求解提供了高效实用路径。

Abstract: Training-free diffusion inverse solvers typically choose between local measurement guidance and costly clean-space posterior updates. Independent posterior refresh can improve global correction by sampling a clean conditional and re-noising it, but its practical realization requires probability-flow ODE integration and clean-space Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC). We propose Direct Conditional Transition Sampling (DCTS), a direct stochastic-flow approximation to the same ideal refresh target. Rather than explicitly drawing a clean sample, DCTS estimates the measurement-conditioned clean mean along a short inner path and transports Gaussian source noise directly to the next noisy state. A denoiser-compatible sufficient statistic and a covariance-scaled operator update enable this conditional-mean estimation. Experiments on four inverse problems demonstrate that DCTS achieves competitive reconstruction quality with up to $16.8\times$ speedups over competing methods.

</details>


### [89] [Open-UniMo: Towards Unified Motion-Language Understanding and Generation in the Open World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14615)
*Guocun Wang,Kenkun Liu,Guorui Song,Jing Lin,Zhe Huang,Luyuan Zhang,Dake Zhong,Choo Sin Wai,Xiaoguang Han,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Open-UniMo，一个在百万级开放世界动作-语言数据上训练的统一大型动作-语言模型，通过扩展词表、动作一致性思维链和两阶段训练，提升动作生成与理解的统一表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作-语言模型将动作视为语言模型的辅助模态，导致表征被文本主导、跨模态交互有限；同时自回归的下一词预测范式不适合长动作序列，会累积预测误差。

Method: 在 Qwen 约 15 万文本词表上扩展 6.4 万动作 token，使动作与语言共享统一 token 空间；引入动作一致性 Chain-of-Thought 作为语言语义与动作动态之间的中间表征；采用两阶段训练：监督微调建立 CoT 引导的双向动作-语言映射，再用 GRPO 提升语义对齐并缓解自回归动作 token 生成的累积误差；同时提出 VLM 引导的 Open-MoBench 评测基准。

Result: Open-UniMo 在传统指标与 Open-MoBench 上均达到 SOTA；消融表明 M2T 理解主要不受动作 token 词表大小限制，将 M2T 与可学习的 T2M 生成路径耦合可获得更强的跨模态表征。

Conclusion: 在自回归动作-语言建模中，生成可以促进理解，统一动作生成与理解具备可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Unified motion generation and understanding is crucial for embodied AI systems that can both synthesize and interpret human actions in open-world environments. Existing motion-language models often treat motion as an auxiliary modality of a language model, leading to text-dominated representations and limited cross-modal interaction. Moreover, the next-token prediction paradigm is not naturally suited to long motion sequences, where autoregressive generation may accumulate prediction errors. To address these challenges, we propose Open-UniMo, a unified Large Motion-Language Model (LMLM) trained on million-scale open-world motion-language data. Open-UniMo promotes modality parity by extending Qwen's vocabulary of about 150K text tokens with 64K motion tokens, enabling motion and language to share a unified token space. We further introduce motion-consistent Chain-of-Thought reasoning as an intermediate representation to bridge language semantics and motion dynamics. Open-UniMo is trained with a two-stage pipeline, where supervised fine-tuning establishes CoT-guided bidirectional motion-language mapping and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) improves semantic alignment while mitigating cumulative errors in autoregressive motion-token generation. To support comprehensive evaluation, we propose Open-MoBench, a VLM-guided benchmark for assessing text-to-motion (T2M) generation, motion-to-text (M2T) understanding, and bidirectional consistency. Extensive experiments show that Open-UniMo achieves state-of-the-art performance on both conventional metrics and Open-MoBench. Furthermore, ablation studies reveal that M2T understanding is not primarily limited by motion-token vocabulary size; instead, coupling M2T with the learnable T2M generation path yields stronger cross-modal representations, demonstrating that generation can facilitate understanding in AR-based motion-language modeling.

</details>


### [90] [ESAFusion: LiDAR--4-D Radar Fusion via Local Geometric Complementation and Multiscale Adaptive Interaction for 3-D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14619)
*Gang Ma,Senjie Hu,Junjie Liu,Chao Wang,Hui Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ESAFusion框架，结合证据感知雷达选择与多尺度自适应交互，实现LiDAR-4D雷达融合的3D目标检测，在VoD数据集上取得最优mAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR与4D雷达融合能结合精确空间几何与雷达的运动和反射率线索，适用于复杂驾驶环境。但雷达观测稀疏、两种模态空间采样不一致，导致跨模态互补困难；同时不同空间区域中模态与特征尺度的相对重要性不同，使自适应融合具有挑战性。

Method: 提出ESAFusion，一个证据感知且尺度自适应的框架，包含三部分：(1) Evidence-Aware Radar Selection (ERS) 模块，利用运动与观测质量证据抑制雷达杂波并保留前景置信度；(2) Pillar-Level Complementary Encoder (PCE)，借助邻近LiDAR pillar的局部几何支撑，在空间采样不匹配下改进跨模态互补；(3) Intra- and Inter-Scale Adaptive Fusion (ISAF) 模块，在BEV空间自适应调整不同模态与特征尺度的贡献。

Result: 在View-of-Delft (VoD)数据集上，ESAFusion在对比方法中取得最高mAP：整个标注区域74.60%，驾驶走廊88.89%；在两个区域均取得Cyclist类别最高AP，运行速度19.23 FPS。在VoD-Fog上的评估进一步证明了在LiDAR观测逐步退化情况下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ESAFusion通过证据感知雷达选择与多尺度自适应交互，有效缓解雷达稀疏与跨模态采样不匹配问题，在VoD与VoD-Fog上验证了精度与鲁棒性，源代码将公开。

Abstract: LiDAR--4-D radar fusion combines accurate spatial geometry with motion and reflectivity cues from radar, offering a promising solution for 3-D object detection in complex driving environments. However, sparse radar observations and differences in spatial sampling between the two modalities complicate reliable cross-modal complementation. Moreover, the relative importance of modalities and feature scales varies across spatial regions, making adaptive fusion challenging. To address these challenges, we propose ESAFusion, an evidence-aware and scale-adaptive framework that combines local geometric complementation with multiscale adaptive interaction. Specifically, we introduce an Evidence-Aware Radar Selection (ERS) module to suppress radar clutter using motion and observation-quality evidence while retaining foreground confidence for subsequent fusion. Then, the Pillar-Level Complementary Encoder (PCE) improves cross-modal complementation under mismatched spatial sampling using local geometric support from neighboring LiDAR pillars. We further design an Intra- and Inter-Scale Adaptive Fusion (ISAF) module to adaptively adjust the contributions of different modalities and feature scales in bird's-eye-view (BEV) space. Extensive experiments on the View-of-Delft (VoD) dataset show that ESAFusion achieves the highest mean average precision (mAP) among the compared methods, reaching 74.60% in the Entire Annotated Area and 88.89% in the Driving Corridor. It also attains the highest average precision (AP) for Cyclist among these methods in both regions while running at 19.23 FPS. Evaluations on VoD-Fog further demonstrate robustness under progressively degraded LiDAR observations. The source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/SenJieHu549/ESAFusion.

</details>


### [91] [PhysBrain 1.5: From Vision-Language Models to Physical Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14973)
*DeepCybo Team,Yu Bin,Haipeng Cao,Zheng Chang,Kai Chen,Youning Chen,Kailin Deng,Yichao Du,Xiaotong Fu,Haoyang Ge,Yunlong Guo,Chenliu Hao,Jiyan He,Xuguo He,Yakun Hou,Kai Hu,Cong Huang,Tuopusen Huang,Yu Huang,Hong Li,Peize Li,Shijie Lian,Xiaopeng Lin,Yun Lin,Haibao Liu,Haochen Liu,Qiuzhi Liu,Shengcai Liu,Zhiqiang Liu,Tao Luo,Peng Ren,Shuo Ren,Chaoyi Ruan,Zhaolong Shen,Yukun Shi,Qiyuan Su,Yuxuan Tian,Yining Wang,Changti Wu,Hao Wu,Xueyin Xu,Ruoqi Yang,Zhaoyang Yang,Hang Yuan,Zhaoyang Zeng,Hanwen Zhang,Ruimeng Zhang,Yao Zhang,Yibo Zhang,Yuxiang Zhang,Zhirui Zhang,Ziyi Zhang,Zubin Zheng,Zishen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysBrain 1.5 是一个统一模型，将物理环境理解、动作生成与未来状态预测整合进一个自回归学习框架，仅用人类交互视频预训练，在 28 个具身理解基准上取得开源最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受"观察—交互—环境变化"这一物理闭环启发，作者希望把理解、行动与预测三种能力统一到同一学习框架中，而现有工作通常将它们分开处理。

Method: 从通用视觉语言模型出发，把语言回复、末端执行器运动、稠密视觉目标编码为离散序列，以自回归下一 token 预测联合优化；预训练监督完全来自人类交互视频，采用以任务为中心的片段，将语义与空间上下文和恢复出的运动及后续观测配对；随后在人类演示、机器人轨迹与模拟经验的混合数据上做监督微调。

Result: 在 28 个具身理解基准上，该 8B 模型平均得分 72.5，刷新开源最好成绩，与 GPT-6-Astra、Gemini 3.6 Flash 等领先闭源模型相当；在 14 个基准上取得开源最佳，同时保持通用多模态能力。定性示例显示模型能生成末端执行器轨迹，并通过空间对齐的 RGB、深度与机器人掩码输出预测未来场景。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，把语言、动作与视觉预测统一为离散序列的自回归学习，并以人类视频作为具身监督来源，是构建通用具身智能模型的有效途径。

Abstract: We present PhysBrain 1.5, a unified model for understanding physical environments, generating actions, and predicting future states. Motivated by the physical loop of observation, interaction, and environmental change, we bring these capabilities into a common learning framework. Starting from a general vision--language model, we encode language responses, end-effector motion, and dense visual targets as discrete sequences and jointly optimize them with autoregressive next-token prediction.
