Motivation: 长视频理解对多模态大模型（MLLMs）仍具挑战：密集编码长帧序列计算代价高昂，而在有限视觉预算下均匀采样可能漏掉稀疏但决定性的证据。近期免训练关键帧选择方法虽提升了推理效率，但大多孤立地为帧打分，未显式考虑候选帧与已选子集之间的互补性，易导致冗余选择与证据覆盖不完整。

Method: MarKey 将关键帧选择形式化为子集感知的贪心优化。每次迭代中，用可处理的替代目标为每个候选帧打分，该目标联合考虑查询相关性、边际覆盖增益与上下文相关的冗余度，并选择效用最高的帧。为提升迭代评估效率，采用一组紧凑的代表性锚点近似全视频覆盖，并用有界的已选帧窗口限制上下文相关比较。整个框架无需训练。

Result: 在涵盖整体视频理解、以人为中心的视频理解与开放式视频理解三类任务的六个基准上，MarKey 一致优于现有方法。进一步分析显示，在不同 MLLM 骨干、模型规模与帧预算下均取得稳定增益。

Conclusion: 通过将关键帧选择从孤立打分转变为子集感知的贪心优化，并借助锚点近似与有界窗口控制计算成本，MarKey 在免训练前提下实现了更高效、覆盖更完整的长视频关键帧选择，具备良好的通用性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Long-video understanding remains challenging for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) because densely encoding long frame sequences is computationally expensive, while uniform sampling under a limited visual budget can miss sparse yet decisive evidence. Recent training-free keyframe selection methods have enabled more efficient inference and yielded promising performance gains. However, many existing methods score frames largely in isolation without explicitly considering how each candidate complements the currently selected subset, potentially resulting in redundant selections and incomplete evidence coverage. To address this limitation, we propose MarKey, a training-free framework that formulates keyframe selection as subset-aware greedy optimization. At each iteration, MarKey scores each candidate using a tractable surrogate that jointly accounts for query relevance, marginal coverage gain, and context-dependent redundancy, and selects the frame with the highest utility. To make this iterative subset-aware evaluation efficient, MarKey uses a compact set of representative anchors to approximate full-video coverage and a bounded window of previously selected frames to limit context-dependent comparisons. Experiments on six benchmarks spanning holistic video understanding, human-centric video understanding, and open-ended video understanding demonstrate that MarKey consistently outperforms existing methods. Further analyses show robust gains across different MLLM backbones, model scales, and frame budgets.

</details>


### [127] [ViCo-SAM3: Vision-Conditioned Alignment for Open-Vocabulary Camouflaged Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15418)
*Qiangqiang Zhou,Wenjun Tang,Yong Chen,Dandan Zhu,Jiawei Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ViCo-SAM3，通过视觉条件对齐框架缓解OVCOS中文本与像素级视觉线索的语义鸿沟，在OVCamo基准上取得SOTA并具强泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇伪装目标分割（OVCOS）需在文本引导下分割未见过的伪装目标。作者观察到SAM3仍存在全局文本语义与细粒度像素级视觉线索之间的显著语义鸿沟；同时全量微调文本编码器会带来沉重参数开销，并易过拟合训练类别，损害开放词汇表征的灵活性。

Method: 提出ViCo-SAM3视觉条件对齐框架。核心为视觉条件（ViCo）模块：用全局视觉上下文动态调制文本嵌入，使文本表征适应当前图像内容，从而弥合视觉-文本语义鸿沟；并进一步设计视觉条件跨模态绑定（ViCoBind）模块，增强视觉与文本表征间的跨模态交互与语义对齐。

Result: 在OVCamo基准上取得SOTA性能，并展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: ViCo-SAM3以简洁设计（无需额外复杂技巧）有效缓解OVCOS的语义鸿沟与微调开销问题，验证了视觉条件对齐在开放词汇伪装目标分割中的有效性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary camouflaged object segmentation (OVCOS) aims to segment unseen camouflaged objects under text guidance. We observe that SAM3 still suffers from a pronounced semantic gap between global textual semantics and fine-grained pixel-level visual cues in OVCOS. Meanwhile, fully fine-tuning the text encoder introduces heavy parameter overhead and risks overfitting to training categories, which compromises open-vocabulary representation flexibility. To address these issues, we propose ViCo-SAM3, a Vision-Conditioned alignment framework designed for OVCOS. Specifically, we introduce vision-conditioned (ViCo) module, which dynamically modulates text embeddings with global visual context, enabling textual representations to adapt to the current image content and thereby effectively bridging the semantic gap between vision and text. Building on this, we further design a vision-conditioned cross-modal binding (ViCoBind) module to enhance cross-modal interaction and semantic alignment between visual and textual representations. Without bells and whistles, ViCo-SAM3 achieves state-of-the-art performance on the OVCamo benchmark and demonstrates strong generalization.

</details>


### [128] [A Conservative OCR-Enabled Workflow for R214 Sodium Screening of South African Packaged Foods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15427)
*Mayimunah Nagayi,Alice Scaria Khan,Tamryn Frank,Rina Swart,Clement Nyirenda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究构建了一套基于包装图像、偏保守的钠含量筛查工作流，用于对照南非R214钠限量标准，可整理证据、识别明确案例，并把不确定案例归入REVIEW而非强行判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接用食品包装图像对照南非R214钠限量进行筛查存在困难，因为筛查决策需要产品身份、营养成分表证据、报告基准以及类别专属阈值等多重信息。

Method: 工作流整合区域检测、OCR、产品身份与钠证据抽取、R214类别归属、确定性阈值比较，并与独立视觉语言模型（Qwen2.5-VL 7B）结果对照。使用442个包装食品、3929张完整包装图像；YOLO26s小检测器生成4195个区域裁剪，严格后处理为每产品一行钠证据。

Result: 集成工作流输出290个OUTSIDE R214 SCOPE、139个REVIEW、7个SCREEN-PASS、6个SCREEN-FAIL；Qwen2.5-VL工作流为387、31、20、4。两者R214类别完全一致415/442（93.9%），类别是否在R214范围内一致416/442（94.1%），最终筛查结果一致307/442（69.5%）。60个产品人工核验显示严格结果一致性低于监管状态一致性，且两个自动流程均把人工判定为INSUFFICIENT DATA的案例排除在SCREEN-PASS与SCREEN-FAIL之外。

Conclusion: 基于图像的保守筛查可有效组织包装证据、识别明确案例，并将不确定案例归入REVIEW，避免强行给出SCREEN-PASS或SCREEN-FAIL结论。

Abstract: Using food package images to monitor sodium and salt content against South Africa's R214 sodium limits is challenging when screening decisions require product identity, nutrition facts panel evidence, reporting basis, and category-specific thresholds. This study presents a conservative image-based workflow that combines region detection, optical character recognition (OCR), product identity and sodium evidence extraction, R214 category assignment, deterministic threshold comparison, and independent vision language model comparison. The evaluation used 442 packaged food products and 3 929 full package images from a real-world South African food packaging dataset. A YOLO26s small detector generated 4 195 region crops, and strict post-processing produced one sodium evidence row per product. The integrated workflow produced 290 OUTSIDE R214 SCOPE, 139 REVIEW, seven SCREEN-PASS, and six SCREEN-FAIL outcomes. The independent Qwen2.5-VL 7B vision language model workflow produced 387 OUTSIDE R214 SCOPE, 31 REVIEW, twenty SCREEN-PASS, and four SCREEN-FAIL outcomes. The workflows agreed on exact R214 category assignment for 415 of 442 products (93.9%) and on whether the assigned category was within R214 scope for 416 of 442 products (94.1%). Final screening outcome agreement was 307 out of 442 products, or 69.5%. Manual verification on 60 products showed lower strict outcome agreement than regulated status agreement, while all manual INSUFFICIENT DATA cases were kept out of SCREEN-PASS and SCREEN-FAIL by both automated workflows. The findings show that conservative image-based screening can organise package evidence, identify clear cases, and assign uncertain cases to REVIEW rather than forcing SCREEN-PASS or SCREEN-FAIL decisions.

</details>


### [129] [AnchorGUI: Asymmetric Memory for Dual-Scale Learning in GUI Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15457)
*Shengjie Jin,Zelong Sun,Hengbo Xu,Yanbiao Ma,Zhiwu Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AnchorGUI 框架，利用认知状态锚（CSA）显式捕捉预期与实际的偏差，通过非对称记忆实现单回合纠错与跨回合经验蒸馏，在多个基准上提升成功率并降低 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型虽可用于 GUI 自主导航，但难以处理并学习密集、连续的视觉历史，这阻碍了单回合内的即时纠错与跨多回合的经验蒸馏。作者将此归因于 GUI 导航中的信息不对称：预期转换常可压缩为轻量文本摘要，而意外结果则需保留截图作为因果证据。

Method: 提出由认知状态锚（CSA）驱动的 AnchorGUI 框架。CSA 作为逐步原语，主动比较预期与实际转换，将被动多模态轨迹转为显式的预测误差信号；这些信号借助非对称记忆组织双尺度学习：单回合内用滑动窗口选择性保留不匹配处的视觉证据以提供即时反馈，跨回合则聚焦计算昂贵的信用分配搜索空间于可能的失败步骤。

Result: 在四个基准上验证有效：AndroidWorld 成功率 57.3%，每步 token 减少 2.4 倍；跨回合蒸馏成功率达 69.2%（提升 11.9%），显著优于标准反思方法，并保持亚线性上下文扩展。

Conclusion: 基于信息不对称设计的 AnchorGUI 能有效结合文本摘要与视觉证据，在提升 GUI 导航成功率的同时降低计算开销，实现了高效的单回合纠错与跨回合经验蒸馏。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) enable autonomous GUI navigation, but agents still struggle to process and learn from dense, continuous visual histories. This bottleneck hinders both immediate error correction within a single episode (intra-trial) and experience distillation across multiple attempts (cross-trial). We trace these challenges to an empirical informational asymmetry in GUI navigation: while expected transitions can often be compressed into lightweight textual summaries, unexpected outcomes benefit from preserved screenshots as causal evidence for accurate diagnosis. Building on this insight, we propose AnchorGUI, a unified framework driven by the Cognitive State Anchor (CSA). The CSA acts as a per-step primitive that actively compares expected and observed transitions, converting passive multimodal trajectories into explicit prediction-error signals. These signals orchestrate a dual-scale learning mechanism via an asymmetric memory. For intra-trial correction, a sliding window selectively retains visual evidence for detected mismatches, providing immediate, visually-grounded feedback. For cross-trial distillation, this asymmetric memory focuses the computationally expensive credit assignment search space on likely failure steps. Experiments across four benchmarks validate the effectiveness of our approach. On AndroidWorld, AnchorGUI achieves a 57.3% success rate with a $2.4\times$ token reduction per step. Furthermore, cross-trial distillation reaches 69.2% success (+11.9% gain), significantly outperforming standard reflection methods while maintaining sub-linear context scaling.

</details>


### [130] [BVB: Benchmarking Agentic Video Understanding via Programmatic Reconstruction in Blender](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15478)
*Yolo Y. Tang,Daiki Shimada,Jiayue Meng,Jing Bi,Pinxin Liu,Yicheng Wang,Yunzhong Xiao,Zhangyun Tan,Zeliang Zhang,Chao Huang,Susan Liang,Qianxiang Shen,Luchuan Song,Ali Vosoughi,Mingqian Feng,Melika Filvantorkaman,Chenliang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 BVB（Blender-VideoBench）基准，通过让多模态智能体以编程方式将真实视频重建为 Blender 动画场景来测试视频理解能力，并在统一沙箱与成本限制下用双重 VQA 和潜在相似度两个维度进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解基准主要通过问答形式评估模型，但问答未必能真实反映智能体对视频的理解程度。作者认为，若智能体真正理解一段视频，就应能通过编程方式将其重建出来，因此需要一个能直接检验这种能力的基准。

Method: 提出 BVB（Blender-VideoBench）基准，要求智能体将真实视频重建为 Blender 动画场景。为保证公平比较，各智能体通过轻量级框架 Mini-BVB 在相同沙箱和共享成本限制下编程实现重建。评估从动画相机渲染重建结果，并采用两个维度：（1）Dual VQA 衡量重建保留了多少时空事实；（2）Latent Similarity 衡量重建与源视频在感知层面的匹配程度。总分为平方根均值，以鼓励均衡表现。

Result: 作者评估了来自 10 个模型家族的 51 种配置，并分析语义保留、感知相似度、推理投入与成本。最佳模型达到 88.6 的 Latent Similarity，但仅保留了 53.7% 的源视频中正确的时空问答答案。增加推理能提升视觉相似度，却无法弥合事实准确性上的差距。在 15 名评分者对 5 种配置的盲测中，Latent Similarity 与人类偏好高度相关。

Conclusion: 结果表明，程序化重建是检验智能体视频理解能力的可行方法，而语义保留仍是当前的主要挑战。

Abstract: Multimodal agents can create complex videos in software such as Blender by coding without relying on diffusion models. Yet video understanding benchmarks still evaluate models mainly through question answering. If an agent truly understands a video, it can reconstruct it programmatically. We introduce BVB, Blender-VideoBench, a benchmark that tests this ability by asking agents to reconstruct real-world videos as animated Blender scenes. To ensure fair comparison, each agent programs the reconstruction through a lightweight harness, Mini-BVB, in an identical sandbox under a shared cost limit. The benchmark renders each reconstruction from its animated camera and evaluates it on two axes: (1) Dual VQA measures how many spatiotemporal facts the reconstruction preserves. (2) Latent Similarity measures how closely the reconstruction matches the source video perceptually. Our overall score, a square-root mean, favors balanced performance. We evaluate 51 configurations from 10 model families and analyze semantic retention, perceptual similarity, reasoning effort, and cost. The best model reaches 88.6 Latent Similarity but retains only 53.7% of the source-correct spatiotemporal answers. Additional reasoning improves visual similarity but does not close this gap in factual accuracy. In a blind study with 15 raters and five configurations, Latent Similarity correlates strongly with human preference. These results show that programmatic reconstruction is a viable test of agentic video understanding, and that semantic retention remains the main challenge.

</details>


### [131] [CatchMonitor: a machine learning system for automated fish discard quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15484)
*Geoff French,Michal Mackiewicz,Mark Fisher,Helen Holah,Rebecca Lamb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文报告了CatchMonitor原型计算机视觉系统的持续开发，用于从渔船REM视频中自动量化丢弃鱼类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渔船上的实际监控条件使拖网渔船视频分析极具挑战性；同时，人工量化丢弃鱼类存在工作量大和主观差异的问题。

Method: 在前期工作基础上，采用半监督学习提升物种识别准确率；使用简单且稳健的目标跟踪方法，并据此构建丢弃量化原型系统。

Result: 改进了物种识别准确率，并实现了基于跟踪的丢弃量化系统；同时分析了多位专家人工量化结果的变异性。

Conclusion: 该系统为自动量化丢弃鱼类提供了可行原型，并以人工量化变异性为基准评估了系统性能。

Abstract: We report on the continued development of CatchMonitor, resulting in a prototype computer vision system designed to automatically quantify discarded fish from video footage collected from Remote Electronic Monitoring (REM) systems on fishing trawlers. The analysis of trawler surveillance footage is a challenging problem due to the real-world conditions on board fishing vessels. Building on our prior work we improve the accuracy of species identification through the application of semi-supervised learning. We utilise a simple and robust object tracking approach, upon which we build our prototype discard quantification system. Finally we analyse the variability of manual discard quantification performed by multiple expert human analysts, using it as a benchmark against which we compare the performance of our system.

</details>


### [132] [SyntheticDoc: A Large Synthetic Dataset for Document Unwarping and Illumination Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15503)
*Daniel Woortmann,Tanguy Magne,Olga Sorkine-Hornung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 SyntheticDoc，一个包含 100 万高分辨率程序化生成样本的大规模高质量文档去畸变数据集，通过物理模拟与路径追踪渲染提供像素级精确标注（UV、法线、反照率、阴影），并证明在 SyntheticDoc 上训练的简单基线模型在文档去畸变与光照校正任务上可媲美最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习文档矫正与光照校正的性能受训练数据制约。近十年来社区主要依赖 Doc3D，但其规模和图像质量日益受限，成为领域发展的瓶颈。因此需要构建更大规模、更高质量的数据集来突破这一限制。

Method: 提出 SyntheticDoc 数据集：包含 1,000,000 个高分辨率程序化生成的训练样本，以及大规模的验证集和测试集；每个样本配备丰富的像素级精确标注，包括 UV 图、法线图、反照率和阴影图。为保证物理准确性与真实感，纸张几何通过基于物理的模拟器生成，并使用路径追踪渲染。基于该数据集训练了一个简单基线模型，并在文档去畸变和光照校正两个任务上与当前最优方法进行对比。

Result: 在 SyntheticDoc 上训练的简单基线模型，在文档去畸变和光照校正任务上的表现与现有最优方法相当（或具有竞争力）。数据集与生成代码已公开提供。

Conclusion: SyntheticDoc 通过大规模、高质量、物理精确的合成数据，为文档去畸变与光照校正研究提供了新的数据基础，有望推动该领域突破长期存在的数据瓶颈。数据集与生成代码均已开源。

Abstract: Deep learning models have become the standard tool for document rectification and illumination correction, yet their performance is fundamentally bound by their training data. For nearly a decade, the community has heavily relied on Doc3D, a pioneering but increasingly limited document unwarping dataset in terms of scale and quality. To address this bottleneck, we introduce SyntheticDoc, a massive, high-quality dataset designed to push the boundaries of document unwarping. SyntheticDoc is composed of 1,000,000 high-resolution procedurally generated training samples, alongside extensive validation and test sets. Each sample is paired with rich, pixel-perfect annotations, including UV maps, normal maps, albedo and shading. To ensure physical accuracy and photorealism, the paper geometries are generated via a physics-based simulator and rendered using a path tracer. To demonstrate the benefit of our dataset, we train a simple baseline model on SyntheticDoc and report on its performance in comparison to state-of-the-art methods on both document unwarping and illumination correction tasks. Our dataset is available at https://igl.ethz.ch/projects/SyntheticDoc/ and the code used to generate it at https://github.com/tanguymagne/SyntheticDoc .

</details>


### [133] [Assessing nnU-Net Generalization across Brain Tumor Populations in BraTS-GoAT 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15524)
*Tristan Kirscher,Vivian Metzger,Philippe Meyer,Xavier Coubez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在BraTS-GoAT数据集上评估异质人群的肿瘤分割性能，使用3D nnU-Net进行五折交叉验证（1351例标注、每折1000轮），最终预测器采用全折平均与测试时镜像。汇总官方验证集上ET、TC、WT的全局DSC分别为0.7805、0.8288、0.8854；匹配fold-0推断时区域Dice从源OOF的0.9058降至汇总验证的0.8310（差-0.0747）。镜像仅带来单折微小增益，残差编码器替代方案均Dice为0.8282。失败样本的参考ET体积显著更小，且在调整ET与WT体积后，较低Dice仍与ET组件更分散、最大组件中ET占比更小相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BraTS-GoAT旨在跨异质人群评估肿瘤分割性能，关注模型在分布偏移（源数据与汇总验证集差异）下的泛化能力，并探究分割失败的影像学/形态学原因。

Method: 训练常规3D nnU-Net于1351例标注数据，五折交叉验证、每折1000轮；最终预测器对全部折进行平均并采用测试时镜像。另评估残差编码器替代方案。在汇总官方验证集与fold-0匹配推断下比较Dice，并对OOF预测中的失败病例进行体积与连通性分析（调整ET和WT体积）。

Result: 汇总官方验证集全局DSC：ET 0.7805、TC 0.8288、WT 0.8854。fold-0匹配推断下区域Dice由源OOF 0.9058降至汇总验证0.8310（-0.0747）。镜像带来单折小幅提升但无明确集成收益；残差编码器方案均Dice为0.8282。失败病例参考ET体积显著更小；调整ET与WT体积后，较低Dice仍与ET组件更分散及最大组件中ET占比更小相关。

Conclusion: 模型在异质人群的汇总验证集上性能明显低于源OOF，存在显著泛化差距；镜像与残差编码器改进有限。分割失败与小ET体积、ET碎片化（多连通组件、最大组件占比低）密切相关。

Abstract: BraTS-GoAT evaluates tumor segmentation across heterogeneous populations. We trained a conventional 3D nnU-Net on 1,351 labeled cases using five-fold cross-validation and 1,000 epochs per fold. The final predictor averaged all folds and applied test-time mirroring. On pooled official validation, global DSC values were 0.7805, 0.8288, and 0.8854 for enhancing tumor (ET), tumor core (TC), and whole tumor (WT). Under matched fold-0 inference, mean regional Dice decreased from 0.9058 on source out-of-fold (OOF) cases to 0.8310 on pooled validation (difference--0.0747). Mirroring gave small single-fold gains but no clear ensemble benefit; a residual-encoder alternative reached 0.8282 mean Dice. In labeled OOF predictions, failure cases had substantially smaller reference ET volumes; after adjustment for ET and WT volume, lower Dice remained associated with more disconnected ET components and a smaller fraction of ET contained in the largest component.

</details>


### [134] [PIVOT: Physics-Grounded Verification for AI-Generated Audio-Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15562)
*Bo Zheng,Kangran Zhao,Xiaoyu Zhang,Weinan Guan,Zhiheng Li,Yize Chen,Haizhou Li,Qingshan Liu,Siwei Lyu,Baoyuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PIVOT，一种基于物理规律约束验证的AIGC检测器（音视频实例），并构建PhysForensics-Bench基准，在多项指标上超越直接使用Gemini检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成模型进步，AIGC内容愈发逼真，削弱了现有检测器依赖的伪影线索。然而当前生成器仍难以忠实复现真实事件的物理行为，因此可借助物理法则的可测约束来判别真伪。

Method: PIVOT从视频和音频中估计物理量，为每个片段选择相关物理定律，并验证其可测约束；除真/假判断外，还返回支持证据（验证结果、时间窗、支持量）。可原则上扩展到其他AIGC模态。同时构建PhysForensics-Bench，包含九类事件场景家族、两种近期音视频生成器的配对真实与生成片段。

Result: 在PhysForensics-Bench上，PIVOT在Real+Seedance上达70.30%准确率、64.29% F1；在Real+VEO上达72.16%准确率、65.82% F1。对比Gemini 3.1 Pro直接检测：Real+Seedance为53.96%准确率、60.09% F1；Real+Veo为57.22%准确率、63.44% F1。

Conclusion: 物理一致性验证可作为结构化、可检验的证据来源，有效补充基于伪影的AIGC检测，具有实用前景。

Abstract: As generative models continue to advance, AI-generated content (AIGC) is becoming increasingly realistic, weakening the artifact cues commonly exploited by existing detectors. Nevertheless, faithfully reproducing the physical behavior of real-world events remains challenging for current generators. We therefore explore detecting AIGC by assessing whether the depicted event satisfies measurable constraints derived from physical laws. We introduce PIVOT, a physics-grounded AIGC detector, instantiated here for audio-video clips, that estimates physical quantities from video and audio, selects physical laws relevant to each clip, and verifies their measurable constraints. Beyond a real/fake decision, PIVOT returns supporting evidence that records the verification outcome, relevant time window, and supporting quantities for each applicable law. Although instantiated and evaluated here on audio-video data, the framework can, in principle, extend to other AIGC modalities whenever the physical quantities required for verification can be estimated reliably. We also introduce PhysForensics-Bench, comprising paired real and generated audio-video clips from nine event-centric scene families and two recent audio-video generators. On PhysForensics-Bench, PIVOT achieves 70.30% accuracy and 64.29% F1 score on Real+Seedance, and 72.16% accuracy and 65.82% F1 on Real+VEO. In comparison, direct inspection with Gemini 3.1 Pro obtains 53.96% accuracy and 60.09% F1 on Real+Seedance, and 57.22% accuracy and 63.44% F1 on Real+Veo. These results demonstrate the practical promise of physical-consistency verification as a structured and inspectable source of evidence that complements artifact-based AIGC detection.

</details>


### [135] [A Unified Vision-Language Model for PSMA PET/CT Report Generation, Visual Question Answering, and Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15603)
*Yang Xing,Jiong Wu,Savas Ozdemir,Yang Zhou,Boxiao Yu,Ying Zhang,Zheren Zhu,Chenyu You,Wei Shao,Yang Lu,Kang Wang,Tinsu Pan,Yang Yang,Kuang Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一PSMA PET/CT视觉-语言模型，集成报告生成、视觉问答与病灶分割，性能优于多个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PSMA PET/CT的AI模型通常只处理孤立任务，缺乏统一框架，而准确的PSMA PET/CT解读对前列腺癌管理至关重要。

Method: 采用LLaVA风格架构，包含PET/CT视觉编码器、MLP-Mixer投影模块、LoRA微调大语言模型和3D分割分支；训练分四阶段：视觉编码器预训练、投影层对齐、VLM微调、多任务调优。语言任务用5,747例配对报告的PSMA PET/CT数据，分割用AutoPET的PSMA子集。

Result: 在报告生成指标上优于PET2REP和CT基线，VQA各题型表现提升，Dice和病灶级重叠F1高于SegAnyPET和nnUNet。

Conclusion: 支持在单一多任务架构中实现结构化、交互式、可解释且具体素级定位的PSMA PET/CT统一分析框架的可行性。

Abstract: Accurate PSMA PET/CT interpretation is central to prostate cancer management, yet existing PET/CT AI models typically address isolated tasks. We propose a unified PSMA PET/CT vision-language model for report generation, visual question answering, and lesion segmentation. The framework adopts an LLaVA-style architecture, comprising a PET/CT vision encoder, an MLP-Mixer projection module, a LoRA-tuned large language model, and a 3D segmentation branch. Training followed a four-stage strategy: vision encoder pretraining, projection-layer alignment, VLM fine-tuning, and final multitask tuning. Language tasks used 5,747 PSMA PET/CT datasets with paired reports, while segmentation used the PSMA subset of AutoPET. The model outperformed PET2REP and a CT-based baseline across standard report-generation metrics, improved performance across VQA question types, and achieved higher Dice and lesion-level overlap F1 than SegAnyPET and nnUNet. These results support the feasibility of a unified framework for structured, interactive, interpretable PSMA PET/CT analysis with voxel-level grounding within a single multitask model architecture.

</details>


### [136] [VideoScout: Learning Agentic Active Exploration with Adaptive Reasoning Pacing for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15606)
*Weixin Xu,Zhenyu Yang,Bing Wang,Shengsheng Qian,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sequential Evidence Acquisition (SEA) 问题视角，并设计多轮推理智能体 VideoScout，通过自适应推理节奏在有限视觉上下文窗口下高效遍历长视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在短视频理解上进展显著，但受限于视觉上下文窗口，在长视频上表现有限。现有多数方法依赖均匀帧采样或粗略到精细的智能体缩放，难以在足够长的视频中定位稀疏且决定性的证据。

Method: 将长视频理解形式化为 SEA 问题：智能体沿时间轴逐轮读取视频，决定观看速度、保留证据、回访不确定片段以及停止作答时机。提出 VideoScout 多轮推理智能体，通过动态控制观看节奏，在有限视觉上下文窗口内高效遍历长视频。训练方面构建 VideoScout-66K（来自 10K 条答案验证轨迹的 66K+ 高质量探索轮次），采用两阶段流程：冷启动监督微调学习每轮输出格式；再用 DAPO 算法进行轨迹级强化学习，复合奖励联合考虑答案准确性、输出格式合规性，以及智能体观看进度与教师答案时序的 IoU 时间对齐。

Result: 在长视频理解与推理基准上的大量实验表明，7B 模型相比现有已训练的 7B 智能体模型取得了强劲性能。

Conclusion: 将长视频理解建模为顺序证据获取问题，并通过自适应推理节奏的多轮智能体与针对性训练流程，可在有限上下文窗口下有效提升长视频理解与推理能力。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress on short video understanding yet remain limited on long videos due to the limited visual context window. Prevailing approaches rely on uniform frame sampling or recent coarse-to-fine agentic zooming, both of which struggle to localize sparse, decisive evidence in sufficiently long videos. We formulate long video understanding as a \textbf{Sequential Evidence Acquisition (SEA)} problem, in which an agent reads the video turn by turn along the temporal axis, deciding at each turn how fast to watch, what evidence to retain, when to revisit uncertain segments, and when to stop and answer. Inspired by this view, we propose \textbf{VideoScout}, a multi-turn reasoning agent that instantiates the SEA paradigm through adaptive reasoning pacing. Specifically, by dynamically controlling the viewing pace, VideoScout enables efficient traversal of long videos within a bounded visual context window, allowing the agent to access more video content while balancing content analysis depth with reading efficiency. To train VideoScout, we construct VideoScout-66K, a set of over 66K high-quality exploration turns from 10K answer-verified trajectories, and adopt a two-stage pipeline: cold-start supervised fine-tuning teaches the agent per-turn output format, while the Decoupled Clip and Dynamic sAmpling Policy Optimization (DAPO) algorithm performs trajectory-level reinforcement learning with a composite reward that jointly considers answer accuracy, output format compliance, and the temporal alignment between the agent's viewing progress and the teacher's answer timing measured by intersection-over-union (IoU). Extensive experiments on long video understanding and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that our 7B model achieves strong performance compared with existing trained 7B agentic models.

</details>


### [137] [ModaLens: Measuring Image Sensitivity in Report-Conditioned Medical VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15635)
*Sebastián Andrés Cajas Ordóñez,Maximin Lange,Quang Bui,Anqi Peter Li,Felipe Ocampo Osorio,Rafi Al Attrach,Kushul Reddy Palakala,Sahil Kapadia,Zakaria Laouabdia Sellami,Xinyue Zhang,Ashley Zhang,Leo Anthony Celi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ModaLens 配对图像替换审计方法，发现报告可用性会降低放射学视觉语言模型对图像的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学报告本身已能回答临床问题，因此难以判断视觉语言模型是否真正利用了图像信息。作者希望量化"模型看到报告"这一条件如何改变其对图像的依赖程度。

Method: ModaLens 配对图像替换审计：在 3,199 对 MIMIC-CXR 病例（来自 293 名患者）上测试 MedGemma-27B，每例 14 个问题（13 个特定征象 + 1 个综合问题）。将每张图像替换为另一项检查的图像（通常来自同一患者），同时固定问题与报告，比较有/无报告条件下生成答案的变化率。

Result: 在明确作答指令下，有报告时答案改变率为 4.26%，无报告时为 20.94%，配对增幅 16.7 个百分点（患者聚类 95% CI 15.6–17.7）；原始提示配合小写首 token 读出为 4.70% 对 17.07%；替换还会在二值预测不变时改变连续答案分数。方向在另外两个模型谱系中均得到复现。

Conclusion: 在该协议下，报告可用性显著降低模型的图像替换敏感性，说明报告的存在可能使模型减少对图像的依赖。由于标签源自报告，关于视觉正确性的结论有限。代码、提示词与全部数值运行记录已公开。

Abstract: A radiology report can already answer a clinical question, so it is hard to tell whether a vision-language model also uses the image. ModaLens, a paired image-swap audit, measures how report availability changes image sensitivity: MedGemma-27B on 3,199 paired MIMIC-CXR cases from 293 patients, all 14 questions per case (13 finding-specific and one composite), each image replaced by one from another study, usually of the same patient, with question and report fixed. Under an explicit answer instruction, the model's generated answer changes on 4.26 percent of trials with the report and 20.94 percent without it, a paired increase of 16.7 points (patient-clustered 95 percent CI 15.6 to 17.7), so report availability reduces image-swap sensitivity under this protocol; the original prompt with a lowercase first-token readout gives 4.70 percent against 17.07 percent, and substitutions also move continuous answer scores where the binary prediction does not change. The labels are derived from reports, which limits conclusions about visual correctness; the direction replicates in two further model lineages. Code, the exact prompts and a run record for every number are at https://github.com/criticaldata/MODALENS.

</details>


### [138] [SAM3D-Part: Interactive Part Selection and Generation from 3D Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15639)
*Jiahao Chang,Dong Du,Wanhu Sun,Yujian Zheng,Chuanyu Pan,Bowen Zhao,Chongjie Ye,Yuanming Hu,Xiaoguang Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SAM3D-Part，一个从输入三维网格中按提示选择性生成部件完整可复用网格的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代三维资产创作常按部件编辑、复用、动画化或制造，用户往往只需若干指定部件而非整物分解。但现有三维生成方法会无差别生成全部部件，提示式三维分割方法通常只输出局部表面而非可复用完整网格；图像条件部件生成器若不直接以源网格为条件，又难以保留被遮挡几何并保证准确摆放。

Method: SAM3D-Part 以源网格与部件提示为输入：先将源几何编码为紧凑网格特征，并与渲染图像、选择性掩码、点图观测通过逐像素通道融合对齐；融合表示条件化前馈生成模型，仅生成被查询部件的完整网格；为将生成部件放回源坐标系，预测稠密逐体素对应，并由分布式空间证据估计部件变换而非单一全局位姿编码；顺序多部件查询时，已生成部件存入部件缓存并作为上下文约束复用，以减少独立请求部件之间的冲突。

Result: 大量实验与消融表明，SAM3D-Part 可显著提升源对齐、降低条件化成本，并实现一致的选择性部件生成，达到当前最优。代码与权重将开源。

Conclusion: 该工作面向按需部件生成，输出完整可复用网格并保持与源对象对齐，通过部件缓存支持顺序多部件查询的一致性，兼顾对齐质量与生成成本。

Abstract: Part-level control is essential for modern 3D asset creation, where objects are frequently edited, reused, animated, or fabricated through their individual components. In many such workflows, users need only several specific components rather than a complete object decomposition. However, existing 3D generation methods produce all parts regardless of user intent, while promptable 3D segmentation methods typically output partial surfaces instead of reusable complete meshes. In addition, image-conditioned part generators further struggle to preserve hidden geometry and accurate placement without directly conditioning on the source mesh. To address these problems, we present SAM3D-Part, a prompt-driven framework for selective part generation from input 3D object meshes. Given a source mesh and a part prompt, SAM3D-Part first encodes the source geometry into compact mesh features and aligns them with the rendered image, selective mask, and point-map observations via pixel-wise channel fusion. The fused representation conditions a feed-forward generative model to produce only the queried component as a completed mesh. To place the generated part back into the source coordinate frame, SAM3D-Part predicts dense per-voxel correspondences and estimates the part transformation from distributed spatial evidence rather than a single global pose code. For sequential multi-part queries, previously generated parts are stored in a part cache and reused as contextual constraints, reducing conflicts among independently requested components. Extensive experiments and ablations demonstrate that SAM3D-Part can significantly improve source alignment, reduce conditioning cost, and enable consistent selective part generation, achieving state-of-the-art. Code and weights will be available at https://github.com/Jiahao620/sam3d-part.

</details>


### [139] [Human-Grounded Calibration for Long-Text Image-Text Congruence in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15640)
*Alessandro Gambetti,Qiwei Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Congruency Score (CS)，一种轻量级校准层，将图文相似度证据映射为有界的一致性分数，并在 DOCCI 与 Urban1k 上验证了检索性能、人类关联度与阈值校准是三个需分别评估的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长文本图文一致性评分对视觉-语言系统日益重要，但双编码器模型的原始相似度分数受模态间隙影响，难以作为可校准的一致性度量进行解释。

Method: 提出 Congruency Score (CS) 轻量校准层，将图文相似度证据映射为有界分数；在 DOCCI 和 Urban1k 上评估四个冻结的视觉-语言骨干模型，并进行基于人类判断的评测。

Result: 投影后质心距离的下降并未一致提升图文检索性能；直接事后校准与人类判断保持高关联性，而部分基于投影的配置虽能降低阈值相关的斜率与截距失真，却以检索性能和关联强度为代价。

Conclusion: 长文本图文一致性评分应被确立为可校准的分数估计问题，检索性能、人类关联度与阈值校准须作为相互独立的目标评估；CS 为揭示和操作化这一区分提供了轻量途径。

Abstract: Long-text image--text congruence scoring is increasingly important for vision-language systems that must evaluate whether detailed textual descriptions match visual content. However, raw similarity scores from dual-encoder models are difficult to interpret as calibrated congruence measures, especially under the modality gap between image and text embeddings. This paper proposes Congruency Score (CS), a lightweight calibration layer that maps image--text similarity evidence into a bounded score. Using DOCCI and Urban1k, we evaluate four frozen vision-language backbones and show that observed reductions in post-projection centroid distance do not uniformly improve image--text retrieval performance. Human-grounded evaluations on DOCCI further reveal a trade-off: direct post-hoc calibration preserves high association with human judgments, whereas selected projection-based configurations can reduce threshold-relevant slope and intercept distortions at the cost of retrieval performance and association strength. These results establish long-text image--text congruence scoring as a calibrated score-estimation problem, where retrieval performance, human association, and threshold calibration must be evaluated as distinct objectives. CS provides a lightweight way to expose and operationalize this separation.

</details>


### [140] [MedDiME: Efficient Latent Diffusion with Adaptive Masking for Medical Counterfactual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15647)
*Yan Zeng,Changlu Guo,Anders Nymark Christensen,Morten Rieger Hannemose,Anders Bjorholm Dahl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MedDiME，一种潜空间分类器引导扩散框架，实现高效、高分辨率的医学反事实生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的医学反事实生成方法速度慢、内存消耗大，难以应用于高分辨率场景；且掩码策略与像素空间耦合，无法兼容潜空间扩散编辑。

Method: MedDiME在潜空间中进行分类器引导的扩散编辑，并引入与潜空间兼容、由梯度驱动的自适应掩码机制，实现空间精确的反事实生成。

Result: 相比先前的分类器引导扩散基线，MedDiME实现了高质量反事实生成，推理速度最高提升40倍，峰值GPU内存降低最多13倍。

Conclusion: MedDiME在保持生成质量的同时显著提升效率，为高分辨率医学反事实生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Medical counterfactual generation modifies images to change model predictions for interpretability. However, existing diffusion-based approaches are often prohibitively slow and memory-intensive, making them difficult to apply in high-resolution settings. Moreover, existing masking strategies are tightly coupled with pixel-space representations, making them incompatible with latent-space diffusion editing. To address these challenges, we propose MedDiME, a latent-space classifier-guided diffusion framework that reduces computational and memory overhead while introducing a latent-compatible, gradient-driven adaptive masking mechanism for spatially precise medical counterfactual generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MedDiME achieves high-quality counterfactual generation with significant efficiency gains compared to prior classifier-guided diffusion baselines, achieving up to 40 times faster inference and 13 times lower peak GPU memory usage.

</details>


### [141] [From Model Patterns to Abstract Semantics in Compositional Zero-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15649)
*Weize Li,Zhicheng Zhao,Fei Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CLEAR，一个基于完形填空式推理的重排序框架，用于组合零样本学习，通过将原语变化视为上下文驱动的具体视觉线索激活，在C-GQA和MIT-States数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖视觉语言模型，试图通过多种表示显式建模原语的上下文变化，但受限于固定的变体容量以及抽象语义与具体语义之间的竞争问题。

Method: 提出新视角，将原语变化视为上下文驱动下具体视觉线索的激活而非独立实体；据此设计CLEAR框架，从原语候选集中以由粗到细的方式提取条件变体，执行完形填空式推理以推断高层语义，并通过重排序纠正对显著具体原语的偏置。

Result: 大量实验表明，CLEAR持续改进基础模型，并在具有挑战性的C-GQA和MIT-States数据集上优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 将原语变化重新定义为上下文驱动的具体视觉线索激活，并采用完形填空式推理与重排序，可有效缓解抽象与具体语义竞争问题，提升组合零样本学习的识别性能。

Abstract: Compositional Zero Shot Learning aims to recognize unseen compositions by recombining learned primitives. Recent methods rely on vision language models and attempt to explicitly model contextual variations of primitives through multiple representations. However, such approaches are limited by fixed variant capacity and competition between abstract and concrete semantics. In this work, we present a new perspective that views primitive variations as the context-driven activation of concrete visual cues rather than independent entities. Based on it, we propose CLEAR, a CLoze-style rEAsoning-based Re-ranking framework inspired by human perceptual processes. CLEAR extracts conditional variants from the primitive candidate set in a coarse-to-fine manner, performs cloze-style reasoning to infer high-level semantics, and re-ranks predictions to correct biases toward salient concrete primitives. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CLEAR consistently improves the Base Model and outperforms state-of-the-art methods on the challenging C-GQA and MIT-States datasets. Code is available at https://github.com/buptLwz/CLEAR.

</details>


### [142] [Benchmarking Intra-Patient 3D Deformable Multimodal Image Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15669)
*Matteo Barbieri,Giammarco La Barbera,Juan Pablo De La Plata,Sabine Sarnacki,Isabelle Bloch,Pietro Gori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对患者内3D多模态可变形配准方法进行了全面基准评测，覆盖三个数据集，发现学习类方法在合成基准上表现优异，但在真实盆腔配准中改进有限；几何指标与图像相似度指标存在系统性不一致，目前尚无方法能在各种解剖结构与模态间实现稳健性能，该问题仍是开放难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态图像配准是众多临床流程的关键环节，但对应解剖结构在不同模态下图像强度差异显著，配准难度大。现有研究缺乏对经典优化方法与现代学习方法在统一框架下的系统比较，尤其缺少跨不同解剖区域和难度的综合基准评测。因此作者希望构建一个覆盖合成形变恢复与真实临床场景的全面基准，以明确当前方法的实际能力与不足。

Method: 构建了患者内3D多模态可变形配准的综合基准，涵盖三个数据集，涉及不同解剖区域与难度等级，包括合成形变恢复与真实临床场景。评测对象包括经典优化类方法和现代学习类方法（含近期深度学习与基础模型）。采用互补的三类指标进行评估：平均Dice相似系数（DSC）、平均95百分位Hausdorff距离（HD95），以及基于MIND自相似性上下文的模态无关结构相似度度量（MIND-SSC）。

Result: 结果显示各数据集间性能差异较大：学习类方法在大规模合成基准上表现更优，而在真实盆腔配准中提升有限。一个重要发现是几何指标（DSC、HD95）与图像相似度指标（MIND-SSC）之间存在持续的不一致，表明重叠度提升并不必然意味着全局多模态对应关系更好。此外，解剖引导方法获得了最高的重叠分数，但在分割区域之外性能下降，揭示了标签驱动对齐与全局结构一致性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 目前没有任何方法能在各种解剖结构和模态下实现稳健性能。患者内3D多模态配准需要多准则评估，包括基于形变的度量，并且仍是一个未解决的开放问题。

Abstract: Multimodal image registration is a key component of many clinical workflows, yet it remains challenging because corresponding anatomical structures often exhibit substantially different image intensities across modalities. In this work, we present a comprehensive benchmark of intra-patient 3D multimodal deformable registration methods across three datasets covering different anatomical regions and difficulty levels, including both synthetic deformation recovery and real clinical scenarios. We evaluate classical optimization-based approaches and modern learning-based methods, including recent deep learning and foundation models, using complementary metrics: Average Dice similarity coefficient (DSC), average 95th-percentile Hausdorff distance (HD95), and a modality-independent structural similarity measure based on the MIND self-similarity context (MIND-SSC). Results show high variability across datasets, with learning-based methods demonstrating superior performance on large synthetic benchmarks, while only limited improvements are observed in real pelvic registration. A key finding of this study is the consistent disagreement between geometric metrics (DSC, HD95) and image-based similarity metrics (MIND-SSC), highlighting that improved overlap does not necessarily imply better global multimodal correspondence. Furthermore, anatomy-guided approaches achieve the highest overlap scores but exhibit degraded performance outside of segmented regions, revealing a trade-off between label-driven alignment and global structural coherence. Overall, our results indicate that no current method achieves robust performance across anatomies and modalities. We demonstrate that intra-patient 3D multimodal registration requires multi-criteria evaluation, including deformation-based metrics, and remains an open problem.

</details>


### [143] [V-ICAL Bench: Evaluating Video In-Context Learning for Multimodal Agents in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15683)
*Ziqian Fan,Shibo Xu,Junjie Li,Xiangyu Zhao,Shengyuan Ding,Yifan Yang,Zhenjie Yang,Haodong Duan,Yue Zhou,Zhihang Zhong,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 V-ICAL 基准，用人类演示视频评估多模态智能体的视频式上下文学习能力，发现现有模型远低于人类水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态上下文学习（尤其是交互环境中的视频示范）研究不足。多模态智能体从视频学习面临挑战：需将示范转化为可执行策略、在新视觉状态中落地、并依据环境反馈迭代优化动作。现有缺乏系统性评测基准。

Method: 构建 V-ICAL 基准，包含 37 个环境下的 342 个交互任务，以人工精选的演示视频作为任务级行为范例，从目标初始状态出发通过持续交互评估智能体，将上下文知识归纳与状态落地、时序记忆、规划、动态适应等核心能力相衔接。对 19 个先进多模态智能体进行广泛评测，并设置对照实验。

Result: 最佳模型 Seed-2.1-Pro 仅得 54.4/100，其他领先模型（如 Gemini-3.1-Pro、GPT-5.6）均未超过 50，远低于人类基线 83.6。对照实验表明当前智能体难以稳定地将视频范例转化为有效策略，无法带来一致性能提升。

Conclusion: V-ICAL 揭示了多模态智能体在上下文学习能力上的关键差距，凸显未来研究的迫切需求。

Abstract: While In-Context Learning (ICL) enables models to adapt from exemplars without parameter updates, multimodal ICL remains largely underexplored, particularly regarding video demonstrations in interactive environments. For multimodal agents, learning from videos presents unique challenges: they must translate in-context demonstrations into executable policies, ground these policies in novel visual states, and iteratively refine actions based on environmental feedback. We introduce V-ICAL, a novel benchmark designed to evaluate video-based ICL for multimodal agents. Comprising 342 interactive tasks across 37 environments, V-ICAL utilizes human-curated demonstration videos as task-specific behavioral exemplars, evaluating agents through sustained interaction from a target initialization. The benchmark seamlessly connects in-context knowledge induction with core agentic capabilities, including state grounding, temporal memory, planning, and adaptation in dynamic environments. Extensive evaluations across 19 state-of-the-art multimodal agents reveal significant limitations: the best-performing model, Seed-2.1-Pro, achieves a score of only 54.4/100, while other leading models (e.g., Gemini-3.1-Pro, GPT-5.6) fail to surpass 50, far below the human baseline of 83.6. Controlled comparisons further demonstrate that current agents struggle to reliably translate video exemplars into effective policies, failing to yield consistent performance gains. Ultimately, V-ICAL exposes a critical gap in the ICL capabilities of multimodal agents, underscoring an urgent need for future research.

</details>


### [144] [VC-Attention: Value Smoothing and Softmax Casting for Low-bit Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15810)
*Xingyang Li,Dongyun Zou,Shining Zhang,Jiacheng Chen,Haocheng Xi,Lvmin Zhang,Jun-Yan Zhu,Song Han,Zhekai Zhang,Yujun Lin,Muyang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VC-Attention 是一个免训练的低比特注意力框架，通过 Value 平滑（V-Smooth）与融合概率转换（ExpCast-FP8）同时解决精度与速度瓶颈，在多个视频生成模型和 GPU 平台上显著提升保真度与推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散 Transformer 在视频生成中表现优异，但长时空序列使注意力成为主要部署成本，亟需既精确又快速的可部署低比特内核。精度受异常值限制：块量化尺度由最大元素决定，使典型元素被压缩到狭窄的可表示范围；已有工作平滑了 query 和 key，但 value 异常值没有固定的通道或时空结构，是输出误差的主要来源。速度受 softmax 限制：低比特 Tensor Core 只加速两个矩阵乘法，中间的高精度指数运算成为数据中心 GPU 上最长的流水线阶段。

Method: 提出 VC-Attention，包含两个组件：1) V-Smooth，通过轻量在线聚类对 value token 重排序，使同一硬件块内的 token 能一起良好量化；它仅量化减去块均值后的残差，并利用在线 softmax 已维护的行和恢复该均值。2) ExpCast-FP8，用一次融合乘加将 log 域分数直接映射为 E4M3 概率编码，消除 FP32 指数运算与格式转换。该框架在 B200、B300、H200、RTX PRO 6000 和 RTX 5090 上实现。

Result: 在 Wan2.2、LongCat-Video、HunyuanVideo-1.5 和 MiniMax-H3 上，VC-Attention 相比低比特基线提升保真度；注意力内核相比 BF16 FlashAttention-4，在数据中心 Blackwell 和 Hopper 上加速 1.46–1.59 倍，在工作站显卡上加速 2.3–3.6 倍；端到端生成一个片段分别加速 1.13–1.19 倍和 1.36–1.70 倍。

Conclusion: 通过 V-Smooth 处理 value 异常值与 ExpCast-FP8 消除高精度指数瓶颈，VC-Attention 在免训练条件下实现了低比特注意力的精度与速度双赢，并在多种视频生成模型与硬件平台上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers deliver state-of-the-art video generation, but their long spatiotemporal sequences make attention the dominant deployment cost, and a deployable low-bit kernel must be accurate and fast. Accuracy is limited by outliers: a block's quantization scale is set by its largest entries, leaving typical entries confined to a narrow range of representable values. Prior work smooths queries and keys, but value outliers follow no fixed channel or spatiotemporal structure and remain the dominant source of output error. Speed is limited by softmax: low-bit Tensor Cores accelerate only the two matrix multiplications, so the high-precision exponential between them becomes the longest pipeline stage on datacenter GPUs. We propose VC-Attention, a training-free low-bit attention framework that addresses both by pairing Value smoothing with a fused probability Cast. V-Smooth reorders value tokens by lightweight online clustering, so the tokens in a hardware block quantize well together. It quantizes only the residual after subtracting the block mean, and restores that mean from the row sum the online softmax already maintains. ExpCast-FP8 maps log-domain scores directly to E4M3 probability codes with one fused multiply-add, eliminating the FP32 exponential and the format conversion. We implement VC-Attention for B200, B300, H200, RTX PRO 6000, and RTX 5090. Across Wan2.2, LongCat-Video, HunyuanVideo-1.5, and MiniMax-H3, VC-Attention improves fidelity over low-bit baselines, speeds up the attention kernel over BF16 FlashAttention-4 by 1.46-1.59x on datacenter Blackwell and Hopper and by 2.3-3.6x on workstation cards, and generates a clip 1.13-1.19x and 1.36-1.70x faster end to end.

</details>


### [145] [Integrating Multi-view Multi-light Surface Reconstruction into Cultural Heritage Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15833)
*Baptiste Brument,Robin Bruneau,Benjamin Coupry,Vincent Demoulin,Jean Mélou,Antoine Laurent,Fabien Castan,Jean-Denis Durou,Lilian Calvet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将计算机视觉的“多视角-多光照”表面重建方法集成到开源摄影测量框架 Meshroom 中，形成面向文化遗产的实用工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化遗产数字化日益依赖基于图像的三维重建，现有摄影测量软件虽已普及，但通常只处理常规多视角数据，未充分利用多视角-多光照数据（如 RTI 穹顶采集的光照变化图像）在精细表面重建上的潜力；研究难点在于如何把已有采集实践与最新视觉方法衔接，并嵌入实际工作流。

Method: 论文不提出新重建算法，而是组装并暴露已有的先进方法到 Meshroom 中，包括完整的光度立体生态（标定、自标定、通用型）、自动物体掩膜，以及多视角法线与反射率积分。

Result: 构建了一个面向文化遗产的可用的集成系统，充当计算机视觉研究代码与实际文化遗产应用之间的中间软件层，使近期技术更易使用与评估。

Conclusion: 通过将先进的多视角-多光照表面重建组件整合进开源摄影测量框架，为遗产领域提供了可操作、可评估的工作流，弥合研究与实际应用之间的差距。

Abstract: Cultural heritage documentation increasingly relies on image-based 3D surface reconstruction, with photogrammetry software making such workflows accessible to archaeologists, conservators, and heritage technicians. These tools have been successful for conventional multi-view acquisition, but they do not routinely exploit richer multi-view, multi-light data, despite its potential for improving fine-scale surface reconstruction. This limitation is particularly relevant in heritage contexts, where controlled-light acquisition devices such as RTI domes are already used to capture illumination-varying image sets. The challenge is therefore to connect these existing acquisition practices with recent computer vision methods in a form that can be used within operational heritage workflows. In this work, we address this need by integrating state-of-the-art components from computer vision for multi-view, multi-light surface reconstruction into Meshroom, an open-source photogrammetry framework. Rather than proposing a new reconstruction algorithm, our contribution is to assemble and expose existing advanced methods, namely a complete photometric stereo ecosystem (calibrated, self-calibrated and universal), automatic object masking, and multi-view normal-and-reflectance integration, within a usable heritage-oriented workflow. The proposed system thus provides an intermediate software layer between computer vision research code and practical cultural heritage applications, making recent techniques easier to use and evaluate.

</details>


### [146] [TRACE: Two-Stage Detector-Response Estimation With Angular Cosine Expansion for Ring Artifact Correction in Photon-Counting CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15834)
*Jigang Duan,Heran Wang,Ligen Shi,Zheng Sun,Ping Yang,Xing Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRACE是一种两阶段无监督正弦图分解方法，用于估计并校正PCD-CT中探测器响应非均匀性引起的条纹与环状伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子计数探测器CT中探测器响应非均匀性会引入系统性投影误差和环状伪影。实测数据中的残余条纹幅度随投影角度缓慢变化，现有的固定偏置模型无法充分刻画这种动态变化。

Method: TRACE将条纹建模为固定偏置加低阶DCT分量，用少量系数描述每个探测器单元的角向变化；采用可学习的分析—合成架构表示理想投影，通过两阶段优化依次分离固定条纹与动态条纹；并引入角度梯度软正交约束，抑制共享DCT梯度子空间内的相关变化，减少物体结构泄漏到伪影估计中。所有参数直接在实测正弦图上优化，无需配对训练数据。

Result: 在实测的QRM小鼠体模和猪蹄数据上，TRACE有效抑制了环状伪影并提升了图像均匀性，同时保留了边缘锐度、软组织纹理和骨小梁细节。

Conclusion: TRACE通过两阶段无监督分解与软正交约束，实现了对PCD-CT响应相关误差的有效校正，且不依赖配对训练数据，具有良好的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Detector response nonuniformity introduces systematic projection errors and ring artifacts in photon-counting detector computed tomography (PCD-CT). In measured PCD-CT data, residual stripe amplitudes vary slowly with projection angle, which fixed-bias models cannot adequately capture. We propose TRACE, a two-stage unsupervised sinogram decomposition method for estimating and correcting these response-related errors. TRACE represents stripes as a fixed bias plus low-order discrete cosine transform (DCT) components, using a small number of coefficients to describe angular variations at each detector element. A learnable analysis--synthesis architecture represents the ideal projections, while two-stage optimization separates them from fixed and then dynamic stripes. An angular-gradient soft orthogonality constraint suppresses correlated variations within the shared DCT gradient subspace, reducing the leakage of object structures into the artifact estimate. All parameters are optimized directly on the measured sinogram without paired training data. Experiments on measured QRM mouse phantom and porcine trotter data show that TRACE suppresses ring artifacts and improves image uniformity while preserving edge sharpness, soft-tissue texture, and trabecular detail.

</details>


### [147] [LynnReal-Omni: Native multi-modal Video Generation for Agentic Visual Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15863)
*Xiaofeng Mao,Peijia Lin,Shaohao Rui,Yibo Zhang,Haibin Wan,Weijie Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LynnReal-Omni：统一多模态视频生成框架，结合智能体视觉创作实现可控高效生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型随机性强、难以精确控制，长时场景易漂移；智能体创作虽提供稳定控制但保真度不足，二者结合可优势互补。

Method: 基于32B共享多模态扩散Transformer，统一文生视频、图像条件、参考引导、结构控制、编辑、修复与长视频生成；另训27B Flash实时模型；构建数据流水线与MSAVP评测体系；通过模型与解码加速（含轻量VAE解码器）降低推理成本。

Result: 单张H100上，22帧540p视频预热生成与解码：LynnReal-Omni为843ms，Flash为377ms。

Conclusion: 为实时流式视频生成奠定基础，使LynnReal-Omni成为统一、可控、高效的智能体视觉创作基座。

Abstract: Video diffusion models are stochastic and hard to control: precise content often requires repeated sampling without guaranteed success, and long-horizon scenes drift in appearance, interactions, and temporal coherence. Agentic visual creation provides explicit references, editable 3D scenes, or executable game states for stable control, but does not by itself guarantee high object or character fidelity. Combining the two can enable stable, high-quality generation. To realize this combination, we present LynnReal-Omni, a native multimodal video generation framework built on a 32B shared multimodal diffusion transformer that unifies text-to-video, image-conditioned generation, reference-guided generation, structural control, editing, degraded video restoration, and long-video generation. It accepts heterogeneous visual inputs, including appearance references, editable 3D renders, and game recordings, allowing agents to compose visual conditions within a unified model. We also train a dedicated 27B Flash shared multimodal diffusion transformer for real-time rendering. We build a systematic data pipeline for video cleaning, subject association, multimodal annotation, and aligned control construction, yielding a curated corpus of multi-shot audiovisual segments, and introduce MSAVP, a 100-prompt, 20-metric evaluation design that separates instruction following, generating plausibility, visual quality, temporal behavior, and audio coordination. LynnReal-Omni-Flash further reduces inference cost through model and decoding acceleration, including a lightweight VAE decoder; on one H100, warm generation and decoding of a 22-frame 540p video take 843 ms with LynnReal-Omni and 377 ms with Flash. These results provide a foundation for real-time streaming video generation, making LynnReal-Omni a unified, controllable, and efficient basis for agentic visual creation.

</details>


### [148] [Anatomical Grounding and Leakage-Aware Multimodal Contrastive Learning for Alzheimer's Disease Classification from Structural MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15888)
*Paul-Gabriel Nicolae,Irina Georgiana Mocanu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文用轻量级切片编码器研究AD分期中的两个问题：图像模型关注解剖无关区域，以及多模态模型依赖标签泄漏变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络虽准确率高，但常关注解剖无关区域；加入临床表格的多模态模型又常依赖用于分配诊断标签的变量，存在标签泄漏。

Method: 在ADNI-1的1,075例基线T1扫描上，使用ResNet18+单层Transformer的切片编码器。用FastSurfer分割作为解剖参考训练YOLOv8定位；用Grad-CAM对比注意力区域；采用CLIP式图像-表格对比框架，将ADNIMERGE变量按标签泄漏程度排序进行融合；并限制输入为内侧颞叶裁剪。

Result: YOLOv8定位AD相关结构mAP_50>0.96；图像分类器常关注颅骨、眼眶和背景。融合认知评分得87.3%三分类准确率（视为泄漏上限），融合区域体积得73.0%。MCI vs CN中，对齐认知评分时仅52.4%，对齐体积时达73.8%。内侧颞叶裁剪将图像三分类从58.7%提升至65.1%。

Conclusion: 结果来自小规模平衡测试集的单次运行，报告了置信区间及妨碍与已发表数字直接比较的协议差异；强调了标签泄漏与注意力区域问题的重要性。

Abstract: Deep networks trained on structural MRI for Alzheimer's disease (AD) staging often reach reasonable accuracy while attending to anatomically irrelevant regions, and multimodal models that add clinical tables frequently rely on variables that were used to assign the diagnostic label in the first place. We study both issues with a deliberately lightweight slice-based encoder (ResNet18 with a one-layer Transformer over slices) on 1,075 baseline T1-weighted scans from ADNI-1. First, we use FastSurfer segmentations as an anatomical reference: YOLOv8 models trained on segmentation-derived labels localize Alzheimer-relevant structures with mAP_50 above 0.96, and a Grad-CAM comparison shows that the image-only classifier frequently attends to the skull, orbits and background. Second, we adapt a CLIP-style image - tabular contrastive framework and organize ADNIMERGE variables along a label-leakage spectrum. Fusion with cognitive scores yields 87.3% three-way accuracy, which we treat as a leakage-driven upper bound rather than an imaging result; fusion with regional volumes yields 73.0%. We observe that the choice of contrastive target changes what the image encoder learns: on MCI vs. CN, the image-only head reaches 52.4% when the encoder is aligned to cognitive scores and 73.8\% when aligned to volumes, although no tabular input is used at inference. Third, restricting the input to a per-subject crop of the medial temporal lobe raises image-only three-way accuracy from 58.7% to 65.1%. All results come from single runs on a small balanced test set, and we report confidence intervals and the protocol differences that prevent direct comparison with published numbers.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Seeing What the Vehicle Sees: Video-Augmented Virtual Reality for Physical Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13224)
*Md Tanjemul Islam,Mohammad Shafin,Md Rafiul Kabir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种视频增强的VR框架，将物理ROS 2自主机器车与Meta Quest 3S上的Unity 6应用耦合，实现乘客对物理自动驾驶行为的沉浸式观察，并在20次闭环导航试验中验证了其低延迟、高一致性的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶有望提升道路安全与效率，但乘客往往不清楚车辆感知到了什么、为何如此决策。VR可安全、可重复地呈现这些信息，然而现有面向乘客的VR研究多依赖完全仿真车辆或预设脚本场景，所展示的运动与感知并非来自真实运行的自主系统，缺乏真实性。

Method: 构建视频增强的VR框架，将物理ROS 2自主机器车与部署在Meta Quest 3S头显上的Unity 6应用相连接。车辆状态与车载实时摄像头视频流通过两条独立通信通道传输，使虚拟车辆镜像物理机器人的运动；乘客可同时通过车内仪表界面查看第一人称摄像头画面与导航决策。在20次重复闭环导航试验中评估该框架。

Result: 系统实现平均状态更新延迟29.63 ms，物理与虚拟车辆之间的平均相对路径进度误差为2.28%，视频以10.006帧/秒传输且帧丢失率为0.25%。所有被监测的导航决策均正确反映在VR界面中，无遗漏或错误通知。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该框架能够为物理自主车辆行为的沉浸式观察提供时间同步、语义一致且路径进度表示准确的支撑。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles are expected to improve road safety and efficiency, but passengers often remain uncertain about what the vehicle perceives and why it acts as it does. Virtual reality (VR) offers a safe and repeatable medium for presenting this information, yet most passenger-facing VR studies rely on fully simulated vehicles or pre-scripted scenarios, so the motion and perception shown to the user do not originate from a physically operating autonomous system. This paper presents a video-augmented VR framework that couples a physical ROS 2 autonomous robot vehicle to a Unity 6 application deployed on a Meta Quest 3S headset. The vehicle state and live onboard camera stream are transmitted over two independent communication channels, allowing the virtual vehicle to mirror the physical robot's motion while the passenger simultaneously views the vehicle's first-person camera feed and its navigation decisions through an in-vehicle dashboard interface. We evaluate the framework over 20 repeated closed-loop navigation trials. The system achieves a mean state-update latency of 29.63 ms, a mean relative route-progress error of 2.28% between the physical and virtual vehicles, and video delivery at 10.006 frames per second with 0.25% frame loss. All monitored navigation decisions were correctly reflected in the VR interface with no missed or incorrect notifications. The results indicate that the framework can support temporally synchronized, semantically consistent, and accurate route-progress representation for immersive observation of physical autonomous-vehicle behavior.

</details>


### [150] [Learning Manipulation-Sufficient Representations via Outcome Bottlenecks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13235)
*Md Selim Sarowar,Sungho Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向网络化操作端点的随机表示学习框架，通过无策略、动作条件下的结果瓶颈来保留各动作的结果分布，用结果对数损失作失真、KL项作速率正则，在抓取选择、共形过滤、视点选择和测试时自适应等任务中验证，并在仿真抓取实验中大幅优于重建几何方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络化操作端点受计算和带宽限制，传统方法交换的是为保真度优化的稠密几何状态，而非面向动作结果的表示，因此传输效率低且与任务目标脱节。需要一种以动作结果为目标的极简统计表示，在有限带宽下保留所有可行动作的结果分布。

Method: 学习一种随机表示，采用无策略、动作条件下的结果瓶颈：以边际结果对数损失提供失真项，用KL项正则化速率。理论上以保持所有可行动作结果分布的最小统计量为动机，实现上作为速率正则的混合预测器评估。同一编码器与结果头支持抓取选择、单例共形过滤、主动视点选择和潜空间测试时自适应。提出有限探针定理，将局部水平集切空间与结果雅可比零空间对应，并用合成oracle和解析代理验证。

Result: 合成oracle验证了定理；扫描物体上解析代理与仿真不变性误差在0.56°以内。13个物体、11,979次仿真抓取中，重建几何扳手分数对提升成功的AUC为0.542（曲面物体低于随机），本表示达0.876。25%承诺下执行抓取成功率为0.503对0.984。512字节接口比一帧RGB-D小288倍，每次CPU决策16ms。未见物体上场景内AUC降至0.569，全反馈更新将经验平均成对覆盖率从0.728提升至0.883。

Conclusion: 以动作结果为瓶颈的随机表示能以极低带宽（512字节）和低延迟（16ms）支持多种操作任务，在抓取成功预测上显著优于重建几何基线，并提供不确定性与覆盖率的经验诊断；但在未见物体泛化上性能下降，需反馈更新来提升覆盖。

Abstract: Networked manipulation endpoints couple perception to actuation across compute- and bandwidth-limited links, yet commonly exchange dense geometric states optimized for fidelity rather than action outcomes. A stochastic representation is learned with a policy-free, action-conditioned outcome bottleneck: marginal outcome log-loss supplies distortion and a KL term regularizes rate. The construction is motivated by the minimal statistic that preserves the outcome distribution of every admissible action, while the implemented finite model is evaluated as a rate-regularized mixture predictor. The same encoder and outcome head support grasp selection, singleton conformal filtering, active viewpoint selection, and latent test-time adaptation. A finite-probe theorem identifies the local level-set tangent space with the null space of an outcome Jacobian. The synthetic oracle verifies this result; on scanned objects, an analytic surrogate agrees with measured simulator invariances within \(0.56^\circ\). Across 11,979 simulated grasps on 13 objects, a reconstructed-geometry wrench score attains 0.542 AUC against lift success and falls below chance on curved objects, while our representation attains 0.876. At 25\% commitment, executed-grasp success is 0.503 versus 0.984. The 512-byte interface is \(288\times\) smaller than one RGB-D frame and runs at 16\,ms per CPU decision. Independent synthetic points track the tested conformal levels; scanned-object all-pair coverage is reported as a clustered empirical diagnostic. On unseen objects, within-scene AUC falls to 0.569, and a full-feedback update raises empirical mean pairwise coverage from 0.728 to 0.883.

</details>


### [151] [GzDRL: Reproducible and Scalable Deep Reinforcement Learning with Gazebo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13243)
*Amal Dev Haridevan,Junjie Kang,Jinjun Shan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GzDRL：面向Gazebo的单进程强化学习框架，通过免中间件同步机制实现确定性、高吞吐、可复现的机器人RL训练与真机迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于中间件的RL-Gazebo集成存在非确定性与不可复现问题，制约了可扩展、可重复的机器人实验，缺乏高效且可复现的仿真训练平台。

Method: 提出单进程RL框架GzDRL，采用系统化的免中间件环境步进机制，直接同步智能体动作与物理更新，实现确定性高吞吐数据采集、高效向量化及可复现训练与评估。

Result: 基准测试显示其在工作站中吞吐最高，在笔记本上可与GPU加速仿真器竞争，并保持精确的智能体-环境同步、多智能体扩展性与实验级可复现性；策略无需微调即可迁移至真实四旋翼。

Conclusion: GzDRL为机器人与自动化领域的强化学习提供了一个易用且可复现的平台，验证了仿真到现实的直接迁移能力。

Abstract: We present GzDRL, a novel single-process reinforcement learning (RL) framework for Gazebo that overcomes longstanding bottlenecks in scalable, reproducible robotics experimentation. Unlike conventional middleware-based RL-Gazebo integrations that suffer from nondeterminism and irreproducibility, GzDRL introduces a systematic, middleware-free environment-stepping mechanism that directly synchronizes agent actions and physics updates. This design enables deterministic, high-throughput data collection, efficient vectorization, and reproducible RL training and evaluation. Comprehensive benchmarks demonstrate that GzDRL achieves the highest workstation throughput among the evaluated frameworks while remaining competitive with GPU-accelerated simulators on laptop hardware, and maintains precise agent-environment synchronization, multi-agent scalability, and experiment-level reproducibility. We further validate sim-to-real transfer by deploying learned policies directly onto a physical quadrotor, without fine-tuning. Our results establish GzDRL as an accessible and reproducible platform for advancing RL in robotics and automation.

</details>


### [152] [Physical Kernel: Structured Visual Latents for Dark Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13244)
*Jinting Hang,Hong Li,Zhenhui Cai,Zhihao Zhao,Jian He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究"暗操纵"（dark manipulation）：在一次短暂点亮写入编码 z0 后，仅凭策略 pi(z) 与开环动力学 f(z,a) 即可完成接触密集技能，无需后续像素输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证在接触密集操作任务中，视觉信息是否只需在写入阶段短暂采集一次，之后便可在无像素的"暗"状态下依靠隐藏状态与开环动力学完成技能；并考察该范式对遮挡、外观漂移等干扰的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 dark_f 流程：先将 z0=Enc(rgb) 短暂点亮写入，随后由策略 pi(z) 与开环动力学 f(z,a) 在无像素条件下执行。在 ManiSkill StackCube 上以五阶梯链（near_A→grasped→lifted→on_B→stacked）评估，与逐步重新编码 lit_reenc、freeze/encode_black 等对照；并设计共用 Write→HOLD 协议测试遮挡、掩码、动作置乱、写入期外观漂移、写入长度 Tw 与模糊/JPEG 压缩的影响，另训练专用 pi_write 并设置 Dreamer 风格与特权状态 MPC 对照组。

Result: dark_f 在 StackCube（n=160）达 68.1% stacked，远高于逐步骤点亮的 lit_reenc（35.6%）与 freeze/encode_black（0%）。在 Write→HOLD 协议（n=40）下，遮挡与相机对齐的 GT 接触邻域掩码使 lit lift 从 43% 降至 0%，而 dark_f 保持 82.5%；动作置乱使动力学 MSE 上升约 9.4 倍；写入期外观偏移导致编码失效（夜间 0% stacked），但 HOLD 期同样偏移不影响 dark_f 的 lift；Tw 在到达停止阶段后影响平缓，提前停止与写入期模糊/JPEG 会显著降低 stacked。专用 pi_write 达 35% 视觉预算 stacked（n=80），而无特权几何信息的 Dreamer 风格/像素对照均为 0%；特权状态 RSSM MPC 在 9D 暗观测下达约 35% stacked，属更强观测的对照，而非匹配的视觉基线。

Conclusion: 接触密集技能可在一次性编码后于无像素的"暗"状态下完成，且对执行期遮挡与外观漂移具有显著鲁棒性；其关键在于写入阶段的编码质量与停止时机，而非执行期的持续视觉反馈。

Abstract: We study dark manipulation: after a brief lit Write encodes z0 = Enc(rgb), a policy pi(z) and open-loop dynamics f(z,a) complete contact-rich skills without further pixels (dark_f). On ManiSkill StackCube (n=160; seed packs 0/1000), dark_f attains 68.1% stacked on the five-rung chain (near_A -> grasped -> lifted -> on_B -> stacked), compared with 35.6% for per-step lit_reenc and 0% for freeze/encode_black. On a shared Write->HOLD protocol (n=40), occlusion and camera-aligned GT contact-neighbor masks drive lit lift from 43% to 0%, while dark_f holds 82.5%; shuffling actions inflates dynamics MSE by ~9.4x; write-time appearance shifts break encoding (night: 0% stacked), yet the same shifts during HOLD leave dark_f lift unchanged; Write length Tw is flat once the stop phase is reached, while earlier stops and write-time blur/JPEG sharply cut stacked. A dedicated pi_write reaches 35% vision-budget stacked (n=80); matched Dreamer-style/pixel nulls without privileged geom stay at 0%. Privileged state-RSSM MPC reaches ~35% stacked with 9D dark observations -- a stronger-observation null, not a matched visual baseline.

</details>


### [153] [Conflict-Predictive Variable Horizons in Multi-Drone Distributed Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13270)
*Linda Mümken,Michael Schwung,Stefan Lier,Andreas Schwung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出冲突预测可变预测时域策略，用于多无人机分布式MPC避碰，在保证递归可行性与稳定性的同时降低计算量并维持安全间隔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定预测时域存在矛盾：短时域计算便宜但对邻近无人机反应迟缓，长时域虽能提前预判冲突，但每步计算成本随时域长度超线性增长。

Method: 每架无人机本地设定可变时域，分布式MPC本身不变。基于邻居位置短历史外推其飞行线，用随预测范围收窄的置信漏斗检验是否与自身航线冲突，闭式求出各冲突时间；取最小的、其规划窗口能覆盖最远预测冲突的可行时域。空域无冲突时收缩至最小值，仅在前方存在冲突时增大。

Result: 在密集对跖交换基准仿真中，可变时域显著降低每步求解成本与总计算量，优于长固定时域；且每次运行均保持安全间隔，而同等每步成本的短固定时域则无法保持。

Conclusion: 只要最小时域满足一个可计算的可行性界，即可证明策略可选的所有时域都保持递归可行性与渐近稳定性；结论适用于线性模型，并通过级联内环将四旋翼平动动力学化为受扰双积分器，推广至线性化模型及（作为实际稳定性）全非线性模型。

Abstract: In distributed model predictive control for multi-drone collision avoidance, a fixed prediction horizon forces a compromise: a short horizon is inexpensive but reacts late to approaching neighbors, whereas a long one anticipates conflicts at a per-step cost that grows superlinearly with its length. We propose a conflict-predictive variable horizon that each drone sets locally, leaving the distributed model predictive control itself unchanged. From a short history of observed positions, a drone extrapolates the flight lines of its neighbors, tests each against its own using confidence funnels that narrow with prediction range, and obtains each time to conflict in closed form. The horizon is then the smallest admissible value whose planning window covers the farthest predicted conflict. It collapses to its minimum in clear airspace and grows only when a conflict lies ahead. Provided this minimum meets a single computable feasibility bound, we prove that recursive feasibility and asymptotic stability are preserved for every horizon the policy can select. These guarantees hold for a linear model, and a cascaded inner loop reduces each quadrotor's translational dynamics to a perturbed double integrator, so they carry over to the linearized quadrotor model and, as practical stability, to the full nonlinear one. In simulation on dense antipodal-swap benchmarks, the variable horizon reduces both per-step solver cost and total computation well below those of a long fixed horizon, and it maintains separation in every run, which a short fixed horizon of comparable per-step cost does not.

</details>


### [154] [Breaking speed scaling in quadrupedal robots via Huygens' coupled-pendulum dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13290)
*Yucheng Tao,Yongbin Jin,Shaowen Cheng,Xianwei Liu,Yanyan Yuan,Yanhong Liang,Chengkai Su,Chaojie Fu,Guorong Lan,Wei Yang,Hongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过利用肢体间惯性耦合而非单纯提升硬件能力，四足机器人实现10.74 m/s（Froude数21.4）的奔跑速度，成为首个突破10 m/s的腿足机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提升奔跑速度的方法主要依靠控制算法改进和增大执行器容量，但速度极限本质上受快速运动所需的力和力矩约束限制，单纯硬件放大成本高且受限。

Method: 受惠更斯耦合摆启发，提出利用肢体间惯性耦合在步态周期内重新分配能量，降低快速周期性运动所需的峰值关节力矩；并将硬件参数作为额外设计变量，构建协同优化框架，系统性地在机器人设计中利用惯性耦合。

Result: 在该框架指导下，四足机器人达到10.74 m/s的奔跑速度（Froude数21.4），100米冲刺用时12.2秒，为首个速度超过10 m/s的腿足机器人。

Conclusion: 惯性耦合是支配高速腿足运动的潜在机制，能够降低力需求，为敏捷机器人系统设计提供新思路。

Abstract: Achieving biological-level running speeds has largely been pursued through advances in control algorithms, which improve the utilization of existing hardware. However, the ultimate speed limits remain governed by the underlying force and torque requirements of rapid locomotion, which are typically addressed through increased actuator capacity. Inspired by Huygens' coupled pendulums, we demonstrate that superior locomotion can emerge from principled exploitation of intrinsic dynamics rather than brute-force hardware scaling. Inter-limb inertial coupling redistributes energy across the gait cycle and reduces peak joint torque required for rapid periodic motion, thereby expanding the achievable speed without proportional increases in actuator capability. Incorporating hardware parameters as additional design variables further extends this analysis into a co-optimization framework, enabling the systematic utilization of inertial coupling in robot design. Guided by this framework, a quadruped robot achieves a running speed of 10.74 m/s (Froude number 21.4) and completes a 100-meter sprint in 12.2 seconds, representing the first legged robot to surpass 10 m/s. These results establish inertial coupling as an underlying mechanism governing high-speed legged locomotion and highlight its role in reducing force requirements, offering new insights into the design of agile robotic systems.

</details>


### [155] [Constraint-Grounded Reinforcement Learning for Variable Impedance Control in Contact-Rich Robotic Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13516)
*Lin He,Min Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出约束锚定强化学习（CG-RL），实现机器人插入任务中可变阻抗的在线增益自适应，无需人工重调即可适应不同力限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不确定性接触的机器人插入任务中，轴向力限制与合适控制器增益随任务变化，单一固定增益难以适用，传统阻抗控制器依赖人工重调参。

Method: CG-RL 以力限制和接触反馈为条件，策略输出残差运动、插入速率和请求增益；控制器将该增益投影到容许范围内，且不向策略暴露该范围，从而实现单一策略适应不同力限制。

Result: 在仿真斜向插入、5个训练种子下，CG-RL 成功率达 85.8±7.7%，不违反力限制且增益保持在容许范围内；固定中点增益基线成功率仅 50.1%。策略能持续根据力限制调整插入速率，并泛化到训练范围外更宽松的力限制；而无入力限制输入的同一 actor 无此适应性。

Conclusion: CG-RL 可保证增益处于容许范围，力限制满足性经实验验证但未被形式化保证，展示了无需重训或人工重调的自适应阻抗控制潜力。

Abstract: In robotic insertion under uncertain contact, the axial force limit and the appropriate controller gain vary across tasks. As a result, a single fixed gain is unlikely to remain suitable across different task conditions, making conventional impedance controllers reliant on manual retuning. To eliminate manual retuning, we propose Constraint-Grounded Reinforcement Learning (CG-RL), a variable impedance framework for online gain adaptation. Conditioned on the force limit and contact feedback, the policy outputs a residual motion, an insertion rate, and a requested gain. The controller projects this gain into the admissible range without exposing the range itself to the policy. This separation allows a single policy to operate under different force limits without retraining or manual retuning. We evaluate CG-RL on simulated oblique insertion across five training seeds. CG-RL achieves an $85.8\pm7.7\%$ (mean $\pm$ SD) success rate of insertions without violating the force limit, while keeping the applied gain within the admissible range. As a comparison, a fixed-gain baseline using the midpoint gain achieves a success rate of $50.1\%$. The policy adapts its insertion rate continuously to the specified force limit and further generalizes to more permissive force limits above the training range. In contrast, the same actor without force-limit input does not exhibit this adaptation. The applied gain is guaranteed to remain within the admissible range, while force-limit satisfaction is validated empirically rather than guaranteed formally.

</details>


### [156] [From Vision to Harvest: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models for Multi-Arm Robotic Fruit Harvesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13606)
*Vrishan Inukollu,Adyan Zaman,Anvi Kudaraya,Carlos Lazcano,Yuankai Zhu,Stavros Vougioukas,Xiaofan Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）在零样本多臂水果采摘规划任务上的综合基准测试，使用真实苹果和柑橘果园图像。研究发现前沿VLM能够零样本生成有效的多臂采摘计划，但实际部署仍受限于精确的3D路径点生成和碰撞感知协调能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多臂机器人采摘有望提升效率并减少对人工劳动的依赖，但实际部署面临两大挑战：需跨多样环境泛化，并需在共享工作空间中高效协调多臂。现有方法往往需要在目标环境中大量采集数据，或依赖简化假设从而限制规划质量。

Method: 构建首个评估预训练VLM在零样本多臂水果采摘规划上的综合基准，采用真实苹果与柑橘果园图像，将基于VLM的规划流水线与传统"感知-规划"流水线进行对比。VLM流水线直接为每条机械臂生成采摘序列和路径点，并配合轻量级轨迹验证器进行碰撞检测。

Result: 前沿VLM能够零样本生成有效的多臂采摘计划，但实际部署仍受限于精确的3D路径点生成以及碰撞感知的多臂协调能力。

Conclusion: 结果表明预训练VLM在多臂机器人采摘中兼具潜力与当前局限性，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Multi-arm robotic harvesting offers a promising path to improve harvesting efficiency and reduce reliance on manual labor. However, practical deployment remains challenging because the system must generalize across diverse environments while efficiently coordinating multiple arms in a shared workspace. Existing methods often require substantial data collection in target environments or rely on simplifying assumptions that limit planning quality. In this work, we introduce the first comprehensive benchmark for evaluating pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) on zero-shot multi-arm fruit harvesting planning. Our benchmark uses real-world apple and citrus orchard images and compares a VLM-based planning pipeline with a traditional perception-and-planning pipeline. The VLM pipeline directly generates harvesting sequences and waypoints for each arm, while a lightweight trajectory verifier checks for collisions. Our results show that frontier VLMs can generate effective multi-arm harvesting plans zero-shot, but a practical deployment remains limited by accurate 3D waypoint generation and collision-aware coordination. These results highlight both the promise and current limitations of pretrained VLMs for multi-arm robotic harvesting.

</details>


### [157] [Compositional Shift Algebra: Extrapolating Mixed Robot Shifts Without Mixed Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13651)
*Jinting Hang,Zhenhui Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出组合式偏移代数（CSA），通过在模块化栈上学习观测、策略与动力学三类单因子偏移算子并复合，无需混合偏移微调即可外推到未见的混合偏移场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际机器人部署中相机、动作接口与物理动力学往往同时发生变化，现有适配方案要么为每种组合重新微调模型，要么需要选择更新哪个模块，缺乏对多因子联合偏移的高效泛化能力。

Method: 将系统建模为模块化栈 z=E(o)、a=g(z,u)、z'=f(z,a)，用精确重置探针分别拟合单因子观测、策略、动力学偏移算子，再通过算子复合外推未见过的混合偏移，且不做混合偏移微调。

Result: 在 ManiSkill StackCube 上，残差 CSA 在未见混合偏移上达到成功率 1.0（10 个种子），比最佳单因子/零样本/参数平均基线高约 67 个百分点；RGB-D 在环复合接近 oracle；延迟对易子压力测试显示策略×延迟的顺序必要性。PickCube 上复合达 1.0 而非组合方法 0.33（n=10），无特权位姿/抓取标志的 L1 视觉控制器保持 0.95 对 0.00。主赛项升级在 PushCube、PegInsertion、BC 等任务上普遍提升约 33–67 个百分点，而更深 BC、公平适配基线、视觉定位 BC 仍存在明显差距。

Conclusion: 组合式偏移算子为多因子联合偏移的机器人适配提供了无需混合微调的高效路径，在多个任务上显著优于非组合与单因子基线，但更复杂策略与公平适配基线仍需针对性复合改进。

Abstract: Robot deployments rarely change one mechanism at a time: cameras, action interfaces, and physical dynamics often shift together. Prior adaptation recipes either finetune a new model for every mix or attempt to select which module to update. We instead learn shift operators on a modular stack z{=}E(o), a{=}g(z,u), z'{=}f(z,a) and compose them. Compositional Shift Algebra (CSA) fits single-factor observation, policy, and dynamics operators from exact-reset probes, then extrapolates held-out mixed shifts by operator composition---without mixed-shift finetuning. On ManiSkill StackCube, residual CSA matches an oracle mixed inverse on held-out mixes (success 1.0 over 10 seeds) while beating best-single / zero-shot / parameter-average baselines by { approx}67 pp. RGB-D vision-in-the-loop composition remains near oracle and far above non-compositional arms; a delay commutator stress shows ordered necessity for policy timesdelay. On a second task (PickCube), residual CSA again reaches compose 1.0 vs. 0.33 non-compositional (n{=}10), and an L1 vision controller without privileged cube/goal poses or grasp flags in the control loop retains compose 0.95 vs. 0.00. Main-track upgrades freeze PushCube (+33 pp), PegInsertion joint8 / pose7 EE (+67 pp each), and thin BC under frozen CSA (+67 pp); deeper BC and fair adapt baselines still need compose (+67 pp each), vision-localized BC needs compose (+56 pp), and delay favors ordered/few-shot deploy. We report Intervention-Gated Adaptation as a negative control.

</details>


### [158] [Does Online Gravity Estimation Matter? Revisiting a Silent Design Split in LiDAR-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13675)
*Jie Xu,Ziyi Jin,Kangjin Yu,Can Jiang,Hongjun Huang,Tongxing Jin,Hongkun Luo,Zhongpu Xia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在FAST-LIO2与LIO-SAM中比较四种重力-偏置状态配置及重力方向因子，结果显示固定重力在连续LiDAR修正下精度变化在±2%内，但在多秒LiDAR中断和动态启动时存在轨迹相关的代价，故建议保持重力与加速度计偏置在线估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LIO系统在初始化后是否继续估计重力存在分歧，需要系统评估不同重力-偏置状态配置对精度与鲁棒性的实际影响，明确在线重力估计的收益与代价。

Method: 在FAST-LIO2和LIO-SAM中各自比较四种重力-偏置状态配置，并单独测试重力方向因子；在12个数据集序列上用FAST-LIO2评估，在4个序列上用LIO-SAM验证；进一步设计多秒LiDAR中断、历史匹配的23D到21D切换、5秒中断以及动态启动等实验。

Result: 在连续LiDAR修正下，固定重力导致垂直与3D位置误差的平均配对变化90%置信区间在±2%以内；LIO-SAM上也未见在线重力的持续收益。多秒LiDAR中断（不同于视场或量程缩减）暴露出固定重力的轨迹相关代价；23D-to-21D切换使3D误差增加出现在LiDAR更新恢复之后。5秒中断下方向因子在Hall05上改善精度但在TUHH在线重力时使两项误差变差，动态启动测试也显示固定偏置在特定起始相位失败。

Conclusion: 建议为提升鲁棒性保持重力与加速度计偏置在线估计；仅在确认目标工况下垂直与3D精度均有提升后再使用方向因子。

Abstract: LiDAR-inertial odometry (LIO) systems differ in whether they continue estimating gravity after initialization. We compare four gravity-bias state configurations in each of FAST-LIO2 and LIO-SAM, then separately test a gravity-direction factor. Across 12 dataset sequences evaluated with FAST-LIO2, fixing gravity under continuous LiDAR correction produces mean paired changes in vertical and 3D position errors with 90% confidence intervals within $\pm 2\%$. Tests on 4 sequences with LIO-SAM likewise show no consistent benefit from online gravity. Multi-second LiDAR outages, unlike reduced range or field of view, reveal trajectory-dependent costs of fixing gravity. A history-matched 23D-to-21D switch places the repeatable 3D error increase after LiDAR updates resume. Under 5-s outages, a direction factor from the same IMU used for preintegration improves accuracy on Hall05 but worsens both errors with online gravity on TUHH. Dynamic-start tests also show fixed-bias failures at particular starting phases. We recommend keeping gravity and accelerometer bias online for robustness; use a direction factor only after verifying vertical and 3D accuracy gains under the intended operating conditions.

</details>


### [159] [How to Better Train VLAs: Lessons Learned From the REAL-I Challenge at ICRA 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13679)
*Jiaming Wang,Jizhuo Chen,Diwen Liu,Wang Song,Qiang Wang,Jie Ren,Chao Fu,Dingkun Zhu,Minchi Ruan,Hongtong Li,Yuhua Jiang,Zhiwei Xue,Yongping Pan,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍ICRA 2026首届REAL-I挑战赛，围绕固定示范预算下的机器人策略学习，通过仿真、真机与现场决赛比较三支队伍的技术方案，总结数据整理、适配、评估与部署的关键经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在示范数据预算固定的条件下，机器人策略如何更有效地学习，并推动数据、适配、评估与部署一体化视角的形成。

Method: 依托共享双臂人形平台，设置仿真、真机评估和现场决赛；描述任务、数据与部署接口，并对比NUS-CLEAR、RCL-Lab和Deeptouch.ai的方案，这些方案结合预训练视觉-语言-动作模型与任务专用模仿策略，在数据整理、分阶段适配、检查点选择和动作空间设计上各有策略。

Result: 团队报告表明：需兼顾部署环境适配与保留先验能力，在合适时间尺度上处理示范质量，并抑制非活动机器人部件的误差；同时揭示离线动作预测指标在预测闭环成功率方面的局限。

Conclusion: 这些观察支持将固定数据机器人学习视为数据、适配、评估与部署相整合的问题框架。

Abstract: How can robot policies learn more effectively from a fixed demonstration budget? The first Real-world Embodied AI Learning (REAL-I) Challenge at ICRA 2026 examined this question through simulation, real-robot evaluation, and an on-site final on a shared dual-arm humanoid platform. We describe the challenge tasks, data and deployment interfaces, and competition results, then compare the approaches contributed by NUS-CLEAR, RCL-Lab, and Deeptouch.ai. Their systems combined pretrained vision-language-action models and task-specific imitation policies with different strategies for data curation, staged adaptation, checkpoint selection, and action-space design. The team reports highlight the importance of adapting to the deployment environment while retaining prior capabilities, treating demonstration quality at an appropriate temporal scale, and suppressing errors in inactive robot components. They also expose the limitations of offline action-prediction metrics for forecasting closed-loop success. These observations motivate a view of fixed-data robot learning that integrates data, adaptation, evaluation, and deployment.

</details>


### [160] [GROOVE: Geometry-Guided Reduction of Operational-Space Jerk in VLA Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13695)
*Sangho Yun,Minsoo Kim,Minwoo Cho,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GROOVE是一种在线调节器，通过搜索方向修正区域来降低分块VLA策略中的抖动，无需重训练或额外VLA推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分块VLA策略每次查询执行多条命令，但块内及重规划边界处的抖动会导致振荡运动和剧烈的执行器瞬态，影响平滑性与硬件寿命。

Method: 以已交付命令为边界条件优化新块；使用二次规划生成一个立方体参考和十三个方向候选；在参考相对偏差上限约束下选择命令空间抖动最低的候选，同时限制累计平移与局部轴角偏差。

Result: 在留出的LIBERO基准上，平移与旋转EEF抖动分别降低33.02%和43.42%，任务成功率95.75%（原始执行为93.75%）；在50对匹配UR5e实验中，TCP平移与旋转抖动分别降低16.39%和19.49%，关节电流变化率降低29.09%。

Conclusion: GROOVE能在不重训练、不额外推理的前提下有效抑制抖动，并在提升平滑性的同时保持甚至提高任务成功率。

Abstract: Chunked vision language action (VLA) policies execute several commands per query, but jerk within chunks and across replanning boundaries can induce oscillatory motion and sharp actuator transients. We present GROOVE, an online regulator that searches directional correction regions around the raw three dimensional end effector (EEF) path, without retraining or additional VLA inference. It optimizes the new chunk using delivered commands as boundary conditions, reducing boundary and within chunk jerk while bounding cumulative translation and local axis angle deviation from the raw plan after every command. Using quadratic programs (QPs), GROOVE generates a cube reference and thirteen directional candidates, then selects the one with the lowest command space jerk under a reference relative deviation cap. On a held out LIBERO benchmark, GROOVE achieves the largest reductions among the evaluated methods, reducing translational and rotational EEF jerk by 33.02% and 43.42%, respectively, with task success of 95.75% versus 93.75% for raw execution.
