Across 50 matched UR5e pairs with measured execution timing, it reduces translational and rotational tool center point (TCP) jerk by 16.39% and 19.49% and joint current slew by 29.09%.

</details>


### [161] [Decentralized Multi-Robot Task Allocation Under Degraded Communication: A Benchmark of Performance, Reliability, and Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13711)
*James Lott,Vahraz Honary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对六种去中心化多机器人任务分配方法在协作访问场景下进行了系统基准测试，揭示了它们在路径性能、通信鲁棒性、可靠性、计算负担和规模敏感性之间的权衡差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为嵌入式自主平台选择去中心化多机器人任务分配方法时，仅考虑路径性能是不够的，还需综合评估通信鲁棒性、分配可靠性、计算负担和规模敏感性等多项指标。

Method: 在协作访问场景下对CBAA、ACBBA、PI、HIPC、DMCHBA和DGA六种分配器进行基准测试。核心研究使用500个配对十目标实例，覆盖25种理想与退化通信条件（含伯努利丢包、Gilbert-Elliott丢失和瑞利衰落），并额外考察预分配、执行集成计算以及对网格大小、机器人密度和目标负载的敏感性。

Result: 在24种受损核心条件下，DGA和DMCHBA取得最低平均MinMax行程（24.49和24.78步）；HIPC以66.95步微弱领先MinSum行程，DGA以67.22步紧随，二者在每种受损条件下均居前二。DMCHBA发布强度最低（每团队步2.08次发布）。十目标预分配中，HIPC和DMCHBA在所有条件下稳定可行，而ACBBA、PI和DGA随通信退化失去稳定性或可行性。理想投递下，中位数全协议计算时间从DMCHBA的4.88 ms到DGA的1.346 s不等。静态路径质量保持DGA和DMCHBA为领先的MinMax方法，DGA在四个目标负载中的三个领先MinSum，HIPC在50目标时领先。随着任务负载增加，静态与执行集成计算的排名出现分化。

Conclusion: 研究结果识别出各分配器在路径目标、通信行为、可靠性和计算约束方面的不同适用区域，为嵌入式部署选择提供依据。

Abstract: Selecting a decentralized Multi-Robot Task Allocation (MRTA) method for embedded deployment on autonomous platforms requires considering more than route performance alone. We benchmark six decentralized MRTA allocators (CBAA, ACBBA, PI, HIPC, DMCHBA, and DGA) in the Collaborative Visit (CV) scenario to characterize tradeoffs among MinMax and MinSum travel, communication robustness and demand, allocation reliability, computational burden, and scale sensitivity. The core study uses 500 paired ten-target instances across 25 ideal and degraded communication conditions spanning Bernoulli loss, Gilbert--Elliott loss, and Rayleigh fading, with additional campaigns examining pre-allocation, execution-integrated computation, and sensitivity to grid size, robot density, and target load. Across the 24 impaired core conditions, DGA and DMCHBA achieved the lowest mean MinMax travel at 24.49 and 24.78 steps, respectively. HIPC narrowly led mean MinSum travel at 66.95 steps, followed by DGA at 67.22, with both methods occupying the top two in every impaired condition. DMCHBA had the lowest publication intensity at 2.08 publications per team step. In ten-target pre-allocation, HIPC and DMCHBA remained viable and stable in every tested condition, while ACBBA, PI, and DGA lost stability or viability as communication degraded. Under ideal delivery, median full-protocol computation $\Cterm$ in the primary ten-target comparison ranged from 4.88 ms for DMCHBA to 1.346 s for DGA. Static route quality preserved DGA and DMCHBA as the leading MinMax methods, while DGA led MinSum at three of four target loads and HIPC led at 50 targets. Static and execution-integrated computation rankings diverged as task load increased. The results identify distinct allocator operating regions across route objective, communication behavior, reliability, and computational constraints.

</details>


### [162] [Learning In-Hand Object Reaching to General 6D Poses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13761)
*Junxiao Lin,Tianyue Wu,Jie Yin,Jia Pan,Kaifeng Zhang,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: POISE是一个面向多指灵巧手的仿真到真实强化学习框架，实现抓取状态下的6D物体位姿到达（位置+姿态联合控制）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式手内操作方法多聚焦于重定向、连续旋转或平移，而许多任务需要同时控制物体位置与姿态；且需避免反复松开重抓以提升效率，因此将能力形式化为手内6D位姿到达问题。

Method: POISE（Palm-relative Object reaching In SE(3)）结合了多样化稳定抓取初始化、目标与几何条件化控制、自适应6D目标课程，以及紧凑的位姿到达与抓取保持奖励设计。

Result: 仿真中，多样化初始化将未见抓取成功率从40.1%提升至51.5%，掉落恢复从33.8%提升至72.9%；课程学习将全范围成功率从6.2%提升至59.5%。硬件上抓取保持奖励将三目标序列成功率从20%提升至80%；真实实验中可连续到达6D目标、跨多种几何与腕部朝向、无需手动重置，并能从外部干扰中恢复。

Conclusion: POISE验证了仿真到真实的6D手内位姿到达可行性，代码将开源以支持后续灵巧操作研究。

Abstract: In-hand manipulation allows multi-fingered dexterous hands to reconfigure grasped objects without releasing and regrasping them. This improves manipulation efficiency by reducing repeated grasp acquisition and large arm motions. However, most learning-based methods focus on reorientation, continuous rotation, or translation, whereas many tasks require joint control of object position and orientation. We formulate this capability as in-hand 6D object pose reaching: starting from an existing grasp, coordinated finger motions move the object to a palm-relative target pose. We present POISE (Palm-relative Object reaching In SE(3)), a sim-to-real reinforcement learning framework for this task. POISE combines diverse stable-grasp initialization, goal- and geometry-conditioned control, an adaptive 6D goal curriculum, and a compact reward scheme for pose reaching and grasp preservation. In simulation, diverse initialization raises held-out-grasp success from 40.1% to 51.5% and post-drop recovery from 33.8% to 72.9%; the curriculum raises full-range success from 6.2% to 59.5%. On hardware, the grasp-maintenance reward improves three-target sequence success from 20% to 80%. In real-world experiments, POISE reaches successive 6D targets without manual reset across multiple object geometries and wrist orientations, and recovers from external disturbances. To support further research in dexterous manipulation, we will release our code at https://junxiaolin.github.io/poise-website/.

</details>


### [163] [Force-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Hybrid Sensorless Force Estimation for Wheeled-Legged Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13779)
*Xuanqi Zeng,Jiaming Wang,Tianlin Zhang,Lingwei Zhang,Botian Xu,Weipeng Xia,Zhongyu Li,Yun-Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向轮腿式移动操作机器人的力感知强化学习方法，通过混合无传感器力估计，在无需末端力/力矩传感器的情况下实现全身协调的力控操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浮基动力学与支撑接触变化使得全身协调运动与机械臂力控极具挑战，尤其当末端缺乏力/力矩传感用于控制时更为困难，因此需要一种无传感器的力感知方案。

Method: 将末端力估计作为显式策略观测，结合广义动量观测、接触约束力旋量投影与时间残差学习：基于模型的部件提取全身扰动的物理结构化部分，残差网络补偿运动相关偏差；并将估计力集成到模式条件全身策略中，配备轴向力/位置选择器，实现自由运动、纯力调节与混合力位控制。

Result: 仿真结果表明无传感器力估计与力控性能得到提升；硬件实验在轮腿平台上通过阀旋转与混合擦拭的定量评估，以及力引导开门与零力人导运动进一步验证了控制器有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法无需末端力/力矩传感器即可实现力感知的全身协调操作，在仿真与真实轮腿平台上均验证了力估计与力控的有效性。

Abstract: Force-controlled loco-manipulation requires a whole-body policy to coordinate locomotion and arm motion while regulating end-effector interaction forces. This is challenging under floating-base dynamics and changing support contacts, particularly when end-effector force/torque sensing is unavailable for control. This paper presents a force-aware reinforcement learning approach with hybrid sensorless force estimation for wheeled-legged loco-manipulation. The proposed method provides a structured estimate of the end-effector force as an explicit policy observation, enabling force-guided contact behavior without using an end-effector force/torque sensor for control. The force estimate is obtained by combining generalized momentum observation, contact-constrained wrench projection, and temporal residual learning: the model-based components extract the physically structured part of the whole-body disturbance, while the residual network compensates the remaining motion-dependent bias. The estimated force is integrated into a mode-conditioned whole-body policy with an axis-wise force/position selector, allowing free-space motion, pure force regulation, and hybrid force/position control within one controller. Simulation results demonstrate improved sensorless force estimation and force-control performance. Hardware experiments further validate the proposed controller through quantitative valve-rotation and hybrid wiping evaluations, together with force-guided door opening and zero-force human-guided motion on a real wheeled-legged platform.

</details>


### [164] [LePlanner: An Iterative Amortized Controller For World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13845)
*Saksham Bansal,Om Naphade,Chayan Aggarwal,Vrishin M*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LePlanner 是一种摊销式迭代控制器，在冻结的世界模型预测器上学习构建与精炼潜在动作序列，用远少于搜索规划器的预测器评估和更低时延，达到或超越 CEM/MPPI/iCEM 等方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）训练的世界模型中进行规划通常面临两难：基于搜索的规划器（CEM、MPPI、iCEM）通过大量预测器 rollout 优化动作序列，性能强但每次决策计算与延迟高昂；基于策略的方法把推理摊销为单次前向传播，却在接触丰富、演示分布多模态的任务上性能退化。因此需要一个既能摊销推理又保持搜索式规划质量的方法。

Method: LePlanner 是一种摊销式迭代控制器，通过冻结的世界模型预测器学习构造并精炼潜在动作序列。训练采用“到达并保持”（arrival-and-hold）目标，鼓励控制器在最早可行的时间步到达目标并保持，从而缓解 horizon-reset procrastination——即重复滚动时域重规划不断推迟到达目标的失败模式。此外引入 action-Gaussian 损失，使生成动作保持在离线数据集支撑集附近。

Result: 在导航、接触丰富操作与连续控制环境中，LePlanner 匹配或超越基于搜索的规划器，同时所需预测器评估减少一个数量级，每次决策墙钟时间降低 3–49 倍。成功率：PushT 98%、Reacher 100%、TwoRooms 100%、OGBench Cube 92%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在线搜索中发现的大量结构可以被摊销进一个轻量级迭代策略，从而实现快速、具备时域感知、非线性的物理控制，无需在线优化。

Abstract: World models trained with joint-embedding predictive architectures learn compact, structured latent representations from physical interaction, yet planning in these latent spaces typically relies on one of two costly approaches. Search-based planners such as CEM, MPPI, and iCEM optimize action sequences through many predictor rollouts, achieving strong performance at the cost of high per-decision compute and latency. Policy-based methods amortize inference into a single forward pass but can degrade on contact-rich tasks where the demonstration distribution is multimodal. We propose LePlanner, an amortized iterative controller that learns to construct and refine latent action sequences through a frozen world-model predictor. LePlanner is trained with an arrival-and-hold objective that encourages the controller to reach the goal at the earliest feasible horizon and remain there. This addresses horizon-reset procrastination, a failure mode in which repeated receding-horizon replanning continually postpones goal arrival. An additional action-Gaussian loss keeps generated actions near the support of the offline dataset. Across navigation, contact-rich manipulation, and continuous-control environments, LePlanner matches or exceeds search-based planners while requiring an order of magnitude fewer predictor evaluations and 3-49x lower wall-clock time per decision. It achieves success rates of 98% on PushT, 100% on Reacher, 100% on TwoRooms, and 92% on the OGBench Cube task. These results show that much of the structure discovered through online search can be amortized into a lightweight iterative policy, enabling fast, horizon-aware, nonlinear physical control without online optimization.

</details>


### [165] [What Makes an Efficient VLA? Navigating Action-Head Design, Scaling, and Latency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13984)
*Luoyang Sun,Guoyang Xia,Fengfa Li,Lei Ren,Xinyu Cui,Haifeng Zhang,Fangxiang Feng,Kaike Zhang,Kun Zhan,Yan Xie,Jun Wang,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在固定骨干与训练流程的条件下，动作头的性能主要由初始化（复用语言骨干最后几层）决定，而非解码器结构、损失或推理预算；该对齐使缩放动作头收益最高，但收益在现有π系列VLA规模附近迅速递减，据此提出紧凑模型EffVLA并验证其跨仿真与真机迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型由预训练视觉编码器、语言骨干与动作头组合而成，但三部分在受控且延迟配对条件下的相对贡献尚未明确，缺少对动作头设计与规模的系统拆解。

Method: 固定骨干家族（SigLIP2与Qwen2.5）和训练流程，扫描动作头设计与模块规模，并将每种配置与实测端侧延迟配对，进行受控对比实验。

Result: 发现三点：(1) 动作头性能主要由初始化决定，将语言骨干最后若干Transformer层复制到动作头是最大且零延迟代价的杠杆，且在各规模下均有效；flow matching与更重的解码器仅在未对齐时有收益、对齐后反转，额外推理次数无可见收益；(2) 对齐后容量才起作用，对齐的动作头是缩放收益最高的模块；(3) 收益在当今π系列VLA所用规模附近急剧递减。

Conclusion: 提出EffVLA：在标准LIBERO上匹配最强开源VLA，在LIBERO-Plus多数扰动维度领先且延迟更低，并以不变配方迁移到真实SO-ARM101机械臂；作者强调该结论是组织测量结果的最佳解释而非已证因果，并给出可验证因果的控制实验。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models combine a pretrained vision encoder, a language backbone, and an action head, but their relative contribution has not been established under controlled, latency-paired conditions. We fix the backbone families (SigLIP2 and Qwen2.5) and the training pipeline, sweep action-head design and module scale, and pair each configuration with measured on-device latency. The study yields three findings. First, action-head performance is governed primarily by initialization rather than decoder architecture, loss, or inference budget: copying the last transformer layers of the language backbone into the head is the single largest lever, at no latency cost, and the only axis that helps at every module scale. Alignment also explains the other axes: flow matching and a heavier decoder pay off only while the head is misaligned and reverse once it is aligned, and extra inference passes give no measurable benefit; expressiveness appears to substitute for missing alignment. We read this as representation transfer: the aligned head keeps attending to the instruction's object nouns and stays close to the backbone in weight space rather than relearning to act from scratch. Because we reach alignment only through initialization, we offer this as the account that best organizes the measurements, not a demonstrated cause, and name the control that would settle it. Second, capacity pays only after alignment: the aligned action head is the highest-return module to scale. Third, those returns diminish sharply near the size today's $π$-series VLAs already use, so further growth buys little in-domain accuracy for its latency. These specify EffVLA, a compact model matching the strongest open-source VLAs on standard LIBERO, leading on most LIBERO-Plus perturbation axes at lower latency, and transferring to a real SO-ARM101 arm with the recipe unchanged.

</details>


### [166] [Vision-Force Admittance Learning for Peg Insertion into a Movable Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14133)
*Yuzhong Chen,Yongqing Liang,Yunzhi Xu,Irving Fang,Chase Kidder,Hui-ping Wang,Raihan Haque,Yubiao Zhang,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉-力导纳学习框架VFAL，融合异步视觉反馈与高频力模型，实现动态环境下毫米级精密插孔操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境（移动机器人基座或未知运动目标）中的精密操作是机器人领域重大挑战。动态性带来大量不确定性，与插孔等精密任务的严格精度要求存在根本冲突。

Method: 提出VFAL框架，将异步视觉反馈与高频力基模型融合，以视觉位姿估计作为正则项；采用先进视觉基础模型获取鲁棒低频位姿信息；引入失败恢复机制，在线自适应调整插入策略。

Result: 真实世界实验表明，该方法成功率高，对不同插销和动态环境具有强适应性，并保持毫米级精度。

Conclusion: VFAL通过视觉-力融合与在线自适应，有效解决动态环境下精密操作的精度与鲁棒性难题。

Abstract: Precise manipulation in dynamic environments, whether induced by a mobile robot base or a target with unknown motion, remains a major challenge in robotics. Manipulation in dynamic environments introduces substantial uncertainty, which fundamentally conflicts with the tight precision requirement of precise tasks such as peg-in-the-hole. We propose a Vision-Force Admittance Learning (VFAL) framework that fuses asynchronous visual feedback with a high-frequency force-based model, using visual pose estimations as a regularization term. VFAL adapts insertion strategies online to dynamic motion while maintaining millimeter-level precision. To obtain robust, low-frequency pose information, we employ state-of-the-art vision foundation models for visual pose estimation. Additionally, we incorporate failure recovery mechanisms to enhance overall robustness. We validate our approach in real-world experiments, demonstrating high success rates and strong adaptability to various pegs and dynamic environments.

</details>


### [167] [When Faster VLA Deployment Changes Closed-Loop Behavior: Task Success-Latency Analysis of SmolVLA Across PyTorch and ONNX Variants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14146)
*Rafiqul Islam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在 RTX 2060 (6GB) 上评估 VLA 模型 HuggingFaceVLA/smolvla_libero 的部署性能，对比 PyTorch+AMP 与 ONNX Runtime CUDA EP，并揭示了 ONNX 伪 FP16/INT8 问题及语言上下文宽度对任务成功率的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型部署可降低推理延迟，但会改变闭环任务行为。作者希望系统评估部署方案（PyTorch+AMP vs ONNX Runtime CUDA EP）在延迟与任务成功率上的权衡，并探究延迟下降背后的真实原因。

Method: 在 RTX 2060、LIBERO Spatial 与 Object 任务（MuJoCo 3.3.2、LeRobot 0.6.1、seed 42）上评测。主实验 100 episodes/suite，配对 rollout 300 episodes/suite。对比 PyTorch+AMP 与 ONNX Runtime CUDA EP（请求 FP16、INT8），并进行图审计（graph audit）与静态语言宽度消融（16/24/32 tokens）。

Result: PyTorch+AMP 在 Spatial/Object 达 70.0%/88.0%，p99 为 1181 ms；请求 FP16 与 INT8 的 ONNX 将 p99 降至 601 ms 和 532 ms，但 Spatial 成功率降至 41.0% 和 40.0%，Object 仍为 89.0%。图审计显示 FP16/INT8 产物与 FP32 图字节完全一致，即并非真正的算子级量化。语言宽度消融显示 16/24/32 tokens 对应 Spatial 成功率为 41.0%/75.0%/71.0%；宽度 24 的 ONNX 在约一半延迟下 Spatial 成功率与 PyTorch+AMP 基线相当（Wilson 区间重叠，两比例卡方检验 p=0.53）。

Conclusion: 上下文宽度是该部署栈中影响成功率的重要因素，但并不能解释 PyTorch 与 ONNX 之间的全部差异。部署评估应同时报告延迟、产物审计、接口约束和闭环成功率。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) deployment can reduce inference latency while changing closed-loop task behavior. We evaluate HuggingFaceVLA/smolvla_libero on an RTX 2060 (6 GB) in LIBERO Spatial and Object (MuJoCo 3.3.2, LeRobot 0.6.1, seed 42), comparing PyTorch+AMP with ONNX Runtime CUDA Execution Provider (CUDA EP). The main evaluation uses 100 episodes/suite; a paired rollout uses 300 episodes/suite. PyTorch+AMP reaches 70.0%/88.0% Spatial/Object success at 1181 ms p99. Requested-FP16 and requested-INT8 ONNX reduce tether-inspect p99 to 601 ms and 532 ms, while Spatial success falls to 41.0% and 40.0% and Object remains at 89.0%. A graph audit shows those artifacts are byte-identical FP32 graphs, so the requested-INT8 row is not operator-level INT8 quantization. A static language-width ablation (16/24/32 tokens) yields Spatial success of 41.0%, 75.0%, and 71.0%; widths 24 and 32 recover much of the Spatial drop while Object success and uniform-bench latency stay approximately stable. Width-24 ONNX Spatial success is comparable to the PyTorch+AMP baseline at roughly half the latency (Wilson intervals overlap; two-proportion chi-squared p=0.53). Context width is an important contributor in this stack; it does not account for every PyTorch-vs-ONNX difference. Deployment evaluation should jointly report latency, artifact inspection, interface constraints, and closed-loop success. Code: https://github.com/rafiqul713/smolvla-libero-onnx.

</details>


### [168] [GIFT: Glove-Inferred Force Transfer: Force-Aware Human-to-Robot Skill Transfer from a Wearable Sensing Glove to a Robot Hand Without Tactile Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14173)
*Tzah Sarusi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GIFT提出一种跨硬件的人到机器人技能迁移流程：人手端用可穿戴手套以牛顿为单位测量指尖力，机器人端通过执行器电流残差估计力，无需共享触觉传感器，实现从五指手套通道到七自由度机器人手的重定向与力传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人到机器人技能迁移依赖共享硬件，如演示者与机器人佩戴同一触觉手套，或需要学习两个触觉传感器之间的对齐关系，限制了泛化性与部署便利性。本文希望将人机接口从"共享传感器"转变为"共享物理单位（牛顿）"，使任意能报告电机电流的位置控制机器人手都能部署。

Method: GIFT流程：演示时无机器人，可穿戴手套记录手指屈曲、标定后的指尖力与腕部朝向，头戴相机记录演示。部署时机器人由执行器电流残差（相对自由空间基线）经标定映射估计牛顿力。策略使用手套空间状态并预测手指位置目标，机器人仅通过两个标定适配器接入：重定向解码器与力估计器。任务为杯子抓取保持，训练两个动作分块策略，一个保留指尖力输入，另一个将该力置零作对照，并进行观测消融实验。

Result: 在预设样本量与指标的50次评估中，两个策略的25次试验均全部成功。使用力输入时保持阶段握力的逐次估计中位数降低53%：1.20 N对2.55 N（单侧Mann-Whitney U检验，p<0.0001）。观测消融中，仅视觉策略0/15成功抓取；给予手部指令状态的策略能完成抓取，而力输入决定握紧程度。

Conclusion: 在人手上测得的一个力通道，能够通过五指手套通道到七自由度机器人执行器的重定向映射，迁移到没有任何触觉硬件的机器人手上，两个系统之间不共享任何传感器。

Abstract: Human-to-robot skill transfer from sensing gloves has so far relied on shared hardware: the same tactile glove worn by the demonstrator and the robot, or a learned alignment between two tactile sensors. We present GIFT (Glove-Inferred Force Transfer), a pipeline in which the interface between human and robot is a physical unit rather than a shared sensor: fingertip force is measured in newtons on the human side and estimated in newtons on the robot side. A wearable glove records finger flexion, calibrated fingertip force, and wrist orientation, while a head-mounted camera records the demonstration; no robot is present. At deployment, the robot estimates force from actuator-current residuals relative to a free-space baseline, through a calibrated mapping to newtons, so any position-controlled hand that reports motor current can serve as the deployment platform. The policy uses a glove-space state and predicts finger-position targets; the robot enters only through two calibrated adapters, a retargeting decoder and a force estimator. We evaluate GIFT on a cup grasp-and-hold task with two action-chunking policies trained on the same demonstrations, with fingertip-force inputs retained in one and zeroed in the other. In a 50-rollout evaluation with sample size and metrics fixed before scoring, both policies succeeded in all 25 rollouts. The median of the per-rollout hold-phase grip-force estimates was 53% lower with force inputs: 1.20 N versus 2.55 N (one-sided Mann-Whitney U, p<0.0001). In an observation ablation, a vision-only policy achieved 0/15 grasps, policies given hand-command state acquired the grasp, and the force inputs determined how hard the policy held. A force channel measured on the human hand thus transfers to a robot hand with no tactile hardware, through a retargeting map from five glove channels to seven robot actuators, with no sensor shared between the two.

</details>


### [169] [DreamSat-Bench: Development and Initial Testing of a Testbed for AI-Based Pose Estimation from 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14183)
*Alex Posadas-Nava,August Berne,Giovanni Lavezzi,Kareena Shah,Alejandro Carrasco,Josiane Uwumukiza,Giacomo Battaglia,Paolo Panicucci,Minduli C. Wijayatunga,Victor Rodriguez-Fernandez,Richard Linares*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 DreamSat-Bench，一个模块化的交会与接近操作（RPO）测试平台，用于对基于 AI 的相对导航技术进行基准评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向未准备（无先验几何模型）空间资产的自主导航需求，缺乏从仿真到现实统一评测的基准平台，难以量化仿真-现实差距及各类参数对位姿估计的影响。

Method: 集成软硬件在环机器人流水线，统一 MuJoCo、Isaac Lab、LeRobot 等工具，用机械臂复现 3D 轨迹；支持轨道环境与光照的广泛定制。以 DreamSat（生成式单视图三维重建）+ FoundationPose（零样本 6-DoF 跟踪）构成端到端视觉导航流水线进行评测，并开展参数化研究与初步硬件在环实验。

Result: 测试覆盖定点保持、绕飞表征等任务代表性轨道段；量化了重建延迟、网格分辨率、轨道距离、光照几何对位姿估计精度的影响；硬件在环实验定性验证了物理部署，并识别出目标对称性与累积跟踪漂移为鲁棒导航的关键因素。

Conclusion: DreamSat-Bench 为在缺乏先验几何模型条件下、面向未准备空间资产的自主导航技术成熟化提供了严谨的评测框架。

Abstract: This paper presents the development and initial testing of DreamSat-Bench, a modular rendezvous and proximity operation testbed designed to benchmark AI-based relative navigation techniques. By integrating a software- and hardware-in-the-loop robotic pipeline, the platform enables a seamless transition from digital simulation to physical reality. DreamSat-Bench unifies state-of-the-art robotic learning tools such as MuJoCo, Isaac Lab, and LeRobot into a single benchmarking platform, utilizing robotic arms to trace 3D trajectories. The platform allows for extensive customization of orbital environments and lighting to evaluate the simulation-to-reality gap. We demonstrate the testbed's utility by evaluating an end-to-end vision-based navigation pipeline that pairs DreamSat, a generative AI framework for single-view 3D reconstruction, with FoundationPose for zero-shot 6-DoF tracking of unseen spacecraft. Initial testing explores mission-representative orbital segments, including fixed-point station-keeping and fly-around characterization. Through a series of parametric studies, we quantify the impacts of reconstruction latency, mesh resolution, orbital range, and illumination geometry on pose estimation accuracy. Finally, a preliminary hardware-in-the-loop campaign qualitatively validates the physical deployment of the pipeline, identifying target symmetry and accumulated tracking drift as critical factors for robust navigation. DreamSat-Bench provides a rigorous framework for maturing autonomous navigation with unprepared space assets in the absence of prior geometric models.

</details>


### [170] [Novel Ex-vivo Calf Brain Model with Integrated Sub-Skull Force Sensors to Access Simulated Neurosurgical Procedures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14198)
*Hamad Binhammad,Matheus Ballestero,Mohammed Babgi,Seana Shaka,Nima Hemati,Rothaina Saeedi,Aiden Mazidi,Bianca Giglio,Rukun Dou,Houssem-Eddine Gueziri,Amir Hooshiar,Rolando F. Del Maestro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并验证了一种便携式离体力感知平台，可在模拟神经外科手术中测量颅脑界面的工具-组织操作力，精度高、可检测毫牛级力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外科组织操作要求高度精准，但在真实条件下工具对组织的操作力量级很少被量化，缺乏客观的力测量手段。

Method: 平台由新鲜牛脑组织（作为脑实质生物替代物）置于3D打印人体颅骨模型构成，配备六自由度力/力矩传感器与实时数据采集系统。设计五项验证方案：与真值对比的准确性与动态保真度、用校准砝码（0.5–50 g）评估静态准确性与线性度、最小可检测力、不同解剖区域的空间一致性、手术铺巾影响及长时间稳定性。

Result: 测量力与参考载荷高度一致（R=0.9997），RMSE小于0.005 N，平均相对误差低于2%；可可靠检测低至1 g（9.8 mN）的力；手术铺巾未引入明显信号失真，长时间记录漂移极小。

Conclusion: 该框架为基于新鲜牛脑组织的技能训练与表现评估提供了客观、高保真的力定量手段，可推广至其他手术的基于力的评估。未来将整合临床实际器械以增强程序真实性，并推进临床试验以评估可用性、教学影响与转化价值。

Abstract: Surgical tissue manipulation demands precision; however, tool-tissue manipulation force magnitudes under realistic conditions are rarely quantified. To address this gap, we proposed and validated a portable ex-vivo force-sensing platform that measures tool-tissue interaction forces across the skull-brain interface during simulated neurosurgery. The system involves fresh calf brain tissue, used as a biological surrogate for brain parenchyma, placed in a 3D-printed human skull model equipped with a 6 degree-of-freedom force/torque sensor and a real-time data acquisition system. Five validation protocols assessed the accuracy and dynamic fidelity of the platform against ground-truth measurement, static accuracy and linearity using calibrated weights (0.5-50 g), minimum detectable force, spatial consistency across different anatomical regions, effect of surgical draping, and long-duration stability. Across protocols, measured forces showed excellent agreement with reference loads (correlation R = 0.9997), with RMSE < 0.005 N and mean relative error under 2%. The platform reliably detected low-magnitude forces down to 1 g (9.8 mN), while surgical drapes introduced no meaningful signal distortion and prolonged recordings exhibited minimal drift. Overall, the proposed framework provides objective, high-fidelity force quantification for skill training and performance assessment using fresh calf brain tissue and may serve as a foundation for force-based evaluation across other surgical procedures. Future work will integrate clinically used surgical instruments to increase procedural realism and will progress toward clinical trials to evaluate usability, educational impact, and translational relevance in practice-adjacent settings.

</details>


### [171] [ITA-LaCAM: A Complete and Scalable TAPF Solver via Assignment-Aware Configuration-Space Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14208)
*Yimin Tang,Han Zhang,Shao-Hung Chan,Junsoo Kim,Erdem Bıyık,Sven Koenig,Jingkai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ITA-LaCAM，一种结合目标分配与路径规划的完整可扩展TAPF求解器，通过在每个关节构型节点携带智能体-目标匹配，并增量修复匹配来避免枚举组合分配空间，在9760个基准实例上实现100%求解率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TAPF问题需要同时为智能体分配目标并规划无碰撞路径，现有方法在完整性和可扩展性方面存在不足，需要一种既能保证完整性又能高效处理大规模实例的求解器。

Method: ITA-LaCAM受LaCAM和ITA-CBS启发，每个关节构型节点携带智能体到目标的匹配；生成后继时，对移动的智能体增量修复匹配，并利用目标引导PIBT后继生成，从而无需显式枚举组合分配空间即可实现自适应重分配。

Result: 在8张地图、5-200个智能体的9760个基准实例上，ITA-LaCAM求解率达100%，而配置DBS-Hungarian的IR-TAPF为95.6%；在84.0%的对比中更快找到初始解，在双方均求解的实例中有65.0%实现了更低的总成本。

Conclusion: ITA-LaCAM在保持LaCAM完整性和可扩展性的同时，通过增量匹配修复机制有效解决了TAPF问题，在求解率和成本优化方面均优于对比方法。

Abstract: Combined Target Assignment and Path Finding (TAPF) requires assigning targets for agents while simultaneously planning collision-free paths. We present ITA-LaCAM, a complete and scalable TAPF solver inspired by LaCAM and ITA-CBS. In ITA-LaCAM, each joint-configuration node carries an agent-to-target matching. When a successor is generated, ITA-LaCAM incrementally repairs the matching for the agents that moved and uses the targets to guide PIBT successor generation. This design enables adaptive reassignment without explicitly enumerating the combinatorial assignment space, while preserving LaCAM's completeness and scalability. Across 9,760 benchmark instances on eight maps with 5--200 agents, ITA-LaCAM solved 100% of the instances, compared with 95.6% for IR-TAPF configured with DBS-Hungarian. ITA-LaCAM found an initial solution faster in 84.0% of the comparisons and achieved a lower sum of costs in 65.0% of the instances solved by both methods.

</details>


### [172] [Task-Specified Active Metrological Inspection with Measurement-Steered VLA Manipulation and Deterministic Evidence Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14219)
*Zhiling Chen,Jingzhan Ge,Ruimin Chen,Matthew P. Castanier,David Gorsich,Farhad Imani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FRAME框架，将检测指令与结构化规格转化为可追溯的合格性证据，用于高混合低产量制造中的主动计量检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高混合低产量（HMLV）制造需检测系统适应不断变化的零件、规格与工单，而现有检测自动化通常假设预设的传感序列，通用机器人智能体则以任务完成为目标，忽视计量证据的完整性与有效性。

Method: 提出任务指定的主动计量检测问题，并设计分层双臂框架FRAME：将学习到的操作与标定激光轮廓测量相结合；任务管理器对需求进行落地与调度，主动表面对应验证物理到规格的定位，证据记忆追踪测量来源、可采纳性与覆盖度；学习组件可提出检测目标与物理访问动作，而确定性的基准测量、可采纳性检查、覆盖审计与合格性评估防止不完整或未验证证据导致PASS。

Result: 一系列物理实验表明，FRAME实现了更高的端到端检测可靠性、更少的错误接受以及更短的任务完成时间。

Conclusion: FRAME通过将学习组件与确定性计量验证机制结合，有效提升HMLV场景下检测证据的可追溯性与可靠性。

Abstract: High-mix low-volume (HMLV) manufacturing requires inspection systems to adapt to changing parts, specifications, and work orders without repeated task-specific programming. Existing inspection automation typically assumes predefined sensing sequences, while general purpose robot agents optimize task completion rather than the completeness and validity of metrological evidence. We formulate task-specified active metrological inspection and propose From Requirements to Admissible Metrological Evidence (FRAME), a hierarchical dual-arm framework that converts an inspection instruction and structured specification into traceable conformance evidence. FRAME coordinates learned manipulation with calibrated laser profilometry: a task manager grounds and schedules requirements, active surface correspondence verifies physical-to-specification localization, and evidence memory tracks measurement provenance, admissibility, and coverage. Learned components may propose inspection targets and physical access actions, but deterministic datum-grounded measurement, admissibility checks, coverage auditing, and conformance evaluation prevent incomplete or unverified evidence from authorizing PASS. A series of physical experiments shows that FRAME achieves higher end-to-end inspection reliability, fewer false accepts, and shorter task completion time.

</details>


### [173] [VGFM: Expressive Robot Policies via Dense Value Guidance in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14261)
*Prajwal Koirala,Mark Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Value-Guided Flow Matching (VGFM)，一种可扩展的离线强化学习框架，通过将策略参数化为动作空间的条件流匹配模型，实现无需 BPTT 和额外算法开销的密集价值引导塑造，并在 OGBench 的机器人任务上取得强劲表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期机器人学习依赖大规模离线交互数据集训练控制策略，富有表现力的生成模型能提供丰富的多模态动作表示。然而，使用多步生成式动作器进行策略改进仍具挑战性：在离线 RL 中沿生成轨迹融入基于价值的目标通常会带来大量训练复杂度，包括时间反向传播（BPTT）、辅助架构或蒸馏损失。

Method: VGFM 将策略参数化为动作（x-prediction）空间中的条件流匹配模型，保证每个中间流步骤都产生一个可被标准离线 RL 评论家直接评估的合法机器人动作。该设计允许在随机采样的流时间上施加价值引导，而无需对整个生成轨迹求导，同时通过改变底层流 ODE 的离散化在推理时保持灵活性，无需重新训练。

Result: 在 OGBench 的机器人运动与操作任务上，在严格的评估协议下，VGFM 在广泛的各类任务中取得了强劲的性能。且仅需极少的超参数调优。

Conclusion: 结果表明 VGFM 为长时程、目标导向的机器人控制中的富有表现力策略学习提供了一种简单、可扩展且有效的方法。

Abstract: Recent robot learning paradigms increasingly rely on large offline datasets of robotic interactions to train control policies. Expressive generative models enable rich and multimodal action representations, expanding the capability of this paradigm for complex robotic control. However, policy improvement with multi-step generative actors remains challenging. In offline reinforcement learning (RL), incorporating value-based objectives along generative trajectories often introduces substantial training complexity, including backpropagation through time (BPTT), auxiliary architectures, or distillation losses. We propose Value-Guided Flow Matching (VGFM), a scalable offline RL framework that enables dense value-guided shaping within a flow-based policy while avoiding BPTT and additional algorithmic overhead. VGFM parameterizes the policy as a conditional flow-matching model in action (x-prediction) space, ensuring that each intermediate flow step produces a valid robot action that can be directly evaluated by a standard offline RL critic. This design allows value guidance to be applied at randomly sampled flow times without differentiating through the entire generative trajectory, while preserving inference-time flexibility by varying the discretization of the underlying flow ODE without retraining. Evaluated on robotic locomotion and manipulation tasks in OGBench, VGFM achieves strong performance across a wide range of tasks under rigorous evaluation protocols. With minimal hyperparameter tuning, these results demonstrate that VGFM provides a simple, scalable, and effective approach for expressive policy learning in long-horizon, goal-oriented robotic control.

</details>


### [174] [Learning Communication-Conditioned Generative Policies for Decentralized Multi-Agent Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14268)
*Prajwal Koirala,Mark Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种去中心化、通信条件化的生成式多智能体避碰框架，通过流匹配策略生成短时域动作序列，实现无需中心规划的高效协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体避碰需要在部分可观测条件下实现协同，但现有方法常依赖中心化规划或显式通信，难以在真实去中心化执行中高效运行，且对通信中断鲁棒性不足。

Method: 利用具备全局状态的特权离线示范训练流匹配策略，示范中无显式通信信号；智能体学习交换并聚合编码交互意图的潜在消息。测试时支持灵活推理：无条件生成对应独立行为，通信条件化生成实现协同交互。执行阶段完全去中心化，仅依赖局部观测与学到的消息，并结合滚动时域推理方案。

Result: 仿真显示接近专家水平的避碰性能，对更密集、未见过的多智能体场景具有强泛化能力，并实现到真实机器人实验的零样本迁移；支持高效单步生成短时域动作序列，在通信丢包下性能优雅退化。

Conclusion: 该框架在去中心化执行下兼顾协同性、推理效率与通信鲁棒性，具备从仿真到真实机器人的迁移潜力。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a decentralized communication-conditioned generative framework for multi-agent collision avoidance. Agents generate short-horizon action sequences using a flow-matching policy trained from privileged offline demonstrations with access to global state. The demonstrations do not include explicit communication signals; instead, agents learn to exchange and aggregate latent messages that encode interaction-relevant intent under partial observability. This formulation supports flexible inference at test time, where unconditioned generation corresponds to independent behavior and communication-conditioned generation enables coordinated interaction without centralized planning. The resulting policies operate in a fully decentralized manner at execution time, relying only on local observations and learned messages. Combined with a receding-horizon inference scheme, the proposed approach enables efficient single-step inference of short-horizon action sequences and degrades gracefully under communication dropouts. Extensive simulation results demonstrate near-expert collision avoidance performance and strong generalization to denser, unseen multi-agent scenarios, along with zero-shot transfer to real-robot experiments.

</details>


### [175] [VLBiMan++: Expanding the Generalization Boundary of Vision-Language Anchored One-Shot Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14310)
*Huayi Zhou,Wei Gao,Yiyang Han,Kui Jia,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLBiMan++ 是一个扩展框架，通过视觉-语言锚定的一次性双臂操作，从单个人类演示出发实现跨任务、物体、场景、本体与长期部署的系统性泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂机器人操作要具备可泛化性，需要可复用的任务先验，以避免大规模遥操作演示和策略重训练的高昂成本。

Method: 基于单次人类演示进行任务感知分解，提取可复用与可适配的技能组件；采用视觉-语言接地的几何适配将技能迁移到新配置而无需重训练；并引入物体状态感知适配与轻量轨迹优化，以应对超越刚性6自由度位姿变化的情形并保持双臂协调。

Result: 大量真实世界实验表明，VLBiMan++ 在上述日益具有挑战性的设置中保持了较强的任务成功率和适应能力。

Conclusion: VLBiMan++ 将一次性双臂操作从孤立的可迁移性展示，推进为面向任务、物体、场景、本体与长期部署条件的更系统、可扩展的泛化框架。

Abstract: Generalizable bimanual robotic manipulation requires a reusable task prior that can persist across increasingly diverse tasks, objects, scenes, embodiments, and execution conditions, thus avoiding the prohibitive cost of large-scale teleoperated demonstrations and policy retraining. In this work, we present VLBiMan++, an extended framework that expands the generalization boundary of vision-language anchored one-shot bimanual manipulation. Starting from a single human demonstration, VLBiMan++ performs task-aware decomposition to identify reusable and adaptable skill components, and employs vision-language grounded geometric adaptation to transfer these skills to novel configurations without retraining. Building on this foundation, we systematically extend generalization along five dimensions: task generalization through diverse and long-horizon skill compositions; object generalization across unseen categories, varying geometries, and more complex articulated or deformable objects; scene generalization under clutter, occlusion, and dynamic interference; embodiment generalization across heterogeneous dual-arm robotic platforms; and deployment generalization through prolonged closed-loop execution under repeated external perturbations. To support this broader scope, we further introduce object-state-aware adaptation and lightweight trajectory optimization mechanisms that accommodate changes beyond simple rigid 6-DoF pose variations while preserving reliable bimanual coordination. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate that VLBiMan++ maintains strong task success and adaptation capability across these increasingly challenging settings. Overall, VLBiMan++ advances one-shot bimanual manipulation from demonstrating isolated transferability toward a more systematic and scalable framework for generalization across tasks, objects, scenes, embodiments, and long-term deployment conditions.

</details>


### [176] [BIG-CBF: Behavior-Imagination-Guided Control Barrier Function with Shared Uncertainty for Mobile Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14343)
*Shibo Li,Zhongcheng Wang,Jiahe Cao,Jianhua Yang,Ke Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出BIG-CBF，一种"行为想象引导+共享不确定性"的双速率导航架构，将低频机动选择与高频CBF安全滤波分离，在基准测试中取得99.78%的任务成功率并显著降低CBF干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小干预CBF滤波器缺乏任务级机动意识，在几何歧义环境中面对多种局部可行机动时可能无法选出有产出的避障方向，导致"安全但停滞"的行为。

Method: 采用双速率架构：低频层在短时域内想象并评估六种闭环反馈行为，使用解析CBF兼容性及兼顾任务进度、冻结、平滑性与切换的轻量目标函数；高频层执行安全滤波。想象层与执行层共享相对运动延迟、障碍预测、零阶保持运动与指令执行残差等不确定性来源，并以硬CBF作为最终安全权威。

Result: 在覆盖九种场景、3600回合的对比基准中，BIG-CBF总体任务成功率达99.78%，为最高，并大幅减少下游CBF干预；在搭载Jetson Orin Nano的全向移动机器人上，15次评估全部完成且无接触事件。与非共享变体的硬件对比显示其CBF干预能量与激活频率更低。

Conclusion: 共享不确定性有助于提升机动选择与安全关键执行之间的一致性，BIG-CBF在保持安全的同时缓解了安全但停滞的问题，并具备实机可行性。

Abstract: Control barrier functions (CBFs) provide a mathematically grounded framework for enforcing local collision-avoidance constraints in autonomous mobile robots, commonly through optimization-based safety filters. However, a minimum-intervention CBF filter lacks task-level maneuver awareness and may fail to select a productive avoidance direction when multiple distinct maneuvers are locally viable, leading to safe but stalled behavior in geometrically ambiguous environments. This paper presents BIG-CBF, Behavior-Imagination-Guided Control Barrier Function with shared uncertainty, a two-rate navigation architecture that separates low-rate maneuver selection from high-rate safety filtering. Over a short horizon, six closed-loop feedback behaviors are imagined and evaluated using analytic CBF compatibility together with a lightweight objective accounting for task progress, freezing, smoothness, and switching. To reduce planning-execution mismatch, the imagination and execution layers share consistent uncertainty sources for relative-motion delay, obstacle prediction, zero-order-hold motion, and command-execution residuals, while a hard CBF remains the final safety authority. In a 3,600-episode comparative benchmark across nine scenarios, BIG-CBF achieves the highest overall task success rate of 99.78% while substantially reducing downstream CBF intervention. On a physical omnidirectional robot with onboard Jetson Orin Nano computation, BIG-CBF completes all 15 evaluation runs without a recorded contact event. Matched hardware comparisons against the non-shared variant further show lower CBF intervention energy and activation frequency, supporting improved consistency between maneuver selection and safety-critical execution.

</details>


### [177] [Orientation Control of Soft Robots via Adiabatic Spectral Submanifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14376)
*Aron Karakai,Roshan S. Kaundinya,Mike Yan Michelis,Robert Katzschmann,George Haller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文扩展了绝热谱子流形（aSSM）识别方法，利用增广可观测量数据集构建软体机器人降阶模型，并升级 aSSM-MPC 控制方案，在高保真有限元仿真中将位置与姿态跟踪误差降低超60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人常用于对安全性要求高的精密环境中，需要精准的位置与姿态控制。模型预测控制（MPC）是可行方案，但其依赖对机器人无穷维非线性动力学既准确又计算廉价的模型，这一矛盾构成了核心难题。

Method: 基于近期关于绝热谱子流形（aSSM）及其在软体机器人中应用的理论，采用数据驱动的降阶方法构建模型；在此基础上，将 aSSM 识别扩展至增广的可观测量数据集，并升级现有的 aSSM-MPC 控制方案。

Result: 在一个压力驱动软体臂的高保真有限元仿真上进行评估，所提出的控制器相比现有数据驱动基线，将位置与姿态跟踪误差降低超过60%。

Conclusion: 通过增广可观测量数据提升 aSSM 识别质量并改进 aSSM-MPC，可显著提升软体机器人位置与姿态控制精度，验证了该方法在安全关键场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Soft robots are commonly sought for safety-critical interactions in delicate environments, where accurate position and orientation control is imperative. Model predictive control (MPC) offers a solution, but it requires a model of the robot's infinite-dimensional nonlinear dynamics that is at once accurate and computationally cheap. Recent theory on adiabatic spectral submanifolds (aSSMs) and their applications to soft robots provide data-driven model-reduction methods to construct such models. Here, we extend these methods to identify aSSMs from enlarged observable datasets and upgrade the currently available aSSM-MPC schemes. Evaluated on a high-fidelity finite-element simulation of a pressure-actuated soft arm, our controller reduces position and orientation tracking error by more than 60% compared to existing data-driven baselines.

</details>


### [178] [Learning-Based Dynamic Obstacle Avoidance for a UAV Using Only Three Range Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14426)
*Mohammad Reza Ranjbar Divkoti,A. Pedro Aguiar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于学习的方法，用于固定高度飞行的无人机在未知动态环境中的在线运动规划，仅用三个低成本方向测距传感器，结合行为栅格地图与PPO深度强化学习，实现了在极端部分可观测条件下的实时避障与目标抵达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在未知动态环境中需实时避开静态与动态障碍，但存在极端部分可观测性；且受限于单自由度偏航控制（类似固定翼的非完整约束运动），难以依赖LiDAR或视觉等密集感知。因此需要一种在稀疏感知条件下仍能可靠导航的状态表示与控制策略协同设计方案。

Method: 提出将行为栅格地图（behavior grid map）表示与深度强化学习（DRL）相结合的框架，采用近端策略优化（PPO）进行连续控制下的稳定策略学习。核心是状态表示与控制策略的协同设计：行为栅格地图将稀疏的三路方向测距传感器测量动态聚合为结构化局部表示，仅用三个低成本传感器即可支持实时避障与目标抵达决策。

Result: 在多种规模与障碍密度的仿真环境中，标准版与增强版方法的成功率均高于PPO变体与模型预测控制（MPC）：小尺度高拥堵场景为94%对79–90%，大尺度高拥堵场景为83%对62–71%，并保持实时性能。四个真实场景实验进一步验证了可行性，目标抵达行为一致且无碰撞。

Conclusion: 行为栅格地图与PPO的协同设计能够在仅使用三个低成本方向测距传感器的极端部分观测条件下实现可靠的无人机在线运动规划，仿真与真实实验均表明其成功率优于PPO变体和MPC，且满足实时性要求。

Abstract: We present a learning-based approach to kinodynamic online motion planning for an Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) operating at a fixed altitude in unknown dynamic environments, where real-time avoidance of both static and dynamic obstacles must be achieved under conditions of extreme partial observability. The UAV is controlled with a single degree of freedom (yaw only), resulting in constrained, nonholonomic motion similar to fixed-wing platforms.
  The proposed framework integrates a behavior grid map representation with Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL), using Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) for stable policy learning in continuous control. The key idea is the co-design of a state representation and control policy that enables reliable navigation using only three low-cost directional range sensors, without reliance on dense sensing modalities such as LiDAR or vision-based systems. The behavior grid map dynamically aggregates sparse measurements into a structured local representation that supports real-time decision-making for obstacle avoidance and target reaching. Extensive simulations across environments of varying sizes and obstacle densities demonstrate that the proposed standard and enhanced methods achieve higher success rates than PPO variants and Model Predictive Control (MPC) (94\% vs. 79--90\% in small-scale high-congestion scenarios, and 83\% vs. 62--71\% in large-scale high-congestion scenarios), while maintaining real-time performance. Real-world experiments across four scenarios further confirm practical feasibility, with consistent target-reaching behaviour and no collisions under the tested conditions.

</details>


### [179] [EMoG: Emotion-Modulated Gait Generation for Expressive Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14432)
*Yi Lu,Tianhao Jiang,Honglong Tian,Yumeng Zhang,Qingrui Zhao,Zhengtao Wang,Xiao-Xiao Long,Qiu Shen,Xun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EMoG框架，通过连续可调的情感风格码调制轻量MLP生成符合指令的周期性步态轨迹，并由统一强化学习策略执行，实现人形机器人富有表现力的运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形运动系统多关注稳定性与任务执行，缺乏表现力与显式运动控制的结合，即在保持指令跟踪的同时实现情感化步态仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 引入连续可调强度的情感风格码；基于该码与物理指令，用轻量MLP实时生成有表现力且与指令一致的周期步态轨迹；由统一强化学习策略跟踪执行；构建大规模情感标注步态数据集（来自专业表演者）并设计自动流程提取物理一致的周期步态周期；集成基于LLM的解析器将自然语言转化为情感风格与运动参数。

Result: 实验表明可实现连续的步态风格调制，具有可感知的表现力线索，同时保持指令跟踪能力。

Conclusion: EMoG为面向人机交互的参数化情感风格行走提供了一种实用方法。

Abstract: Existing humanoid locomotion systems primarily focus on stability and task execution, while integrating expressiveness with explicit locomotion control remains challenging. We propose EMoG, an emotion-modulated gait generation framework for expressive humanoid locomotion. EMoG introduces an emotional-style code with continuously adjustable intensity. Conditioned on this code and physical commands, a lightweight MLP generates expressive, command-consistent periodic gait trajectories in real time, which are tracked by a unified reinforcement learning policy for physical execution. To support training, we collect a large-scale emotion-annotated gait dataset from professional performers and develop an automated pipeline to extract physically consistent periodic gait cycles. EMoG also integrates an LLM-based parser that converts free-form language into emotional style and motion parameters for interactive control. Experiments demonstrate continuous gait-style modulation with perceptible expressive cues while maintaining command tracking. EMoG provides a practical approach to parameterized emotional-style walking for human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [180] [EcoBoat: Design and Experimental Validation of an Autonomous Body-Board Boat For Cleaning Water Bodies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14450)
*M. Aman Ansari,Saifullah Khan,Rahul Kulkarni,PB Sujit*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EcoBoat，一种基于改装船体的低成本自主水面航行器，用于室内外水域漂浮垃圾收集。室内用超声波传感器结合随机游走与边界跟随，室外用GPS电子围栏结合随机游走覆盖。核心设计是受特斯拉阀启发的收集篮，前进时吸入垃圾、转向或制动时防止逃逸。泳池与湖泊实验验证了极简感知与计算方案的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 清理游泳池、湖泊等水体通常依赖大量人工或昂贵的传感器密集型机器人系统，成本高、部署难。因此需要一种低成本、感知与计算极简的自主方案，能够适应室内外不同水域的漂浮垃圾收集需求。

Method: 基于改装船体构建低成本自主水面航行器EcoBoat。室内环境采用超声波传感器检测边界与障碍物，将随机游走运动与边界跟随行为相结合；室外环境采用基于GPS的电子围栏配合随机游走策略实现区域覆盖。关键设计为受特斯拉阀启发的收集篮，利用单向流动特性在前向运动时允许垃圾进入，在转向或制动时阻止其逃逸。

Result: 在游泳池和湖泊中开展实地实验，通过迭代改进验证了设计。结果表明，采用极简的感知与计算方案即可在多种水域中实现有效且通用的漂浮垃圾收集。

Conclusion: 低成本、低感知复杂度的自主水面航行器可用于室内外多样化水体的漂浮垃圾收集，特斯拉阀式收集篮有效解决了收集过程中的垃圾逃逸问题，为轻量化水体清洁机器人提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Cleaning water bodies such as swimming pools and lakes typically demands significant manual effort or reliance on costly, sensor-intensive robotic systems. This paper presents EcoBoat, a low-cost autonomous surface vehicle built on a modified hull, designed to collect floating debris in both indoor and outdoor water bodies. For indoor environments, EcoBoat uses ultrasonic sensors to detect boundaries and obstacles, combining random-walk motion with boundary-following behavior. For outdoor environments, it relies on GPS-based geofencing paired with a random-walk strategy for area coverage. A key design feature is a Tesla-valve-inspired collection basket that allows debris intake during forward motion while preventing its escape during turning or braking. Field experiments in pools and lakes validated the design through iterative refinement, demonstrating that a minimalist sensing and computation approach can achieve effective, versatile debris collection across diverse water bodies.

</details>


### [181] [Fault Diagnosis for Underwater Vehicles using Moving Horizon Estimation and Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14539)
*Fotis Panetsos,Kostas J. Kyriakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向水下机器人的模型化故障检测与诊断框架，针对执行器故障，显式处理未建模动态问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人执行器故障诊断面临未建模动态干扰，传统方法难以区分未建模动态与故障效应，导致诊断鲁棒性不足。

Method: 采用移动地平线估计器（MHE）估计包含未建模与故障效应的集总扰动；用高斯过程（GP）近似未建模动态并给出均值与不确定性预测；在线运行时对MHE集总扰动估计与GP预测的残差进行广义似然比检验（GLRT）。

Result: 通过引入GP预测提升对未建模动态的鲁棒性，实现有效的故障检测与隔离以及对故障幅度的准确量化估计；在实验室水箱中完成实验验证，开环与闭环控制下均表现可靠。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效区分未建模动态与故障效应，实现鲁棒的故障检测、隔离与量化，并通过实验验证其可行性。

Abstract: This work proposes a model-based fault detection and diagnosis framework for underwater vehicles subject to actuator faults that explicitly accounts for the presence of unmodeled dynamics. To this end, a Moving Horizon Estimator (MHE) is developed to estimate the lumped disturbance, capturing both unmodeled and fault effects. Gaussian Processes (GPs) are employed to approximate the unmodeled dynamics, providing predictions of the corresponding mean and uncertainty across diverse operating conditions. During online operation, the residual between the MHE lumped disturbance estimate and the GP prediction is evaluated using a Generalized Likelihood Ratio Test. By incorporating GP-based predictions within the diagnostic framework, robustness to unmodeled dynamics is achieved, enabling effective fault detection and isolation as well as accurate quantitative estimation of fault magnitude. The proposed methodology is experimentally validated in a laboratory water tank, demonstrating reliable diagnostic performance under both open-loop and closed-loop control.

</details>


### [182] [NavPatch: Evidence-Guided Object-Level Costmap Correction with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14543)
*Shiji Sun,Xingyu Tao,Hao Wang,Ling Wang,Zhengyi Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 NavPatch，一种对象级修正层，利用视觉语言模型为导航相关物体类别分配 ADD、REMOVE 或 EXTEND 语义修正，以弥补几何地图对障碍物表示的偏差，在真实机器人试验中取得 86.0% 的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人通常依赖几何地图进行避障与路径规划，但几何表示与物体“应如何影响导航”之间并不一致：低矮电缆可能被漏检，柔性窗帘可能造成虚假阻挡，交通锥可能需要比其观测足迹更大的禁行区域。因此需要一种对象级的语义修正机制来弥补几何地图的不足。

Method: NavPatch 通过周期性的场景理解，使用视觉语言模型为导航相关物体类别分配 ADD、REMOVE 或 EXTEND 修正；采用开放词汇接地定位物体实例，并利用 LiDAR 与 RGB-D 观测提供 3D 支撑；同时引入观测质量过滤与跨帧维护机制，决定每个修正补丁何时被提交、替换或撤销。

Result: 在五种布局下的 50 次真实机器人试验中，NavPatch 总体成功率达到 86.0%。包含 200 次运行的四种配置消融实验表明，相比仅基于当前观测的更新方式，NavPatch 将成功率从 70.0% 提升至 86.0%，并将错误提交率从 68.4% 降至 40.7%。

Conclusion: NavPatch 通过对象级语义修正与跨帧维护，有效改善了机器人导航中几何地图的障碍表示偏差，在提升导航成功率的同时显著降低了错误提交率，验证了引入视觉语言模型进行对象级导航修正的有效性。

Abstract: Mobile robots typically rely on geometric maps for obstacle avoidance and path planning, but the resulting obstacle representation does not always match how an object should affect navigation. A low lying cable may be missed, a flexible curtain may create spurious blockage, and a traffic cone may require an exclusion region larger than its observed footprint. We present NavPatch, an object level correction layer that assigns ADD, REMOVE, or EXTEND to navigation relevant object categories through periodic scene understanding with a vision-language model. Open vocabulary grounding localizes object instances, and LiDAR and RGB-D observations provide 3D support. Observation quality filtering and cross frame maintenance determine when each correction patch is committed, replaced, or revoked. In 50 real robot trials across five layouts, NavPatch achieves an overall success rate of 86.0%. An ablation study of four configurations with 200 runs in total shows that NavPatch improves the success rate from 70.0% to 86.0% and reduces the false commit rate from 68.4% to 40.7% compared with updates based only on the current observation.

</details>


### [183] [GLAM: Training a latent world model over global spatiotemporal memory for active exploration and navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14561)
*I-Tak Ieong,Ruizhi Feng,Zhaoyang Lu,Yifei Cao,Jiayao Zhao,Leon Li,Senhua Zhu,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GLAM——一种基于全局时空记忆的目标条件潜世界模型，并构建了完整的导航系统GLAM NAV，在HM3D-ObjectNav设定下超越BSC-Nav基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动探索与语义导航要求具身智能体能够从部分观测中构建记忆、预测已观测空间记忆的演化如何支持未来运动，并将该预测转化为可执行规划，现有方法难以在统一表示空间中同时建模未来空间上下文与导航意图。

Method: GLAM以历史地图token、导航目标与当前机器人位姿为输入，在全局时空记忆上训练，联合预测未来地图表示与以机器人为中心的航点潜变量；采用类JEPA的潜空间预测范式，直接作用于地图级潜token而非RGB重建，并使用预训练航点编码器-解码器在GLAM NAV中监督与解码导航规划。训练数据通过回放Habitat中HM3D v0.2场景资产上的ObjectNav专家轨迹，切片为多时间尺度预测样本。

Result: 在受控的HM3D-ObjectNav子集复现设定中，GLAM NAV在成功率与路径长度加权成功率两项指标上均优于复现的BSC-Nav基线。

Conclusion: 在共享潜空间中联合预测空间记忆演化与导航意图具有可行性，地图级潜预测结合预训练航点编解码可有效提升具身导航性能。

Abstract: Active exploration and semantic navigation require an embodied agent to build memory from partial observations, predict how the evolution of observed spatial memory may support future motion, and convert that prediction into actionable plans. We present GLAM, a goal-conditioned latent world model trained over global spatiotemporal memory, and GLAM NAV, the complete navigation system built around it. Given historical map tokens, a navigation goal, and the current robot pose, GLAM jointly predicts future map representations and robot-centric waypoint latents, allowing future spatial context and navigation intent to be inferred in a shared representation space. The model follows a JEPA-like latent prediction paradigm, operates directly on map-level latent tokens rather than RGB reconstruction, and uses a pretrained waypoint encoder-decoder to supervise and decode navigation plans within GLAM NAV. Training data are collected by replaying ObjectNav expert trajectories in Habitat over HM3D v0.2 scene assets and slicing them into multi-timescale prediction samples. On a controlled HM3D-ObjectNav subset reproduction setting, GLAM NAV improves over a reproduced BSC-Nav baseline in both success rate and success weighted by path length.

</details>


### [184] [Learning Multi-Agent Task Assignment and Navigation in the Factory: from Simulation to Real Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14567)
*Abdalwhab Bakheet Mohamed Abdalwhab,Giovanni Beltrame,David St-Onge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FMAPPO算法，融合2D LiDAR与任务状态信息，实现多机器人多机床服务的去中心化强化学习，并通过仿真到现实的完整流水线验证其在物理平台上的可行性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习（MARL）在物理多机器人工业环境中部署仍面临挑战。本文旨在探究去中心化MARL在真实多机器人多机床服务场景中的实际适用性，解决仿真到现实迁移、安全导航与任务分配等关键问题。

Method: 提出Feature-fusion Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization（FMAPPO），将2D LiDAR测量与任务特定状态信息进行特征融合，实现安全的多机器人去中心化任务分配与导航。构建了基于高保真机器人仿真与ROS2的完整仿真到现实流水线，并部署于物理移动操作平台上（实验期间机械臂禁用）。此外，研究了学习策略对指令更新频率的敏感性问题。

Result: 仿真对比表明，FMAPPO显著优于现有基线且效应量大：相比MAPPO和SMAPPO，零件配送分别提升106%和21%，零件收集分别提升48%和11%；机床利用率分别提高31和10个百分点；碰撞分别减少18%和15%；安全分数分别提高14和6个百分点。真实世界实验验证了去中心化策略能在真实传感与控制约束下协调多机器人服务多台机床并保持安全运行。

Conclusion: FMAPPO证明了去中心化MARL在真实工业多机器人多机床服务场景中的可行性与有效性，融合感知与任务信息的策略在仿真与物理平台上均表现出更优的性能与安全性，为实际部署提供了可参考的仿真到现实流水线。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has shown considerable promise for robotic decision-making, yet deploying multi-agent RL (MARL) on physical multi-robot systems in industrial environments remains challenging. This paper investigates the real-world applicability of decentralized MARL for multi-robot multi-machine tending. We propose Feature-fusion Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (FMAPPO), which fuses 2D LiDAR measurements with task-specific state information to enable safe decentralized multi-robot task assignment and navigation. A complete simulation-to-reality pipeline was developed using high-fidelity robotic simulation and ROS2 and deployed on physical mobile-manipulator platforms operating under realistic real-world conditions, with the robotic arms disabled during the experiments. We further investigate the sensitivity of the learned policy to command update frequency, an important consideration for real-world deployment. Comparative evaluation in simulation demonstrated that FMAPPO significantly outperformed state-of-the-art baselines with a large effect size, achieving improvements of 106\% and 21\% in parts delivery and 48\% and 11\% in parts collection over MAPPO and SMAPPO, respectively. FMAPPO also increased machine utilization by 31 and 10 percentage points, respectively, while reducing collisions by 18\% and 15\% and increasing the safety score by 14 and 6 percentage points compared with MAPPO and SMAPPO, respectively. Furthermore, real-world experiments demonstrated that the learned decentralized policies can coordinate multiple robots to service multiple machines while maintaining safe operation under real-world sensing and control constraints. Videos of the real-world experiment are available online https://anonymouspapers123.github.io/FMAPPO/.

</details>


### [185] [Robotic Servo Tracking of Moving Targets with Dynamic Imitation Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14589)
*Yazhe Luo,Sipu Ruan,Yifei Li,Diansheng Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模仿轨迹约束的伺服跟踪方法，通过在视觉伺服中引入动态轨迹约束，实现移动目标的高精度、平滑跟踪并具备动态避障能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人视觉伺服中施加显式的轨迹约束十分困难。现有跟踪方法通过将视觉残差映射为控制速度来实现快速响应，但对中间运动过程的约束较弱，导致轨迹不连续、振荡或行为保守。因此，需要一种能够对移动目标实现受约束跟踪的方法。

Method: 1) 建立并分析描述机器人接近移动目标的动态模型及其收敛性；2) 引入时间可伸缩形变机制，以及结合形状与幅度分量的轨迹调制方法，实时生成一系列轨迹；3) 从这些轨迹中自适应确定跟踪点，形成动态约束；4) 通过目标位姿微分或跟踪关键特征计算机器人速度，使其沿受约束轨迹运动。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验表明，与多种当前先进方法相比，所提方法在复杂环境中能够实现动态避障和高精度收敛。

Conclusion: 基于模仿轨迹约束的伺服跟踪方法可有效施加中间过程约束，兼顾跟踪平滑性与精度，并在复杂环境下优于多种先进方法。

Abstract: Imposing explicit trajectory constraints in robot visual servoing remains challenging. Existing tracking methods achieve fast responses by mapping visual residuals to control velocities, but they have weak constraints on the intermediate motion process, which lead to trajectory discontinuity, oscillation, or conservative behaviors. To enable constrained tracking for moving targets, this paper proposes a servo tracking method based on imitation trajectory constraints. A dynamic model describing the robot approaching a moving target is formulated and analyzed for convergence. A time-scalable deformation mechanism and a trajectory modulation incorporating shape and amplitude components are introduced to generate a series of trajectories in real time, from which tracking points are adaptively determined to form dynamic constraints. The robot velocity is then computed from target pose differentials or tracked key features to follow the constrained trajectory. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that the proposed method can achieve dynamic obstacle avoidance and high-precision convergence compared with several state-of-the-art methods in complex environments.

</details>


### [186] [REVOLVE: An Automated Closed-Loop Framework for Evolving Robot Manipulation with Minimal Human Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14633)
*Hanyu Liu,Qian Li,Yizhu Ding,Jiayi Wen,Keqiang Ren,Yunsheng Ma,Tao Jian,Zhihua Wang,Zhuofan Yu,Xinran Li,Zhigong Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REVOLVE是一个自动化闭环机器人操作进化框架，集成数据采集、策略训练部署、失败恢复与持续学习，通过自动重置与环境纠正及双环进化机制，在四项真实任务中显著提升策略成功率与智能体判断准确率，并大幅降低人工介入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的机器人操作策略在任务执行与泛化上进步明显，但真实部署仍高度依赖人工进行失败评估、纠正与环境重置，且模型难以从失败与纠正经验中持续学习，因此需要一种低人工干预的自动化进化方案。

Method: 提出REVOLVE（Robot Evolving via Orchestrated Loops, Verification, and Experience），基于统一软件平台将数据采集、策略训练与部署、失败恢复和持续学习整合为单一闭环流程；其中Automated Reset and Collection（ARC）架构自动重置环境并干预纠正策略失败；Dual-Loop Evolution（DLE）将真实交互与失败—纠正数据反馈到策略学习，并利用外部不匹配记忆优化智能体判断。

Result: 在四个真实世界操作任务上，经过五次迭代后，平均策略成功率提升18.5%，智能体判断准确率提升8.5%，数据采集与部署测试中的人工工作量分别减少94.4%和95.1%。

Conclusion: REVOLVE将真实世界部署转化为闭环学习过程，能够持续积累并利用执行经验，在显著减少人工干预的情况下实现策略与监督模型的持续进化。

Abstract: Recent advances in data-driven robot manipulation policies have substantially improved task execution and generalization. However, real-world deployment still relies heavily on humans for failure assessment, correction, and environment reset, while models often fail to continually learn from failures and corrective experience. We present REVOLVE (Robot Evolving via Orchestrated Loops, Verification, and Experience), an automated closed-loop framework for evolving robot manipulation with minimal human intervention. Built on a unified software platform, REVOLVE integrates data collection, policy training and deployment, failure recovery, and continual learning into a single closed-loop workflow. Its Automated Reset and Collection (ARC) architecture automatically resets the environment and intervenes to correct policy failures. Dual-Loop Evolution (DLE) continually improves the manipulation policy and agent by feeding real-world interaction and failure--correction data back into policy learning and using an external mismatch memory to refine agent judgments. Experiments across four real-world manipulation tasks show that, after five iterations, REVOLVE improves average policy success rate by 18.5% and agent judgment accuracy by 8.5%, while reducing human effort in data collection and deployment testing by 94.4% and 95.1%, respectively. These results demonstrate that REVOLVE transforms real-world deployment into a closed-loop learning process that continually accumulates and uses execution experience, enabling continual evolution of both the policy and supervisory model with substantially less human intervention.

</details>


### [187] [Skill Composition for Legged Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14647)
*Daniel Gigliotti,Flavio Maiorana,Fabio Patrizi,Luca Iocchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文主张将"独立子策略的组合"作为独立研究问题，而非实现细节；可靠组合是构建可复用技能库的关键，并能让"做什么"的决策交给可预先检验的规划器或符号控制器，使机器人行为可验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个专门控制器训练快、收敛可靠、可单独验证，而端到端统一策略难以覆盖所有任务；但技能之间的"切换"仍然脆弱。作者认为可靠组合决定了分散技能能否成为可复用、可扩展、可共享的技能库。更进一步，若控制权能在任意时刻安全地在多个专门策略间传递，则"下一步做什么"可交由性质完全不同的组件（规划器、自动机、符号控制器）决定，从而让机器人的可信行为变得可预先检验。

Method: 这是一篇立场/观点性论文，未提出具体算法或实验，而是提出研究议程：把子策略组合视为独立研究方向，并讨论由外部（规划器、自动机、符号控制器）发出决策、专门策略仅负责执行的架构设想。

Result: 无实验结果。论述性结论为：当前单个专门技能已较成熟，瓶颈在于技能间的转换与组合；可靠组合机制是技能库可用的前提条件。

Conclusion: 应以研究问题的方式对待独立子策略的组合；若能在任意时刻安全交接控制权，则高层决策可与低层执行解耦，交由可预先审查的符号或规划组件，从而使机器人行为具备可验证性。原文未给出实现与验证结果。

Abstract: Robots, and humanoid robots in particular, are increasingly competent at individual behaviors, each obtained by training a specialized controller. A specialized skill is quick to train, converges reliably because the problem it faces is narrow, and can be validated on its own, none of which is true of a single end-to-end policy asked to cover everything. What remains fragile is the transition between them. We argue that the composition of independent sub-policies deserves to be treated as a research problem in its own right, rather than as an implementation detail left to whatever mechanism happens to be at hand. Reliable composition is what turns a collection of separate skills into a repertoire that can be used, extended and shared. More fundamentally, if control can be passed between specialized policies safely, and at any moment, the choice of what the robot should do next can be delegated to a component of an entirely different nature, such as a planner, an automaton or a symbolic controller, whose behavior can be inspected in advance. The policies would then only ever have to act, and what the robot can be trusted to do would become verifiable.

</details>


### [188] [A Novel Robot-Assisted Learning Pedagogy for Children with ASD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14710)
*Laura Boccanfuso,Erin Barney,Marilena Mademtzi,Claire Foster,Quan Wang,Colette Torres,Lisa Chen,Brian Scassellati,Pamela Ventola,Frederick Shic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种融合"教学相长"（learning-by-teaching）教学法的新型机器人辅助自闭症干预方法，比较机器人与真人助手对自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）学龄儿童社交技能任务表现的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往机器人辅助自闭症干预多将机器人作为教师、临床助手或能力更强的同伴，且依赖专业治疗师的参与。然而，绝大多数ASD儿童就读于主流学校，80%以上的时间在普通课堂度过，但将经过验证的课堂教学法融入机器人辅助干预的研究却很少。

Method: 采用"教学相长"教学法，设计并评估一种新型机器人辅助自闭症干预方案，对比机器人与真人助手（human confederate）两种条件，考察儿童在一组社交技能任务中的表现。

Result: 80%的参与者在机器人条件下表现更好（机器人条件平均表现63%，真人助手条件37%），且与场景顺序无关；90%的参与者在机器人条件下参与度显著更高（机器人平均参与度61%，真人助手32%）；分析表明机器人条件下的整体参与度有助于提升任务表现，而该效应在真人助手条件下未出现。

Conclusion: 在学习相长的情境中引入机器人，可能有助于提升ASD儿童的参与度并改善其在简单社交技能任务上的表现。

Abstract: Interaction paradigms used in robot-assisted autism intervention have historically employed robots as teachers, clinical assistants, or more-abled peers to promote a variety of social skills. These modalities often leverage the expertise of trained practitioners to ensure that child-robot interactions are productive or clinically grounded to yield positive therapeutic benefits for children across the autism spectrum. Yet, despite the fact that the majority of children with autism spectrum disorder (ASD) attend mainstream schools and spend 80% or more of their time in the general classroom [27], there is a paucity of research incorporating validated classroom teaching pedagogies into robot-assisted autism interventions. In this work, we introduce a novel teaching methodology for advancing social skills in school-aged children with ASD. We evaluate the effectiveness of a novel robot-assisted autism intervention which incorporates the learning-by-teaching pedagogy and explores the comparative benefits of employing a robot versus a human confederate for improved performance on a set of social skills tasks. Results show that 80% of study participants performed better in the robot condition (mean performance in the robot condition=63%, mean performance in the confederate condition=37%), irrespective of the scenario order. Further, 90% of all participants were significantly more engaged in the robot condition (mean engagement: robot=61%, confederate=32%) and, while the effect did not result in the confederate condition, analyses indicate that overall engagement in the robot condition contributed to improved performance. These results suggest that robots employed in a learning-by-teaching context may help enhance engagement and improve performance on a simple social skills task for children with ASD.

</details>


### [189] [Beyond Dead Reckoning: A Point of View on Camera--DAS--GNSS Continuity in Road Tunnels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14748)
*Lyes Saad Saoud,Hesham A. Rakha,Mona Jaber,Moussa Ayyash*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该观点文章提出，隧道内定位应被视作"基础设施辅助的跨模态轨迹连续性"问题，而非依赖最后一次可信卫星定位的外推。文章主张融合可信的洞口卫星解、分布式声学传感（DAS）连续运动证据、稀疏摄像头身份与车道锚点、车载传感短期动力学，以及边缘计算的关联、融合、完整性监测与受控重捕获，并将真值仅用于离线标定与验证，进而提出一套可证伪的研究与部署议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隧道遮蔽卫星信号，而联网自动驾驶车辆仍需要连续、可溯源且具有完整性约束的定位。现有依赖最后可信卫星定位进行外推的做法难以保证连续性与可信度，因此需要重新界定隧道定位问题的本质。

Method: 采用"观点文章（Point of View）"形式，提出将隧道定位重构为基础设施辅助的跨模态轨迹连续性问题，并设计多源证据融合架构：洞口卫星解提供全局锚点，DAS提供连续运动证据，摄像头提供稀疏身份与车道锚点，车载传感提供短期动力学，边缘计算负责关联、融合、完整性监测与受控重捕获。

Result: 文章提出了一套可证伪的研究与部署议程，旨在推动从同步多模态证据走向经验证的、具备完整性感知的隧道定位连续性；并明确将真值定位用途限定于离线标定与验证环节。

Conclusion: 隧道定位应被重新定义为基础设施辅助的跨模态轨迹连续性问题，通过多源异构传感与边缘计算的协同融合及完整性监测，实现连续、可溯源、完整性有界的定位，而非单纯依赖卫星定位外推。

Abstract: Road tunnels remove satellite visibility where connected and automated vehicles still require continuous, attributable, and integrity-bounded positioning. This Point of View argues that tunnel localization should be treated as infrastructure-assisted cross-modal track continuity, not as extrapolation from the last trusted satellite fix. A trusted portal satellite solution provides the global anchor, distributed acoustic sensing (DAS) continuous motion evidence, cameras sparse identity and lane anchors, onboard sensing short-term dynamics, and edge computing association, fusion, integrity monitoring, and guarded reacquisition. Ground truth is reserved for offline calibration and validation. The article develops a falsifiable research and deployment agenda for progressing from synchronized multimodal evidence to validated, integrity-aware tunnel positioning continuity.

</details>


### [190] [An Adaptive Fixed-Time Line-of-Sight Guidance Scheme for 3D Path Following of Underwater Vehicles: Theory and Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14756)
*Hanzhi Yang,Jalil Chavez-Galaviz,Nina Mahmoudian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于AUV三维路径跟踪的鲁棒固定时间自适应LOS制导框架，通过固定时间稳定性理论与LOS制导结合，保证收敛时间与初始条件无关，并开发固定时间自适应估计器补偿洋流引起的时变侧滑扰动，同时引入时变前视机制提升曲线路径跟踪性能。仿真与Iver 3 AUV实船实验表明其精度、收敛速度和抗干扰能力优于现有自适应LOS方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV在动态不确定海洋环境中可靠路径跟踪至关重要。传统LOS制导依赖渐近收敛，扰动恢复慢且跟踪性能不可预测；现有鲁棒控制方法通常需修改底层载体控制器，限制了在商用AUV平台上的实际应用。因此需要一种制导层面的实用解决方案。

Method: 将固定时间稳定性理论与LOS制导相结合，构建固定时间自适应LOS制导框架，使路径跟踪在预设时间内收敛且与初始条件无关；设计固定时间自适应估计器快速补偿洋流引起的时变侧滑扰动；引入时变前视机制改善曲线路径跟踪性能；并通过Lyapunov分析证明框架的固定时间稳定性。

Result: 仿真中，时变前视变体在曲线路径跟踪时将横向与垂直跟踪RMSE分别降低69.37%和67.46%。在Iver 3 AUV现场实验中，相比传统自适应LOS制导，所提固定时间制导在直线路径评估中平均跟踪误差降低56.35%，曲线路径评估中降低27.59%。数值仿真和物理实验均表明该框架在跟踪精度、收敛速度和抗干扰能力方面优于现有最优自适应LOS方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为AUV在复杂海洋环境中实现可靠自主导航提供了实用的制导层面解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable path tracking is crucial for autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) operating in dynamic and uncertain marine environments. However, traditional line-of-sight (LOS) guidance methods rely on asymptotic convergence, resulting in slow disturbance recovery and unpredictable tracking performance. Existing robust control methods typically require modifications to the underlying vehicle controller, limiting their practical application on commercial AUV platforms. This paper proposes a robust fixed-time adaptive LOS guidance framework for 3D path tracking for AUVs. By combining fixed-time stability theory with LOS guidance, this method guarantees path tracking convergence within a preset time range, with the convergence time independent of initial conditions. Furthermore, a fixed-time adaptive estimator is developed to rapidly compensate for time-varying sideslip disturbances caused by ocean currents. A time-varying look-ahead mechanism is also introduced to improve tracking performance on curved paths. Lyapunov analysis proves the fixed-time stability of the proposed framework, and numerical simulations and physical experiments demonstrate that, compared to state-of-the-art adaptive LOS methods, this framework exhibits superior tracking accuracy, convergence speed, and anti-interference capability. In simulation, the time-varying look-ahead variant reduced cross-track and vertical-track RMSE by 69.37\% and 67.46\%, respectively, during curved-path tracking. In field experiments with an Iver 3 AUV, the proposed fixed-time guidance reduced average tracking error by 56.35\% in straight-path evaluation and 27.59\% in curved-path evaluation compared with conventional adaptive LOS guidance.The proposed method provides a practical guidance-level solution for achieving reliable autonomous navigation of AUVs in complex marine environments.

</details>


### [191] [A Personalized Dynamic Balance Evaluation Paradigm for Hip Exoskeleton-Assisted Walking under Unexpected Ground Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14765)
*Yun Chen,Oluwasegun T. Akinniyi,Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种受试者特定的复合平衡代价（融合7个生物力学子指标），结合经验贝叶斯层次模型，在髋关节外骨骼扰动实验中高效筛选最佳辅助条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 髋关节外骨骼有望改善意外步态扰动后的恢复，但个性化辅助困难：平衡是多维的，且人在回路实验样本小、噪声大，难以有效评估和选择辅助条件。

Method: 构建整合7个生物力学子指标（稳定裕度、质心动力学、全身角动量）的受试者特定复合平衡代价；将子指标转为方向对齐的无量纲代价特征，在单纯形上学习非负融合权重；结合经验贝叶斯层次模型，用"学习复合选择器"估计各条件的后验最优概率P(best)及高概率候选集（大小K0.8）。3名受试者以1.1 m/s行走、单侧皮带滑移扰动，测试46种髋辅助条件。

Result: 全预算分析（每条件4次重复）下，选择器将80%后验概率集中在46个条件中的1–5个，优于等权融合（2–12）和PCA融合（4–37）；选中条件试验的复合代价低于无力矩试验（P2、P3名义p<0.05）；留一重复重拟合得到正的平均留出秩相关，权重与候选集具中等稳定性。

Conclusion: 概念验证结果支持使用受试者特定复合平衡评估来筛选候选条件，可缩短个性化实验并减少受试者反复暴露于扰动的次数。

Abstract: Hip exoskeletons may improve recovery from unexpected gait perturbations, yet personalizing assistance remains difficult because balance is multidimensional and human-in-the-loop experiments are small-sample and noisy. We present a participant-specific composite balance cost that integrates seven biomechanical sub-metrics spanning margin of stability, center-of-mass dynamics, and whole-body angular momentum. The sub-metrics are converted to direction-aligned, dimensionless cost features, and nonnegative fusion weights are learned on the simplex. Coupled with an empirical-Bayes hierarchical model, the learned-composite selector estimates each tested condition's posterior probability of being best, P(best), and a high-probability candidate set with size $K_{0.8}$. The framework was evaluated with three participants walking at 1.1 m/s during unilateral belt-slip perturbations across 46 hip-assistance conditions. In the full-budget analysis (B = 4 repeats per condition), the selector concentrated 80% of the posterior probability within 1 to 5 of 46 conditions, compared with 2 to 12 for equal-weight fusion and 4 to 37 for principal component analysis fusion. This smaller candidate set could shorten personalization experiments and limit participants' exposure to repeated perturbations in future studies. Selected-condition trials showed lower observed composite costs than no-torque trials, with nominal p < 0.05 for P2 and P3. Leave-one-repeat-out refits yielded positive mean held-out rank correlations for all participants and moderate stability of the learned weights and candidate sets. These proof-of-concept results support participant-specific composite balance evaluation for candidate selection in perturbation-based human-in-the-loop experiments.

</details>


### [192] [Language-Guided Representation Learning for Robust Cross-Sensor Material Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14783)
*Mashood M. Mohsan,Muhayy Ud Din,Binzhao Xu,Ahmad Abubakar,Irfan Hussain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出语言引导蒸馏框架，实现传感器无关的触觉表征学习，显著提升少样本与跨传感器泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作需要触觉感知来获取柔软度、纹理、接触稳定性等难以仅凭视觉推断的属性。但视觉式触觉传感器因光学、弹性体特性与光照差异，对同一材质产生不同观测，导致在单一或多传感器上训练时泛化性差。

Method: 语言编码跨越硬件不变的触觉高层语义属性（如粗糙、柔软、光滑），作为天然的传感器无关监督信号。构建 39K 样本的触觉-语言数据集（含人工标注材质标签），训练触觉编码器将传感器特定的触觉图像与语言嵌入对齐到共享语义空间。

Result: 在 100-shot 少样本设置下达到 95% 准确率；跨传感器迁移平均提升 13.3% 准确率；在六个已有触觉数据集上最高获得 19% 准确率增益。

Conclusion: 语言引导蒸馏能够实现可扩展且硬件无关的触觉表征学习，代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Robots need touch to manipulate objects safely and reliably, as many properties, such as softness, texture, and contact stability, are hard to infer from vision alone. However, vision-based tactile sensors yield different observations of the same material due to variations in optics, elastomer properties, and illumination, leading to poor generalization when trained on a single or multiple sensors. We propose a language-guided distillation framework for learning sensor-robust tactile representations. Language encodes high-level semantic properties of touch (e.g., rough, soft, slippery) that remain invariant across sensing hardware, providing a natural sensor-agnostic supervisory signal. We construct a 39K-sample touch-language dataset with human-annotated material labels and train a tactile encoder to align sensor-specific tactile images with language embeddings in a shared semantic space. We evaluate our approach for few-shot learning and cross-sensor transfer and benchmark it on six existing tactile datasets. Our method achieves 95% accuracy in the 100-shot setting, improves cross-sensor transfer by an average of 13.3% accuracy, and yields up to 19% accuracy gains across six existing tactile datasets. These results demonstrate that language-guided distillation enables scalable and hardware-agnostic tactile representation learning. Code and dataset are available at https://mashood3624.github.io/Language_Tactile/

</details>


### [193] [Belief-Adaptive Online Autonomy for Quadrotor UAV Navigation under GNSS Degradation in Urban Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14806)
*Deepak Kumar Panda,Weisi Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种信念自适应在线自主框架，通过显式GNSS信任建模、二阶在线信念自适应和延迟感知的乱序测量处理来增强EKF，从而在GNSS退化城市空域中实现更稳健的四旋翼导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市空域中GNSS测量受多径、遮挡和延迟影响，产生非平稳、时间相关的误差，导致传统GNSS-IMU融合性能下降，因此需要可靠的在线自主能力。

Method: 在扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）基础上引入显式GNSS信任建模；将GNSS信任表示为一个潜在信念状态，用于调节测量权重和多径偏差不确定性；利用EKF一致性信号在线更新；采用二阶在线信念自适应，并处理延迟感知的乱序测量。该方法无需先验环境知识或离线训练。

Result: 在具有相关多径、随机延迟和障碍约束的仿真城市空中交通场景中，与朴素、自适应和一阶基线相比，该方法实现了更好的信念收敛、更平滑的轨迹，并降低了估计与跟踪误差。

Conclusion: 该框架保留了经典GNSS-IMU融合结构，可直接集成到现有飞控管线中，支持GNSS退化环境下的稳健在线自主。

Abstract: Reliable online autonomy is critical for quadrotor operation in urban airspaces, where global navigation satellite systems (GNSS) measurements suffer from multipath, blockage, and latency issues, introducing non-stationary, temporally correlated errors that degrade conventional GNSS-IMU fusion. This paper presents a belief-adaptive online autonomy framework that augments an extended Kalman filter (EKF) with explicit GNSS trust modelling, second-order online belief adaptation, and latency-aware out-of-sequence measurement handling. GNSS trust is represented as a latent belief state that modulates measurement weighting and multipath bias uncertainty, and is updated online using EKF consistency signals. Unlike reactive covariance tuning, the proposed approach enables proactive and stable sensor trust adaptation without prior environmental knowledge or offline training. Evaluation in simulated urban air mobility scenarios with correlated multipath, stochastic latency, and obstacle constraints demonstrates improved belief convergence, smoother trajectories, and reduced estimation and tracking errors compared to naive, adaptive, and first-order baselines. The framework preserves classical GNSS-IMU fusion structure and can be integrated directly into existing flight control pipelines, supporting robust online autonomy in GNSS degraded environments.

</details>


### [194] [Primitive-Informed Sampling-Based MPC for Multi-Fingered Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14868)
*Emek Barış Küçüktabak,Karankumar Patel,Jinda Cui,Zhaodong Yang,Kazuhiro Sasabuchi,Jun Takamatsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于操作基元的采样MPC框架，用于多指灵巧手操作，通过低维基元引导采样并优化关节级残差，在物理Allegro手上实现稳定的手内旋转、抓取与臂手协同任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样式MPC无需对复杂接触动力学求梯度，但直接在高维关节空间探索效率低，且性能高度依赖采样分布。因此需要引入低维协调信息来引导采样，提高采样预算的有效利用率。

Method: 用编码协同手指运动的低维操作基元对采样进行偏置，同时优化关节级残差，使基元适应当前手-物构型；在前向仿真中引入任务相关的rollout约束，剔除不可行轨迹。在物理Allegro手上结合同步的MuJoCo数字孪生进行验证，基元可来自仿真训练策略或人手运动数据。

Result: 消融实验表明基元与残差对可靠连续手内旋转均必不可少；单纯增加采样预算无法恢复这种协调性；rollout约束显著提升成功率。从单一物体提取的基元在不同物体尺寸和模型失配下仍有效；框架还支持抓取、物体重定向以及臂手协同的reach-grasp-transport任务。

Conclusion: 低维操作基元引导采样并配合关节级残差优化与rollout约束，可有效提升高维灵巧操作中采样式MPC的效率和鲁棒性，并具备跨物体尺寸、跨任务与跨基元来源的泛化能力。

Abstract: We present a primitive-informed sampling-based model predictive control (MPC) framework for multi-fingered dexterous manipulation. Sampling-based MPC avoids the need for gradients through complex contact dynamics, but direct exploration of the high-dimensional joint space is inefficient and makes performance strongly dependent on the sampling distribution. Our framework biases sampling using low-dimensional manipulation primitives that encode coordinated finger motions, while simultaneously optimizing joint-level residuals to adapt these motions to the current hand-object configuration. Task-related rollout constraints reject infeasible trajectories during forward simulation, improving the effective use of the sampling budget. We evaluate the approach on a physical Allegro hand using a synchronized MuJoCo digital twin. Ablations show that both the primitive and residual are necessary for reliable continuous in-hand rotation, that increasing the sampling budget alone does not recover this coordination, and that rollout constraints substantially improve success rate. A primitive extracted from a single object remains effective across object sizes and under model mismatch, and the framework further supports grasping, object reorientation, and coordinated arm-hand reach-grasp-transport, using primitives extracted from both a simulation-trained policy and human hand-motion data.

</details>


### [195] [Real-World Reinforcement Learning with MPC Scaffolding for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14878)
*Emek Barış Küçüktabak,Karankumar Patel,Zhaodong Yang,Jinda Cui,Kazuhiro Sasabuchi,Jun Takamatsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以采样MPC为脚手架的真实世界灵巧强化学习框架，无需人类示范，7分钟在线训练即达100%成功率，20分钟后转速达MPC的5倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界灵巧操作RL受限于早期探索低效与失败代价高；现有方法往往依赖人类示范或纠正动作，成本高、干预多。作者希望在不依赖人类示范的前提下，实现高效、低干预的真实世界灵巧RL。

Method: 以采样型MPC作为学习脚手架：先用少量MPC轨迹填充离线回放缓冲并预训练actor与critic；在线阶段MPC间歇性引导数据采集，off-policy SAC同时从先验MPC经验与新采集物理交互中学习，控制权逐步过渡到学习策略。任务为16自由度Allegro手的连续手中旋转。

Result: 初始化用20条硬件采集的MPC轨迹（12分钟）后，在线RL 7分钟即在纯策略评估中达100%成功率（5/5），平均约3次掉落；20分钟后转速超MPC五倍以上，并连续1000次旋转、超110分钟无掉落。消融显示MPC预训练、保留MPC经验与在线MPC引导各有互补增益；还可快速适应不同物体几何并完成目标条件重定向。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了采样型MPC脚手架能让真实世界灵巧RL高效、低干预地完成学习，兼顾快速收敛与长期稳定，并具备对新物体与目标条件的适应能力。
