Abstract: Real-world reinforcement learning (RL) offers a promising route to dexterous manipulation policies that can adapt directly from physical interaction, but learning is hindered by inefficient early exploration and costly failures. We propose a framework that uses sampling-based model predictive control (MPC) as scaffolding for real-world dexterous RL, providing structured prior experience and task-directed guidance during learning without human demonstrations or corrective actions. A small set of MPC trajectories is first used to populate an offline replay buffer and to pretrain the actor and critic. During online learning, MPC intermittently guides data collection while an off-policy Soft Actor-Critic learner trains from both prior MPC experience and newly collected physical interaction, with control gradually transitioning to the learned policy. On continuous in-hand rotation with a 16-DoF Allegro hand, the method reaches 100\% success in policy-only evaluation (5/5 trials) after 7 minutes of online RL, following initialization with 20 MPC trajectories collected on hardware in 12 minutes. Online training incurs about three object drops on average. After 20 minutes of online learning, the policy achieves more than five times the rotation speed of the MPC controller. It completes 1000 consecutive rotations over more than 110 minutes without a drop. Ablations show complementary benefits from MPC-based pretraining, retained MPC experience, and online MPC guidance. We further demonstrate rapid adaptation to different object geometries and successful goal-conditioned reorientation, showing that the framework enables efficient, low-intervention, real-world dexterous RL.

</details>


### [196] [HydroMap: Probabilistic Water Surface Elevation Mapping for Semantic Scene Representation in Inland Waterways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14903)
*Zhongbi Luo,Yunjia Wang,Herman Bruyninckx,Peter Slaets*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HydroMap框架，通过立体视觉重建水面高程并与结构地图融合，生成持久概率高程图与统一2.5D语义地图，服务于内河航道自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内河航道自主水面载具需持续感知周边结构与水面。LiDAR建图对水面回波稀疏或缺失，导致该操作界面在重建场景中缺席，因此需补充水面的持久表示。

Method: HydroMap采用里程计解耦框架：从立体观测重建水面高程，逐帧水面点与传播的立体及位姿不确定性构成联合栅格观测，连续观测融合为持久概率高程图；随后语义地图转换将高程图与结构几何结合，形成涵盖水面、边界、结构及上方区域的统一2.5D表示。

Result: 在Pohang Canal与Leuven Vaart数据集上，相对于同一地图坐标系下的LiDAR参考，高程RMSE低于5 cm；高程图与语义图分别以2 Hz和1 Hz发布。

Conclusion: HydroMap以持久水面表示补充LiDAR地图，为内河航道下游导航提供支持。

Abstract: Autonomous surface vehicles operating in inland waterways require a persistent representation of both surrounding structures and the water surface. LiDAR-based simultaneous localization and mapping often produces sparse or missing water returns, leaving this operational surface absent from the reconstructed scene. We propose HydroMap, an odometry-decoupled framework that reconstructs water surface elevation from stereo observations and integrates it with the structural map. Per-frame water points form joint cell observations with propagated stereo and pose uncertainty, and successive observations are fused into a persistent probabilistic elevation map. Semantic map conversion then combines the elevation map with structural geometry in a unified 2.5D representation of water, boundaries, structures, and overhead regions. On the Pohang Canal and Leuven Vaart datasets, the elevation RMSE remains below 5 cm relative to LiDAR references expressed in the same map frame. The elevation and semantic maps are published at 2 Hz and 1 Hz, respectively. HydroMap thereby complements LiDAR maps with a persistent representation of the water surface for downstream navigation in inland waterways.

</details>


### [197] [Exact Feasibility Certification and Optimal Responsibility Allocation for Multi-Robot CBF Safety Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14935)
*Chandan Kumar Sah,Jishnu Keshavan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文为多机器人CBF安全滤波器提出精确可行性证书，通过分离安全约束的"需求"与执行器的"供给"量化可行性裕度，揭示不可行成因，并提出共享安全约束的最优分配算法，大幅降低不可行控制步与安全违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人控制障碍函数（CBF）安全滤波器可能变得不可行，但二次规划（QP）失败本身并不能说明冲突为何发生、由谁引起，也无法指明如何解决。现有方法缺乏对不可行性的定性与定量诊断手段。

Method: 针对具有异构控制仿射动力学与凸输入集的多智能体CBF滤波器，构建精确可行性证书：将安全约束所施加的"需求"与执行器可提供的"供给"分离，得到可行性储备量；据此判断CBF增益调节或增加驱动能否解决不可行问题，并定位责任智能体与交互。进一步提出最优分配共享安全约束的算法，最大化最差局部可行性裕度，对多面体输入集转化为线性规划求解。

Result: 在320组配对闭环仿真中，所提分配方法将不可行控制步从约50%降至6.2%，安全违规运行从118/160降至24/160；在52次不可行事件中，证书识别出的交互其松弛可在94%的情况下恢复可行性。

Conclusion: 该可行性证书为多智能体CBF滤波器的不可行性提供了可解释的诊断与归因，并支持通过约束重分配有效缓解冲突，显著提升多机器人系统的安全滤波可行性。

Abstract: Multi-robot Control Barrier Function (CBF) safety filters can become infeasible, but a failed quadratic program (QP) does not indicate why the conflict occurred or how to resolve it. To address this, we develop an exact feasibility certificate for multi-agent CBF filters with heterogeneous control-affine dynamics and convex input sets. The certificate quantifies a feasibility reserve by separating the demand imposed by safety constraints from the available actuator supply. This decomposition shows when CBF gain tuning or increased actuation can and cannot resolve infeasibility, and identifies the agents and interactions responsible for the conflict. We further propose an algorithm to optimally allocate shared safety constraints by maximizing the worst local feasibility margin, yielding a linear program for polyhedral input sets. In $320$ paired closed-loop simulations, the proposed allocation reduces infeasible control steps from roughly $50\%$ to $6.2\%$, and reduces safety-violating runs from $118/160$ to $24/160$. In addition, across $52$ infeasibility events, the certificate identifies an interaction whose relaxation restores feasibility in $94\%$ of cases.

</details>


### [198] [Comparing Trajectories from Positions Alone: Curvature-Based Time Alignment and Drift Error Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14936)
*Effie Daum,Daniele De Martini,Claire Dune,François Pomerleau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一套轨迹评估协议，结合基于曲率信号的时间对齐方法与按行驶距离归一化的误差指标，用于标准化、可靠的SLAM与定位精度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域内缺乏比被评估估计更准确的独立大规模参考轨迹；ATE与RPE等指标依赖未明确说明的假设与评估参数，导致误差结果可能误导并妨碍公平比较。

Method: 提出标准化轨迹评估协议：采用基于曲率信号的新型时间对齐方法，并结合按行驶距离归一化的误差指标；显式处理时间同步、采样对齐与外参标定，并通过敏感性分析量化其影响。

Result: 通过敏感性分析量化了时间同步、采样对齐与外参标定等因素对评估结果的影响。

Conclusion: 该协议有助于实现更严谨、可复现且标准化的轨迹评估。

Abstract: In field robotics, acquiring independent large-scale reference trajectories more accurate than the evaluated estimates remains an open challenge. The domain is widely reliant on Absolute Trajectory Error (ATE) and Relative Pose Error (RPE), computed with automated tools, that rest on assumptions and evaluation parameters rarely made explicit. When unreported, the errors can be misleading and hinder fair comparisons. This paper introduces a trajectory-evaluation protocol for standardized and reliable accuracy assessment in state estimation, localization, and Simultaneous Localization And Mapping (SLAM). The approach combines a novel temporal alignment method based on curvature signals with an error metric normalized by travelled distance. We explicitly account for temporal synchronization, sampling alignment, and extrinsic calibration, quantifying their influence through a sensitivity analysis. The proposed protocol contributes to more rigorous, reproducible, and standardized trajectory evaluation.

</details>


### [199] [Two-Stage Personalized Gait Phase Estimation in Stroke Survivors During Exoskeleton-Assisted Walking: An Offline Feasibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14984)
*Hyungseok Ryu,Pilwon Hur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种基于功能性IMU对齐与两阶段序贯自适应的方法，用于脑卒中幸存者的个性化步态相位估计，并在动力膝关节外骨骼上验证了离线可行性与嵌入式推理时序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑卒中幸存者步态异质性高，健康步态预训练模型难以直接适用，需要个性化自适应方法，同时需评估其在外骨骼嵌入式系统中的实时计算可行性。

Method: 使用大腿IMU信号估计步态相位，以足跟力敏电阻作为参考标签。采用两阶段自适应：阶段1通过蒸馏正则化建立受试者专属模型，阶段2利用低秩自适应（LoRA）进行条件式细化。评估LSTM、TCN与Transformer三种模型，在5名使用动力膝关节外骨骼的脑卒中幸存者中，采用留一受试者超参数选择与序贯测试-自适应重放，并进行策略消融与嵌入式时序测试。

Result: 相较未自适应基线，阶段1+2使三种模型的受试者平均相位RMSE分别降低84.2%、77.0%、60.7%。Transformer最终误差最低（2.90±1.13%步态周期），足跟触地时序误差为23.7±4.5ms。每周步更新通常误差最低或接近最低，条件更新降低更新频率且精度差异小。个性化后对齐带来模型相关的相位误差变化，同时保持或改善足跟触地检测。嵌入式测试中TCN与Transformer在阶段2更新时保持100Hz推理无超时，LSTM有6.6%推理超过10ms截止时间；所有更新均在0.8s内完成。

Conclusion: 结果支持所提框架用于外骨骼辅助步行的离线可行性与嵌入式计算时序性能。

Abstract: This study evaluated personalized gait phase estimation for stroke survivors using functional inertial measurement unit (IMU) alignment and two-stage sequential adaptation of models pre-trained on healthy gait. The estimator used signals from a thigh-mounted IMU. Heel force-sensitive resistor measurements provided reference phase labels for offline adaptation and evaluation. Stage 1 established a distillation-regularized participant-specific model, and Stage 2 performed conditional refinement using low-rank adaptation. Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN), and Transformer models were evaluated in five stroke survivors walking with a powered knee exoskeleton using leave-one-subject-out hyperparameter selection and sequential test-then-adapt Stage 2 replay. Relative to the non-adapted baselines, Stage 1+2 reduced the mean participant-wise phase root mean square error by 84.2%, 77.0%, and 60.7%, respectively. The Transformer achieved the lowest final error (2.90 +- 1.13$% of the gait cycle) and heel-strike timing error (23.7 +- 4.5ms). Policy-specific ablations showed that every-cycle updates generally produced the lowest or near-lowest error, whereas conditional updating reduced the update frequency with small accuracy differences. After personalization, alignment produced model-dependent changes in phase error while preserving or improving heel-strike detection and reducing heel-strike timing error for the LSTM and Transformer. Concurrent embedded tests showed that the TCN and Transformer maintained 100-Hz inference during Stage 2 updates without deadline misses, whereas the LSTM missed the 10-ms deadline in 6.6% of inferences. All updates completed within 0.8s. These results support the offline feasibility and embedded computational timing of the proposed framework for exoskeleton-assisted walking.

</details>


### [200] [From Learned-Mode AV-Traffic Pairing to Planner Decisions: A Marginal-Preserving Study on Argoverse 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14997)
*Jingyu Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过"边际保持乘积对照"干预，检验自动驾驶车辆(AV)未来轨迹与周围交通的联合配对结构是否影响规划器决策，发现配对结构会改变规划器选择，但无法将其记录结果效应与条件集中度变化分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合运动预测为每个AV未来配对了周围交通，但现有actor-level指标无法反映这种配对结构对规划器是否重要，作者希望直接检验该结构对规划决策的影响。

Method: 设计一种边际保持的乘积对照，在学习到的模式中移除AV-交通配对，同时保留固定匀速配对，并保持轨迹、规划器条件化前的actor-level边际、候选、代价项及权重和回退机制不变；在Argoverse 2的1,400个留出场景上进行十二次运行，并进行配对强度与温度扫描以及近似集中度匹配分析。

Result: 在τ=4 m时干预改变3.0%的路线级离线选择，对照减联合的记录轨迹遗憾为-0.026和-0.118，交叉和种子t区间跨零；τ=1 m时87.9%的路线评估相对代价变化、8.1%的路线级离线选择变化。集中度匹配前描述性结果偏向对照，匹配后差距大部分消失，剩余对比为+0.112和-0.047且区间跨零。actor-level预测指标保持不变，配对/温度扫描显示决策对比随配对移除和条件化变锐而增大。

Conclusion: 配对结构确实会改变规划器决策，即使actor-level指标不变；但匹配分析无法将学习模式AV-交通配对的记录结果效应与条件集中度的伴随变化区分开来。

Abstract: Joint motion forecasts pair each autonomous-vehicle (AV) future with surrounding traffic, but actor-level metrics do not show whether that structure matters to a planner. We study this question with a marginal-preserving product control that removes AV-traffic pairing among the learned modes while retaining the fixed constant-velocity pair and holding trajectories, actor-level marginals before planner conditioning, candidates, the cost terms and weights, and fallback fixed. The intervention also changes candidate-conditioned concentration. Across twelve runs on 1,400 held-out Argoverse 2 scenarios, the intervention changes 3.0% of route-level offline selections at $τ=4$ m. Control-minus-joint recorded-trajectory regret is $-0.026$ and $-0.118$ at the two training sizes; crossed and seed-$t$ intervals span zero. At $τ=1$ m, relative costs change in 87.9% of route evaluations and route-level offline selections in 8.1%. Before concentration matching, descriptive outcome estimates favor the control. Most of this gap disappears along an approximate concentration-matching path; the remaining contrasts are $+0.112$ and $-0.047$, and both crossed intervals span zero. Actor-level forecast metrics remain identical. Pairing-strength and temperature sweeps show that the decision contrast grows with pairing removal and sharper conditioning. The intervention changes planner decisions even though actor-level metrics remain unchanged. The matching analysis, however, cannot separate any recorded-outcome effect of learned-mode AV-traffic pairing from the accompanying change in conditioned concentration.

</details>


### [201] [IMPACT-VLA: Interaction-aware Multimodal Propagation Attribution via Counterfactual Trajectories for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15005)
*Jinwoong Kim,Sangjin Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出IMPACT-VLA，一种基于反事实轨迹的交互感知多模态传播归因方法，用于揭示VLA策略中视觉、本体状态与语言指令在不同执行阶段的贡献及其跨阶段依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有归因方法仅测量局部敏感度或时间聚合重要性，难以捕捉阶段依赖的贡献与跨阶段依赖，也无法说明各模态在哪个执行阶段对任务成功起作用，以及输入干预如何沿后续状态、观测和动作传播。

Method: IMPACT-VLA从成功参考轨迹的动作转换中构建行为阶段，并将其与策略查询边界对齐，定义"阶段-模态块"为归因单元；通过闭环反事实重执行量化每个块对最终任务成功的贡献；进一步分析跨阶段非加性交互与轨迹传播，并区分行为恢复与功能恢复。

Result: 在30个LIBERO机器人操作任务（OpenVLA-OFT）上，25个任务（83.3%）出现主导模态转换；闭环归因比静态动作扰动更忠实识别任务关键信息；早期阶段输入替换下，负交互对的后期块边际增益约增加3.3倍；功能恢复可在无行为恢复的情况下发生。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了多模态输入在何时支持任务成功，以及其贡献在闭环执行过程中如何产生条件耦合，为VLA策略的可解释性与阶段化归因提供了新工具。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies perform robot manipulation tasks using multimodal inputs such as visual observations, proprioceptive states, and language instructions. However, it remains unclear at which execution stages each modality contributes to final task success and how input interventions propagate through subsequent states, observations, and actions. Existing attribution approaches primarily measure local sensitivity or temporally aggregated importance, limiting their ability to capture phase-dependent contributions and cross-phase dependencies. We propose Interaction-aware Multimodal Propagation Attribution via Counterfactual Trajectories for Vision-Language-Action Policies (IMPACT-VLA). IMPACT-VLA constructs behavioral phases from action transitions in a successful reference rollout, aligns them with policy query boundaries, and defines phase-modality blocks as attribution units. It then performs closed-loop counterfactual re-execution to quantify each block's contribution to final task success. We further analyze cross-phase non-additive interactions and trajectory propagation while distinguishing behavioral from functional recovery. Across 30 LIBERO robot manipulation tasks using OpenVLA-OFT, dominant-modality transitions occurred in 25 tasks (83.3%), and closed-loop attribution identified task-critical information more faithfully than Static Action Perturbation. Later-block marginal gains for negatively interacting pairs increased by approximately 3.3x under early-phase input replacement, while functional recovery could occur without behavioral recovery. These results reveal when multimodal inputs support task success and how their contributions become conditionally coupled during closed-loop execution.

</details>


### [202] [Atomic Motion Coordinate for Language-Steerable and Force-Responsive Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15012)
*Jiaqi Zhai,Jingkai Zhao,Chen Yang,Siyuan Ma,Yutian Zhang,Liwen Yang,Qinglian Wu,Weiqi Fan,Yifei Wang,Yi Zheng,Chenxi Gu,Dong Wei,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Atomic Motion Coordinate（AMC），一种基于几何的坐标表示，用于实现可操控且力响应式的 VLA 操作策略，通过语言指令即可重定向末端执行器动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究核心问题是：仅改变语言指令能否重定向 VLA 策略的末端执行器，还是视觉驱动的运动先验占主导地位。作者希望让策略动作在语言引导下更具可操控性，并能响应接触力。

Method: 每条机械臂拥有十三个带符号的平移、旋转和保持原子，这些原子由文本与前向运动学在屏蔽视觉条件下建立；该坐标通过加权码本对齐注入每个动作专家模块。接触历史通过有界球面残差调制同一坐标，从固定名义隐变量重新计算，仅生成未执行的剩余时段。

Result: 7,520 次离线时段干预中，对立原子分离度达到 92.5/83.1%（单臂/双臂），而 LA4VLA 风格方法仅为 39.1/24.0%。每任务 50 次真机试验中，AMC 将 OOD 水果任务进度从 60.5% 提升至 87.8%；力适应将 Plug/Vase 从 59.0/71.5% 提升至 78.5/75.2%。

Conclusion: AMC 表明语言指令可有效重定向 VLA 动作，几何接地坐标显著优于视觉运动先验主导的方法，并提升了分布外任务与力敏感任务的性能。

Abstract: Can changing only the language instruction redirect a VLA policy's end effector, or does the visually driven motion prior dominate? We present Atomic Motion Coordinate, a geometry-grounded coordinate for steerable and force-responsive manipulation. Each arm owns thirteen signed translation, rotation, and hold atoms grounded from text and forward kinematics with vision withheld, and the coordinate is injected into every action-expert block via weighted codebook alignment. Contact history modulates the same coordinate through a bounded spherical residual that is recomputed from a fixed nominal latent to regenerate only the unexecuted horizon suffix. Across 7,520 offline horizon interventions, opposite-atom separation reaches 92.5/83.1% (single/dual) versus 39.1/24.0% for LA4VLA-style. Across 50 real-robot trials per task, AMC raises OOD fruit progress from 60.5% to 87.8%; force adaptation raises Plug/Vase from 59.0/71.5% to 78.5/75.2%.

</details>


### [203] [Steering Generative Robot Policies with Lexicographic Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15014)
*Yixuan Jia,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在推理阶段对冻结的生成式机器人策略（扩散或流匹配）进行引导的方法，使其遵守按字典序排列的部署目标优先级，无需修改策略权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练生成式机器人策略虽能产生有效行为，但部署时的需求与偏好可能未在训练中出现，且不同部署中这些目标的优先级顺序会变化（如先满足具身可行性约束，再由用户偏好引导）。因此需要一种能在推理时按优先级顺序满足多重目标的方法。

Method: 对采样器做两处修改：(1) 对采样轨迹施加动态障碍引导，约束低优先级更新，使高优先级代价（一阶上）不增加；(2) 用级联方式按各优先级依次过滤候选样本，选择最终执行样本。策略权重保持不变。

Result: 在导航基准上，方法提升了成功率、可通行性与偏好合规性，且合规性显著优于调参的加权和基线；在 LIBERO 的流匹配操作策略上同样有效，提升合规性且不降低任务成功率；受控操作研究表明，在固定权重可匹配目标顺序的场景下，动态障碍能在更宽的参数范围内达到相当的最佳性能。

Conclusion: 冻结的生成式机器人策略可通过推理时的动态障碍引导与级联样本筛选，按字典序优先级满足部署目标，效果优于加权和基线且可跨扩散与流匹配、跨导航与操作任务迁移。

Abstract: Pretrained generative robot policies can produce effective behaviors across diverse environments, but deployment can lead to requirements and preferences that may not have been represented during training. Furthermore, at deployment, an operator, user, or application may assign these requirements and preferences a priority order that can vary across deployments. For example, embodiment-specific feasibility constraints may need to be satisfied first, while user-specific preferences guide behavior among the feasible options. We show that a frozen generative robot policy---based on either diffusion or flow matching---can be steered at inference time to respect such lexicographically ordered deployment objectives.
  To achieve this, we introduce two modifications to the sampler. First, we apply dynamic-barrier guidance to sampled trajectories, constraining lower-priority updates so that higher-priority costs do not increase (up to first order). Second, we select the executed sample using a cascade that successively filters candidate samples according to each priority level. The policy weights remain unchanged.
  On a navigation benchmark, we demonstrate that our method improves success, traversability, and preference compliance over the frozen policy, and achieves substantially better compliance than tuned weighted-sum baselines. The same method transfers to a flow-matching manipulation policy on LIBERO, where it improves compliance without reducing task success. A controlled manipulation study further shows that, in settings where a fixed weight can match the desired ordering, the dynamic barrier reaches comparable best performance over a substantially wider range of parameter settings.

</details>


### [204] [Task-Distribution-Aware Counterweight Synthesis and Constrained Co-Design for Serial Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15082)
*Mohammad Abbadi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种任务分布感知的被动配重综合框架，将操作分布显式引入设计，给出配重矩的闭式最优解，并以三连杆机械臂验证不同任务分布下最优配重相差超40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动配重是简单的重力补偿器，但从单一位姿选出的配重对机械臂实际执行的各种构型和任务通常并非最优，因此需要将操作分布纳入设计过程。

Method: 建立任务分布感知的综合框架，令操作分布ρ(q)显式进入设计；对配重矩p=m_c r_c，用加权均方残余重力矩求闭式最优解；若负载重力矩对负载质量仿射，则最优配重也仿射；固定静矩下分析配重惯性与质量的欠定关系；以三连杆机械臂为案例，比较不同任务分布的最优质量，并用非支配前沿、额定扭矩筛选和集中点质量轨迹进行研究。

Result: 在r_c=0.20 m时，零负载等效最优质量随分布变化：均匀关节空间0.672 kg、近似均匀任务空间0.683 kg、典型取放任务0.713 kg、高重力偏置分布0.952 kg，变化超40%；额定扭矩筛选使零负载可行任务空间覆盖率从78.1%提升到93.7%；轨迹研究给出从无配重到更强补偿的暂定过渡。

Conclusion: 配重最优设计强烈依赖实际操作分布，质量-半径选择需给定工程约束才有定解；这些执行器与动力学结果属于工程后果研究，而非物理验证。

Abstract: Passive counterweights are simple gravity compensators, but a counterweight selected from a single pose is not generally optimal for the configurations and tasks a manipulator actually executes. This paper develops a task-distribution-aware synthesis framework in which the operating distribution $ρ(q)$ enters the design explicitly. For a counterweight moment $p=m_c r_c$ with gravity torque $-gpφ(q)$, the weighted mean-square residual gravity torque has the closed-form minimizer $p^*=E_ρ[τ_gφ]/(gE_ρ[φ^2])$. If payload gravity torque is affine in payload mass, the optimum is also affine: $p^*(m_p,ρ)=p_0^*(ρ)+m_pK_p(ρ)$. For fixed static moment, added counterweight inertia is $I_c=pr_c$ while mass is $m_c=p/r_c$, so mass-radius selection is underdetermined unless physical constraints are specified. A recovered three-link manipulator is used as a case study. At $r_c=0.20$ m, zero-payload equivalent optima are 0.672 kg for uniform joint-space operation, 0.683 kg for approximately uniform task-space operation, 0.713 kg for a representative pick-and-place family, and 0.952 kg for a high-gravity-biased distribution, a change of more than 40% caused solely by the operating distribution. Nondominated fronts show that preferred mass-radius pairs depend on declared engineering bounds. A rated-torque-referenced all-joint screen increases zero-payload feasible task-space coverage from 78.1% without compensation to 93.7% for the uniform-distribution design. A lumped point-mass trajectory study gives a provisional crossover from no counterweight at very aggressive motion to stronger compensation as motion slows. These actuator and dynamic results are engineering consequence studies rather than physical validation.

</details>


### [205] [Legislating World-Model-Based Planning with Legal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15113)
*Dylan Waldner,Yiannis Kantaros,Guido Governatori,Risto Miikkulainen,Amir Banifatemi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: As robotic systems grow more general, legal norms are needed to integrate them into society. This paper extends the isomorphism problem of aligning legal source texts with their encodings, and measures two key challenges to robot normative control: (1) the \textit{grounding isomorphism gap}, where perception error grounds false atoms for legal reasoning, and (2) the \textit{ontological isomorphism gap}, where one legal conclusion admits many faithful translations into planning constraints. The paper introduces a legal planning stack that employs Defeasible Deontic Logic (DDL) to constrain a motion planner. The stack leverages learned world models to plan and to provide legal context, enabling \textit{ex ante} governance that intervenes before an illegal action is executed. It was deployed on a simulated robot arm pushing a cube across a $3\times3$ grid. The findings were (1) the legislated agent abided substantially more often than the non-legislated one, and modeling perception uncertainty lifted abidance even further, (2) the legal reasoning ran efficiently at runtime and its verdicts were auditable, and (3) the stack adapted to exogenous signals and endogenous rule changes. Both gaps were measured: (4) world model and probe error corrupted the factual input for the DDL reasoner, and (5) a single law admitted several faithful metric interpretations yielding drastically different abidance. Thus, \textit{ex ante} legislation functions as intended, and closing these gaps with a standardized mapping from the law to runtime constraints and improved fact grounding from perception will yield robust laws that align robot behavior with society's norms.

</details>


### [206] [C$^2$Nav: Compare Before You Commit for Zero-Shot Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15142)
*Runtian Zheng,Congpeng Zhang,Ying Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 C2Nav，一种免训练的零样本 VLN-CE 框架，让 VLM 只做"比较式"判断，几何与执行留在物理侧，在 R2R-CE 上取得 31.0%–44.0% SR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本 VLN-CE 系统通常让 VLM 输出基数型结果（路点、像素、朝向、进度值或绝对到达判定），把生成式回答与几何量级或不可逆决策耦合在一起，容易出错且难以纠正。作者希望探索一种互补的"模型—机器人"接口：由 VLM 比较控制器构造的候选方案，而几何、阈值、动作幅度与执行仍由物理侧负责。

Method: 提出免训练框架 C2Nav，包含三个协同模块：(1) Seeing——在物理校验过的候选视图中进行序数式"注视选举"；(2) Remembering——维护紧凑路线草图并比较相邻指令段假设；(3) Arriving——结合犹豫阶梯、回看比较与可撤销回退实现可靠停止。

Result: 在 OpenNav R2R-CE 100 协议上，Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 版 C2Nav 取得 41.0% OSR、31.0% SR、16.7% SPL；同一接口配标准 GPT-5.5 模型达到 54.0% OSR、44.0% SR、29.0% SPL。整模块消融显示去掉 Seeing、Remembering、Arriving 后 SR 分别降至 14.0%、25.0%、29.0%；把比较式回答替换为基数/绝对式问题的对照实验，在空间、过渡、终止槽位上 SR 分别降至 12.0%、28.0%、21.0%。

Conclusion: 受限的决策接口与更强的 VLM 推理能力是互补关系，而非可互相替代；比较式接口设计本身对零样本 VLN-CE 性能有实质性贡献。

Abstract: Zero-shot vision-and-language navigation in continuous environments (VLN-CE) increasingly places foundation vision-language models (VLMs) inside the navigation loop. Existing systems commonly request cardinal outputs such as waypoints, pixels, headings, progress values, or absolute arrival decisions, coupling a generative response to geometric magnitude or an irreversible commitment. We study a complementary model-robot interface: the VLM compares controller-constructed alternatives, while geometry, thresholds, action magnitude, and execution remain on the physical side. We instantiate this idea in C2Nav, a training-free framework with three coordinated faculties. Seeing performs ordinal Gaze Election over physically vetted candidate views; Remembering maintains a compact route sketch and compares adjacent instruction-leg hypotheses; and Arriving combines a hesitation ladder, look-back comparison, and revocable walk-back for reliable stopping. On the public OpenNav R2R-CE 100 protocol, C2Nav with Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct obtains 41.0% OSR, 31.0% SR, and 16.7% SPL, while the same interface with the standard GPT-5.5 model reaches 54.0% OSR, 44.0% SR, and 29.0% SPL. Whole-faculty ablations reduce SR to 14.0% without Seeing, 25.0% without Remembering, and 29.0% without Arriving. Matched role inversions that replace only the comparative answer form with cardinal/absolute questions reduce SR to 12.0%, 28.0%, and 21.0% in the spatial, transition, and terminal slots, respectively. The results indicate that a constrained decision interface and stronger VLM reasoning are complementary rather than interchangeable.

</details>


### [207] [LieSpline-DP: Lie-Group B-Spline Diffusion Policy for Smooth Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15162)
*Erxuan Xie,Bang Liu,Pingyun Nie,Xingkai Liu,Zhuang Fu,Bo Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LieSpline-DP，一种在SE(3)李群上生成的B样条扩散策略，通过共享相邻动作块的边界控制位姿保证全程C²连续，显著降低轨迹抖动并提升真实机器人任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略（DP）虽是强大的机器人示教学习方法，但其轨迹不连续、不平滑。现有基于样条的动作表示虽能提升单个动作块内的平滑性，却无法保证跨块C²连续，也未考虑SE(3)的群结构。

Method: 提出LieSpline-DP：一种李群B样条扩散策略，直接在SE(3)上生成末端执行器轨迹，并通过共享相邻规划的边界控制位姿将连续动作块耦合起来，从而保证整条规划轨迹的C²连续性。

Result: 在三个真实机器人任务中，LieSpline-DP相比DP基线产生更低的轨迹抖动和更高的任务成功率。在涉及液体和柔性物体的真实任务中提升尤为明显：倒液和挂桶任务成功率均达100%，而DP基线分别仅为10%和30%。

Conclusion: 通过在SE(3)上以B样条表示并结合跨块边界控制位姿共享，LieSpline-DP实现了全程C²连续的平滑轨迹，在真实机器人操作任务中优于扩散策略基线，尤其适用于液体与柔性物体等对平滑性敏感的场景。

Abstract: Diffusion Policy (DP) is a powerful Learning from Demonstration (LfD) method for robotic manipulation, yet it suffers from discontinuous and non-smooth trajectories. Spline-based action representations promote smooth motion within individual action chunks, but existing spline-based methods neither guarantee cross-chunk $C^2$ continuity nor account for the group structure of $\mathrm{SE}(3)$. We therefore propose LieSpline-DP, a Lie-group B-spline diffusion policy that generates end-effector trajectories directly on $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ and couples consecutive plans by sharing their boundary control poses, ensuring $C^2$ continuity throughout the entire planned trajectory. Across three real-robot tasks, LieSpline-DP produces lower trajectory jerk and higher task success rates than the DP baseline. The gains are particularly pronounced in real-world tasks involving liquids and flexible objects: in our real-robot experiments, LieSpline-DP achieved a 100% success rate on both pouring and bucket hooking, whereas the DP baseline achieved only 10% and 30%, respectively.

</details>


### [208] [HarnessVLN: Unifying Training-Free Embodied Navigation through an Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15195)
*Yang Chen,Lirong Che,Zhenyu Huang,Wenbo Fu,Chuang Wang,Xu Cao,Daqi Liu,Yuzhe Yang,Jian Su,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HarnessVLN是一个零样本、免训练的具身导航框架，通过统一的Agent Harness协调感知、检索、定位、导航、恢复与终止，并借助分层事件记忆与时空图验证规划动作，在多个基准上超越免训练SOTA，并实现人形机器人真实部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身导航需要智能体理解视觉观察、积累空间知识并执行动作以遵循指令或定位目标。基于训练的方法面临泛化挑战；免训练方法虽利用多模态大模型，但通常缺乏机制来协调所提动作与空间证据、任务进度及执行失败之间的关系。

Method: 提出HarnessVLN，一个零样本、免训练框架。其Agent Harness通过统一工具接口协调感知、检索、定位、导航、恢复和终止，将规划器提案与空间证据、几何可行性和子目标一致性进行验证，并将结构化工具反馈纳入后续决策。分层事件记忆跟踪任务进度与执行历史，持久时空图维护可复用的空间证据和失败标注以支持验证与恢复。可替换的导航执行器将验证后的目标转换为可执行运动，使同一Harness协议同时支持指令跟随和目标导航。

Result: 在R2R、RxR、HM3D-v2和HM3D-OVON上分别达到60.8%、53.9%、76.0%和59.3%的成功率，超越此前免训练SOTA结果。人形机器人部署进一步证明其在真实环境中对两类任务的适用性。

Conclusion: HarnessVLN通过统一的规划验证、记忆与时空图机制，有效弥补了免训练MLLM导航中动作与证据脱节的问题，在多个基准及真实人形平台上验证了其泛化性与实用性。

Abstract: Embodied navigation requires agents to interpret visual observations, accumulate spatial knowledge, and execute actions to follow instructions or locate objects. Training-based methods face generalization challenges, while training-free methods exploit multimodal large language models (MLLMs) but often lack mechanisms to reconcile proposed actions with spatial evidence, task progress, and execution failures. We present HarnessVLN, a zero-shot, training-free framework whose Agent Harness coordinates perception, retrieval, grounding, navigation, recovery, and termination through a unified tool interface. The Harness validates planner proposals against spatial evidence, geometric feasibility, and subgoal consistency, incorporating structured tool feedback into subsequent decisions. Hierarchical event memory tracks task progress and execution history, while a persistent Spatiotemporal Graph maintains reusable spatial evidence and failure annotations for verification and recovery. A replaceable Navigation Executor converts validated targets into executable motions, allowing the same Harness protocol to support instruction-following and object-goal navigation. HarnessVLN achieves success rates of 60.8%, 53.9%, 76.0%, and 59.3% on R2R, RxR, HM3D-v2, and HM3D-OVON, respectively, surpassing prior training-free SOTA results. Humanoid deployment further demonstrates its applicability to both tasks in real-world environments. The project page is: https://harnessvln.netlify.app/.

</details>


### [209] [PredTac: Learning Contact-Rich Manipulation with Predicted Touch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15198)
*Weijia Fan,Daqiang Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 PredTac 框架，用从视觉与机器人状态预测的触觉替代真实触觉传感器输入，在仿真与真机接触丰富任务中逼近实测触觉性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实触觉传感器带来硬件、标定、同步与维护成本，增加策略学习与部署难度，因此作者探索"预测触觉"作为实测触觉输入的替代方案。

Method: 先用触觉监督训练触觉预测器，从因果视觉观测和机器人状态推断触觉状态；随后将预测触觉作为显式接口用于下游策略训练与执行，无需实测触觉输入。在三个接触丰富任务（USB、Barbed-spike、Valve）上开展仿真与真机评估，并通过固定策略干预分析性能对预测接触内容的依赖。

Result: 仿真目标偏移下，预测触觉策略在 USB、Barbed-spike、Valve 成功率分别为 27.0%、52.0%、44.7%，较纯视觉基线提升 8.0–13.7 个百分点。真机上预测触觉 ACT 在三任务成功率分别为 70.0%、50.0%、90.0%，均值 70.0%，接近实测触觉 ACT 的 72.2%，远高于视觉 ACT 的 21.1%。固定策略干预显示性能对预测接触的空间结构敏感，固定数值分布下的空间重排使 Valve 成功率下降 10.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: 预测触觉可为接触丰富操作提供有用的接触信息，无需将触觉传感作为策略输入，从而降低硬件与部署成本。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation benefits from tactile feedback, yet physical tactile sensors introduce hardware, calibration, synchronization, and maintenance costs that complicate policy learning and deployment. We formulate predicted touch as an alternative to measured tactile input and present PredTac, a framework that learns to infer tactile states from causal visual observations and robot states and uses the predicted touch as an explicit interface for policy learning and execution. A tactile predictor is first trained with tactile supervision and then used to provide contact information without requiring measured tactile input during downstream policy training or execution. We evaluate PredTac across three contact-rich manipulation tasks in simulation and on a real robot, and further examine how policy performance depends on the predicted contact content. In simulation goal-offset evaluations, predicted-touch policies achieve 27.0%, 52.0%, and 44.7% success on USB, Barbed-spike, and Valve, respectively, improving over the visual baseline by 8.0-13.7 percentage points. On the real robot, predicted-touch ACT achieves 70.0%, 50.0%, and 90.0% success on USB insertion, Barbed extraction, and Valve rotation, respectively, with a three-task mean of 70.0%, approaching measured-touch ACT at 72.2% and substantially outperforming visual ACT at 21.1%. Fixed-policy interventions further show that performance is sensitive to the spatial structure of predicted contact, with spatial rearrangement at fixed value distributions reducing Valve success by 10.7 percentage points. These results demonstrate that predicted touch can provide useful contact information for contact-rich manipulation without requiring tactile sensing as a policy input.

</details>


### [210] [X-WBC: A Cross-Embodiment Foundation Model for Humanoid Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15213)
*Juntong Zhang,Chun Gu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出跨本体全身控制基础框架X-WBC，将共享的人体运动语义与特定本体的物理执行分离，实现多机器人联合训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常为每个机器人单独训练策略，导致运动经验在不同本体间相互孤立；向通用部署扩展全身控制需要大规模人体运动语料和跨机器人共享的训练经验。

Method: 以人为中心的命令token对齐完整人体运动、机器人参考运动和稀疏VR观测；用因果Transformer从多机器人混合rollout中学习可复用的时序结构；轻量级机器人专属模块将共享表示映射到各机器人本体感知与动作空间。

Result: 在九个仿真本体、外部运动和四个真实机器人上实验：联合训练提升跟踪性能，对齐表示支持跨命令源的一致控制，学习策略在训练语料之外仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 结果支持将异构人形机器人作为联合数据源，确立跨本体联合训练是通向全身控制基础模型的实用路径。

Abstract: Scaling humanoid whole-body control toward general-purpose deployment requires large human motion corpora and training experience shared across robot bodies. Existing methods usually train one policy per robot, leaving motion experience isolated across embodiments. We introduce X-WBC, a cross-embodiment foundation framework that separates relatively shared human motion semantics from embodiment-specific physical execution. Human-centered command tokens align full human motion, robot reference motion, and sparse VR observations. A causal Transformer learns reusable temporal structure from mixed multi-robot rollouts, while lightweight robot-specific modules map the shared representation to each robot's proprioception and action space. Across nine simulated embodiments, external motions, and four real robots, experiments show that joint training improves tracking, the aligned representation supports consistent control across command sources, and the learned policy remains competitive beyond the training corpus. These results support heterogeneous humanoids as joint data sources and establish cross-embodiment joint training as a practical route toward whole-body control foundation models.

</details>


### [211] [A Vision Based Framework Integrating Attention and Action Cues for Interpretable Cognitive Workload Assessment in Human Robot Collaborative Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15232)
*Junyan Xionga,Naiyi Feng,Xingke Xia,Qihang Fan,Suchang Chen,Daqiang Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于视觉的注意力-动作框架（HRC-CWL），用于人机协作装配中连续、可解释的工作负荷评估，无需穿戴生理传感器，实验验证其与生理信号具有显著关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协作装配中操作员需实时协调手动任务、任务信息与机器人行为，工作负荷需求动态波动；但传统工作负荷评估依赖穿戴式生理传感器，部署复杂、实用性受限，因此需要一种无需额外传感器的连续、可解释评估方法。

Method: 构建基于视觉的注意力-动作框架：融合RGB-D观测、机器人状态与标定的任务相关区域，形成经时间确认的操作员行为表征；该表征可识别任务需求集中位置，并解释当注意力与动作分离、任务情境变化或操作员犹豫时需求如何演变。

Result: 在十名参与者、三级协作变速箱装配实验中，以主观评分和同步生理信号为独立参考进行验证：NASA-TLX评分确认工作负荷随条件递增，整体及心理、时间维度效应显著；视觉推导的HRC-CWL输出在九名参与者中七名与ECG特征显著相关；同步交互片段显示检测到的犹豫与生理活动在时间上对应；实时部署证明无需穿戴额外传感器即可运行。

Conclusion: HRC-CWL可作为认知工效分析与自适应机器人辅助的可解释行为代理指标，而非工作负荷的直接心理生理测量。

Abstract: The introduction of human-robot collaboration (HRC) in industrial assembly operations is revolutionizing the manufacturing landscape. In this evolving environment, operators are required to seamlessly coordinate their manual tasks with real-time task information and robotic behaviors. These demands fluctuate during operation, yet conventional workload assessments depend on body-worn physiological sensors that complicate practical deployment. Here, we present a vision-based attention--action framework for continuous and interpretable workload-related assessment in HRC assembly. The framework combines RGB-D observations with robot states and calibrated task-related areas to construct a temporally confirmed representation of operator behavior. This representation identifies where task demand is concentrated and explains how it develops when attention and action diverge, the task context changes, or the operator hesitates. We evaluated the framework in a three-level collaborative gearbox assembly experiment with ten participants, using subjective ratings and synchronized physiological signals as independent references. Raw NASA-TLX ratings confirmed increasing perceived workload across conditions, with significant effects on overall workload and its mental and temporal dimensions. The vision-derived HRC-CWL output was significantly associated with ECG-derived features in seven of nine participants with complete correlation data. Synchronized interaction episodes further showed temporal correspondence between detected hesitation and physiological activity. Real-time deployment demonstrated that the framework can operate without requiring operators to wear additional sensors. These findings support HRC-CWL as an interpretable behavioral proxy for cognitive ergonomics analysis and adaptive robot assistance, rather than a direct psychophysiological measure of workload.

</details>


### [212] [Distributed Safe Cooperative Vector Field for Trajectory Curvature Constrained Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15266)
*Zhouru Xiao,Tao Teng,Weijia Yao,Hui Zhang,Xiangke Wang,Yaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分布式安全协同向量场方法，用于受轨迹曲率约束的多机器人系统，实现协同路径跟随与安全避碰，并通过仿真和实物实验验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际多机器人系统的机器人转向能力有限，存在固有的轨迹曲率约束。若忽略这些约束，机器人可能无法完成分配任务，且轨迹会偏离预期路径。因此需要一种能够显式考虑曲率约束的协同控制方法。

Method: 方法由两部分向量场组成：一是协同向量场，用于处理多机器人协同运动；二是安全导向的避碰向量场，其具有自适应可调的反应边界，以适应机器人的运动学曲率约束，保证避碰机动在物理上可行。此外，所提向量场仅需每个邻居机器人的单个虚拟变量，即可实现协同运动并确保避障与机器人间避碰。

Result: 通过仿真以及在实际多机器人平台上的实物实验，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提分布式安全协同向量场方法能够在曲率约束下实现多机器人的协同路径跟随与安全避碰，且通信需求低，具备实际可行性。

Abstract: Trajectory curvature constraints are inherent in practical multi-robot systems due to the limited turning capabilities of the robots. Without properly accounting for these constraints, robots may fail to accomplish assigned tasks, and their trajectories may diverge from the intended paths. This paper proposes a distributed safe cooperative vector field approach for multi-robot systems subject to trajectory curvature constraints. The proposed approach is composed of a cooperative vector field and a safety-oriented collision avoidance vector field, aiming to address the problems of cooperative motion and safe collision avoidance in multi-robot path-following tasks. A safety-oriented collision avoidance vector field with adaptively adjustable reactive boundary is developed to accommodate the kinematic curvature constraints of robots, thereby ensuring the physical feasibility of collision avoidance maneuvers. The proposed vector field requires only a single virtual variable from each neighboring robot to achieve cooperative motion and ensure both obstacle avoidance and inter-robot collision avoidance. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is validated through both simulations and real-world experiments on an actual multi-robot platform.

</details>


### [213] [Low Clearance Hinge Joint Mechanism Based on 3D Printing on Sheet Fabrication Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15276)
*Jaehyung Jang,Euibin Shin,Allison M. Okamura,Jee-Hwan Ryu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"薄板3D打印"工艺的低间隙铰链关节机构，简化了铰链制造并摆脱传统折纸工艺对胶粘剂的依赖，适用于数十厘米尺度的机器人。通过对比三种铰链机构并分析扭转角与弯曲力，实现了0.1 mm间隙，实验验证塑料变形扭矩足以约束不需要的自由度，并成功应用于3自由度delta机械臂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统折纸式制造在装配时通常需要使用胶粘剂，工艺复杂且存在局限，难以适用于数十厘米尺度的机器人。作者希望简化铰链机构的制造，同时实现低间隙、高精度的运动性能。

Method: 采用"薄板3D打印"制造方法；对比三种铰链关节机构的优缺点，选择可针对不同面板厚度设计低间隙的铰链；对所选铰链的扭转角与弯曲力进行分析，实现0.1 mm间隙；通过实验评估扭转阻力，测量由塑性变形和间隙引起的扭矩。

Result: 实现了0.1 mm的间隙；实验表明，塑性变形产生的扭矩足以约束铰链关节不需要的自由度，而由间隙引起的扭转所需扭矩极小。将该铰链应用于3自由度delta机械臂，展示了低间隙下的精确运动。

Conclusion: 所提出的低间隙铰链关节机构可简化制造、免除胶粘剂使用，并凭借塑性变形扭矩约束多余自由度，在数十厘米尺度机器人中实现精确运动，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: This paper presents a low-clearance hinge joint mechanism based on the 3D printing on sheet fabrication method. This approach simplifies the fabrication of hinge mechanisms and overcomes limitations of conventional origami manufacturing by eliminating the need for adhesives commonly used during assembly, making it suitable for robots at the tens-of-centimeters scale. The advantages and disadvantages of three types of hinge joint mechanisms are compared, and a hinge joint that can be designed with low clearance for various facet thicknesses is selected. Based on the selected hinge joint, the twisting angle and bending force are analyzed, leading to the implementation of a clearance of 0.1 mm. Torsional resistance is experimentally evaluated to measure the torque required for twisting caused by plastic deformation and clearance. The results show that the torque associated with plastic deformation is sufficient to constrain the undesired degrees of freedom of the hinge joint, while the torque required for twisting due to clearance is minimal. Based on the analyzed data, the proposed hinge joint mechanism is applied to a 3-degree-of-freedom delta robot manipulator, demonstrating precise motion with low clearance.

</details>


### [214] [Planning in the Backbone: DiffAdapterVLA for Native Continuous Trajectory Generation with Driving VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15322)
*Changxin Lu,Xiaoliang Meng,Yu Wu,Rui Huang,Honglin Li,Tao Chen,Kaixuan Zhou,Yadong Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DiffAdapterVLA，将轨迹token注入VLM后层，实现"骨干内的规划"，通过轻量层间DiffAdapter递归精化轨迹，在NAVSIM上以低延迟、少参数实现高质量闭环规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶视觉语言模型虽整合视觉、路线、语言与驾驶上下文形成丰富先验，但其表示目标与连续驾驶规划相分离。现有方法通常在VLM形成最终条件后才开始生成轨迹，使深度维度的条件计算游离于轨迹状态的逐步形成过程之外。

Method: DiffAdapterVLA将显式轨迹token注入VLM选定的后层，使轨迹状态进入骨干前向计算，并在不同深度与驾驶条件协同演化。轻量层间DiffAdapter将计算组织为递归轨迹精化，非对称联合注意力保持从条件流到轨迹规划的有向引导。规划内置于骨干计算中，仅适配轻量轨迹模块即可将现有驾驶先验转化为高效连续规划能力。

Result: NAVSIM实验结果（已省略具体数值）显示，该方法以少量可训练参数实现高质量闭环规划，并保持较低的端到端延迟。

Conclusion: 在VLM后层计算中联合演化轨迹状态与深度维度驾驶条件，可有效实现连续轨迹规划，为驾驶先验到连续规划的转化提供高效路径。

Abstract: Pretrained driving vision-language models (VLMs) integrate visual, route, language, and driving context into rich driving priors, yet their representation objectives remain separated from continuous driving planning. Existing methods typically begin trajectory generation only after the VLM has formed a final condition, leaving depth-wise condition computation outside the stepwise formation of trajectory state. We introduce DiffAdapterVLA, which realizes Planning in the Backbone: it injects explicit trajectory tokens into selected VLM late layers, bringing trajectory state into backbone forward computation, where it co-evolves with driving conditions at different depths. Lightweight layer-wise DiffAdapters organize this computation into recursive trajectory refinement, while asymmetric joint attention preserves directed guidance from the condition stream to trajectory planning. By placing planning within existing backbone computation rather than relying on an independent trajectory planner, DiffAdapterVLA adapts only lightweight trajectory modules to turn existing driving priors into efficient continuous planning capability. NAVSIM results show that it achieves high-quality closed-loop planning with low end-to-end latency using few trainable parameters, and demonstrate that jointly evolving trajectory state and depth-wise driving conditions in VLM late-layer computation effectively realizes continuous trajectory planning.

</details>


### [215] [Assistance Torque Estimation via Dynamics-Aware Optimization for Lower-Limb Exoskeleton in Complex Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15352)
*Xiao-Yin Liu,Guotao Li,Weiqun Wang,Zeng-Guang Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于动力学模型的辅助力矩估计方法，直接生成电机辅助力矩并训练数据驱动网络实现户外实时预测，显著降低代谢率、心率和肌肉激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实人体关节力矩估计依赖运动捕捉系统，户外可用性受限且部署成本高；同时直接将真实关节力矩缩放为电机力矩指令未必最优。受人体运动生成过程启发，需一种低成本、可户外使用的辅助力矩估计方法。

Method: 从优化角度出发，基于动力学模型直接生成电机辅助力矩，降低数据采集成本；随后训练数据驱动辅助力矩预测网络，实现复杂户外环境下的实时预测。

Result: 优化的辅助力矩与步态轨迹具有更好的相位一致性，且与任务特征更匹配。相比零力矩条件，预测力矩使代谢率降低11.8%-17.7%，心率降低8.9%-14.3%，峰值肌肉激活水平降低28.2%-54.0%。

Conclusion: 该方法为低成本自适应外骨骼辅助提供了新视角，兼顾成本、户外可用性与实时预测能力。

Abstract: Ground-truth human joint torque estimation relies on motion capture systems, which suffer from limited outdoor usability and significant deployment expenses. Furthermore, direct scaling of ground-truth joint torques to obtain motor torque commands is not necessarily the optimal strategy. To address the aforementioned limitations, inspired by the human motion generation process, this paper proposes a novel assistance torque estimation method based on the dynamic model. From an optimization perspective, the proposed method directly generates motor-assist torque and lowers the cost of data acquisition. Then, a data-driven assistance torque prediction network is trained to enable accurate real-time prediction under complex outdoor environments. Experimental results demonstrate that optimized (estimated) assistance torque exhibits better phase consistency with gait trajectories and better alignment with task characteristics. Relative to the Zero torque condition, the predicted torque can decrease metabolic rate by 11.8%-17.7%, heart rate by 8.9%-14.3%, and peak muscle activation levels by 28.2%-54.0%, respectively. This provides a new perspective for low-cost adaptive exoskeleton assistance.

</details>


### [216] [Understanding User Preferences of a Slope-Aware Variable-Admittance Filter for a Robot Guide Dog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15362)
*Federico Esposito,Mario Selvaggio,Aaron Link,Fabio Ruggiero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究坡度自适应变导纳滤波器的参数对机器导盲犬力交互中用户偏好的影响，采用田口L9实验设计，发现上坡增大刚度、下坡减小刚度可提升客观与主观指标，阻尼无显著主效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为视障人士提供基于四足机器人的物理引导（导盲犬替代方案），需要优化人机力交互的柔顺性以提升用户体验。

Method: 构建带无传感器刚性手柄的四足机器人系统，结合路径跟踪、基于动量的交互力估计，以及由深度相机提取坡度信息在线调节刚度与阻尼的变导纳滤波器；通过高保真仿真与蒙眼视力正常被试的人体实验，采用田口L9设计比较多种策略。

Result: 初步主效应结果表明，上坡增大刚度、下坡减小刚度可同时改善客观与主观指标；阻尼无显著主效应。

Conclusion: 坡度自适应的刚度调节策略对机器导盲犬的力交互体验有益，阻尼参数影响有限，为后续参数优化提供依据。

Abstract: This letter investigates how the parameters of a slope-aware variable-admittance filter influence user preferences in force-based interaction with a robotic guide dog for visually impaired individuals. The proposed system consists of a quadruped robot equipped with a sensor-free rigid handle for physical guidance. The framework combines path following, momentum-based interaction-wrench estimation, and a variable-admittance filter whose stiffness and damping are adapted online from slope information extracted by the robot's depth camera. The adaptation policies are evaluated through high-fidelity simulations and a human-subject study involving blindfolded sighted participants. Multiple strategies are compared using a Taguchi L9 design of experiments. Preliminary main-effect results suggest that increasing stiffness uphill and decreasing it downhill improves both objective and subjective metrics, whereas damping shows no significant main effect.

</details>


### [217] [From Prediction to Decision: World-Model-Guided Action Selection for Continuous Pile Excavation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15382)
*Ailing Zhang,Fan Gao,Song Zhang,Kawa Leong,Ziyu Wu,Yafei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出世界-动作模型（WAM）用于轮式装载机挖掘这一序贯决策问题，通过联合预测地形变化与装载体积对候选铲斗动作进行实时排序，在仿真与真机实验中验证了决策层面的性能提升与闭环可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮式装载机挖掘是序贯决策问题，每一次铲掘都会改变后续动作可用的地形。实际可用的世界模型必须能准确预测动作后果、实时对候选动作排序，并能嵌入全尺寸机器的闭环中运行。

Method: WAM 生成多个候选铲掘动作，剔除几何上不可行的候选，联合预测带符号的地形变化与装载体积，执行预测装载量最大的候选，并根据新观测到的地形重新规划。研究还比较了不同输入表示、空间支持与五种架构。

Result: 在32个几何不相交的MinSlope测试回合中，将世界模型排序加入匹配的扩散 proposals 后，平均铲掘次数由651.8降至540.6（降低17.1%），32/32全部完成，且每对回合均有改善。完整系统对比中，WAM完成32/32，独立训练的 soft actor-critic 策略完成29/32。ROS2/TensorRT 实现在 Jetson AGX Orin 上处理5个候选仅需72.4 ms。

Conclusion: 仿真结果证明了决策层面的收益，物理实验则证明了真实世界闭环的可行性。同时识别出一个准确且高效的物理结构预测器，并在事件不相交的全尺寸装载机数据上进一步评估了接口。

Abstract: Wheel-loader excavation is a sequential decision problem in which every scoop changes the terrain available to subsequent actions. A practical world model must predict action consequences accurately, rank candidates in real time, and operate inside the closed loop of a full-size machine. We present the World-Action Model (WAM), which proposes multiple scoops, rejects geometrically inadmissible candidates, jointly predicts signed terrain change and loaded volume, executes the candidate with the largest predicted load, and replans from the newly observed terrain. On 32 geometry-disjoint MinSlope test episodes, adding world-model ranking to matched diffusion proposals reduces the mean scoop count from 651.8 to 540.6 (17.1%), preserves 32/32 completion, and improves every paired episode. In a complete-system comparison, WAM completes 32/32 episodes versus 29/32 for an independently trained soft actor-critic policy. Comparisons of input representations, spatial support, and five architectures identify an accurate and efficient physics-structured predictor. We further evaluate the interface on event-disjoint full-size-loader data and deploy the complete perception-proposal-prediction-selection-execution loop for autonomous excavation. The ROS2/TensorRT implementation processes five candidates in 72.4 ms on a Jetson AGX Orin. The simulation results establish decision-level gains, while the physical experiments demonstrate real-world closed-loop feasibility.

</details>


### [218] [Dynamics-Informed Reinforcement Learning for Agile and Energy-Efficient Locomotion of a Monopedal Hopping Quadcopter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15399)
*Ruigang Chen,Qi Zhang,Zhicheng Zhong,Zhuorui Yun,Yizhar Or,Mingyi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出动力学信息强化学习框架，通过将目标比能量嵌入奖励，实现单腿跳跃四旋翼高效稳定高速跳跃，能耗分别降低82%和73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中腿式机器人兼具敏捷与高效，但复杂混合动力学下的高速跳跃控制困难；强化学习虽具潜力却易出现"奖励作弊"导致能效低下。

Method: 面向单腿跳跃四旋翼的动力学信息强化学习框架：在奖励中嵌入目标比能量，将优化约束到物理可行的能量流形；奖励相位一致行为以激励仿生的支撑相冲量；惩罚机电功率浪费促使电机产生高效冲量，从而无需启发式状态机即可在弹簧回弹阶段严格注入能量。MuJoCo仿真验证。

Result: 在严重的姿态-接触耦合下实现稳健的高度调节与前向速度跟踪（最高2.0 m/s）；获得高敏捷跳跃步态，相比悬停基线和低效基线分别降低能耗82%和73%。

Conclusion: 将物理能量约束与相位一致奖励融入强化学习，可有效抑制奖励作弊，实现高效、稳定、敏捷的高速跳跃控制。

Abstract: Although aerial-legged robots offer combined agility and efficiency, controlling high-speed hopping under complex hybrid dynamics is challenging. Reinforcement Learning (RL) is promising but prone to energy-inefficient "reward hacking". We propose a Dynamics-Informed RL framework for a monopedal hopping quadcopter. By embedding a target Specific Energy into the reward, we constrain the optimization to a physically viable energy manifold, ensuring stable hopping behaviour. By rewarding the phase-consistent behavior, it can encourage bio-inspired stance-phase impulse. Furthermore, penalizing the electro-mechanical power waste induces the motors generate an efficient impulse. This enables the policy to inject energy strictly during spring restitution without heuristic state machines. MuJoCo simulations validate robust height regulation and forward velocity tracking up to 2.0 m/s despite severe attitude-contact coupling. Ultimately, our approach yields a highly agile hopping gait, reducing energy consumption by 82% and 73% compared to hovering baselines and inefficiency baseline, respectively.

</details>


### [219] [Learning to Exploit Passive Dynamics for Energy-Efficient Target Hopping of a Spring-Legged Quadcopter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15447)
*Ruigang Chen,Qi Zhang,Zhicheng Zhong,Zhuorui Yun,Yizhar Or,Mingyi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种直接由估计状态到电机的PPO策略，使单足四旋翼跳跃机器人无需显式状态机或底层姿态PID即可协调推力与被动接触动力学，在硬件实验中较调优PID控制栈降低30.7%循环平均电功率和49.8%平均归一化推力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单足四旋翼结合空中推力与弹簧跳跃，适合复杂地形运动，但启发式PID调参难以协调主动推力与被动接触动力学。

Method: 提出直接估计状态到四个电机的PPO策略，不使用显式跳跃状态机与底层姿态PID；奖励由能量流形塑形（质量归一化垂直能量跟踪与顶点状态锚定）和效率塑形（基于历史感知功率估计器，惩罚一般功率使用、附加空中功率成本和空中近静止）组成。

Result: 在代表性硬件运行中，相比调优PID控制栈，PPO控制栈使循环平均实测电功率降低30.7%，平均总归一化推力降低49.8%，同时保持可重复的指令高度跳跃和更集中的着陆。

Conclusion: 结果与更好地利用被动动力学和降低实测电力需求相一致，表明直接状态到电机的PPO控制可有效协调主动推力与被动接触动力学。

Abstract: Combining aerial thrust with spring-loaded hopping makes monopedal quadcopters promising for locomotion over complex terrain, but heuristic proportional-integral-derivative (PID) tuning limits coordination between active thrust and passive contact dynamics. We present a direct estimated-state-to-motor Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) policy that commands four motors without an explicit hopping state machine or low-level attitude PID. Its reward combines Energy-Manifold Shaping for mass-normalized vertical-energy tracking and apex-state anchoring with Efficiency Shaping, which uses a history-aware power estimator to penalize general power use, impose an additional airborne-power cost, and penalize airborne near-stationarity. In representative hardware runs, the PPO-based control stack reduced cycle-averaged measured electrical power by 30.7% and mean total normalized thrust by 49.8% relative to the tuned PID-based control stack, while retaining repeatable commanded-height hopping and more concentrated landings. These observations are consistent with improved use of passive dynamics and reduced measured electrical demand.

</details>


### [220] [InterSocialBench: Benchmarking Human and LLM Preferences for Companion-Robot Social Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15455)
*Yaodan Xu,Boyang Guo,Yuqing Gu,Qingxin Zhang,Yiwen Deng,Meng Liu,Lintian Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了InterSocialBench基准，包含210个家庭场景和18种高层行为，结合100名人类参与者的偏好判断与7个大语言模型在16种人格条件下的23,520条响应，用于评估社交行为选择，强调保留个体差异而非单一共识标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伴随机器人常面临多种可行行为均合适的日常情境，但不同人偏好不同回应。现有评估往往用单一共识标签替代个体判断，忽略了人类偏好的多样性，因此需要一个能刻画分歧与偏好的基准。

Method: 构建InterSocialBench：涵盖360个（实为210个）家庭场景、18种高层行为；每位人类标注包含首选动作及明确合适与不合适的候选；采用结构化流程处理行为备选、竞争性情境线索及相关的历史与未来任务；评估区分"首选一致"与"明确拒绝"，并使用按场景分组的划分训练可训练预测器；设置频率与人格投票基线。

Result: 在测试提示下，模型与人类的行为分布存在差异，且匹配响应数量后多样性差距仍存在：人类每场景有4.68种不同选择，模型为2.06–3.46种；人类场景级多数人一致率为51.5%，说明这是分歧而非普遍预测上限。

Conclusion: InterSocialBench支持在不以单一共识标签取代个体判断的前提下评估社交行为选择，揭示了模型与人类在行为分布和多样性上的显著差距。

Abstract: Companion robots face everyday situations in which several feasible behaviors may be appropriate, yet different people prefer different responses. We introduce InterSocialBench, a benchmark of 210 domestic scenarios and 18 high-level behaviors, pairing judgments from 100 human participants with 23,520 responses from seven large language models under 16 personality conditions. Each human annotation preserves a preferred action alongside explicitly appropriate and inappropriate candidates. A structured construction pipeline covers behavioral alternatives, competing situational cues, and relevant history and future tasks. Evaluation distinguishes preferred-choice agreement from explicit rejection, using scenario-grouped splits for trainable predictors. Simple frequency and persona-voting baselines illustrate these objectives. Across the tested prompts, model and human behavior distributions differ, and the diversity gap remains after matching response counts: humans exhibit 4.68 distinct choices per scenario, compared with 2.06--3.46 for the models. Human scenario-level plurality agreement is 51.5%, describing disagreement rather than a universal prediction ceiling. InterSocialBench supports evaluating social behavior selection without replacing individual judgments with a single consensus label.

</details>


### [221] [P-POSEMEM: Projective Semantic Memory for Consistent Language Grounding under Pose-Graph Rewrites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15475)
*Ha Sier,Ali Salmasi,Mengya Xu,Haizhou Zhang,Jie Lu,Zhuo Zou,Xianjia Yu,Tomi Westerlund*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出P-POSEMEM，一种在SLAM位姿图优化、回环与压缩过程中保持语义记忆一致性的方法，通过将观测存为出生关键帧上的不可变事件并保留被边缘化关键帧的贝叶斯树消元条件，实现对位姿、锚点与身份的联合后验积分，用Dproj指标度量推理等价图间的语义分布差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人按语言指令行动时，语义记忆需在底层SLAM位姿图被优化、回环闭合与压缩时仍将同一物理对象命名为同一目标。然而传统将每次检测提交到世界坐标的地图方法存在问题：回环会移动测量所依赖的锚点，或求解器边缘化该锚点，导致查询选中不同对象，尽管两个图表示的是同一后验。

Method: P-POSEMEM将每个观测存为出生关键帧上的不可变事件，保留每个被边缘化关键帧的贝叶斯树消元条件，并对位姿、锚点与身份的联合重建后验积分语义似然。引入Dproj指标，即推理等价的完整图与边缘化图之间语言目标分布的总变差缺陷，直接度量该问题。

Result: 在40个HM3DSem场景和112,000次查询中，P-POSEMEM复现完整图oracle（Dproj = 0），并相对所有记忆削减基线减少目标翻转。在761次回环改写地图达47 m的八次运行中，当消元跟随回环时Dproj保持在10^-13以下且目标翻转0/288，而所有消融与插入时提交坐标均出现翻转；在实时有界求解器下，同一记忆翻转23/288，冻结坐标则为53/288。预注册的阴性对照被Dproj检出，同时校准误差与导航成功率不变。

Conclusion: Dproj与校准误差、导航成功率度量捕捉的是不同失效模式；检索由共享冻结检测器固定，从而将增益隔离到记忆一致性上。方法在多样场景下验证了其保持语义记忆一致性的有效性。

Abstract: A robot following language instructions needs its semantic memory to keep naming the same physical object while the SLAM pose graph underneath is optimized, loop-closed and compressed. Maps committing each detection to a world coordinate cannot: a closure moves the anchor it was measured from, or the solver marginalizes that anchor, and the query then selects a different object although both graphs represent the same posterior. P-POSEMEM stores each observation as an immutable event at its birth keyframe, retains the Bayes-tree elimination conditional of every marginalized keyframe, and integrates the semantic likelihood over the reconstructed joint posterior of poses, anchors and identities. Dproj, the total-variation defect between the language-goal distributions of inference-equivalent full and marginalized graphs, measures this directly. Over 40 HM3DSem scenes and 112,000 queries, P-POSEMEM reproduces the full-graph oracle (Dproj = 0) and reduces goal flips against every memory-reducing baseline. On an eight-run campaign whose 761 closures rewrote the map by up to 47 m, Dproj stays below 10^-13 with 0/288 goal flips when elimination follows the closures, where every ablation and a coordinate committed at insertion flip goals it does not; under a live bounded solver the same memory flips 23/288 against 53 for that frozen coordinate. A pre-registered negative control is detected by Dproj while leaving calibration error and navigation success unchanged, indicating that these measures capture distinct failure modes. Retrieval is held fixed by a shared frozen detector, isolating the gain to memory consistency. Code and data: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/posemem-2328/.

</details>


### [222] [StereoPatch: Patch-Aligned RGB-Depth Fusion for Spatial Perception in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15509)
*Yanan Zhou,Zhaoyan Qian,James Zhao,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 StereoPatch，一种补丁对齐的 RGB-D 表征，将配准的度量几何直接绑定到用于动作预测的 RGB 补丁上，在六个真机任务上超越多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉相似的场景可能因目标位置、物体高度或接触几何变化而需要不同动作。预训练 RGB 特征可能把几何上不同的状态映射为相似的策略输入；而简单加入深度则要求策略在同等的有限示教数据中额外学习 RGB-深度对应关系。

Method: 在共享的二维补丁网格上，通过非对称交叉注意力将深度信息注入对应的 RGB 特征，然后再进行动作解码，生成具有几何感知能力的 StereoPatch Tokens，可在不改变原有学习目标的前提下为通用视觉运动策略提供条件。

Result: 在六个真机任务中，闭环成功率高于仅外观、仅几何、原始 RGB-D 和后期融合等基线；在三套仿真环境中进一步评估了跨策略架构兼容性、空间泛化与工作范围限制。

Conclusion: 解决与控制相关的几何歧义，更好的做法是将深度直接与用于动作预测的视觉特征对齐，而非把深度作为独立模态输入。

Abstract: Recent advances in robot imitation learning have produced visuomotor policies that predict actions directly from visual observations. Yet visually similar scenes can require different actions as target position, object height, or contact geometry changes. Pretrained RGB features may map these geometrically distinct states to similar policy inputs, while simply adding depth requires the policy to learn RGB-depth correspondence from the same limited demonstrations used to learn control. We introduce StereoPatch, a patch-aligned RGB-depth representation that binds registered metric geometry directly to the RGB patches used for action prediction. On a shared 2-D patch grid, asymmetric cross-attention incorporates depth information into the corresponding RGB features before action decoding. The resulting StereoPatch Tokens provide a geometry-aware visual representation that can condition general visuomotor policies without changing their underlying learning objectives. Across six real-robot tasks, StereoPatch achieves higher closed-loop success than appearance-only, geometry-only, raw RGB-D, and late-fusion baselines. Additional experiments across three simulation suites evaluate compatibility across visuomotor policy architectures, spatial generalization, and operating limits. Results suggest that resolving control-relevant geometric ambiguity benefits from aligning depth directly with the visual features used for action prediction, rather than supplying it as an independent modality. Project page: https://aus.bot/research/stereopatch/.

</details>


### [223] [DIDO: Distilling Interaction-Centric Dynamics into One-Step Denoising for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15570)
*Jing Lyu,Shuanghao Bai,Runze Xiao,Zhenyu Liao,Wenxing Tan,Zihan Tang,Ruochuan Shi,Cheng Peng,Yuheng Ji,Yihao Wang,Badong Chen,Pengwei Wang,Zhongyuan Wang,Xiaoguang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DIDO，将多步视频模型收敛的交互动力学蒸馏到单步去噪，在保持操纵关键动力学的同时大幅降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World Action Models借助视频生成模型预测未来视觉动态以支撑机器人操纵，但多步迭代去噪带来额外延迟，不利于闭环控制；经验发现去噪过程中视觉内容收敛速率不同：静态背景结构早期成形，而夹爪与被操纵物体在首步后仍模糊，其交互动力学需后续去噪才显现。因此直接把多步视频模型截断为单步虽能保留场景结构，却丢失对操纵最关键的交互动力学。

Method: DIDO将多步视频模型收敛的动力学蒸馏进单步去噪，结合分布匹配蒸馏与以交互为中心的表示引导。除把多步生成压缩为一次前向，DIDO还用有监督的边界框视觉推理token显式建模夹爪、被操纵物体及其交互，并将目标物体在多个模型层的表示与预训练DINOv3编码器特征对齐。

Result: 在LIBERO上平均成功率99.0%，LIBERO-Plus上76.6%，RoboTwin上92.0%，并有效迁移到真实世界机器人操纵的长时程与泛化任务。

Conclusion: 以交互为中心的引导能在单步内同时保留相关实体及其未来动力学，同时显著降低推理延迟。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) use video generation models to predict future visual dynamics for robotic manipulation, but iterative denoising introduces additional latency for closed-loop control. We empirically find that visual content converges at different rates during denoising. Static background structure forms early, whereas the gripper and manipulated object remain blurry after the first step, with their interaction dynamics emerging only through subsequent denoising. Consequently, naively truncating a multi-step video model to one step preserves scene structure but loses the interaction-centric dynamics most critical for manipulation. To address this issue, we propose DIDO, which distills the converged dynamics of a multi-step video model into a single denoising step. DIDO combines distribution matching distillation with interaction-centric representation guidance. Beyond compressing multi-step generation into one forward pass, DIDO explicitly models the gripper, manipulated object, and their interaction using supervised bounding-box visual reasoning tokens. Additionally, DIDO aligns the target object's representations across multiple model layers with features from a pretrained DINOv3 encoder. This interaction-centric guidance helps the distilled model preserve both the relevant entities and their future dynamics in a single step, while substantially reducing inference latency. DIDO achieves an average success rate of 99.0\% on LIBERO, 76.6\% on LIBERO-Plus, and 92.0\% on RoboTwin, while also demonstrating effective transfer to long-horizon and generalization tasks in real-world robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [224] [An Information-Space Perspective to Scene Graph Sufficiency for Robotic Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15587)
*Başak Sakçak,Francesco Verdoja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在信息空间框架下形式化基于场景图的规划，通过信息映射定义派生场景图与商转移系统，给出场景图对任务规划充分性的两个条件，并用示例展示充分与不充分的约简场景图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂环境规划需要以对象、关系和可供性为表征的任务规范，场景图满足这一需求，但在大环境中规模过大而阻碍高效规划。已有的任务感知剪枝与层次抽象方法缺乏一个通用的、以任务为中心的"充分场景图"形式化定义，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在信息空间框架下对场景图上的规划进行建模，定义场景图转移系统及导航与操作的动作语义；通过信息映射合并与剪枝节点，引入派生场景图，并诱导出附加运动基元的商转移系统，以刻画合并节点上的高层动作。

Result: 将充分性刻画为两个条件：(i) 信息映射产生确定性商系统；(ii) 任务在派生轨迹上适定，从而保证在派生模型上找到的规划在最大系统上可行。并通过示例环境中的任务展示了充分与不充分的约简场景图。

Conclusion: 该工作为面向规划的充分场景图提供了通用的任务中心形式化框架，为场景图约简与抽象规划建立了理论基础。

Abstract: Planning in complex environments requires task specifications grounded in representations that capture objects, relations, and affordances; scene graphs meet this need, but their size in large environments hinders efficient planning. While task-aware pruning and hierarchical abstractions have been explored, a general, task-centric formalization of what constitutes a sufficient scene graph for planning remains open. This paper provides such a formalization by modeling planning over scene graphs within an information-spaces framework through the definition of scene graph transition systems and relevant action semantics for navigation and manipulation. We then introduce derived scene graphs via information mappings that merge and prune nodes and induce quotient transition systems augmented with motion primitives to capture higher-level actions over merged graph nodes. Sufficiency is characterized by two conditions: (i) the information mapping yields a deterministic quotient, and (ii) the task is well-posed over derived traces, ensuring plans found on the derived model are feasible on the maximal system. We illustrate the framework using a task over an example environment, showing both sufficient and insufficient reduced scene graphs.

</details>


### [225] [Flow-Matched Motion Priors: Online Optimal-Transport Rewards for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15631)
*Yilin Zou,Chenghua Liu,Chenglong Wu,Fanghua Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Flow-Matched Motion Priors (FMP)，用基于熵最优传输耦合与流匹配训练的标量奖励替代 AMP 判别器，在 G1 上实现更稳定、泛化的运动先验学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMP 使用对抗式判别器提供运动先验奖励，但当策略与专家支撑集相距较远时对抗目标变得无信息。朴素最优传输方法将匹配的专家后继平均为重心目标，跨步态相位平均会削弱目标关节运动。因此需要一种更稳健、可泛化的在线标量奖励。

Method: FMP 从当前 rollout 历史到专家动作库之间的路径学习在线标量奖励：用熵最优传输给出耦合，在每次策略更新前，沿 rollout 到专家的路径用流匹配训练神经势函数，并辅以端点梯度监督与相对价值校准。Actor 仅接收物理观测，奖励保持标量，与 AMP 一致。

Result: 受控奖励模型实验显示，FMP 在拟合 rollout 之外的泛化显著优于仅价值或仅端点拟合。在 Unitree G1 的 5000 万转移匹配实验中，FMP 从示范重置出发实现 0.727 m/s 稳定前行，从固定默认姿态出发为 0.338 m/s；固定姿态条件下跌倒 129 次，而仅端点对照为 243 次。相比静态分数梯度教师，动态流匹配在插值比例 0.25 和 0.50 处降低分数增量误差，且离线拟合时间减少 29%。

Conclusion: FMP 通过流匹配沿 rollout–专家路径学习标量奖励，避免了对抗目标无信息与重心平均削弱运动的问题，在真实机器人实验中比 AMP、重心 OT 奖励及嵌套消融更稳定，且计算更高效。

Abstract: Learning a motion prior requires a reward that guides a policy from its current behavior toward demonstrated motion. Adversarial Motion Priors (AMP) provide such a reward with a discriminator. However, adversarial objectives can become uninformative when policy and expert supports are far apart. A naive use of optimal transport (OT) averages matched expert successors into a barycentric target. Averaging across gait phases can weaken the target's joint motion. We introduce Flow-Matched Motion Priors (FMP), an online scalar reward learned from paths connecting current rollout histories to an expert motion bank. Entropic OT supplies the coupling. Before each policy update, we train a neural potential with flow matching (FM) along the rollout-to-expert paths, endpoint-gradient supervision, and relative-value calibration. The actor receives only physical observations and the reward remains a scalar, as in AMP. Controlled reward-model experiments show substantially better generalization beyond the fitting rollout than value-only or endpoint-only fitting. On Unitree G1, matched 50-million-transition experiments compare FMP with AMP, a barycentric OT reward, and nested ablations under demonstration and fixed-pose initialization. FMP produces stable forward walking at 0.727 m/s from demonstration resets and 0.338 m/s from a fixed default pose. In the fixed-pose condition, it incurs 129 falls versus 243 for the endpoint-only control. Against a static score-gradient teacher, dynamic FM reduces score-increment error at interpolation fractions 0.25 and 0.50 while using 29% less offline fitting time.

</details>


### [226] [Tracking the Ground: Online Lidar Identification of Robot-Induced Soil Deformation in Agricultural Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15667)
*Tom Montagnon,Johann Laconte,Benoit Thuilot,Wonjae Cho,Roland Lenain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于降阶参数化模型的框架，利用激光雷达观测在线量化并估计机器人行驶引起的土壤变形，实现土壤状态的连续可观测表征，为"土壤感知机器人"作业奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业面临诸多挑战，机器人系统可提升田间作业效率与可持续性。其中保持土壤健康至关重要，因为车辆与土壤的相互作用会破坏土壤结构并造成不良的地表变形。实现土壤感知机器人的关键一步是显式考虑车辆行驶如何使地面变形，然而土壤状态通常并未被视为一个变量。本文正是为填补这一空白而提出相应框架。

Method: 提出一个量化行驶引起土壤变形、并从激光雷达观测中在线估计其演化的框架。方法依赖一个降阶参数化模型，通过具有物理可解释性的参数来表征土壤行为，从而给出持续更新且可观测的土壤状态表征。并在不同土壤条件下开展了实验。

Result: 在不同土壤条件下的实验表明，该方法能够捕捉由机器人引起的土壤变形。

Conclusion: 通过使土壤响应在作业过程中可测量、可解释，该框架为土壤感知机器人作业建立了基础，其中估计出的状态可用于调整机器人行为，以减少土壤退化。

Abstract: Agriculture faces many challenges, and robotic systems can play an important role in addressing them by improving the efficiency and sustainability of field operations. Among these challenges, preserving soil health is a critical concern, as vehicle-soil interactions can degrade the soil structure and produce unwanted surface deformation. A key step toward soil-aware robotics is to explicitly account for how vehicle traffic deforms the ground, yet soil state is typically not treated as a variable. We address this gap by proposing a framework to quantify traffic-induced soil deformation and estimate its evolution online from lidar observations. The method relies on a reduced-order parametric model that represents the soil behavior via physically interpretable parameters, yielding a continuously updated and observable representation of soil state. Experiments conducted in different soil conditions demonstrate the ability of the approach to capture deformation induced by the robot. By making soil response measurable and interpretable during operation, the proposed framework establishes a basis for soil-aware robotic operation, in which the estimated state can be exploited to adapt robotic behaviors in order to reduce soil degradation.

</details>


### [227] [Volumetric Harmonic Field Navigation for Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15680)
*Shuxiu Jia,Amartya Mukherjee,Yating Yuan,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将预计算的三维调和场与受约束预测规划器耦合，在四旋翼上首次完成体调和场导航的物理验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂乱三维环境中的四旋翼导航需要全局引导，同时局部运动受碰撞与运动学限制。调和势能可从全局边值问题提供稠密引导，但如何把体积调和场与受约束的物理四旋翼运动耦合，仍是未解决的实验问题。

Method: 将预计算的体积调和场与受约束预测规划器耦合；规划器在预测位置查询该场，而非提取一条全局参考路径。

Result: 在结构化三维测试中，调和引导相比匹配的Dijkstra引导具有更大的最小间隙和更低的RMS加加速度，但路径更长；当两者使用同一通道时该规律依然成立。长迷宫测试覆盖远超单个预测时域的路线，Crazyflie试验验证了物理执行。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是体调和场导航在物理四旋翼上的首次演示；结果表明全局构建的调和引导可直接支持物理四旋翼上的局部受约束运动生成。

Abstract: Quadrotor navigation in cluttered 3-D environments requires global guidance while local motion remains subject to collision and motion limits. Harmonic potentials provide dense guidance from a global boundary value problem, but coupling a volumetric harmonic field to constrained physical quadrotor motion remains an open experimental problem. We couple a precomputed volumetric harmonic field with a constrained predictive planner that queries the field at predicted positions instead of extracting a global reference path. In Structured 3-D tests, harmonic guidance yields larger minimum clearance and lower RMS jerk than matched Dijkstra guidance, at the cost of longer paths; the same pattern remains when both methods use the same passage. Long maze tests span routes far beyond one prediction horizon, and Crazyflie trials validate physical execution. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first physical quadrotor demonstration of volumetric harmonic field navigation. The results show that globally constructed harmonic guidance can directly support local constrained motion generation on a physical quadrotor.

</details>


### [228] [Continuous Manifold-Decomposed Impedance Retargeting for Contact-Rich Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15716)
*Jiahao Liu,Kento Kawaharazuka,Tasuku Makabe,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CMDIR 将固定阻抗示教扩展为连续变阻抗控制器，并提出 TMIR 表示用于模仿学习监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将固定阻抗示教转化为连续可变阻抗控制器，并作为模仿学习的结构化监督。

Method: CMDIR 扩展 MDIR；TMIR 将演化任务坐标系与控制器指令配对；Demo-relative Compromise 动力学保留移动基座传输与度量失配，导出位移、反冲量与扰动敏感性准则；Quality-to-Fast 从开发路径编译求解器结构并多分辨率认证。

Result: 三个真实接触任务共 225 次试验中，完整 CMDIR 相比离散 MDIR 提升任务代理保持率，降低姿态偏差、力波动与峰值力；FastMPO 相比 C-MPO 提速 5.8–9.4 倍且闭环效果相当。

Conclusion: 下游实验表明完整 TMIR 监督界面可学习；成功执行中力波动与峰值力更低，但完成可靠性在不同任务与环境间仍不均衡。

Abstract: CMDIR extends Manifold-Decomposed Impedance Retargeting (MDIR) to transform fixed-impedance demonstrations into continuous variable-impedance controllers, which can also serve as structured supervision for imitation learning. Continuous Task-Manifold Impedance Representation (TMIR) pairs an evolving task frame with controller instructions. Demo-relative Compromise dynamics retain moving-basis transport and control/physical metric mismatch, yielding displacement, reaction-impulse, and perturbation-sensitivity criteria. Quality-to-Fast automatically compiles a solver structure from development paths within a predefined finite space, re-instantiates that structure for each demonstration, and certifies the resulting candidate by multi-resolution evaluation. Across 225 retargeted-controller trials in three real contact tasks, full CMDIR improves mean task-proxy retention and reduces mean pose deviation, force fluctuation, and peak force relative to discrete MDIR. FastMPO achieves a $5.8$--$9.4\times$ speedup over C-MPO with comparable closed-loop outcomes. Downstream experiments demonstrate learnability of the complete TMIR supervision interface; lower force fluctuation and peak force are observed among successful executions, while completion reliability remains uneven across tasks and environments.

</details>


### [229] [Bench2Dex: Benchmarking Visuo-Tactile Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation Across Dexterous Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15726)
*Zhenjie Yang,Yideng Zhang,Dongjie Zhang,Chenyu Jiang,Xianshuai Liu,Yufeng Li,Zuhao Ge,Xingyu Jiao,Zheng Zhang,Kaiyu He,He Wang,Yuwen Zhong,Yi Deng,Muyun Jiang,Xianliang Huang,Haisheng Su,Donghang Zhang,Jian Zhang,Xue Yang,Hongyang Li,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang,Xiaosong Jia,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出仿真基准Bench2Dex，覆盖12种灵巧手的视触觉双手操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉硬件设计不统一、仿真触觉与真实传感器存在差异，导致难以在一致实验环境下研究不同灵巧手上的视触觉操作。

Method: 设计共享仿真触觉接口，将局部接触几何转换为类图像触觉观测，统一不同手部形态的观测格式；构建含26个双手操作任务、约1.3K人类遥操作演示的基准，提供视觉、触觉、本体、动作和物体状态同步观测及可执行任务指标；并将7类扰动划分为不变轴与等变轴以评估鲁棒性。

Result: 在Bench2Dex上评估了ACT、Diffusion Policy、pi0.5和GR00T N1.5，报告了它们的性能与失效模式。

Conclusion: Bench2Dex为跨灵巧手的视触觉学习提供统一平台，不假设仿真触觉可替代真实触觉，而是在硬件与仿真仍在演进时为算法开发提供共享设置。

Abstract: Tactile sensing provides contact information that can be difficult to infer from vision alone, but tactile hardware for dexterous hands has not converged to a common design. Dexterous hands differ in finger structure, contact surfaces, and sensor layouts, while simulated tactile signals still differ from measurements produced by physical sensors. These factors make it difficult to study visuo-tactile manipulation across diverse dexterous hands within a consistent experimental setting. We present Bench2Dex, a simulation benchmark for visuo-tactile bimanual manipulation across 12 dexterous hands. We adapt existing robot models with a shared simulated tactile interface that converts local contact geometry into image-like tactile observations. The interface provides a consistent observation format across different hand morphologies without attempting to reproduce the output of a specific physical tactile sensor. Bench2Dex includes 26 bimanual manipulation tasks that involve tool use, articulated-object interaction, and multi-stage manipulation, together with about 1.3K human-teleoperated demonstrations. The benchmark provides synchronized visual, tactile, proprioceptive, action, and object-state observations, together with executable task metrics. For robustness, we group seven perturbation types into invariance axis, where the correct action does not change, and equivariance axis, where the correct action changes together with the perturbation. We evaluate ACT, Diffusion Policy, pi0.5, and GR00T N1.5 on Bench2Dex and report their performance and failure modes. Bench2Dex is meant as a platform for studying visuo-tactile learning across dexterous hands. It does not assume that simulated tactile observations can replace real tactile sensing; it offers a shared setting for algorithm development while tactile hardware and simulation models are still evolving.

</details>


### [230] [JEPLO: Joint-Embedding Predictive Learning for LiDAR-Based Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15770)
*Qihao Yuan,Yixuan Qiu,Ziyu Cao,Ming Cao,Kailai Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JEPLO是一个用于腿式机器人基于LiDAR的感知运动的单阶段无建图学习框架，通过PE-JEPA世界模型与CJTS师生训练流程实现仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR在腿式机器人感知运动中的探索少于RGB-D，且现有LiDAR方法多依赖显式建图。

Method: 提出关节嵌入预测学习框架JEPLO，包含本体-外部感知JEPA（PE-JEPA）世界模型，从机载原始LiDAR扫描学习预测性自我中心地形表示；并提出并发JEPA师生（CJTS）流程，在仿真中用深度强化学习与简单奖励训练由JEPA潜表示引导的运动策略。

Result: 实现成功的仿真到现实迁移，可全方位穿越多样地形（长楼梯、高箱），机载计算轻量；相比现有感知运动框架更鲁棒，尤其在遮挡、稀疏和噪声导致的感知退化下表现更优。

Conclusion: JEPLO能在挑战条件下保留任务相关信息，验证了无建图LiDAR感知运动的有效性，并开源实现、数据集与硬件设计。

Abstract: Light detection and ranging (LiDAR) remains less explored than RGB-D sensing for perceptive legged locomotion, and existing LiDAR-based approaches often rely on explicit mapping. We present JEPLO (Joint-Embedding Predictive learning for legged LOcomotion), a single-stage learning framework for mapping-free, LiDAR-based perceptive locomotion for legged robots. We introduce a proprio-exteroceptive JEPA (PE-JEPA) world model to learn predictive egocentric terrain representations from onboard observations, including raw LiDAR scans. A concurrent JEPA-teacher-student (CJTS) pipeline is further proposed to train a locomotion policy informed by JEPA latent representations in simulation using deep reinforcement learning with a simple reward formulation. The framework achieves successful sim-to-real transfer, enabling omnidirectional traversal of diverse terrains, including long staircases and high boxes, with lightweight onboard computation. Evaluations demonstrate greater robustness than existing perceptive locomotion frameworks, particularly under degraded perception caused by occlusion, sparsity and noise. Further analysis validates JEPLO's ability to retain task-relevant information under these challenging conditions. We open-source our implementation, experimental datasets, and hardware setup designs https://github.com/ASIG-X/JEPLO.

</details>


### [231] [Uncertainty-Guided Sparse Refinement for Action Chunking Transformer Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15840)
*Chenyang Wang,Yuntian Wang,Xiaoxiong Yang,Dingde Jiang,Siao Liu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出不确定性引导的稀疏细化框架UGR，针对动作分块策略中关键时间步预测误差主导失败的问题，仅对最不确定的时间步进行残差修正，在RoboTwin双臂操作任务上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
